**What his pictures showed undone, and the Writer's list** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «В комнате Писателя дай возможность регулировать ширину левой колонки со списком документов. Не вижу всех твоих правок по моим заказам. Набросал тебе скринов»). The Writer: an edge between the documents and the document (.wr-resizer, the Librarian's way of 1162): dragged, the width kept in yue2.wrSideW (200–640 px), ← → by 16 px, Home or a double click for its 280 px. The Librarian: no peek in the corner on hover any more (his picture had it open over the cards, the take's style and lyrics in it, which is what he had taken out of the player's tip); the sheet on click stays. The Score's tools follow its tabs (the gap that stretched between ABC and Print fixed at 8 px, the gaps 6 px), so the full screen keeps its row in the take's narrow column. The Writer's document in hand on a line of its own under the Writer's row (a flex break), a chip: ✎ its name, ✕ in a round of its own. The LoRA strength over the pointer 16 → 14 pt. Recite: his question said «59 lines do not fit and are left out» (120 lines of rap on a 2/4 score of 247 bars at 83 BPM): where eighths leave lines out and the score's unit can write a sixteenth (a native score's L:1/16), lay() lays them again a syllable a sixteenth (its breaths and the line's last syllable halved too) and offers that when it leaves fewer out; the question says which. The suite: the edge (→ 16 px kept, a double click 280 px), no peek after 700 ms on a card, the Score's gaps, the chip below the row, the tip at 18.67 px, a lyric of three lines on four bars laid in sixteenths with none left out (two left out in eighths). Patch 1268.

**The lyrics' keys, Ctrl+S and F5** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Пометка строк через Alt+K в редакторе не персистентна»; «Шорткат на попап/fullscreen редактора Лирики — Alt+E (перебиваем шорткат браузера). Блокируем браузерные Ctrl+S и Ctrl+Shift+S. Оба шортката сохраняют все изменения в текущей сессии. Если нет правок в комнатах — при F5 не выводим диалоговое окно»; «Если лирика поверх всего, и применил Shift+Tab — схлопывание этого попапа и переносимся в сам фрейм попап»). The marks were kept (yue2.lyrMarks.lyrics, with the text's signature) but looked for only at the start and on typing; the text the page puts into the box itself (the form after a reload, a take loaded) sends no input event, so they were never found: the box's own value setter looks for them now (the same text, its marks; another, none), as 1262 did for the score. Alt+E lifts the lyrics and puts them back (window capture, so neither the browser's menu nor the frames' keys see it); Shift+Tab over the lifted lyrics puts them into the form, lifts its frame and puts the cursor where it stood. Ctrl+S and Ctrl+Shift+S (the Writer's own Ctrl+S included) keep the form's draft, the settings on disk and the Writer's open document (HeresyWriter.flush), and say so. F5, Ctrl+R and Ctrl+Shift+R ask only after something was typed in a room (a trusted input in a field of the rooms or the lifted lyrics, since the page came or since Ctrl+S); else the page reloads at once. The suite: a mark back with its text and not with another, Alt+E both ways, Shift+Tab into the lifted form, Ctrl+S's words and the page counted unedited after it; the reload checks of 1202 type first (ruachEdited). Patch 1269.

**Draw is Stop** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Запустил два раза генерацию картинки в комнате Художника. Ноль выхлопа. Кнопку DRAW нужно заменять на STOP»). The lab (lab d7cbf2b): POST /artist/stop {id} marks the run's job stop (and cancel, so a run still waiting for its card or its turn leaves the queue: wait_card and the lab's turn raise Cancelled), and ends a drawing one's painter (the job keeps its process: terminated, killed after five seconds if it lingers); work() then ends the run «stopped», not failed, with the pictures it finished; a run not drawing answers 400. Tried on the test lab (its own KIT, the jobs on GPU1/GPU2): a run drawing on GPU1 (its first step painted) and one waiting behind it, both stopped in 1.1 s, no painter left, the card's memory back to 266 MiB; a second Stop: «this run is not drawing». The page: while a run waits or draws, Draw is Stop in the theme's colour for what ends something (the drawing run first, else the newest waiting); Ctrl+Enter draws no second run meanwhile; a stopped run says «stopped · N of M kept», and the room says so when it ends. The suite: Stop on a drawing run asks the lab for that run, it says stopped, the button is Draw again; the 1257 section draws its second run after its running one is done. Patch 1270.

**The Artist in three columns** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Канвас комнаты Художника можно разделить на три колонки, каждая регулируется по ширине. В левой промптинг с параметрами. В средней проявка изображения и активное выбранное как в InvokeAI, и ниже данные по прогону самого изображения, правая колонка — библиотека генераций. Справа у нас остаётся свёрнутой вкладка с takes»). The room's form, bar and runs moved into a grid (.ar-cols: var(--ar-l, 380px) 12px minmax(280px, 1fr) 12px var(--ar-r, 560px)), the outer two with edges as the Writer's (yue2.arL 280–640, yue2.arR 300–900, ← → by 16 px, Home or a double click their own widths), the prompt's column and the stage sticky; one column under 1280 px. The stage (#arStage, paintStage()): a run painting shows its step there at the stage's size (its shape kept, never taller than the window: fitStage, a ResizeObserver for a room shown after a hidden tab), with the run's data; a waiting one says so; else the picture picked in the library (a click there picks, InvokeAI's way, instead of opening the window over the page), else the newest; its thumb at once and the full PNG when it has come (1263's way), its shape and size, seed, painter, when, how long, which card, the prompt, and To the form, Set as cover, Download, Over the page, ☆ (last: the server log's chip sits over the row's start). The stage's picture, Over the page or a double click in the library opens the window over the page, walking the gallery as shown or the run. A run that ends puts its last picture on the stage; a picked picture stays while another run paints until the next Draw or the room entered again. The stage paints again only when what it shows changes (a key of run, step, picture, star and take), so the poll does not restart the picture's loading. Probed through the dev window on the test lab's ten pictures: the fox at 9:16 on the stage, the gallery on the right, its thumb marked. The suite: the three columns at 1920, a painting run's step on the stage with its data, the newest picture, a pick (no window over the page, its shape kept, its data, its thumb marked), the stage's picture over the page, the prompt's edge (→ 16 px kept, a double click 380 px); the checks of 1255, 1257 and 1263 open the window through the stage. Patch 1271.

**Six rooms, the icons by language, the log narrower, one TODO** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Спасибо за RC3. Огонь. На сайте обнови фавку. Комнат у нас уже шесть. Перепроверь в README.md и на сайте»; «Всё же на полной развёртке фрейм серверного лога очень широкий. ужми ширину на 150-200px»; «Составь глобальный TODO документ по всем нашим планам и идеям, с отметкой готовых пунктов… Этот TODO.md держи прямо в нашем рабочем репо. Этот чат не скрываем от паблика»). The visiting card: six rooms in its numbers, its heading and its line (the Artist paints the song's pictures), the Artist's card with the picture of its three columns, in seven languages (the catalogs' keys renamed with their translations); each language's page wears its own icon, the name's first letter on the logo's cloud (Р for ru, uk, be; Π for el; R for the rest), the icons' addresses versioned so a browser lets go of the old ones; a favicon.ico of our own beside the page (the server's shared favicons.conf still answers /favicon.ico for every site with its own icon, a year in the cache: a location of the site's own is Viktor's call on his server). The README: six rooms, the Artist in the rooms' table (the picture of its three columns, until the shots of 2.0.0). The unfolded server log with its cards 900 → 720 px, tall 1160 → 980. TODO.md in the release's root: the chats of 30.09–09.10, the finish-line notes, the audits and Viktor's first list of 28.09 gathered, done ✅ with its release or patch, open ☐, his call ❓, ideas 💡; found again on the way: the sound when a run ends (his first list), the page offline (abcjs and the fonts from CDNs), the transcriber's first bar, REAPER's lyric events, the fewer requests during a render. The suite: the dock's width 700–740 at 1920. Patch 1272.

**The logo and the icon for the languages planned next** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Пока горячо, по языкам, что в плане, допиши фавки и лого, чтобы к этому больше не возвращаться»). heresy-logo-words.js carries the words for French (ROUAH STUDIO, a proposal: French writes the sound ou), Portuguese (RUACH ESTÚDIO), German (RUACH STUDIO), Japanese (ルアハ スタジオ) and Chinese (鲁阿赫 工作室), and the icon's letter for the last two (ル, 鲁; the Latin ones keep R); the brand's public i18n (src/brand) has them, and the visiting card's build makes the icon of each language it is given. The languages themselves come later (TODO.md). Patch 1273.

**The page needs no internet** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «abcjs и шрифты ложи всё в репо. abcjs периодически проверять на новые релизы. Минимум интернета из Студии на локальной машине»; «Добавь ещё в движок шрифты Raleway, Roboto, Lato, если какого-то из них нет»). abcjs 6.7.1 (MIT, from npm, its licence beside it) is in build/tools/console/vendor/, and build.sh's page step inlines vendor/*.js into a script and vendor/*.css into a style as it does the heresy-*.js; cdnjs and Google Fonts are gone from index.html. vendor/fonts.css holds the faces of the ten families (Noto Sans, Noto Sans Mono, IBM Plex Sans, IBM Plex Mono, Bodoni Moda, Space Grotesk, Michroma, and Raleway, Roboto, Lato at his word), their Latin, Cyrillic and Greek subsets: 99 faces in 45 WOFF2 files, 1.3 MB (Lato has no Cyrillic or Greek and Raleway no Greek: the page falls back there). The lab serves them from lab/fonts/ (OFL.txt with each family's notice) at GET /fonts/NAME.woff2 with a year's immutable cache (send_file takes a cache header now). Engine → Appearance lists the ten as the app's own; a font of the app's own is never listed again among the computer's (the measure took Noto Sans, and would take Roboto and Lato, for installed once their faces are in the page). heresy/tools/check-vendored.sh: abcjs against npm's latest (--abcjs takes it), --fonts takes the fonts again with the same subsets. The suite: no remote script or stylesheet, the faces from /lab/fonts/, abcjs in the page; the fonts' menu with the ten and none of them among the computer's. Patch 1274.

**A sound when a run ends** (Viktor 28.09.2026, his first list for the studio: «звуковое оповещение при окончании генерации»). 🔔 in the player after Play on (on by default, kept in yue2.runChime, Lucide's bell): chime() makes two sine notes in the page (WebAudio, no file), a fifth up (A5, E6) when a run is done and down (E5, A4) when it failed, each fading in 0.9 s, at a fifth of the player's volume and none when the player is muted or at nothing. The Creator's runs ring through chimeFor(): a song, a score planned, a transcription, a new version; not a run from elsewhere (the API, another browser), not one that ended while the page was away, not a cancelled one, and not when the new song starts playing by itself (Play new takes: the song is the sound); a queue of runs (versions, runs started one after another) is heard once, when its last one ends, a failure at once. The Artist rings when a run ends while its room looks on (not a stopped one), the Refiner when the chain it started is done or stopped at a step, the Trainer when a training run ends done or failed while its room looks on (a toast says so too now). The suite: the button and its bell, the sound up and down, none muted, at nothing or off (kept, and back); the failed run rings low, the cancelled ones nothing, the song that plays itself nothing, a song done with Play new takes off rings up. Patch 1275.

**Find and replace** (Viktor 09.10.2026, in his audit of the Creator: «В редактор можно добавить F4/Ctrl+H, с учётом регистра и без»). heresy-find.js: F4 or Ctrl+H in a song's box (the boxes Ctrl+F knows) opens the find bar with a second row, the words to put instead (#findR), *Replace* and *All*; from the bar's own find field the keys add the row. Enter in it puts the words in place of the match in hand and goes on to the next after them, Ctrl+Enter replaces every match at once (the text rebuilt and put in as one insertText, so one Ctrl+Z takes it all back; the box keeps its scroll; the bar says how many were replaced); each replacement goes in as typed (execCommand, the lyrics editor's way of 1169). A match keeps covering a stress mark, so обещано replaces обе́щано whole. **Aa** (Alt+C from either field) finds only as the case is typed, kept in yue2.findCase: fold() and pattern() keep the case then, and his hand's kin stay kin in capitals (О finds O, Е finds Ё). The bar wraps into two rows (up to 440 px). The suite: F4 opens both rows; any case finds three, Aa one (kept); Enter replaces it, Alt+C turns the case off, Ctrl+Enter replaces the two left (counted), one undo brings them back; a stressed word replaced whole; Ctrl+H opens it too. Patch 1276.

**The lyrics as plain text** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «Кнопку экспорта/импорта содержимого редактора в голый txt формат, авто имя файла по Title трека»). Two buttons beside ⤢ in the Lyrics head (Lucide's file-down and file-up). The first saves the lyrics through downloadText as «Title.txt»: the signs a file name cannot hold (\ / : * ? " < > | and the control characters) become spaces, the spaces run together, a leading dot goes, 120 characters at most, «lyrics.txt» without a Title; UTF-8, the line ends as LF and one at the end. The second takes a .txt through a hidden file input: a byte-order mark, Windows line ends and blank lines at the end dropped; a file with a character UTF-8 cannot read (U+FFFD after decoding: a cp1251 file, say) or longer than the box's 10 000 is refused with a toast that says why, never put in cut or garbled; with words in the box it asks first (*Replace*); the text goes in as typed over the whole box, so one Ctrl+Z gives the old words back; an empty Title takes the file's name. The buttons stop their click, so the Lyrics head does not fold. The suite: the name and the bytes of a save; a .txt with a BOM and CRLF put in after the question, the Title kept, one undo back; a cp1251 file refused out loud; an empty Title filled and an empty box not asked. Patch 1277.

**A second and a third tag on a line** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «`[` вторым/третьим в одной строке после валидного тега. Авто пробел между тегами в одной строке. Но такое только с подсказкой несекционных тегов»). heresy-complete.js: context() finds a «[» after one or two whole tags at a line's start too (chain), and fill() offers offerChain() then: the cheat-sheet's «Who sings» rows (Male Vocals ×25, Female ad-libs, the narrator, Spoken: …) with Female Vocals after Male Vocals, and Break and Silence at the end, the three marked TEST (none of the 110 examples writes them; he writes [Break] [Silence]); no section among them. A «[» typed right after a «]» of such a line gets a space before it (spaceBefore(), as typed, so Ctrl+Z takes it back); Ctrl+Space there types « [»; a chained tag goes in without a new line, and a «…» in it is left selected for the words in its place. A fourth tag on a line is offered nothing. The suite: [Interlude] then «[» gives «[Interlude] [» and the list (Male Vocals first, Break and Silence there, no section); br picks Break, Enter keeps it on the line; a third the same way; a fourth opens nothing and gets no space; Ctrl+Space after [Chorus]; [Spoken: …] with its «…» selected; a line's start still offers the sections. Patch 1278.

**Paragraphs with air between them** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «По всей студии добавь спейсинг между параграфами в тултипах, гайдах и читшитах. Так красивее типографически, но не сломай редактор лирики»). A plain tip (data-tip) whose text has line ends is drawn as paragraphs (tipParagraphs in app.js: .tip-p, 0.45 em apart, 0.8 em after a blank line; the tip's white-space normal, each paragraph pre-line), the line ends kept as text between them, so what the tip says reads the same to whoever reads its textContent; one line stays plain text. The rich tips kept their 7 px. The guide's items, each a paragraph, 0.55 em apart (3 px before); the cheat-sheet's «How the probes were made» in three paragraphs (the probes; what an instrument does to the style; an unlucky seed), its translations in the six languages split at the same sentences. The lyrics' box, the copies of its layout that the marks and the find measure, and the Style's box are not touched (a textarea draws no air between its paragraphs). The suite: a tip of three lines (a blank before the last) as three paragraphs, 0, 6.3 and 11.2 px apart, its text unchanged, one line plain; the guide's items 8 px apart; the cheat-sheet's prose in three. Patch 1279.

**The take's menu in the player** (TODO.md, from the rooms' audit). playerTakeMenu() in app.js: a right click on the player's wave (#wave) or on its picture and title (.playbar-id) opens takeMenu() for the take in the player (STATE.playerTake), the menu a right click on it in a list opens (Play or Pause first); a take kept only in this tab (no library) has none. A left click on the wave still jumps there; the right one does not. The suite: a take opened from the list, a right click on its wave opens the menu near the pointer and the time stays, one on its title opens it too. Patch 1280.

**The voice's gender at a click** (from SUNO v6, Viktor's screenshots of 09.10.2026; TODO.md: «Vocal gender at a click (male, female): its words put into the Style»). ♂ and ♀ (Lucide's mars and venus) at the start of the Style's head end (#styleLabelEnd, before the WARNING and the cheat-sheet's ?). The Style says a gender when it holds the word male or female (male vocals, Female Vocals, raspy male, female alto): that one is lit. A click on the other turns that word alone (whispery Female Vocals → whispery Male Vocals, the case and the words around kept); with no gender in the Style, «male vocals» or «female vocals» go in as a tag of the first line, before a closing «… BPM» (BPM stays last), else at its end; a click on the lit one takes out the tag that says it (with its words: «polished female vocals» goes whole). The new Style goes in as typed over the whole box, so one Ctrl+Z gives the old one back; the head does not fold. The words are the ones the approved style probes use (male vocals, female vocals). The suite: female vocals before 96 BPM, ♂ turns them, ♂ again takes them out, an undo brings them back, the case kept on a second line untouched, an empty Style, «raspy male» lit, the head not folded. Patch 1281.

**Sections on the wave** (from SUNO v6; TODO.md: «Sections on the wave: the take's sections from its score… as labelled blocks over the player's waveform; a click on one goes there»). When the player loads a take (loadPeaks), loadWaveSections() takes its score: its own from its request when it has one (has_score, getRequest's cache), else the one written from its sound (GET /lab/score, asked once a take); HeresyAbc.markers gives each «% section» start in seconds at the score's tempo. drawWave() lays a thin line at each start (24 % ink) and the section's name in small capitals at the foot of its block, on a light ground, where the name has room (else the line alone); the time under the pointer reads «1:23 · chorus»; a click within 6 px of a line goes to the section's very start, elsewhere where it lands. A Direct take with no score has none. SUNO's Song Editor draws its sections as blocks; ours come from the score the music was made from. The suite: a score's sections as times (intro at 0, the verse two bars of 100 BPM later, 4.8 s); a click 3 px by a line lands on its start, one far from any where it is. Patch 1282.

**Engine → Folders** (Viktor 09.10.2026: «время в движок Студии допилить кастомные пути для пользовательских данных… Предусмотри, чтобы RC3 автоматом подтягивал существующие пути развёртки, и в Engine — опции по изменению локации с переносом данных с одного места в другое»). The card over the lab's places.py of 1261 (heresy-places.js), a row of its own under Updates, About still the last card. Each folder of the user's work a row: Songs (with the workspaces fetched from Hugging Face), Trash, Pictures, Writer, what it holds, where it lives (in the studio's folder, or linked from it), its files and size (counted by the lab in the background, «counting…» meanwhile) and the room left on its disk. **Move…** asks where (the studio's own dialog, up to 1000 characters; a Windows path under WSL), GET /lab/places/plan says what the move will do or why not (no folder made out of a typo, not inside itself, not inside the studio's folder, the room with a gigabyte to spare), and only a second yes POSTs /lab/places/move; while it runs the card says its phase (counting, copying with its percent, bytes and files, checking, switching), every other move waits, and the card asks every 2 s while it is seen; when it is done it says where the old copy stays, renamed, to be removed after a look. **Back home** moves a linked folder into the studio's folder again; **Link again** where the record knows a place the studio's folder does not point to (a disk mounted late, a fresh clone given the old user/). A test page (?scope=…) sends its scope and the lab moves nothing. The suite: four rows from a mocked lab (a linked one with Back home, one not there with Link again and no Move), Move… asked, planned (another disk: 2.0 GB, 100 GB free), moved on the second yes, its progress «Copying Songs: 50 %…», the other moves off meanwhile. Patch 1283.

**The Artist's gallery in even tiles** (Viktor 09.10.2026, his screenshot of 21:06: «Колонка с картинками у Художника - всё свалено в кучу. Сделай честный тайлинг»). The gallery laid its pictures in a wrapping row of one height, each as wide as its shape: squares, wide ones and tall ones side by side, the rows ragged. Now .ar-gallery is a grid of equal columns (as many as the size, S 130, M 200, L 300 px, lets the column hold, filling it edge to edge), every tile the same box: square while every shape is shown, the shape's own when one is (--cell from the filter: 16:9 tiles for 16:9), each picture whole in its tile (object-fit contain on the field's ground), none cropped. The suite: seven pictures of three shapes in one size of square tile, in equal columns, the last ending at the column's edge; the 16:9 filter gives 16:9 tiles. Patch 1284.

**The downloads made whole** (Viktor 09.10.2026, his first install through Pinokio, on his laptop's Quadro RTX 5000 of 16 GB: «Некоторые вещи не скачались. Смотри в логе. Sliders и другое»; «LoRA пока что не скачались. Только Standard VAE»; «в логе `the Kit's models` - они нахер не Kit's. YuE2 - это m-a-p's models»; «BF16 не скачалась… можно ли будет со студии докачать BF16?»). His log: the backbone, the VAE and SheetSage2 came (Q8_0 for the card, rightly), the two extra decoders' checkpoints too; the sliders failed after 1.2 GB, then Whisper and UniverSR failed at once, and the install stopped. Read: heresy/fetch-heresy.sh fetched ntc-ai/yue2-particle-sliders whole, 4.1 GB in 974 files (its archive, evidence and samples), while convert-extras.py makes the sliders from the 16 weights its catalog names, 245 MB; a burst of a thousand requests earns a 429 from Hugging Face, and both repos after it loaded fine at the same revisions an hour later. Every download's own words went to /dev/null, so «failed» said nothing more. Now: the sliders take their catalog, licences and weights only; every download runs through again() (its output kept in tmp/fetch-heresy.last.log, tried once more after 30 s) and a failure says why() (the downloader's last two lines); a folder that differs from the pin but whose .cache/huggingface/download holds the pinned revision is ours unfinished, so the next run goes on with it (it was «kept, --replace fetches it»: Install again could never finish it); the legacy and blend decoders and the sliders are made into GGUF right after their checkpoints by convert-extras.py alone (the Kit's convert-models.sh, which the install advised, wants the backbone's own checkpoints, which no install fetches), with the Kit's links build/checkpoints and build/models made where a released tree has none (and ignored in its .gitignore). The LoRAs come with the install: the Kit's library through tools/download-loras.sh (11 adapters from 8 repos, 1.3 GB) and the studio's own through heresy/fetch-ruach-loras.py (goldhub/Ruach_Studio_LoRAs at its pinned revision, its 15 safetensors, 0.8 GB, each at loras/<folder>/<file> as the takes of the 💎 sets name them, its samples left on Hugging Face). --backbone BF16|Q8_0|Q6_K|Q5_K_M fetches another backbone beside the install's, asked with its size (and, on a card of compute 7.5, a word that BF16 runs there without tensor cores, slower than Q8_0); Pinokio's More models offers BF16 where it is missing. fetch-models.sh names its first part YuE2: m-a-p's models as GGUF (Serveurperso/YuE2-GGUF). The root's .gitignore keeps .venv-art/ and what Pinokio writes beside its scripts (logs/, ENVIRONMENT, its agents' notes) out of git, so Update keeps working. The release's scan for this machine's traces lets the fonts' licence pass (Raleway's names its designer with a mail address). Tried on a released tree on the Forge: --check names all 25 missing; the fetch brought them, failed 0 (sliders 246 MB, the decoders and the 16 sliders made, Whisper, the stems, UniverSR, the Kit's library, the 15 adapters); a slider's weight taken away came back by itself at the next run; --check --backbone BF16 says it is missing with its size. Patch 1285 (heresy and its root only, nothing in the page).

**The veil** (Viktor 09.10.2026, the veil in Pinokio: «OSEM с девизом сделай на 20% крупнее, дай больше пространства между Лого и OSEM, а также сам прогресс бар опусти в подвал, 160 пикселов от пола»). .boot-osem a fifth larger (clamp 14.4 px, 1.14 vw, 19.2 px) and 32 px further from the logo; .boot-line absolute at the veil's foot, 160 px above the window's floor; «Loading the Sound Heresy» stays under the slogan. The suite measures it at 1920: 19.2 px, 56 px under the logo, the line's bottom at 920 of 1080. Patch 1286.

**Memory within N GB** (Viktor 09.10.2026, the studio on his 16 GB laptop card: «Ручной пресет памяти не даёт мне выбрать объём VRAM из доступных 16GB VRAM. В реалии я бы на этом железе выбрал бы 14GB, чтобы не было OOM»). Engine → Compute's memory preset lists Auto (the whole card), then Auto within N GB for every N from a gigabyte under the card down to 8 (each with the context and VAE tiles autoKnobs gives that much), then Manual; kept as yue2.memPreset «auto:14», shown again while the knobs are still that budget's. The backbone and keeping models loaded stay the user's. The Compute card's hint says, when BF16 is not on the machine, where it comes from (Pinokio's More models, or heresy/fetch-heresy.sh --backbone BF16). Patch 1287.

**The seconds' pattern and Ctrl+N** (Viktor's screenshot of 21:29, the studio in Russian under Pinokio: the 💎 sets' window titled «The studio's 💎 set c», its button «Open the set c»; «можно будет в Пинокио Ctrl+N использовать»). The six catalogs kept «{0}s» for seconds («12s» → «12 с»): {0} took any text, so every English string not yet translated that ends in s lost it («с» in Russian, Ukrainian and Belarusian, « s» in Greek, Spanish and Italian). The key is «{#0}s» now, a number only. Ctrl+N (by its key, any layout) is New song, in the Creator, asked first when the form holds words: Pinokio's window hands the page the key, a browser keeps it for its own new window (Pinokio's code binds no shortcut of its own). The suite: the pattern in all six languages (an untranslated «… sets» left as it is, «12s» and «2.5s» seconds); Ctrl+N from the Writer opens the Creator and asks. Patch 1288.

**Models and LoRAs in the Engine** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «И нужно в Engine добавить докачку моделей и LoRA»). The card stands under Sliders (About, Updates and Folders a row lower) over the lab's new weights.py, which runs heresy/fetch-heresy.sh, the one list of what the studio needs beside itself, with its pins, its sizes checked file by file, its resume of a download cut short and its second try after half a minute; nothing of that is written twice. fetch-heresy.sh learns --only extras,whisper,stems,upscale,loras (none: only what the extras' options ask for), and each part's heading says its name for it. A whole run, the install's own too, keeps its verdicts in tmp/fetch-heresy.list (a line each: the verdict, what, its words; a part's line starts with ##; the counts and when at the end), put in place only when the run is over: the card shows the last whole run at once and asks no one (the rule «Минимум интернета из Студии на локальной машине»: a check asks Hugging Face for the pinned lists only on Check again, and only what is not cached). A part not whole lists what is not here and has its Fetch (--only that part, then a check, so the list says where everything stands); Fetch what is missing takes them all; each is asked first with the part's size, measured on the Forge (the decoders and sliders 1.3 GB and as much again made from it, Whisper 2.9, the stems 0.9, UniverSR 0.2, the LoRAs 2.1). The extras a row each, their variants with fetch-heresy.sh's own sizes and notes, a variant here by its files (a sharded model by every shard its index names): the backbone BF16, Q8_0, Q6_K or Q5_K_M (BF16 said slower without the tensor cores of Ampere), the style listener Q8_0, Q5_K_M, Q4_K_M or bf16 with the VRAM each needs, the Artist's painter by the biggest card (Krea 2 Muse Q4, or Q4 and Q8, from 16 GB; SDXL below), the trainer's base bf16 or int8; fetched with --only none and the option, --yes after the page asked. One run at a time; a test page (?scope=) is refused; /health counts a download under way among the lab's jobs, so a restart of the lab waits for it. While it runs, its words (a verdict coloured) and how much less the disk has free than when it began; when it is over, what came, and where a backbone, a decoder or the sliders came (the engine reads those when it starts; LoRAs it reads each time, and the page asks for them again at once), Restart the engine through the lab's /studio/restart, or, under Pinokio or ./start.sh, a word to stop and start it there. And .engine-grid .btn[hidden] is hidden now: .btn's own display (inline-flex) won over the attribute, so Updates' Update and What's new showed with nothing new (getComputedStyle: display flex with hidden set), as the GPU card's rule already said for its own button. Tried end to end on a second lab over the released test tree: one LoRA taken away, Check again named it missing, its part's Fetch brought it back (56 MB, 6 s) and the check after it said all here. The suite: the card's place, the last run, a part's rows and Fetch, the extras' variants and a variant here, the questions with their sizes (No sends nothing), the run's words with every button off, the end with and without the restart, and no Engine button shown while hidden. Patch 1289.

**The converted models come converted, from goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «зачем нам конвертировать модели каждый раз? Может в репо положим сконвертированные и пропишем их в коде вместо тех, что ты конвертируешь каждый раз у юзера?»; «Да, заливай goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2… Но... тысячу раз перепроверь, что именно мы используем как рабочие веса, и в этот goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 у нас идёт только продакшн»; «checkpoints/, по сути, это артефакт YuE2-Kit репо… Если checkpoints останется пустым, тогда его в gitignore»). What the engine runs on, read from the live studio's own command line: the four backbones, the default decoder and SheetSage2 Q8_0, as GGUF from Serveurperso/YuE2-GGUF and never converted here; the legacy and blend decoders (--vae legacy, --vae blend) and the 16 sliders with their catalog (--sliders), these three converted on every install from 1.3 GB of checkpoints (m-a-p/YuE2-Vae-legacy @ 5ddd12f, Mothersuperior/YuE2-Vae-merge-0.666 @ b00bdfc, ntc-ai/yue2-particle-sliders @ 33cf42f) by convert-extras.py, which wants gguf, numpy and safetensors in the user's Python and the Kit's links build/checkpoints and build/models (what failed on his laptop). The conversion is deterministic: the Forge's production copies of 30.09.2026 and a fresh conversion of 09.10.2026 match by SHA-256, all 19 files. Those 19 alone went into the public goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 with the decoders' licence (CC BY-NC 4.0, as M-A-P publishes it), their third-party notices, the sliders' code licences, the card (heresy/docs/hf-models-v2-README.md) and SHA256SUMS; commit 434cc28, every file checked against the sums on the Hub, the guard against his voice passed (0 adapters among 42 of his). SheetSage2-F32, which the engine does not load, stays out, as do the backbones Serveurperso publishes already; goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models stays as it is for the releases before. fetch-heresy.sh's extras part is hf_into now: the files of a repo laid out as the studio's folders, straight into place at the pin, what is missing fetched (the downloader keeps a file that matches by its SHA-256), a file that differs from the pin kept and said (--replace sets it aside into tmp/replaced/ and fetches the pinned one). Tried on the released test tree: 1.3 GB in 23 s, byte for byte the production files; a slider taken away came back alone; one changed by hand was kept, and --replace put the pinned one in its place; the live studio's own files were found here as they are. On the way, its local list of differing files was first named odd, the very counter say_odd adds to: under set -u the line died on «sliders: unbound variable»; it is wrong now. start.sh starts the sliders only when their GGUFs are there (the release keeps their catalog alone) and says where they come from; the release ignores checkpoints/ whole and ships no checkpoints/.gitkeep (the listener and the trainer's base make the folder when asked), and /.cache/ (the downloader's notes beside the files it puts in place). The Models card says 1.3 GB for the extras (lab 0ee52bf). Patch 1290.

**The server log's frame keeps its height** (Viktor 10.10.2026, the studio under Pinokio: «из-за графа GPU во фрейме лога сервера вообще прикол… после этого не захотел фрейм сворачиваться в его исходную высоту. А теперь, началось с половины экрана, и с каждым тиком графа GPU фрейм Лога сервера опускается на пиксел или два вниз… где-то тик баров перебивает высоту фрейма»). heresy-gpulive.js set the dock's --gl-h to the cards' column's scrollHeight at every tick, and the column's height is --gl-h itself: at full height the column was the window's, so --gl-h took 60 % of it and kept it after folding back; and a border-box column whose content is shorter has a scrollHeight of its height less its borders, so every second took the column's 1 px border off, the frame sinking until its floor. Now the height is the last card's bottom (with the column's padding and border), set only when it changes; and a card's roles wrap to a second line, so one card doing all three says «Studio · Training · Lab jobs» whole. The suite: one card with all three roles, full height and back (222 → 871 → 222 px), still 222 four ticks later. Patch 1293.

**The Trainer's knobs explained, measured, and a run that goes on** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «В тренере смотри на RANK - подсказка под полем инлайн. И можно ли на завершённом тренинге продолжить по большему количеству эпох?»; «В гайде по LoRA Trainer ты не объяснил все параметры. Я так и не смог полностью сориентироваться по AR WEIGHT и AR ANCHOR, Пересмотри этот гайд, пожалуйста»). From lab/trainer/train.py: every step's loss is flow + ar_loss_weight × ar_ce + ar_kl_weight × KL(base ‖ adapted); the music half's cache reaches the sound half detached, so the flow loss never trains the music half, and the KL is reckoned only when the AR weight is above 0; AdamW (eps 1e-6, weight decay 1e-2) with the music half's adapter at lr × ar_lr_multiplier, both halves' gradients clipped together at 1.0. AdamW sizes every step by its own running gradients, so the size of the AR weight barely matters: what counts is whether it is 0, the anchor's share of it, and AR speed. Measured on GPU2 (Viktor's own run, trained on GPU1 meanwhile, untouched): eight runs of 40 steps on voice-natalia-korneva (60 readings), rank 16, lr 0.0001, one seed; the size of the adapter's change to the music half's matrices (B·A) and how far each run's came out from the first's: 0.5 · 0.2 · 0.5 1.32 (the same again: 1 %); 1.0 · 0.4 · 0.5 1.36 (5 %); no anchor 1.44 (23 %); the anchor as large as the weight 1.21 (17 %, its KL halved); AR speed 1.0 2.32 (99 %, its KL 0.443 against 0.257); AR weight 0: all zeros. The sound half moved 4 to 13 % between rows against 2 % for the same run twice (the shared clipping). VRAM 10.5 GB with the KL, 9.1 without, 8.2 with the music half off (rank 16, bf16). One run went on from its save at step 40 to 60 («going on from …_000000040.safetensors»). The guide's Trainer chapter in seven languages: section 3 every field of the form in its order (what a step is: a 60-second window for the sound half, the song from its start for the music half; whole epochs, 2016 tracks for 12 epochs are 24 192 steps; the saves and the optimizer's state; the learning rate as the sound half's step; Mode and separation for AI-Toolkit) with the kinds' knobs in a table; «Rank and the Other Knobs» keeps the rank and the sample rate; a new section, «The Music Half's Three Knobs», the lesson and the anchor, the loss, each knob, the measured table and what to turn when; Telemetry its tiles (ar_kl), the stacked curves, the log. The form: the rank's share and size are its tip's first line (data-tip-head), not a line under it; the AR knobs' tips say the same as the guide, and «How they work ›» opens its section; a finished run of the studio's own trainer goes on to more epochs from its ⋯ menu (asked how many in all, four more offered; the same run, set and knobs, the steps for them: the lab's run of the same name takes the last save and the optimizer's state); Steps' max 20 000 → 1 000 000. Suite 544/0, live 544/0, i18n 18/0, player 3/0. Patch 1300 (kitchen 10ad075).

**The Artist paints from a reference picture; one card for everything, tried for real** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Артиста по модели InvokeAI постарайся на максимум вывести со всем возможным ахуем»; «На единственной видеокарте нужно автоматом выгружать модели… Иначе никак»). diffusers has no image to image for Krea 2 (Krea2Pipeline paints from noise; its modular blocks were not tried), so artist_job.py does what InvokeAI does: the picture covers the shape and is cut from the middle, goes through Krea 2's VAE (the Wan 2.1 VAE, a frame of one), is normalized as the pipeline decodes (latents · std + mean, here the other way), packed, and noised to a sigma; the pipeline then runs the tail of its own 8 sigmas from there (the scheduler shifts them with mu 1.15, Krea 2 Turbo being distilled: the 8 sigmas 1 … 0.125 begin at 1, 0.96, 0.91, 0.84, 0.76, 0.66, 0.51 once shifted; the strength asked takes the nearest, two steps at least). Tried on GPU2 with Krea 2 Muse Q4 on one of Viktor's covers (heresy/MISC/claude-artist/img2img): 0.51 kept the picture and changed its lettering (11 s), 0.76 kept the composition and took the prompt's stone walls (20 s), 0.91 painted the prompt's scene in the picture's light (28 s), 8.6 GB; the job itself through a copy of the lab beside the real artwork (kit-test): a 1:1 and a 16:9 from one 1:1 picture, 21 and 26 s, its live preview at every step. artist.py takes the picture as a data URL (PNG, JPEG or WebP by its first bytes, 40 MB at most) or as {id, file} of an earlier run, with the strength (0.2 to 1.0), keeps it beside the run (ref.png|jpg|webp, served with the run's pictures), and the run says where it came from; each picture's row says its first sigma. The page: the form's reference place under the prompt (drop, paste in the room, Choose…, or the stage's «As reference»), its thumbnail, «How much it may change» kept in the browser, ✕; Draw sends it, «Again» sends the run's own; the stage says «from a reference · 0.76». Suite 555/0, live 555/0, i18n 18/0, player 3/0; pictures of the form by tools/claude-ref-probe.mjs. Patch 1305 (kitchen 64e16f7, lab 8482b54). And 1297 tried against a real engine on GPU2 (heresy/MISC/claude-vram/test_unload_real.py: its own port and outputs, Keep models loaded on): after a song it held 6956 MiB of models and the card 14.8 GB in all; the lab's free_gpu() asked for 2 GB more than was free, only GPU2 allowed: the engine let its models go (said: «the engine's models left its card for the real test (6956 MB)»), the card fell to 575 MiB and the job had it, in 4.9 s.

**The Artist repaints only what a mask says** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Артиста по модели InvokeAI постарайся на максимум вывести со всем возможным ахуем»). Inpainting on the same latents as the reference (1305), as InvokeAI does it with models that have no inpainting pipeline of their own: the mask (white repaints) covers each shape as the reference does, goes to the packed latents' tokens (at the latent's size, softened by one latent pixel, each token the largest over its 2 × 2 patch), and a callback at every step puts the kept tokens back as the reference noised to the step's next sigma (the scheduler's own; the last puts back the reference itself), chained before the live preview's; the finished picture takes the reference's own pixels outside the mask, feathered by 12 px, so nothing outside moves. Tried on GPU2 with Krea 2 Muse Q4, strength 1.0, a round mask over a cover's fireplace and «a tall gothic stained glass window…»: a stained glass window in the log wall, the rest untouched, the seam soft (38 s). artist.py takes the mask with a reference only (a PNG data URL, or the run's own mask.png for «Again»), keeps it beside the run, and the run says «masked». The page: «Mask…» beside the reference opens the picture over the page with a red layer painted by the pointer (Paint and Erase, B and E; the brush, its ring following the pointer, [ and ], kept; Invert; Clear; «No mask»; Done or Enter; Esc), the mask kept as a PNG at the picture's own size, «Mask ✓» and the thumbnail's red; a new reference drops it; Draw sends it, «Again» the run's own. Suite 558/0, live 558/0, i18n 18/0, player 3/0; pictures of the editor by tools/claude-mask-probe.mjs. Patch 1306 (kitchen 6763cf9, lab 34266b5).

**The Artist paints around a picture** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Артиста по модели InvokeAI постарайся на максимум вывести со всем возможным ахуем»). Outpainting on the mask's machinery (1306): with «Paint around it» each shape holds the whole reference in its middle (contained, not covered and cut); under the edges it leaves, the VAE sees the reference stretched over the shape and blurred (48 px), so the new edges begin in its colours; the mask is the edges, reaching softly 6 % into the picture (blurred: the new paint meets the old across a band), with the user's own mask placed as the picture is; the run paints from the noise there (strength 1.0, the middle kept by the mask) and keeps the reference's pixels by the same soft mask. Tried on GPU2 with Krea 2 Muse Q4, one of Viktor's square covers into a 16:9 with «a cozy wooden cabin interior…»: with a hard edge first, the walls did not run on and a seam stayed; with the band, the cabin runs on to both sides with shelves, jars, plants, lanterns and barrels, no seam (49 s). The page: «Paint around it» under the strength (one column with it), the strength's label giving way to «the edges painted anew»; Draw and «Again» send it; the stage says «painted around». **The mask's options** (Viktor 10.10.2026, driving the mask: «По маске не хватает добавления латентного зерна поверх непрорисовываемых частей, блюра по краям маски»; his ComfyUI workflow's GrowMaskWithBlur and DifferentialDiffusion; A1111's Soft inpainting, AGPL-3.0 as we are). A run takes «mask_opts» {grow, edge, soft, grain} (grow -64 to 128 px, edge 0 to 96, grain 0 to 0.6): the mask grown or shrunk a pixel at a time; its edge blurred by edge px (the composite's feather, 12 before); soft: the tokens keep the blurred edge's values and each is painted only from the step where the noise has come down to its own value (differential diffusion), so a grey edge changes a little at the end; grain: the kept part takes that share of the painting at each step and in the composite, its grain and light sewn to the new paint. artist_job.py's blender() and composite() serve Krea 2 and now Qwen-Image 2512, whose reference, mask and painting around go through the same latents and steps (ref_latents and mask_tokens take Qwen's VAE and its 2 × 2 packing as they are, ref_sigmas the pipeline's own shift: Lightning's log 3); diffusers' Qwen inpaint had binarized the mask. Without options a mask works as before 1320. The request's fade_in (0 to 30 seconds, checked with fade_out): the take's first seconds brought up from silence on a half cosine (audio_fade_in, fade_out's mirror), after the decoding in both of the server's paths (a take rendered and a take decoded again from its kept latents); written back into the replay request, so a Retake keeps it. The take's «Fade in…» beside End at… asks the seconds (2 offered, a comma read as a point, 0.2 to 30 and not more than half the take) and replays the same take whole, from its kept latents when it has them (the very sound, in seconds), titled «· fades in N s»; an ended take stays ended (its request's end_at and fade_out go along). **Loop…** (from SUNO v6, TODO.md: «A loop from the song: 1, 2, 4 or 8 bars cut on the score's bar lines, seamless, as a WAV (SUNO's Sounds: loops and one-shots with BPM and key)»). lab/takeloop.py cuts a stretch of the take's own audio.wav (its rate and depth kept: PCM 24 at 48 kHz on the Forge) and makes it a loop: its first 30 ms are crossfaded on a half cosine from the sound that follows the stretch in the song into its own, so played round the end runs into the start as the song ran on (measured on one of Viktor's takes: the step at the seam 0.00004 against 0.0125 between neighbouring samples, 0.05 for a plain cut; the rest bit for bit). At the take's very end both edges are faded instead. GET /loop?name=&start=&seconds= (a quarter of a second to two minutes, within the take) keeps the newest forty in cache/loops/. The page: the take's «Loop…» in Files reads its score (its own, else the one written from its sound) with HeresyAbc: its bars in seconds at its tempo, its key where the loop begins; from the bar where the player stands (else the first) it asks 1, 2, 4 or 8 bars (4 offered), refuses bars past the score or the take, and saves «Title · N bars from m-ss · BPM · key.wav» (the name made by the page: a header would not carry Cyrillic). Suite: its check beside End at…'s on the CDP take. Patch 1325.

**Lightning, a switch; steps kept within their range** (Viktor 10.10.2026, after «The Artist cannot draw: steps: 4 to 16 for this painter with Lightning» with 16 on the screen: «нужно отключать Lightning Guidance, если Lightning LoRA не подключен»; «я в своём случае хочу отключать Lightning режим, потому как это для прототипов, а не для финальных выхлопов… С Lightning можно генерить авто обложки на треки, но не кастомный профессиональный реализм»; the plan he took: fp8 fine-tunes without Lightning by default, 30 steps and guidance 4; GGUF Q4/Q6 with it; the takes' covers with it; an explicit switch with its strength beside it, guidance only without it, no switch without its file). How Lightning is laid in: lightx2v's 8-step adapter for Qwen-Image 2512 (Qwen-Image-2512-Lightning-8steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors, Apache-2.0; on the Forge a link to Viktor's own copy, byte for byte the same) is loaded by artist_qwen.py as one more LoRA under the user's own, at the strength asked, before the fp8 transformer is cast; with it the scheduler takes Lightning's shift (base_shift = max_shift = log 3, no terminal shift), the guidance is 1 (one pass a step) and the steps 4 to 16; without it the painter keeps Qwen's own schedule with true CFG (two passes a step, guidance 4 by default) in 10 to 60 steps. The cause of the error: the page clamped the steps for the screen (4 to 16) but kept and sent the number typed (more than 16), and the lab refused it as it should. Now the page keeps them clamped and sends them clamped; Lightning is lightOn beside its strength (kept while it is off; an older run's strength above nought reads as on); the time, the stage's line and To the form ask the switch. The lab: a fine-tune paints without Lightning unless its strength is sent (OFF_BY_DEFAULT), a GGUF painter with it; Lightning asked where its file is not here is refused with where it goes; the steps' range follows the same rule. CSS: «.ar-samp-row .field[hidden]» (a box's display beat its hidden: Guidance stood beside Lightning on); the checks now look at what shows (offsetParent), not only at the attribute. And the gate of the ship scripts: «grep " 0 failed"» over a whole log took the words from a check's own text (the Models card's «2 fetched, 0 failed») and let 1325 pass with one check red (its own cleanup, the page right); live-green.sh reads each log's summary line. Suite 593/0. Patch 1328.

**The score asked about; the adapters that wrote it** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Для кнопки Generate song, если есть уже Score в поле, тогда попап с вопросом: Генерим на существующей партитуре, или новая? И нужно иметь сторожа для LoRA адаптеров и силы их весов. При изменении силы партитура может сильно изменяться, особенно с Industrial Rock и Instrumental. Поэтому в таких случаях, когда score в FULL режиме введён, могут быть проблемы, и я увидел это много раз. При FULL режиме всегда держим Supply Your Own Score открытым, если поле партитуры не пустое»). A score in the form remembers the adapters' music halves that wrote it, kept in this browser under the score's own text (yue2.scoreFrom): a plan's request; a take's own score through Retake, its request or the pencil (only where the text is not known already: a score planned with other adapters keeps its writers). A pasted, transcribed or example score has none known and asks only the question. Under the score an amber line names the adapters then and now when they differ; Generate with a score (Full or Melody, no codes loaded) asks: On this score, A new score (the field emptied: the song model writes one), Stay; the difference is said in the question. In Full the drawer opens itself and stays open while the score holds anything. The Kit's checks have the question off (yue2.askScore, as playing on), its own check turns it on. Suite 596/0. Patch 1327.

The Trainer's tab moves into the middle between the logo's right edge and the workspace button's left one wherever the logo stands centred (a ResizeObserver on the tabs and the workspace button: a language or a workspace name moves it again); the dock with the cards 920 px (full height 1180), their column 390, their heads' numbers of one width (tabular figures: the line that wrapped and unwrapped each second made the frame jump); its outer edge (the left one on the right, the right one on the left) drags its width from its state's own up to 1200 px, kept in this browser, a double click forgets it. Suite 598/0. Patch 1329.

**Seed modes** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Для SEED сделай рандом -1, рандом fixed, fixed, next, prev»; «можно и в творце»). heresy-seedmode.js: a select beside a seed field, its mode kept in this browser for each field (yue2.seedMode.music, .sound, yue2.arSeedMode): Random (−1) a new seed each run, the field empty; Random, fixed a random seed put in at once and kept; Fixed the one written; Next and Previous move the field after a run past the seeds it used (the Creator's versions for the music seed, its sound variations for the sound seed, BigInt for the 64-bit seeds; the Artist's variations, from the seed the lab answered). Compatible with what a seed field always meant: a number in it is that seed (Random turns Fixed when one is typed or the page puts one in: Retake, a prompt file, a plan); an emptied field is a new seed each run (Fixed turns Random); only Random fixed, Next and Previous put a random seed in when the field is empty, to keep it or move from it. In the Creator the mode stands on its own row under the seed (the dice sits inside the field's right edge); in the Artist beside ⟲, which is Random again. Suite 600/0. Patch 1330.

**The guard's empty card** (Viktor 10.10.2026, 16:10: «GPU1 свободен, а Артист пытается на GPU2 всё вальнуть. По сути, должен выбирать свободную карту и не ждать очереди»; «Минус 512 MB. Это CUDA runtime у меня, а у других может быть и больше»). His fp8 run with three adapters asked 24047 MB (22500 and the adapters' files) while each empty 3090 showed 23862 free: no card ever fit, and it waited in the queue (the GPU2 he saw at work was the sampling matrix of the test lab). gpu_guard.pick: an empty card (no one's process on it, no limit of its own) takes a job whose estimate fits its whole memory less 512 MiB (the estimates are measured peaks with a margin: 21.0 GB measured for fp8 with an adapter); wait_card: a job no card here could take even empty is refused at once, saying what to drop. Tried on made-up cards (test_guard_1331.py); live 16:15. Patch 1331.

**The Artist's room, tidier; upscale by 2K and 4K** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Над кнопкой РИСОВАТЬ/ОСТАНОВИТЬ пустой тултип. Центровку текста нужно внутри кнопки. И сделай капс в ней. В левой колонке сделай дропдаун селекторы адаптивными с минимальной длиной по полной длине моделей. При растяжке колонки адаптируй всё на полную ширину. В самый низ колонки можно приделать прогресс бар генерации. Апскейл… делай кнопками выбор размеров. 2K и 4K максимум. Нехер ручные значения вводить»; «Artist не переводи как Артист на русский. Только как Художник»). The room's header takes the room's name as the tab does (ARTIST_ROOM: «Художник»; the catalogs' «Artist» is the Trainer's performer and stays «Артист»). Draw has a tip of its own when it is not Stop (it had an empty one), its word centred in capitals. The painter's and the fine-tune's choices are the row wide and never narrower than their names; the column's fields stretch with it. Under Draw a bar: picture N of M and the step of the one painting, or striped while it waits and why. Upscale: the row's buttons are 2K and 4K, the long side 2560 or 3840 (QHD and UHD: a square cover 2560² or 3840², a 16:9 2560×1440 or 3840×2160), each tip saying the size it gives, off for a picture as large already; the lab takes a target, runs the model once or twice (a 1x restorer once) and brings it to the exact size by Lanczos (1920×1080 → 3840×2160 in 40 s, 1280² → 2560² in 27 s with 4x_Nomos8kDAT on GPU1); 1322's factor stays for an older run's Again. And the sampling matrix (heresy/MISC/claude-artist/matrix_artist.py, 30 pictures through the test lab on GPU2, one prompt, seed 1328): every run sane by its numbers; by eye, Qwen-Image 2512 with Lightning paints the subject twice under Karras and Exponential (two lighthouses), Simple and Beta keep it whole; without Lightning Euler SDE paints the fine-tune and 2.1 soft and hazy, Euler keeps the detail. Suite 602/0. Patch 1332.

**His samplers and schedules** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «По моему набору семплеров хочу попросить, чтобы ты их внедрил. Некоторые из них их кастомного пакета нод KJNodes. У меня весь код в ComfyUI инсталяции. Семплеры: er_sde, res_*s, res_*s_ode, heun(pp2), exp_heun_2_x0(_sde). Планировщики: bong_tangent, sgm_uniform, exponential, beta, kl_optimal, beta57.»). The Artist's sampling for its flow-matching painters is the studio's own now (`lab/artist_samplers.py`, a scheduler over diffusers' FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler): ER-SDE (the third-order ER-SDE solver of Cui et al., one evaluation a step), RES 2s and 3s with their deterministic _ode twins (the refined exponential solver of Zhang et al.: two stages at ½, three at ½ and 1; the stochastic ones put half the noise back each step, as a rectified flow takes it), Heun++ (Carzit's, MIT: three evaluations a step) and the exponential Heun of data prediction in log-SNR time (SEEDS-2 with r = 1; η 1 for its SDE), all on x0 = x − σ·v and λ = log((1−σ)/σ); the schedules Beta 57, KL optimal, SGM uniform and Bong tangent beside diffusers' Simple, Karras, Exponential and Beta. Written from the methods' papers and ComfyUI's formulas (GPL-3.0, which the studio's AGPL may take); RES4LYF was read for its mathematics only, its licence barring commercial services, so none of its code is here. A method of several evaluations a step lays its stage points into the timesteps as diffusers' Heun does, so the mask's blender and the previews read each step's noise right, and each picture of a run noises by its own seed. Against the exact map of a toy flow at 8 steps they rank as their orders say (Euler 0.138, Heun++ 0.111, exponential Heun 0.084, RES 2s 0.058, RES 3s 0.046). The form says what each costs (its evaluations a step) and times the run by it. The second matrix (`heresy/MISC/claude-artist/matrix-1333`, 24 pictures through the test lab on GPU2, one prompt, seed 1328): every new sampler kept the picture whole on Krea 2 Muse, on 2512 Q4 with Lightning and on an fp8 fine-tune; KL optimal under Lightning painted two lighthouses on 2512 Q4, as Karras and Exponential did in the first matrix, while Beta 57, SGM uniform and Bong tangent kept it whole; the warning under the sampling names all three (1333c). Suite 622/0. Patches 1333, 1333b, 1333c.

**The Artist's log; the log's own window** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Сделал 4 вариации... не вышло. Скорее всего ошибка. И ошибки, если не успеешь прочитать в попап сообщении, не найдёшь его. Нужен отдельный логгер для Художника. Можно прикрутить его к фрейму логгера сервера отдельной вкладкой. И по фрейму логгера идея... детачить, переносить на другой монитор, поддерживать поверх окон и т.п.»). The server log's frame has two views, Server and Artist, each room its own (the Artist's room opens on the Artist's). The Artist's log, kept in this browser (its last 400 lines): what was asked (painter, fine-tune, shapes, seed, sampling, adapters), what the lab answered (a refusal in its own words), each run's way to its end (waits and why, paints on GPU N, done, stopped, failed and why) and the lab's lines about the Artist. Copy takes the view's lines; the folded line says the view's last. ⧉ opens the log in a window of its own, a mirror of both views: Chrome's Document Picture-in-Picture keeps it over every window, elsewhere a pop-up to carry to another monitor. Suite 607/0. Patch 1334.

**The adapters together** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «И по LoRA адаптерам сделай также суммарную силу как у Творца, чтобы не было прежёга из-за наложения адаптеров. МНогие этого вообще не знаю, что нужна суммарность, а не поднебесная сила каждого адаптера.»). Under the Artist's adapters, from two of them, their strengths together on the Creator's road: green to 1.2, amber to 1.8, red past it, a first measure for pictures to be set by eye; its tip says why stacked adapters burn a picture however modest each one is. Dragged, it moves them all at once, each in proportion to where it stood when the drag began, on the 0.05 grid, none past 1.5; one strength moved, the sum follows; Draw carries what the rows show. Suite 615/0. Patch 1335.

**A picture's menu** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «По максимуму на картинки контекстное меню»). A right click on a picture (a run's, the gallery's, the stage's, the one over the page) opens the studio's menu: Over the page; To the stage; To the form (its prompt, seed and shape, as the stage's button); All of it to the form (its painter, fine-tune, sampling and adapters as well: Draw paints that picture again); As reference; Compare…; Set as cover; Upscale › 2K, 4K with the sizes they give and the upscaler the stage's row would take; Star it; Pick it; Copy the prompt; Copy the seed; Download; The file in a new tab; Its run; To the trash. A run developing on the stage has none. Over the page the menu stands over the view and has the keys while it is open (Esc closes the menu, the next Esc the view). Suite 615/0. Patch 1336.

**Trash (Art)** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «И по Trash хотел спросить... картинки у нас куда удаляются с обложек треков и остального? Может отдельный Trash сделать в колонке воркспейсов? `Trash (Art)`. Вижу Trash у Артиста, но пока что очистки и удаления нет.»). Where they went: the Artist's trashed runs to `trash/artist/`, a picture put aside stayed in its run marked so, a cover taken off a take (by a redraw, by Remove, by a new cover) lay in that take's `artwork-removed/`; nothing was ever deleted for good. The library's column has Trash (Art) under Out of sight now: the three kinds in one list, each with its picture, their number in the column and what they weigh; ticked, given back (a run to the room, a picture shown again, a cover to its take with the one it has now kept in its place, newer than the take's MP3s, which are made again) or deleted for good, asked first with what goes and how much; none ticked, Wipe out the art's trash. A frozen workspace's covers stay and say why; what is not a cover in `artwork-removed/` is never touched; nothing goes while its run draws or its take's picture is being drawn. The Artist's Trash look has Delete for good on each run and each picture put aside, and Wipe out for all it shows; the covers wait in the library's Trash (Art). The lab's side on a KIT of its own (`test_artbin_1337.py`, 15/15) and live on the test lab. Suite 619/0. Patch 1337.

**Engine → Artwork offers the Qwen painters** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «И в настройках движка, в Engine не забудь прописать выборы всех генеративных art моделей. Там только qwen сейчас.»; the plan he took that day: «Автообложки дублей с Lightning»). Engine → Artwork offered Krea 2 Muse and SDXL only. Beside them now are the Qwen painters this machine has, as the Artist room lists them: Qwen-Image 2512 Q4 and Q6 with Lightning, each fp8 fine-tune in `artwork/Qwen-Image-2512/checkpoints/` its own line, 2.1 with its licence said (not for a song that is sold). A take's artwork is painted by the one chosen through the Artist room's own loader: 2512 with Lightning at 1.0 and 8 steps, a fine-tune with Lightning at 0.55 and 12, drawn at 1328 px and kept at 1024, through the same content filter; a fine-tune not here gives way to the cards' choice, and the artwork says who painted it. Live on the test lab's GPU2: 2512 Q4 91 s with its loading and the prompt, a fine-tune 101 s, the one not here gave way to Krea 2 Muse. The peak, 15.8 GiB, was not the painter's but Qwen-Image's text encoder (Qwen2.5-VL-7B in bf16), which read the prompt on any card of 20 GB or more: a 24 GB card the guard gave to 2512 Q4's 15.5 GB with something else on it could run out of memory there. It reads the prompt on the card now when the room free there holds it, else on the processor (1338b): on the card 15.8 GiB and 82 s, on the processor 15.1 and 71 s, the same cover of one seed; the Artist room's own Qwen path takes the same rule. Suite 621/0. Patches 1338, 1338b.

**The page in all six languages, wall to wall** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «Жги переводы дальше, бро, и доводи всё до всех запланированных языков»). What was missing, found two ways: the kitchen suite run with an observer that kept every English text the page showed (4,051 of them), and every tr() literal of the page's code (389). What the catalogs lacked was translated in batches (heresy/MISC/claude-i18n/1339): build_1339.py checks each form's placeholders, the key's numbers, the Latin names and that no Russian word has the root «раб», writes one block a batch into each heresy-i18n-LANG.js (a second run replaces its own block, and a key the catalog had already is left to it), and check_cover.mjs asks the translator itself, in Node, what each catalog still leaves English. Where no catalog could reach, the code changed: whole sentences instead of glued fragments (the Artist's «1 picture» and «N pictures», each language's plural forms), a dialog's own paragraphs through tr() where its body holds a list or the release notes (the release offer, the 💎 sets, the machine as the studio found it), the art's trash counted in the language's forms, the Artist's shape chips left in English for the translator; what a person or a machine named stays as written (a GPU's name, About's links, the Writer's documents and notes, a set's name, a derived take's name, the run panel's style). The lyrics' mirrors (.lyr-mirror, .lyr-tagsl, .tc-mirror) joined KEEP: a typed line equal to a catalog key (End) was translated under the box and the marks after it slid off their letters; the gutter's section name became a text of its own, so 1168's «куплет + мост» reaches it. A repo name in a lab text (igrbible/Ruach_Studio, goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2) rides in a placeholder: the build's guard keeps the folder name out of the page, and a user whose folder is called Ruach_Studio builds as before. About 1,560 keys a language more; every tr() of the code has its key. The cheat-sheet's descriptions of the 200 instruments and 275 styles still show English with Russian beside them: their own languages are the next step. Suite 626/0 (1339: the counts' forms in six languages, a Russian dialog with its paragraphs apart, the mirror as typed, the gutter in Russian). Patch 1339.

**The Artist by Viktor's evening list** (10.10.2026). «Перестали генериться все три размера. Только 1:1» and «76% @ 45/59 — остановка. Сбой»: the lab capped a whole run at 180 s + 240 s a picture (900 s for three shapes), and an fp8 painter without Lightning (59 steps at 6.2 s) passed it in its second picture; lab/artist.py now stops a painter only when it is quiet, no line on stdout or stderr (tqdm's progress counts) for QUIET_LOAD (600 s) while it loads or QUIET_PAINT (300 s) once it paints, and the run's error says so instead of the last progress line. «Разреши батчинг/очередь до двух батчей»: Draw stays Draw while a run goes and asks a second one, which waits behind it, two at a time (RUNS_MAX in heresy-artist.js and in the lab, which refuses a third); Stop (#arStop) is a button of its own beside Draw, stopping the drawing run, else the waiting one, as 1270's did. «Левая колонка Художника не прокручивается независимо»: the form (.ar-left) scrolls on its own within --ar-avail, the window's height under the sticking columns, kept by paintAvail() as the room scrolls; «Среднюю колонку фрейм промпта растянуть до пола»: the stage takes the same height (.is-tall while it sticks) and its prompt frame fills what the picture leaves, the picture keeping room for a few of its lines. «По повторному клику на миниатюру… вернуть текущую картинку в проявке»: a thumbnail clicked again while its picture is on the stage and a run paints unpins it, and the stage follows the run again. «Картинки предпросмотра LoRA адаптеров… без обрезки»: the LoRA library's cards fit their pictures whole (object-fit: contain). The cheat-sheet's second line (1339e): the instruments', styles' and lyric tags' descriptions in uk/be/el/es/it from the catalogs, the Russian Viktor's own. Tests: suite 633/0 (suite-1340.part: one run going and two, the thumbnail three times, the tall stage, the form's own scroll at 760 px, the LoRA card); the lab's side without a card in MISC/claude-i18n/1339/test_artist_1340.py (a third run refused; a slow painter talking past four quiet spans runs to its end; a quiet one stopped after its span, loading or painting, its run and the log saying why). Patch 1340.

**The Artist by Viktor's night list** (10.10.2026, after 1340). 1341 «Как у Творца мы можем отключить адаптер, не удаляя его из включенных»: the Creator's right-click menu on an adapter's row (Mute: left out of the run, its strengths kept / Unmute / Take it off), the row dimmed with its tag, out of the together-bar and its drag; Draw sends neither a muted adapter nor one at nought, and lab/artist_loras.chosen leaves one at nought out too (Viktor's four adapters, one at 0.00, had been refused for 24 328 MB). 1342 «Четыре адаптера убили VRAM. Адаптивный offload на CPU можно предусмотреть в таких случаях?»: lab/artist_qwen.place() puts the fp8 painter on the card whole when the room free there holds its weights and WORK_ROOM (2 GB: the activations, the VAE's tiles, the context); else the blocks that fit stay and the rest stream from RAM through diffusers' group offloading on a wrapper of the tail blocks (pinned copies over a CUDA stream; non_blocking off, since the lazy prefetch hook would live on the wrapper's forward, which never runs, and a block could read weights still on their way); fewer than half staying, every block streams with diffusers' own prefetching. lab/artist.py: when wait_card says no card here takes the run whole, the run waits again for STREAM_NEED (6 GB) with ART_STREAM=1; the painter's own line ({stream, weights_gb, free_gb, kept, of}) is kept in the run and said in the log, and its done line carries the card's peak (peak_gb, the encoder's moment left out). Measured on GPU2 (JibMix fp8, Lightning 12 steps, 1328²): two adapters whole 41.6 s at 21.7 GB; four adapters, 55 of 60 blocks kept, 48.9 s at 22.0 GB; every block streamed 55.6 s at 2.3 GB; each streamed picture identical to the whole one bit for bit (heresy/MISC/claude-artist/stream-1342). The OilPaint adapter in qwen-image-2512/Styles reaches no layer of Qwen: an adapter of another model, said so when picked. 1343 «детач лога сервера сделай вместе с GPU колонкой… при детаче сразу сворачиваем открытый фрейм… при закрытии… обратно открываем»: the log's window has the cards beside the log (heresy-gpulive.js paints them there too, ticked by that window's own timer, its own pixel ratio and styles); the dock folds as it opens, its kept choice untouched, and unfolds when it closes if it was open. 1344 «зафиксировать прокрутку страницы, если vh имеет как минимум 920px»: #view-artist.ar-fixed, the columns the window's height under them, the form and the library scrolling on their own. 1345 «Уехало и слилось — кнопки, оценочная длительность генерации и прогресс»: Draw's row wraps (the cost a line of its own, Stop and Draw under it), sticks at the form's foot and ends above the log folded on the left (--ar-foot); #arStop[hidden] hidden at last (a .btn showed through [hidden] since 1340); «Прогресс бар… поверх проявки»: #arProgress over the stage's top, its whole width, out of the flow; «ПРОМПТ подсказку вынеси в тултип с нашим SVG i»: the prompt's, the reference's and the seed's hints, the painter's note and the sampling's warning (a ⚠ in its summary) are .info; «Фолдинг СЕМПЛИНГА запоминаем»: yue2.arSampOpen; «ВАРИАЦИИ ужми, а СИД лучше растянуть»: one row (.ar-seedcount); «фрейм с промптом убрать в… тултип вправо от картинки поверх правой колонки — при наведении… Инфу по самой генерации тоже»: the stage is the picture and its buttons, .ar-stage-tip beside it over the library on hover (under it on a narrow window). Suite 647/0 (suite-1341, 1343, 1344 parts; 1270's and 1340's checks read Stop as it shows and the fixed room); the lab's side without a card: test_artist_1340.py, test_artist_1342.py. Patches 1341–1345.

**The Artist's canvas, as InvokeAI's** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «определять точное положение (позировать) референсного изображения на холсте проэцируемых соотношений сторон… скейлить референс»; «очень хочу… канваса аутпеинта, инпеинта именно так, как в InvokeAI»). heresy-canvas.js opens over the page from Canvas… beside Mask…: each shape's frame (InvokeAI's bounding box, 1280², 1920×1080, 1080×1920) a tab saying how it will be painted; the board dotted, the frame's bare part a checkerboard, the rest dimmed; Move (drag the picture, or a corner with the opposite corner kept), Brush and Eraser (the mask in the picture's own proportions, at most 1536 px, red over it as the form's Mask… paints it), Hand (H, Space or the middle button); the wheel zooms about the pointer, Alt+wheel scales the picture about it, arrows nudge it a pixel (Shift: ten); the side: the layers (the inpaint mask with Invert and Clear, the reference with its opacity on the board only), the place in numbers, what the frame will be; Fit (the whole picture in the middle), Fill (the frame covered), Centre, Same in all (as many pixels wide in every frame, its middle in the same share); thirty steps of undo. A place is {x, y, s}: the picture's middle in the frame's share and its width in frame widths; the form keeps the places with its reference and sends the picked shapes' with Draw (with the mask's options: the band where the painting meets the picture), Again its run's own. lab/artist.py checks them (_places: x, y from -1 to 2, s from 0.05 to 6); artist_job.placed() lays the picture in the drawn frame: covering it, the run paints from it at the strength asked, the mask placed with it; leaving some bare, the bare part is painted from the noise over the picture's blurred cover, a soft band of 6 % into the picture on the edges that lie inside the frame, the picture kept and put back each step (1306's blender) — Krea 2 and Qwen-Image 2512 the same. Tried on GPU2 with Krea 2 Muse Q4: a square at 38 % of a 16:9 and in the middle of a 9:16, both painted around without a seam (MISC/claude-artist/canvas-1346; the probe MISC/creator/tools/claude-canvas-probe.mjs, DRAW=… paints on the test lab). Suite 652/0 (suite-1346.part). Patch 1346.

**The Artist's models to fetch, and the fine-tunes in our repository** (Viktor 10.10.2026: «перепроверь наш модельный репо v2. Залей в него все модели диффузные, что у нас накопились, и LoRA адаптеры, и настрой выборочное скачивание прямо в студии Художника, чтобы было всё под рукой»). Every diffusion file of the Forge was looked up on Civitai by its SHA-256 and its model's permissions read (the API's allowCommercialUse): only «Sell» lets a file be shared, so only those went up: the four fp8 Qwen-Image 2512 single files (Jib Mix Qwen v6 by J1B, IntoRealism Qwen v1 by enzino, Real-Qwen-Image v2 by wikeeyang, Civitai's own fp8 of 2512, Apache-2.0) and seven adapters (vizsumit's Cinematic Movie Still and Cinematic Dark Lighting, Danrisi's Lenovo UltraReal for Krea 2 and Qwen-Image 2.1, tarn59's Frank Frazetta, bluejaysierra's Film Photography, FASCIUM's Photostyle SFW), 84 GB in 27 minutes by hf upload-large-folder from hard links (MISC/models-v2/stage-1347, upload-1347.log), each checked back by its sum; the card names each with its author, its page and its permissions; v2 pinned at 5c2ef65. Not shared: the adapters whose authors allow only images and running (Detailz, Qwen Enhancer, Skin Detailer, Kulenovic, Da Vinci, CinemaGrade, Cinematic Photography, Cinematic Shot, Cinematic Painterly, Midjourney Style, Hyperdetailed Illustration, Movie Poster, Pencil Sketch Anime, Wacky Artsy Paint), the two NSFW-capable influencer adapters and the Nudity Fixer, lightx2v's Lightning (fetched from lightx2v itself), qwen-sketch-smudge (its source unknown), and never Viktor's private adapters (guard-own-voice.sh: clean). heresy/fetch-heresy.sh --qwen-fine jibmix-v6|intorealism-v1|real-v2|base-fp8 (with 2512's parts and Lightning when they are not here) and --art-loras all; lab/weights.py's extras qwen-fine and art-loras (Engine → Models and LoRAs); the Artist's Get painters and adapters… (heresy-artist.js openGet over HeresyWeights: its list of artwork, qwen, qwen-fine and art-loras, the fetch through Engine's own question and run, a ruach-weights event as the lab answers, the painters and the LoRA library read again when it ends). Suite 654/0 (suite-1347.part). Patch 1347.

**The GGUF Qwen painters stream from RAM too** (1342 for Viktor's fp8 with four adapters; the same for the small cards). lab/artist_qwen.STREAMS takes qwen2512-q4 and -q6: diffusers' group offloading moves a GGUF parameter's data as any other's, the GGUF layer dequantizing it on the card as it computes. Measured on GPU2 (Q4, Lightning, 1328²): whole 30.6 s at 15.1 GB; half the blocks streamed, copied in turn, 37.4 s at 8.7 GB; every block streamed and prefetched 33.9 s at 2.2 GB; the three pictures the same bit for bit.
