**The Pinokio launcher's folder: pinokio/ → launcher/** (Viktor 11.10.2026: «Добро, переименовывай pinokio/ → launcher/»; found 10.10 on his laptop, Pinokio's environment page answering 500 on /env). Pinokio 8.2 keeps an app's ENVIRONMENT, its notes for agents and its session logs in `<app>/pinokio/` whenever such a folder exists (kernel/environment.js get_root), while the launcher is found beside pinokio.js (kernel/api launcher_path) and the environment page reads ENVIRONMENT there. heresy/root/pinokio is heresy/root/launcher now (git mv; every script names its folder; make-release carries launcher/; the gitignore takes what Pinokio writes beside pinokio.js, /ENVIRONMENT, /logs/, /cache/, the notes for agents, and the old /pinokio/). An install of rc5 or before updates through the old pinokio/update.sh, whose pull takes its own folder's scripts away: it stops at ③ (pinokio/env.sh gone, ruach_tools undefined), leaving in pinokio/ only what Pinokio wrote. pinokio.js then, while nothing of ours runs (from launcher/ or the old pinokio/), moves that folder out of Pinokio's way, its ENVIRONMENT to the root unless one is there and the rest to tmp/pinokio-until-rc5-<time>, and marks the update unfinished (tmp/update-unfinished): the menu's default is Finish the update (launcher/update.js), Start as it is beside it. launcher/update.sh writes the same mark after its pull and clears it after its last step, launcher/install.sh clears it too, so any update stopped halfway is said in the menu. Tried on a clone of the published rc5 with Pinokio's files in pinokio/, pulled to an rc6 candidate made by make-release: the old folder left with Pinokio's files only (git status clean), the old update's env.sh gone, the menu leaving it alone while the old Update runs and moving it when idle, Finish offered, launcher/update.sh (its heavy steps stubbed) ending on the usual menu. check-pinokio 74/0 (the old folder's move on throwaway trees), in the live tree and in the candidate. Patch 1352.

**The Artist's adapters by Viktor's list of 11.10** (1353; «В LoRA менеджере заведи контекстное окно... инфо, триггер, удалить, etc.»; «Разреши добавление до 8, но... активными только макс 4. Автодобавление триггерных слов в промпт»; «Оба адаптера от медведя — мертвецы»; «Тултип по наводке на центральное изображение сделай с задержкой появления и исчезновения»; «По кнопке Upscale дропдаун отцентрирован ниже влево»). The bear's two adapters were not dead: OilPaint's keys are PEFT's without a prefix (transformer_blocks.0.attn.add_k_proj.lora_A.default.weight); artist_qwen.adapter_dict took «.default.» out, which turned off diffusers' own Qwen conversion (the one that adds «transformer.»), and diffusers' loader keeps only «transformer.» names: every layer dropped without a word, «reaches none of this painter's layers». artist_loras.plain_named gives such a file its prefix («diffusion_model.» for Krea 2's own names; files with alphas left to diffusers' converter), in both painters: on an empty Qwen-Image 2512 transformer a PEFT copy of Film Photography reached 0 layers the old way and 840 now, as the original; Lightning 720 either way (heresy/MISC/claude-artist/1353/test_plain_named.py). made_for() reads an adapter's header alone, the layers' names and widths: Qwen-Image 2512 3072 wide, 2.1 4096 (their configs: 24 and 32 heads of 128), Krea 2 6144, SD's cross-attention 768/1024/2048, FLUX's and SD3's blocks, Wan's; all of Viktor's adapters read as their folder's. The trash (POST /artist/lora-trash): the adapter and its sidecars to trash/loras/STAMP/ with a note; the Librarian's Trash (Art) lists them (kind lora), gives them back (refused over a file of its name) or deletes them for good. **The upscalers' notes, an upscale in passes, Upscayl looked into** (1355; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Пройдись по репо всех моих апскейлеров и собери инфу по каждому, и оформи это в попап подсказки, чтобы человек видел, на что какой годится. И можно ли двух-трёх проходный апскейл делать наподобие того, как у меня в ComfyUI workflow? Вникни в код https://github.com/upscayl/upscayl… может быть можно этот Upscayl использовать под капотом»). The 38 models of his artwork/upscalers/ (his DIFFUSION_MODELS_etc/upscale_models) were matched to OpenModelDB's database (its data/models, cloned) by their SHA-256, else by their original names, the rest by the Civitai ids in the notes beside them; lab/upscalers.json keeps each one's title, author, architecture and licence as published, our own words on what it is for (photos, faces, art) and its time and card peak measured in the studio's own upscale on GPU2 (1024² in: SPAN 0.9 s, Real-ESRGAN Compact 0.7, ESRGAN 2.6, DAT 14–17, HAT-L 20 at 5 GB, 8× ESRGAN 6.4 at 8 GB; heresy/MISC/claude-artist/1355/bench_up.py, bench.json, make_info.py). The measuring found 4x_Nomos8k_atd_jpg, the upscaler of his own workflow, failing in the studio: spandrel's ATD says it takes bfloat16 and fails in it («expected scalar type Float but found BFloat16»); artist_upscale.load() tries each precision on a 64² tile first (ATD: float32, 28.9 s, 6.75 GB). The page: an i beside the choice, its tip the chosen one's notes, a click the window of them all (grouped, quickest first, the non-commercial marked, each with its page; a row picked is the choice); the list read anew each time Upscale… opens. The passes: his AIO QWEN IMAGE 2512 HERESY Draft #4 (user/default/workflows, traced through its Set/Get nodes) feeds a 2× second latent pass through CR Multi Upscale Stack (span otf strong ×2, then Nomos8kDAT ×4, Remacri ×1.5, UltraMix Balanced ×2) and CR Apply Multi Upscale (bilinear), then DF_Image_scale_to_side 7680 (Lanczos) and ColorMatch hm-mvgd-hm. Comfyroll's apply (read in his custom_nodes) runs each model over the picture as it stands and brings the result to the ORIGINAL size × the pass's factor, supersampling 8× before each resize. artist_upscale.chain() does the same, without the supersampling (PIL's downscale filters already average every pixel they cover) and with a pass's input brought down when its model would make more than 600 MP; the long side; match() is hm-mvgd-hm (histograms, Monge-Kantorovich mean and covariance, histograms) in numpy, its statistics at a megapixel, applied band by band. The tiles go into 8-bit as they come (the same rounding, a fourth of the memory). POST /artist/upscale takes {passes, resample, long, match}; the run says the pass it is on (chain_at). The page: Passes… beside 2K and 4K: up to four rows of a model and a factor, how they land, the long side, the colours, his chain one click away (where its four models are here), the size and time said by each pass's measured time; Again redoes the passes. Tried on the test lab: his chain over a 1920×1080 picture, 7680×4320 in 246 s (2.1 + 128.6 + 78.0 + 11.0), no seam, the colours kept. Upscayl (github.com/upscayl/upscayl, AGPL-3.0, read at a00d55f): an Electron and Next.js shell over upscayl-bin (upscayl/upscayl-ncnn, a fork of Real-ESRGAN-ncnn-vulkan) spawned per picture with -i -o -s -m -n -g -f -w -c -t -x (input, output, scale, models folder, model, GPU, format, custom width, compression, tile, TTA): files in, files out, its progress on stderr; seven models of its own in ncnn's form (Standard, Lite, High Fidelity, Remacri, UltraMix Balanced, UltraSharp, Digital Art; three of them marked non-commercial) and custom ones as .param and .bin pairs; Double Upscayl runs the binary twice. Under the hood it could serve, but it would give less: ncnn on Vulkan runs the ESRGAN and Compact families only (none of DAT, HAT, ATD, SPAN, OmniSR, the best of his folder), its models must be converted, and the studio already runs the same .pth on CUDA through spandrel. What it teaches is taken or noted: passes (Double Upscayl, more general here), TTA (eight flips averaged) and a folder upscaled at once as TODO. Suite 671/0 (suite-1355.part), live 671/0, i18n 18/0. Patch 1355.

**Crop, Remove a section, Speed** (1356; from SUNO v6, TODO.md: «Crop and Remove a section on the score's bar lines, each cut healed by a short crossfade (SUNO: Crop, Remove Section, Heal Edits)»; «Adjust speed without changing the pitch (a time stretch), the new tempo written into the score»; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Идеи из SUNO внедряй для релиза 2.0.0, чтобы не возвращаться к этому больше. Это базовые быстрые функции»). lab/takeedit.py cuts the take's own audio.wav: the stretches kept (Crop) or the gaps around those taken out (Remove), each join a 60 ms crossfade on a half cosine with its middle on the cut, as a loop's seam is healed (the length and the cuts' times kept; a start or end cut into the song faded over 10 ms); Speed runs ffmpeg's rubberband filter (pitchq=quality, transients=smooth; ffmpeg's atempo where it has no rubberband), ×0.5 to ×2. Either makes a new take beside it (its own audio.wav; the old request and meta with its title, length, the page's score and «edited»: from which take and how; no latents and no parent, so it is neither decoded again nor taken for the same music under another decoder); POST /take/edit under the CPU lock. The page: an Edit row among the take's tools (Crop…, Remove a section…, Speed…). The take's score (its own, else the one written from its sound) is split into parts by its «% name» lines, each part its time from the score's tempo and its own lines, the last part running to the take's end (its tail); the studio's pick window lists them with their times: Crop keeps from the first ticked to the last, Remove takes out those ticked, each saying the length left on its button; the score of the parts kept is the new take's (checked by HeresyAbc.parse, else none, said). Speed reads «99» as the BPM, «×1.1», «1.1x» and «90%» as factors (rounded to a whole BPM, the factor following it), «1.25» where there is no score; the score's Q: line written anew. Titled «· without verse», «· verse to outro», «· 99 BPM». test_takeedit.py 10/0 (lengths, the seam's step no higher than the sound's own, refusals, a 220 Hz tone kept at ×1.25); on the test lab's waltz 21 s → 19 (remove), 4 (crop), 19.09 (×1.1 by rubberband). Suite 676/0 (suite-1356.part), live 676/0. Patch 1356.

**The wave's sections on the music's own changes** (1358; from SUNO v6, TODO.md: «The boundaries snapped to the music's own changes (spectral novelty) and the instrumental breaks found by the voice's absence; a slice of the spectrogram in the card (Viktor 10.10)»). lab/takechanges.py, GET /changes: the take's sound at 22 050 Hz, its timbre (20 MFCCs) and harmony (12 chroma bins) every 0.25 s, each frame normalised; their cosine self-similarity, mirrored past the song's ends (zero padding had made its first and last seconds look like changes), and Foote's novelty, a Gaussian-tapered checkerboard 8 s wide slid along the diagonal; the peaks 4 s apart at least, above the curve's median plus half its spread and above 0.15 (a noisy beat's own wobbles stayed under it), none within a second of the ends; kept beside the take as changes.json for its audio's time and size. On a synthetic song of three 20 s parts (a chord, a noisy beat, another chord) its seams came out at 20.25 and 40.0 s with strengths 0.95 and 1.0, the next strongest 0.13; on a real take of 209 s, 29 changes in 2.0 s (heresy/MISC/claude-tc/1358, test_changes.py 7/0, try_real.py over a copy). The page: each of the wave's sections (from the lyrics' timing, else the score) starts at the strongest change within 1.5 s of it, never past its neighbours; where the timing hears no voice for 7 s, a Break is laid at the silence's start, and the section goes on after it when the gap falls inside one (a gap a section of the lyrics already fills, a tagged solo, stays as it is); a song's long start or end without a section of its own becomes Intro or Outro. GET /spectrogram: 64 mel bands from 20 Hz to 16 kHz in dB against the stretch's own loudest, drawn night blue to amber as the studio's wave is, a PNG kept in the lab's cache (the newest 400); the section's card shows it over the section, 300 × 56. Found on the way: 1356's two insertions into lab.py had gone in twice (its once-only test, «the new text there and the anchor gone», cannot hold when the new text carries its anchor): the second import and the second /take/edit route, unreachable, taken out; the patches' swap() takes a mark now. Suite 682/0 (suite-1358.part), live 682/0. Patch 1358.

A text whose line breaks show is now looked up whole by a key that holds it (a key of its own lines, or a pattern whose key had them: the sliders' notes, «Play this version\n{0}»), else a line at a time, each line a sentence of its own (1339 had made the scan a sentence a line); only when no line of it is known does the whole go to the patterns as before, and it is taken only where it says something new. A text whose breaks do not show (the page source's own wrapping) is read across as before. **From Civitai… in the LoRA library** (1360; Viktor 11.10.2026: «ПО CivitAI согласен»: an adapter whose author does not let it be shared is not in our models repository, and the user fetches it from Civitai with their own key, by a button in the room). lab/civitai_fetch.py: a link read as Civitai writes them (a model's page, its ?modelVersionId=, a download link, an AIR urn:air:…:civitai:MODEL@VERSION, a bare number: a version's, else a model's); POST /artist/civitai-look gives the version's model name and type, its name, base model and trigger words, the file that would come with its size and SHA-256, the family its base model suits (Civitai's own names, its /api/v1/enums: Krea 2; Qwen for Qwen-Image 2509 and 2512; Qwen 2.1; SDXL 0.9, 1.0, LCM, Lightning, Hyper, Turbo, Distilled, Pony, Illustrious, NoobAI) and the adapters here that are that very file (the lab's cache of hashes). POST /artist/civitai-fetch asks the version again and checks it (an adapter: LORA, LoCon, DoRA; a .safetensors, the model's own before a pruned one, the primary first: a pickle, .ckpt, .pt, .bin, is code, not weights, and never taken; room on the disk), then in a thread the file into FAMILY/.NAME.part, its SHA-256 checked against Civitai's and its header read as a safetensors' before it becomes NAME.safetensors, Civitai's answer for the version beside it as NAME.civitai.info (the library's name, base model and words come from there) and its first picture rated safe as NAME.preview.EXT; the same file asked again is said to be here and not fetched, a different one of the same name comes as NAME.vVERSION; one at a time, Stop leaves nothing behind; GET gives its progress. The key goes to Civitai alone as an Authorization header, never in an address, a log, an error or a file; the download's redirect to Civitai's storage is followed by hand without it (urllib would have carried it there); a test page looks but fetches nothing, as the Models card. The page: From Civitai… beside Look up on Civitai, a window of the link and the key (Remember the key, as the Writer's OpenRouter one: in this browser only, heresy-settings.js keeps yue2.civitaiKey out of user/settings.json, which the network can read), what the link names with a word where it goes (an adapter for another family goes into that family's folder, said; a base no painter is known to take, warned; a checkpoint is no adapter and Fetch stays off), Fetch with its size, its progress and Stop, Fetched and a toast (when the window was closed before the end too), Add it to the list for this painter's family; the lab's words in the six languages with the window's own. test_civitai_fetch.py 19/0 against a Civitai of its own (its storage on another host name: the key never reached it), and once against the real one in a kit of its own, taken away after: Viktor's Sketch Line looked at (LORA, Qwen, 225 MB), and a tool adapter of 12.97 MB fetched without a key in 2.2 s, its hash right, its answer and its picture beside it. Suite (suite-1360.part). Patch 1360.

**The player goes on** (1361; TODO.md: «A take that does not play on late in the long page test (buffered at readyState 1), and the WAV fallback that does not resume a take asked to play»). The fallback (1053) set the WAV and never played it. It was meant to ask whether the element was playing, and could not: Chrome pauses a failed element silently before its error is heard (measured on the mock, the events in order: play, error with a capture listener still seeing it playing, the page's handler seeing it paused, no pause event between). The play asked for is now what the events say: a play event sets it, a pause event ends it, and a new take loaded without A/B's same-bar switch clears it (a pause followed at once by a new address loses its pause event: the load drops the element's queued events, which is why loadPlayer clears it itself); the fallback plays the WAV when it was asked (probe: the time ran on, 0.6 to 3.4 s of a 4 s take, then play-on went to the next take), and a take only loaded stays paused. The long test's take that sat buffered at readyState 1 (not understood) is met where it shows: a take asked to play whose sound is all here, and that stays short of playing with its time standing for 4 s, is loaded again in place and asked once more (the console says so); a second time it is paused and said. A slow line is not this, since its sound is not all here, and waits as before. stepTake's play is no longer swallowed: a refusal says why, as every other play does. Suite (suite-1361.part, the click's play as a user gesture; the stall made by hand, since a real play would stand the watch down). Patch 1361.

**build.sh's page guard and the updater under Pinokio** (1362; TODO.md, from 1259's audit: «build.sh's page guard and folders with common names; the in-page updater under Pinokio»). The page step stopped when the page held the install's folder name, so a clone into a folder named as a word built nothing: the page says «studio» 940 times, «music» 1951, «test» 647, «work» 1344 (measured on the built page). What must not be baked in is the install's own path, so that is what the guard looks for: the root as given and as resolved, any home path, and the folder's name heading one of the tree's own folders (/NAME/build/, /NAME/outputs/ …), which the page has for no name measured (studio, music, ruach, Ruach_Studio, yue2, app, src, build, kit, songs, ai, test, home, opt, work); build.sh passes the root itself, and the kitchen's page.sh too (a bare name from an older caller still works). test_page_guard.py 10/0: installs in /srv/songs/studio, /srv/songs/music, /opt/ruach, a home folder and a bare name build; the root, a path inside it, a relative path through its folder and a home path put into the page stop it, said; «a studio for music» is words. The in-page updater (1169) under Pinokio would have checked out a tag (Pinokio's Update, a git pull on main, then on a detached head), built, and left the studio to a start.sh Pinokio does not run: launcher/lab.sh now says the lab runs under Pinokio (RUACH_LAUNCHER), and can_run answers to update there, by Update in Pinokio's menu, which the page shows and its dialog says; a machine without systemd-run, which the update runs in, is told so with the way by hand instead of an error about a missing file. test_updates_1362.py 5/0, check-pinokio 74/0; the two answers in the six languages. Patch 1362.

**Fewer requests while a song renders** (1363; TODO.md: «Fewer requests from the page while a song renders (the browser's six connections a host)»). Measured first, on the mock through the Network domain (claude-requests-probe.mjs: 20 s idle, then a render held long with MOCK-HOLD, 40 s): every poll already waits for its own answer before it asks again (the run's /job each second, the lab's activity every 3 s, the hardware every 5, the props every 10, the artwork list every 20, the line's ping every 20), so a slow server gives at most one of each in flight; the most at once was 10, and not in the render: a take opened asks for ten things together (its sound and wave, the timing, the score, the music's changes, the gloss, the inspection, the derived tree, the chain), over the browser's six a host, with the log stream and the audio holding theirs. Now, while a song renders, the props and the artwork list are not asked (they do not change then) and the hardware every 15 s (render-phase requests 102 to 94: hardware 9 to 5, props 4 to 1, artwork 2 to 1); and the panels' kept answers on a take's opening go at low priority (Fetch Priority: Chrome marks them Low, as it marks the audio's own requests, where they were High and went before the sound). Not done: holding the panels back until the sound plays; the measure did not ask for it. Patch 1363.

**TTA and the pictures picked upscaled at once** (1364; TODO.md, from Upscayl: «TTA (eight flips averaged, steadier, eight times slower) and a folder upscaled at once»). artist_upscale.py: _tta() sends a tile through the model in its eight orientations (four turns, each mirrored), turns each result back and averages them; upscale() and chain() take tta, and a batch hands upscale() the model it loaded once. Measured on GPU2 with two small models over a 512×640 crop of a test picture: the same size, 42.9 and 43.1 dB from the plain pass (close, not the same: the averaging), 2.1 and 1.9 times its time (a small model's own work is little beside a tile's way to the card; a DAT nears eight). artist.py: {items: […]} upscales the pictures picked in one run (up to 64), each as one picture would be (a model to 2K or 4K, each picture's own size, or ×2 or ×4; or the passes), its pictures 01-, 02-… in their order, each saying its source and TTA, dealt to the lab's cards as a painter's are (ART_ONLY; an upscaler's process counted at 6 GB of RAM). The page: TTA beside the upscaler in the row (kept), sent with one picture and with the passes; «Upscale…» on the bar of the pictures picked opens 2K and 4K by the upscaler chosen, the passes kept and TTA. Found by the batch's own test: a picture made in passes (1355, …-up4p) failed the pictures' name check, so the lab served neither it nor its thumbnail (400, «bad picture name»): the page showed it broken; the check takes -upNp now (none of Viktor's runs had used passes yet). try_batch.py 8/0 through the test lab (three pictures to 2K with TTA in 24 s on GPU2, two in two passes, the refusals), test_tta.py 2/0; suite (suite-1364.part). Patch 1364.

**An adapter's strength over the steps** (1365; TODO.md: «Their strength by steps (keyframes, as Viktor's ComfyUI hooks)»). An adapter may carry keyframes, [[a share of the steps, a multiplier]…] (up to eight; a share 0–1, a multiplier 0–2), which shape its own strength as ComfyUI's LoRA hooks keyframe theirs: «0%:1, 50%:1, 60%:0» keeps it whole for half the steps and lets it go by 60 %. artist_loras.py: keyframes() checks them (in order, all at 1 is none), key_at() reads the multiplier at a share (straight between keyframes; before the first its value, after the last the last's; two at one share make a step), step_weights() the adapters' strengths at a step. artist_job.py: lora_sched() (a0, a1… with their strengths and keyframes, when one has them), lora_start() sets step 0's before the run, lora_steps() each next one before its step, the callback after the others (the live picture, the mask) whose answer it passes on; in Krea 2's run, and in the Qwen painters' through keyed() before each of their four calls, Lightning (speed) in every step's set at its own strength, since set_adapters lets go of an adapter not named. The page: ⏱ beside an adapter opens its field (focused), read as typed (a share as a percent, a multiplier; «half:1» or past 2 said where it stands, nothing kept), kept with the adapter, ⏱ lit while it has them, sent with Draw and said in the run's line; ⏱ again closes the field and lets them go. test_keys.py 10/0 (the keyframes, the reading between them, the calls on a stand-in pipe: step 0 before, ten more, Lightning in every set, the other callback asked and its answer passed on); try_keys.py 4/0 on GPU2 through the test lab (Krea 2 Muse Q4, Cinematic Movie Still at 0.8, one seed: keyframes all at 1 gave the very picture of the strength all the way, 99 dB; whole for half the steps then gone gave another, 29.4 dB from it; the keyframes said in the run; 277 s for four pictures). Suite (suite-1365.part). Patch 1365.

**The Artist's columns by the window** (1366; Viktor 11.10.2026, with a picture of his: «Выровняй высоты полей ввода в пар с дропдаун селекторами. При сжатии колонки РАСПИСАНИЕ остаётся на той же строке и врубает скроллинг горизонтальный. Поставь минимум 25% ширины экрана, максимум 30%. На правую колонку минимум 20%, максимум 35%»). Measured on the live page (claude-arform-probe.mjs): the text and number fields stood 41.7 px (9 px above and below) to the selects' 35.7 (6 px), the seed's mode 32.6 (its 12 px type); now all 36 px, the seed's ⟲ too. The sideways scroll, in Russian with Qwen-Image 2512 fp8, Exp Heun 2 x0 SDE and Bong tangent: the sampling block, a flex item of the form, could not be narrower than its own least width, and that was its summary's line (16 шагов · Exp Heun 2 x0 SDE · Bong tangent · ведение 3.5, a single row that does not wrap: 364 px, the block 382), so from a column of about 370 px down every field of it stood past the column's edge, while its own fields could wrap down to 163 px; the block may be narrower than that line now (min-width 0: the line ends in «…», the fields wrap, Schedule on the next row). The painter's and the fine-tune's selects narrow too (their longest name had held them); they had stood a row each only because of that width, .field's flex: 1 1 auto having beaten 1345's 100 %, so their rows are said again. The columns: the edges' bounds and their own widths are shares of the window, measured each time and again when it changes (25 % and 28 % by default, a double click or Home brings them back), and the grid clamps them in vw as well; under 1281 px the room is one column as before. Probed at 2560 (640 · 1078 · 717 px), 1536 (384 · 597 · 430) and 1300 (325 · 498 · 364): no field past its column, every field 36 px. Suite (suite-1366.part). Patch 1366.

**A picture protected** (1367; Viktor 11.10.2026, of a picture he would make his album's cover: «добавь Protect опцию для картинок. Такое нельзя ни в коем случае упустить в корзину, а потом ненароком в бездну»). The lab: POST /artist/protect {items: [{id, file}], protect} marks the pictures in their run.json ("protected": true; one put aside comes back into the room), not while their run draws (the drawing run writes its own run.json); aside() leaves a protected picture in the room and returns it among "kept"; trash() refuses a run that holds one; the deletions for good (_picture_gone, _run_gone) refuse whatever is protected, whatever brought it there (a run.json edited by hand, a trash from before). The page: the shield on the stage (its Trash off while it is lit), in the gallery's corner, over the picture in the view, in the picture's menu and on the picked bar (it protects them all; when all are already, it takes the protection off); Trash with protected ones among the picked puts the rest aside and says how many stayed; the gallery's filter of the protected. Found on the way: «empty» listed each adapter of the art's trash twice (1353's line went in twice), and the second deletion came back «not found» among those that stayed. test_protect.py 10/0 through the test lab on my own runs. Suite (suite-1367.part). Patch 1367.

**A take protected** (1368; Viktor 11.10.2026: «И на аудио треки в студии тоже защиту добавь в опцию и в фильтр в Библиотеке не забудь эту опцию»). The lab: user/collection.json "protected", the op «protect» {names, on} of POST /collection (only a take in the library; a frozen one is guarded by its freeze, the op refused there as every other), the catalog saying it of each take; trash_move() leaves a protected take in the library and returns it as "protected" (the locked ones stay "locked"); trash_empty() never deletes one, whatever brought it to the trash, asked or by the days (the hourly empty passes the marks now). The engine's own /library/delete is guarded on the page, as the workspaces' locks (1070) are: the take's Delete is off while it is protected and refuses aloud, «Delete non-favourites…» passes the protected by and says how many stay, a protected card in the Creator's column has no ✕. The page: the shield (Lucide's, ISC) beside the star on the Librarian's cards, in the Creator's column and in the take's head; in the take's menu, «Protect it» or «Take its protection off», and «Protect N» for the checked (all protected: the protection comes off); the checked bar's shield; the filter's «protected» among the marked (fourteen ticks now) and «N protected» in the Librarian's head; whatever stayed for its protection, said (the trash, a workspace deleted with its takes). Undone with Ctrl+Z as the other marks, only the takes it changed. **The Artist's library by the arrow keys** (1369; Viktor 11.10.2026: «в галерее картинок сделай навигацию стрелочными кнопками клавиатуры. Lightning и Ведение с кнопкой сброса на следующую полосу перенеси»). ← → the picture before and after (the gallery as its filter shows it, or the runs' pictures in their order), ↑ ↓ a row of the gallery as it is laid out (in the runs, the run before and after, at the same place); the stage follows and the thumbnail comes into sight. The keys are the library's in the Artist's room while it has a picture, at its ends too (the player's ← → seek is everywhere else); never while typing, nor over a window, a menu, the column's edge or the picture over the page (it has its own ← →). **The Artist's painter kept between runs** (1370; TODO.md «A painter that stays loaded between runs (the two minutes of loading)»; Viktor 11.10.2026, to my offer to let it go after ten minutes idle or at once when the card is wanted: «Да, отпускай модели Художника через 10 минут. Это правильно. И на кнопку ВЫГРУЗИТЬ в Баре тоже подключи выгруз не только YuE2 моделей, но абсолютно всего, что у нас в VRAM студийного»). artist_job.py serves (ART_SERVE=1, no run folder): runs come one by one on stdin, each answered as a process of its own answers it (its rows, then «done»), and the painter stays: a run of the same painter, the same fine-tune and the same adapters in the same order (Lightning on or off as before, for Qwen) paints at once, said («kept»: in the run and the lab's activity), its own scheduler set again first and its adapters at the run's strengths (its keyframes step by step as 1365); any other loads anew in the same process, the painter before let go. After each run the painter's weights go back to RAM and its card is free (rest()), so a run on the card meanwhile, the engine's songs or a training find it as before; a run whose painter streamed from RAM, Qwen-Image 2.1 and the upscalers keep a process of their own. The lab: one serving process a card (Served), claimed by a run of a kept painter (krea2-q4/q8, qwen2512-q4/q6/fp8), with 1340's watchdog and Stop as before; a second run on a card whose painter paints paints apart; let go after ART_KEPT_IDLE_S (600 s) idle, when RAM lacks what another painter needs (the other cards' idle ones), on POST /artist/unload, or when it fails; GET /activity says the kept painters. A card's rows are read by one function now (_row), the one-shot path's and the kept one's. The page: the Bar's Unload (and the Engine page's) lights and says so while a painter is kept, the Engine's card has a row for it, and a click lets the painters go with the engine's models (a painting one said: Unload again when its run ends). A test lab keeps to its own cards (LAB_JOBS_ROLE_ONLY=1, 1370b): a run of my test, sent while GPU2 was still held by the painter putting its weights away, went to GPU0 for a minute (idle then, nothing of Viktor's on it); a run of a kept painter now waits for that (a minute at most, 1370c) and takes the card of its idle painter. Measured on GPU2 through my test lab, one picture: Krea 2 Muse Q4 63.9 s cold, 48.5 s kept; the fp8 fine-tune of Qwen-Image 2512 (JibMix v6) with Lightning at 0.55, 12 steps, 82.4 s cold, 64.5 s kept, with the files in the page cache (Viktor's runs paid 83 to 98 s of loading when they were not). The pictures kept and cold the very same (99 dB), an adapter's strength and Lightning's changed and set back the very first again, Karras then the painter's own schedule the very first again: nothing of a run stays in the next. Sent back to back, the second run waits while the painter puts its weights away (about 16 s for the fp8 one), so the saving comes whole to runs drawn a moment apart, as they are when one looks before drawing again. try_kept.py and try_kept_qwen.py; Suite (suite-1370.part). Patch 1370.

**A long upscale pass no longer stopped as silent** (1371; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Не вижу, чтобы апскейлер отработал»). His chain SPAN ×2 → DAT ×4 → Remacri ×1.5 → UltraMix ×2 with TTA started at 17:19:30 and was stopped at 17:29:40 by 1340's watchdog («its painter said nothing for 10 minutes»): measured on GPU2 a tile of 544, SPAN 0.02 s (0.15 with TTA), DAT 3.12 s (24.2 with TTA), ESRGAN 0.46 s (3.5), so the DAT pass over 2560² (36 tiles) ran about 14.5 minutes without a line, the whole chain with TTA about 25 minutes (3 without it). _through() now prints «upscale · tile 13 of 36 · 24.20 s/it» on stderr, five seconds apart at most and at its last tile; the lab's watchdog hears it (its «s/it» counts as painting, 1340's five minutes a line). The same tiles, the same picture. try_long_pass.py: SPAN ×2 then DAT ×4 with TTA on my own 1280² picture through my test lab (GPU2): 750 s, 5120×5120. Shipped to the lab at once (a job's script: no restart). Patch 1371.

**A picture that is a take's cover, to the trash** (1372; Viktor 11.10.2026: «когда мы ставим изображение Художника как обложку трека, перепроверь, чтобы при удалении этой обложки с того трека не трогалось изображение в галлерее. И наоборот. Если стоит обложкой на треке то, что у Художника, при удалении изображения из галлереи спрашивать, или снимать, или оставить»). Checked first: a cover is the take's own copy (artwork.jpg, 1024 px, its artwork.json naming the run and the file, an A/B pair's other letter given the same), so taking it off the take (Remove current artwork, the art's trash) never touches the picture, and the picture going (aside, deleted for good) never touches the cover. Now the lab says which takes have a picture as their cover now (POST /artist/covers {items | id}, by the takes' artwork.json and a cover still there), and aside and the run's trash take covers «off»: those covers taken off as Remove current artwork takes them (kept beside each take, the MP3s made again), a frozen workspace's take keeping its own. The page asks only when there is a cover: «“Lantern Song” has this picture as its cover: take it off the take too, or leave it? The take keeps a copy of its own.» — Take it off too · Leave it · Cancel (for a run, in the run's own question); what was taken off is said and the takes' covers drawn anew. test_covers.py 6/0 in a kit of its own; suite (suite-1372.part). **♂ and ♀ in the middle of their buttons** (1373; Viktor 11.10.2026, with his picture: «В хедере стиля иконки муж. жен. смещены в своей клетке вправо»). Measured on the live page (claude-voicetog-probe.mjs): the two buttons had 10 px of padding a side, .btn.small's (two classes) over .voice-tog's own 0 (one class), so in a button of 24 px the drawing of 16 px had 2 px and ran out on the right; .voice-togs .voice-tog { padding: 0 } now. Patch 1373.

**The wave's scope** (1374; Viktor 11.10.2026: «когда я навожу мышку на волну трека в полноформатном трейлере, выводить спектральный граф полной длиной на весь трек, а тултип с отрезком текста выше. Займёт наверное около 30-40% высоты всей страницы, но это норм. Максимум анализа по волне»). heresy-wavescope.js: the pointer a quarter second on the full player's wave (two rows, the wave across: the Creator, the Refiner, the Librarian; never in a room whose player is one row) brings the take's whole spectrogram up on the player's top edge, as wide as the wave's own drawing (its border left out) and in time with it pixel for pixel, 36 % of the window high (220 px at least), under the section's card (1315), which now stands over it and leaves its slice of the spectrogram (1358) to the scope. The take is measured once, by the lab: heresy-spectrum.js's spectrum (1020: STFT Hann 2048 hop 2048 at the file's own rate, 0.5 dB steps), one cache with the take card's Spectrum (HeresySpectrum now gives data, lut and drop). Drawn: the spectrogram in the window's own pixels (each column the loudest of its frames, on the player's time, so the take's end falls where the wave's does), log or linear (kept in the browser), the frequencies' and times' grids, the sections with their names, the spectral centroid as a magenta line (magnitude-weighted as librosa reckons it, half a second smoothed in octaves, broken below −70 dBFS), the RMS of each moment in a lane at the foot (RMS² = 4/3 · Σ|X|² of the scaled Hann STFT; 0 to −60 dBFS, the take's own average dashed), the playhead. Under the pointer: its time and section, the moment's RMS and centroid, the spectrum of that moment drawn beside the line (its column's frames and one each side, averaged) with its loudest frequency (30 Hz up, a parabola between bins) and note (A4 = 440, cents); up in the picture a crosshair, the frequency there, its note and its level. A click goes there as on the wave (the same waveSeek); the pointer out of the wave, the scope and the strip between them, or Esc, takes it down (Esc until the pointer leaves the wave); a click on the wave before it came only goes there. The switch beside 🔔 (yue2.waveScope) turns it off. Measured on his take «01. МАНИФЕСТ_ Крик. v4.0.10 DS» (6:31) at 2560×1440: up 0.4–0.5 s after the pointer came, drawn 4.9 s the very first time (the lab computing the spectrum) and 2.3–2.8 s from the lab's cache (9.4 MB fetched, derived, painted), at once after; 2514×518 px. Patch 1374.

**The Artist's painters after a reload** (1375; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Не сохраняется выбор моделей у Художника после F5. Ничего не сохранилось кроме PROMPT поля»). Found: 1370 named the kept painters' list painters() in lab/artist.py, below the room's own painters() (1318), and Python kept the later one, so from the lab's restart at 18:54 GET /artist/painters answered []: the page had its two Krea options alone, put Krea Q4 in the choice, and a word typed in the prompt wrote that over the painter kept in the browser. The kept painters' list is kept_painters() now (test_painters_1375.py on my test lab and the live one: the room's list a dict with Krea's and Qwen-Image's painters, the activity's kept painters a list); the page keeps the painter and its fine-tune only once the lab's list has come or the user picks one (suite-1375.part). Found on the way in his pictures: «From the take» read «Семплов: From the» in Russian and the like in the five other languages: the catalogs' «{0} take» and «{0} takes» took any words before take(s); taken out (a count has its plural keys), and «From the take» and the watchdog's «its painter said nothing for N minutes: stopped» put in the six languages. Patch 1375.

**Whisper no longer waits behind the Artist** (1376; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Да, делай Whisper мимо замка»). Measured that evening: his upscale of 29 minutes held the lab's one heavy turn, and the lyrics' timings of his two new takes waited 24 and 21 minutes while GPU2 stood empty. A Whisper job (the timing, the lyrics check, Listen) now has a turn of its own (whisper_turn: one Whisper at a time): it takes a card of the lab's jobs that has room for it beside what runs (never another role's card, nor the idle engine's models sent away for it), asking every 5 s, and still takes the heavy turn as before when that comes first. Two jobs no longer under one lock must not take one card in the seconds before a process holds its memory: a card given to a job is claimed for its need for 90 s (gpu_guard.pick's less; more_gpus too), a look that only asks (wait_card) claims nothing, and a job's claims go with its turn (1376b: an Artist run's claim had outlived the run and sent the next Whisper to the processor). On my test lab (its jobs on GPU2 alone): three Krea Q4 squares from 20:26:18; the timing asked 20 s in waited out the painter's claim, started on GPU2 at 20:27:51 beside the painter and was done in 9 s, 47 s before the Artist's run (test_whisper_1376.py; the guard's pick with claims checked apart). Patch 1376.

**The Artist's run goes where its painter is kept; the lab's jobs keep to their own cards** (1378; Viktor 11.10.2026: «Смотрю повторный ран выбрал другую видеокарту. Первый был на GPU2. Модель сразу выгрузилась, скорее всего, затем на GPU1 пошёл очередной, - без каких либо изменений в настройках моделей и LoRA. Это неправильно», and then: «В движке в настройках GPU Художник под Студией или под Лабой работает? Если под Лабой, то неправильно сейчас на GPU0 ушла генерация картинки»). Measured: his qwen2512-fp8 with three adapters asks 22 500 + 141 + 1 125 + 281 = 24 047 MB; the card of his kept painter (1370: its weights in RAM between runs, its card free) had 23 428 MiB free, its own serving process holding 428 MiB of CUDA context, so it was neither roomy enough nor «empty» (gpu_guard.empty_fits wants the card's whole memory less 1 GiB free). _kept_card was refused its own card: at 21:02 the run went from GPU1 to GPU2, at 21:08 from GPU2 to GPU1, each loading the painter again (five minutes), and at 21:14, with an idle painter on each of the lab's cards, the run spilled onto the studio's GPU0 (free_gpu: the role's cards, then any card the guard passes) and took 22.7 GB of it. With one card only, the second run would have waited the ten minutes until its own painter was let go. Since 1378 a run of a kept painter gives back the memory of every idle kept painter (artist._kept_held: nvidia-smi's used memory of each serving process), for that very process paints a run that lands on its card (_served): gpu_guard.pick(held=) counts it as free on its card, through lab.free_gpu(held=) and wait_card(held=, a function asked each time). And the lab's jobs keep to the cards ticked for them in Engine → GPUs, as training does: free_gpu's spill is False by default (the jobs' cards are never none: every card by default, a saved list never empty, so a machine of one card keeps its jobs). Tests: test_kept_card_1378.py on the live cards (his idle painters on GPU1 and GPU2, 428 MiB each: refused before, given with held, 5/0 on each); test_role_only_1378b.py on the cards of 21:14 (refused instead of GPU0, GPU0 before, GPU2 with held, one card keeps its jobs: 4/0); test_kept_gpu_1378.py on my test lab, GPU2 alone (qwen2512-fp8 and Qwen Enhancer v1, 23 625 MB: the first run 241 s, the second asked after it started in 1 s on GPU2 by the kept painter, its run kept: true, 202 s; the timing of the two was not a test of its own, the painting itself is most of it); test_painters_1375 2/0 and test_whisper_1376 2/0 again. Patch 1378.

**Five more languages** (1379; Viktor 11.10.2026: Chinese back, and French, Portuguese, German and Japanese for 2.0.0). The 4 803 keys of 10.10–11.10 translated by me in 90 parts (MISC/claude-i18n/new5: show.py N, the five tables NNN.tsv, put.py N checks each key's placeholders, line breaks and numbers), the 28 keys come since in add.tsv; build_new5.py makes heresy-i18n-fr/pt/de/ja/zh.js (4 829 keys each, in the Russian catalog's order; the gone «{0} take», «{0} takes» left out; plural forms {one, other} for fr, pt and de, one form for ja and zh). Tests: the kitchen suite 723/0 with suite-1379 (twelve languages, each <html lang> and rooms, the choice of words translated and the name not); claude-i18n-new5-probe.mjs walked every room and Engine in each new language at 2560×1440: what the page shows untranslated is of the size the older languages have (fr 290, pt 287, de 283, ja 202, zh 197 against es 286 and ru 197: the take names, the adapters' names, the data), and it found a fault older than these languages: the Artist stage's buttons keep the language they were made in when the language changes (Spanish left in Russian too; a reload mends it), in TODO. **The Artist's second pass** (1384; the rest of Viktor's ComfyUI workflow, AIO QWEN IMAGE 2512 HERESY Draft #4, read node by node: «1st Pass + 2x Upscale» is DF_Image_scale_by_ratio 2× nearest-exact, VAEEncodeTiled, InjectLatentNoise+ 0.6, then «#2 KSampler (Advanced)» from step 20 of 60 at CFG 4.5 with the same sampler and schedule as the first pass, both from one SamplerParameterPacker, er_sde and bong_tangent as it is saved; the res_3s and kl_optimal in that node's own fields are overridden by its inputs). The lab: POST /artist/draw takes "scale" 1.5 or 2 with a picture to paint from (a picture of an earlier run); artist_job.sized() draws each shape at that many times its size (multiples of 16) and keeps it at that many times its kept size, both painters (Krea 2 and Qwen-Image 2512; 2.1 edits by words and is refused, and so is a mask or the canvas); the picture is brought up by nearest ("ref_resample": ref_rs() into ref_latents), noised to the strength's sigma and painted from there as 1305's references are. The card asked for is the painter's own need and 6 GB more at ×2 (second_pass_mb(): 6000 · (s² − 1) / 3), whole or streamed. The page: Second pass… beside Upscale… (not on an upscale's picture nor on Qwen-Image 2.1's) opens a row of ×1.5 and ×2, each saying the size it makes, and how much it may change (0.5 first, kept); the draw it asks is the run's own painter, fine-tune, adapters, prompt, seed and shape, with the form's sampling when the form paints with that painter (his recipe: many steps, no Lightning), else the run's; Again of such a run paints it again at its size; its line says «second pass ×N». Measured on the test lab's GPU2 (3090): Krea 2 Q4 1280² → 2560² in 86 s, 14.5 GB at the peak (10.0 for the first); Qwen-Image 2512 fp8 (BASE fine-tune, Lightning and 12 steps as the fine-tune's default) 1920×1080 → 3840×2160 in 220 s, 22.9 GB, its blocks streamed (59 of 60 on the card). At strength 0.6 both kept the scene and moved its parts: Krea's brush grew finer, Qwen's surface smoother with Lightning's few steps; 0.5 is the row's first value for that. Not taken: the InjectLatentNoise's extra noise (the strength's own noise does that part). With it, the guide's own guard read: check_guide.py had said since the Artist's sections of 1330 that the root Viktor keeps out had slipped into the Ukrainian, Belarusian and Greek guides (робить, робота, зроблена…; δουλειά), and nobody read its last line: taken out in seventeen places. Kitchen suite 729/0 with suite-1384; the probe claude-1384-probe.mjs (the row in English and Russian, mocked runs). Patch 1384.

**Extra noise** (1385; Viktor 12.10.2026: «InjectLatentNoise можно именно в reference изображения на силу перерисовки. Ну... и куда ещё посчитаешь уместным»). His node is comfyui_essentials' InjectLatentNoise+ (MIT): samples + randn · strength, in ComfyUI's own latent space, which for Qwen-Image and Krea 2 (the Wan 2.1 VAE) is the VAE's raw space. Ours are normalized ((z − mean) / std) and packed (each VAE channel four times), so artist_job.jolt() adds inject · n / std[c] to a packed channel of VAE channel c: the number means what it means in his graph. Its noise has its own seed (the picture's + 1385); the clean latents stay for what a mask keeps (the blender), so only the start moves. POST /artist/draw takes "inject" 0 to 2 with a picture to paint from (a reference, the canvas, a second pass); the run keeps it, Again too. The page: Extra noise under the reference's How much it may change (0 first: nothing changes for anyone who does not move it; kept) and in the second pass's row (0.6 first, his value; kept); a run's line says «extra noise N». Tried on the test lab's GPU2 from the 1384 lighthouse (Krea 2 Q4): a second pass ×2 at 0.5 with 0 and with 0.6 kept the same composition, the 0.6 one with a more broken, livelier brush in the sky (102 and 78 s, 14.5 GB both); a reference at ×1 with 0.6 in 25 s. The guide says it in twelve languages. Kitchen suite 732/0 with suite-1385. Patch 1385.

**Qwen-Image 2.1 on a 16 GB card, and a card's limit as its ceiling** (1386; TODO: «For 16 GB cards a quantized DiT (GGUF)»). No GGUF of 2.1 is on the Forge (DIFFUSION_MODELS_etc/unet/QWEN holds 2511 and 2512), and none was fetched: the same weights stream instead. Its parts are a transformer of 14 GB and a Qwen3-VL text encoder of about 16 GB in bf16; enable_model_cpu_offload puts each whole on the card in turn (16.4 GB at the peak from words, 19.1 editing). artist_qwen.place21() keeps that where the room free holds the larger part and its work; else (the lab's ART_STREAM=1, or the room) the transformer streams block by block (diffusers' group offload over a CUDA stream) and the encoder leaf by leaf, the VAE on the card in tiles. STREAM_NEED qwen21 9.5 GB. Tried on the test lab's GPU2 under a VRAM limit of 15.5 GB: from words 1328² in 135 s at 4.1 GB at the peak (whole: 120 s, 16.4), an edit by words of the 1384 lighthouse (a snowy winter night) in 129 s at 7.9 GB (whole: 122 s, 19.1); the edit kept the scene, the snow where it should be. The try found a fault of 1294's limits: wait_card() told «no card here can take this» by the cards' whole size, so a run above a card's limit was told «no card can take this now» and waited for ever, and a painter that could stream never did; the largest card is now its size or its limit, whichever is less. Patch 1386.

**Regions** (1387; TODO, «The most of InvokeAI»: «regional prompts»). Up to three regions, each a box of the frame and words of its own. artist_job.regions_set() wraps the transformer's forward once (a pass-through until a picture sets its regions, so a kept painter never carries a run's regions into the next): a guided pass (its prompt's first tokens the run's own) is asked once more for each region with the main prompt and the region's words, and its answers blend by the regions' boxes as soft-edged token weights (mask_tokens, soft, an edge of a twenty-fifth of the shorter side), shared where boxes overlap, the rest the main prompt's: Latent Couple's way, not InvokeAI's attention masks, and it needs nothing from the model. The unguided pass of a guided painter goes through untouched. Krea 2 (its prompts padded to one length, so its position ids hold) and Qwen-Image 2512 (any length); Qwen-Image 2.1 is refused. POST /artist/draw takes "regions" (three at most, a box inside the frame and a twentieth of it at least), checked before the run's folder is made. The page: Regions under Sampling, folded: a row for each, a place (halves, thirds, quarters, the middle) with a small map of it, words, ✕; kept in this browser; a region without words is not sent; not for Qwen-Image 2.1 (said under the rows); Again sends the run's own; the time said grows by a pass a region; the run's line says how many. Tried on the test lab's GPU2 with «two animals sitting in a snowy forest clearing at dawn», a red fox on the left half and a white snowy owl on a stump on the right, seed 1387, 16:9: Krea 2 Q4 painted two vague cats alone (48 s) and the fox and the owl in one scene with the regions (137 s); Qwen-Image 2512 fp8 two bears alone (141 s), the fox and the owl with them (281 s). The guide has a stop of its own for them, in twelve languages. With it, a correction of 1381: its guide probe clicked the tabs and read the stops while the first tab's were still in view (13 × 5 = the «65 stops» it said); it now opens each tab itself, and all eleven translations hold the English one's 83 stops, each found by its id. Kitchen suite 736/0 with suite-1387. Patch 1387.

**Edit by words, and a style reference** (1388; TODO: «a style reference», «for the room an «Edit by words» beside the reference»). Qwen-Image 2.1 reads a condition picture through its Qwen3-VL encoder; given the 1384 lighthouse in oils and «Paint a completely new picture: a red fox sitting in a snowy forest at night under the moon. Use only the style of the given image: its painting technique, brushwork, palette and light. Do not keep its subject, objects or composition», it painted the fox and the moon in that brush, nothing of the lighthouse kept (123 s, 20.2 GB, a 3090). So a style reference is that ask around the prompt's own words (artist_qwen.STYLE_ASK), and editing by words is 2.1's own way with a reference (1318). POST /artist/draw takes «ref_as»: «edit» or «style», with a picture and Qwen-Image 2.1 only (refused otherwise, aloud). The page: «Use it» under the reference: to paint from (as before), to edit by words, for its style only (the last two off when 2.1 is not here; kept in this browser); with either, the run goes to Qwen-Image 2.1 whatever the form's painter, without the form's model, sampling, adapters, mask, canvas or extra noise (the strength and Paint around it dimmed); the run's line says «edited by words» or «in the picture's style»; Again asks the same. Its licence holds (research and evaluation only), said in the tip. Test lab: the three refusals, the style through «ref_as» in 117 s. Kitchen suite 739/0 with suite-1388. Patch 1388.

**The Artist in Depth** (1389; Viktor 10.10.2026: «И как ты Lightning устроил? Это обязательно, и всё остальное, в мегагайд Художника»; 12.10: «Пиши мега-гайд по художнику. И в общем, Художника закрыли на 95%, пока что только на английском как основу. Позже выправим и во все языки»). docs/ARTIST.md, about 6,600 words in twenty sections: what the room is for, a first cover in six steps, writing a prompt, the painters (a table of licence, card, time and use), how a run goes inside (the queue, the card and its limit, streaming from RAM, several cards, a painter kept, the shapes drawn and kept, the live preview, the content filter, the files), Lightning, the samplers and schedules with what two matrices showed, adapters (folders, Civitai by hash and by key, Together, keyframes, how they load), Regions, a picture to paint from (Use it, the strength as a point on the schedule with its table, extra noise, the mask and its four options, Paint around it, the canvas), the second pass as his workflow makes it, upscale (tiles, passes, colour match, TTA), the stage, library and trash, covers, nine recipes, a table of what goes wrong and why, the numbers measured on a 3090, the API for scripts, the licences, what comes next. Each claim checked against the patches it came from (1255 to 1388). Linked from the guide's Artist tab (English) and the README; «Coming to the Artist» brought up to date in all twelve guides (lettering, the versions of a take's picture, SeedVR with the DAW phase in 2.1.0). The other languages follow later, on his word. Patch 1389 (docs only).

**The Writer says what went wrong** (1390; Viktor 12.10.2026: «В Писателе укороти `Max output tokens` заголовок… `Max tokens`… Дефолт выставь на 32K, безопасно»; «Пустой ответ или провал от LLM в Писателе никак не сообщается. Сделай оверлейный попап для этого. И при полученном ответе от LLM перекручиваем блок выхлопа вверх фрейма браузера»). The field «Max tokens» (the catalogs' key the Creator's sampling has), 32 768 by default (a value kept at the old default, 8192, taken as the default); its tip as before; the status line at the output limit names it and is in the eleven catalogs now (it was in none). An answer with no text, and a failure (the server, OpenRouter, the request), come in the studio's dialog (HeresyDialog.alert, translated line by line) besides the log; an answer that came scrolls its draft (or the raw answer when there is no draft) to the window's top. Kitchen suite 743/0 with suite-1390 (the field, an empty answer, a 500, the draft at the top). Patch 1390.

**The lyrics' language for Whisper from the sung lines** (1391; Viktor 12.10.2026: «С последними треками Whisper не справился полностью по определению секций… Можно автоматом из Title/Lyrics вычислять основной язык для транскрибатора, если на авто сейчас»). Measured on his takes of the day: the lyrics' English directions in parentheses and the [tags] gave 1182 Latin letters beside 1489 Cyrillic, no script reached the 85 % the timing asked, and Whisper was left to guess (it guessed Russian; 60 % of the lines heard on «01 v4.0.11», 85 % on its -ds take). timing.sung() now counts the sung lines only (no [tags], no (directions), inline ones too) and the main language wins at 60 %: «ru» at 96 %. The language was not the whole of it, said aloud: on the cached words, collapsing his stretched vowels (Светоно́оосная) on both sides moved the lines heard by one in a hundred; what Whisper hears in the full mix is the limit, and the vocal stem is the next step (TODO). Patch 1391.

**2.0.0-rc6, cut and published** (Viktor 12.10.2026: «Режь RC6 и пушь, с оформлением тарбола пререлиза»; «Сделай это своим скиллом»: the procedure is now my skill ruach-release, the dev studio and the public tree never confused). make-release into tmp/release-next (3366 files, 78 MB, the scan clean, .github with the issue templates); the clean clone: build 138 s, the page suite 743/0, the player 3/0, Pinokio 74/0, the places 26/0.
