Ruach_Studio.Release main a697397..2a1a214, annotated tag v2.0.0-rc6, the pre-release with the tarball Ruach_Studio-2.0.0-rc6.tar.gz (24.4 MB, git archive of the tag) and its SHA-256: https://github.com/igrbible/Ruach_Studio/releases/tag/v2.0.0-rc6. The visiting card on Orpheus for rc6 in twelve languages, the five new ones' guides on GitHub now (200 each).

**The models' link and the story without a script** (1392; Viktor 12.10.2026: «`Models on Hugging Face` в README показывает на начальный репо, тогда как у нас версия 2 репозитория»; «Да, визитку на Орфее обновляй, конечно. Закончи перевод сайта-визитки на все 12 наших языков Студии»). README, the site's template, its eleven catalogs (keys and values) and the twelve built pages point «Models on Hugging Face» and the footer's Hugging Face to goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2. The little story was shown in the page's own language by the page's script only: the HTML itself carried the English one visible on every page, so a reader without scripts and a search engine read English. site/build.py now hides the English story and shows the page's own in the HTML. Rendered headless (de, ja): no English left in the visible text; what stays English on purpose is the slogan (OSEM) and the studio's pictures.

**«Simply» in the page's language, AWESOME in capitals** (1393; Viktor 12.10.2026: «Да, девиз OSEM как есть, но `Simply` можно на другие языки. И `AWESOME` всё заглавными»). The slogan under the title reads «Simply AWESOME»; site/build.py puts the first word in each page's language (Просто, Проста, Απλά, Simplemente, Semplicemente, Tout simplement, Simplesmente, Einfach, とにかく, 简直), OSEM and its «Open Source, Engaged & Musical» stay as they are.

**Lettering on a cover, read back** (1394; Viktor 12.10.2026: «не хочу, чтобы это было отдельное наложение, которое контрастует… Нужен и кернинг правильный, и интерлиньяж»; on the four tries: «A & B - лучшие. B - реально круто. Впиливай всю эту ересь! 🔥»; «Для надписей будет отдельное поле промпта?», and on my answer: «По надписи всё правильно»). lab/artist_letters.py: the lettering's words in fields of their own (title, second line, the letters' look up to 400 characters, where, how); A paints them with the picture (Qwen-Image 2512 or 2.1: a phrase after the prompt, a take's «No text» taken out), B letters the reference (Qwen-Image 2.1: the order written from the fields alone, the prompt may be empty). Each picture is read back by the listener (Qwen2.5-Omni Q8_0 through llama-mtmd-cli, art_job's critic; 5 s a picture on a 3090: on the tries it read «RUOCH STUDIO» and «RUUCH STUDIO» as they were) and compared word by word, case and lines aside; a misspelled one is painted again in rounds with the seed +1000, +2000, up to three tries (ART_LETTER_TRIES), the painter whole on the card put in RAM while the listener reads (no room when streamed: read on the CPU, 64 s); the first exact kept, the other tries into misspelled/ (kept, never deleted); none right: the nearest kept and said misspelled with what it reads; no listener: kept and said unread, never shown as spelled. artist.py checks «letters», artist_qwen.main paints in rounds and keeps letters_prompt on the run. The page: Lettering under Regions (kept in this browser; the take's title; Clear), Letter it on the stage and in a picture's menu, ✓ ⚠ ? beside each lettered picture, the run's line. On the test lab (GPU2): «LANTERN SONG / RUACH STUDIO» lettered on the reference by 2.1, exact at once (118 s); «ПЕСНЯ МАЯКА / РУАХ СТУДИЯ» painted by 2512 fp8, exact at once (230 s with loading; fp8 without Lightning: 30 steps, CFG 4, 150 s a try); two variations by 2512: one exact at once, one misspelled in all three tries («RUCH STUDIO», kept and said so; 743 s). His «ФОСФОРИДА | Возвращение Шехины» in the style of his own prompt (his fine-tune, sampling and two adapters; the person his prompt names and her adapter left out): the title «ФОСФОРИДА» right in all nine pictures; the second line in lowercase («Возвращение Шехины») right in none of six tries (read «Вовуяеные Шехни», «Вевхиавенве Шахина»: the lowercase Щ and Ш scribbled), in capitals («ВОЗВРАЩЕНИЕ ШЕХИНЫ») Щ and Ш right in all three and only the last letter wrong («ШЕХИНЯ»); every one kept and said misspelled, as it should be. At the end of his 3,200 characters the lettering drowned (three pictures without a letter): the phrase is now a paragraph of its own, second. About 5 minutes a try with his res_3s (three looks a step) and CFG 3.5. Unit checks 9/0, the suite's part 5 checks. The site: the theme's button drawn larger, a half moon in SVG instead of a font's ◐ (Viktor: «Кнопка режима тем на сайте очень маленькая, мизерная»), on Orpheus.

**Why your own adapters** (1395; Viktor 12.10.2026: «Допиши в гайда Тренера совет.... преимущество содрать все свои наилучшие треки с СУНО... или из других источников и оттренить на них СТИЛЬ. Это поднимает YuE2 модель на следующий уровень. И нужно дать понять пользователю, что голая опен сорс модель, какой бы крутой на ни была, всё равно будет "не добирать" по инструментам, стилям и голосам, так что собственные адаптеры - это збрУя профессионального аудио/музыкального воина»). A stop of its own in the Trainer's guide, before «1 Material», in twelve languages: the gap a bare model leaves, the best tracks trained into a Style adapter (SUNO's on a paid plan, or any whose rights you hold), a Voice adapter beside it. His «збруя» kept in Russian; the Ukrainian and Belarusian say «створені/створаныя» for «made» (no root «роб»).

**Lettering on Krea 2 Muse; the hard letters' shapes** (1396; Viktor 12.10.2026: «Для `Ы` и других проблемных кириллических букв… добавлять что-то наподобие "slavic Ы/ы renders like flipped capital Latin P with I following it"»; «И на Krea 2 Muse тоже тесты прогони»; «И Ф ты зря удалил. Лучше оставить как паритет заглавной греческой Φ»). artist_job.main letters in rounds as artist_qwen does; artist.py lets Krea 2 letter. artist_letters: GLYPHS for the letters a Latin-trained painter rarely saw, said for those the words carry (Ы, Щ, Ш, Ж, Ю, Ъ, Ь, Э, Ц, Ч, Й, Ё, Є, Ї, Ґ, Ў; Ф as the Greek Φ). Measured on his Fosforida: Qwen-Image 2512 with the shapes no better («ВОЗВРАЩЕНИЕ ШЕХИНЯ», Ы still Я, one try lost Щ's tail); Krea 2 Muse wrote «ФОСФОРИДА / ВОЗВРАЩЕНИЕ ШЕХИНЫ» exactly (184 s, two variations). A spelled hint was tried and dropped: Krea painted its hyphens («ФОС-ФО-РИ-Д-А») and the listener read one of them smooth; it is asked now to keep hyphens and gaps.

**Two guidances, the music's and the lyrics'** (1397; Viktor 12.10.2026: «Проверь, или yue2.cpp принимает CFG отдельно. На музыку один CFG, а на лирику - другой»; «Если нужно патчить код yue2.cpp - то это нам не в первОй»; after the grid: «абсолютно разделение CFG на независимые внесло дополнительный слой качественного скачка. И скажу... это наша величайшая находка за всю неделю… На 2.6/2.6 и тем более в 4.0/4.0 сразу слышу пропуски гласных… Одобряю на все 1001%»). yue2.cpp guided the music stage by one scale, its unconditional branch dropping the style and the lyrics together. Now a third branch, the prompt without its lyrics (the style and the score kept): logits = U + g_music·(S − U) + g_lyrics·(C − S); equal scales reduce to the one guidance and keep two branches (an old take retakes byte for byte). Request field cfg_lyrics (−1: as cfg_scale). On a test engine (the kitchen's build, GPU1, his library as its outputs, the workspace «CFG · музыка и лирика»): a folk song (public domain) over music 1.6/3.0/4.0 × lyrics 1.6/2.2/2.6 and two seeds more, about 25 s a minute of music with or without the third branch; the same seeds part ways at another lyrics scale; Whisper's share of the words (blind to burn: it fills words from context) 0.64 at 1.6/1.6, 0.71 at 1.6/2.2, 0.09 at 4.0/1.6 (one seed), about 0.6 at 4.0/2.2 and 4.0/2.6. His ear: 4.0/2.2 the brightest, 3.0/2.2 as bright, 3.0/1.6 its own depth, 2.6 brings the shaker forward, 1.6 dissolves the words velvety; equal ones lose the С and skew the vowels' stress.

**The section card without its spectrogram** (1398; Viktor, during RC6: «Из обычного режима подсветки тултипом убери спектрограмму. Пусть спектрограмма будет только в полном режиме на весь трек. Пусть это будет уже в RC7»). 1358's slice gone from the wave's card; the scope (1374) shows the spectrogram whole.

**The take's medcard** (1399; Viktor 12.10.2026: «У нас в медкарте трека не видны токены музыки и голоса, время на их генерацию. И остальное из параметров генерации по планировщику партитуры и музыкальным токенам… нужна полная амбулаторная карта трека»). The engine writes meta.json «stages»: the score's tokens and seconds, the music tokens written (and kept, and whether supplied) with their seconds and the guidance applied (the default resolved, the lyrics' own), the sound stage's seconds and the decoder's. The page: a row under the seeds, Score Planner | Music Tokens | The run, each field a key of its own; an older take shows what its request says and a dash where nothing was kept.

**The guidances' lights** (1400; Viktor: «пределы 4.0 / 2.6 и в бейс до 1.0 / 1.0. Ограничивай в формах эти максимумы, но не жёстко, а тултипом и семафором»; «Оба CFG должны отличаться один от другого хотя бы на 0.1, а в идеале на все 0.4-1.0»). A light beside Guidance and Lyrics guidance: green inside his range, amber a little past, red far past; the lyrics' light red when the two are less than 0.1 apart, amber under 0.4; nothing clamped (Guidance was held at 1–4); Guidance past 2.6 with the lyrics following it says so. A new song and a reset start at 1.6 / 2.2.

**The profiles at 1.6 / 2.2, his own editable** (1401; Viktor: «И обнови профили настроек… мой третий сделай редактируемым и перезаписываемым в Студии… Переименуй его в `Musical Heresy of Creator's Ear`. Сразу во все стили 1.6 / 2.2 забей»; «выставь в нём макс музыку с 8 до 10 минут»). The three built-in profiles carry cfg_lyrics 2.2 beside cfg_scale 1.6; the third, «Russian · conservative (Viktor's)» before, renamed, 600 s and 15000 music tokens, saved over by its own name on Forge (shown in the built-in group with ✎), Delete bringing its built-in values back; a profile carries cfg_lyrics now. All three applied in the suite without an error.

**SDXL gone** (1405; Viktor 12.10.2026: «SDXL безнадёжен… если SDXL нигде не светится, тогда удаляй её. Лучше жёстко квантованных Krea 2 и Qwen Image 2.1 использовать, чем SDXL»; «Да, удаляй лишний обвес SDXL. Лучше Krea 2 Q4, чем SDXL Q8»; «смело удаляй SDXL из репо и с диска… В HF репо сам наводи порядок»). It shone only in a take's artwork (the cards' choice: Krea 2 Muse Q4 from 16 GB, else SDXL). Now: art_job paints with Krea 2 Muse or a Qwen painter only; on a card too small for Krea whole the lab says ART_STREAM=1 and Krea's blocks stream from RAM (artist_qwen.place, as the Artist room's do), Krea's text encoder and the prompt writer go to the CPU when the card has no room for them (the Artist room's Krea gains that too: on a real 8 GB card its encoder would not have fitted). lab.py: ART_STREAM_NEED (Krea Q4 7 GB, Q8 7.5 GB) counted on the cards the lab's jobs may take; art_painter Krea Q4 on any card; SDXL out of machine_state, art_refusal, the cards' choice. Measured on the test lab (GPU1 under an 8 GB limit): a take's artwork by Krea 2 Muse Q4 streamed, 68 s (the prompt 14.7 s, the painting 53.3 s). Out of the code, the fetchers and the page: weights.py's small-card variant, civitai_fetch's SDXL bases, the LoRA library's sdxl family (an SDXL adapter is still told by its architecture, and refused), fetch-heresy.sh (Krea for any card), launcher/extras.sh, the MCP's text, heresy/tools/upload-artwork.sh (it uploaded CyberRealistic to the first models repo), Engine → Artwork (its option, its line, the About list), the catalogs; the lab's main requirements without diffusers (SDXL's alone). The guide in twelve languages (the pro tip of another SDXL painter out), ARTIST.md, README, INSTALL, the site's credits. «SD/SDXL-style prompt» in the Writer stays: it names a way of writing tags.

**Trash (Art) as tiles** (HERESY 1406; Viktor 12.10.2026: «Корзину треша Арта перерисуй в тайлы, но аудио корзину оставь списком как есть»). His screenshot showed the prompts of 336 cards running over each other in a column a thumb wide: the art's trash card (1337) wore `.coll-art`, the class of a take's cover thumb (1155: absolute, 46 px; 28 px in the list), and shrank to it. Now the card is `.coll-binart` in a grid of its own (`coll-grid artbin`: auto-fill, 168 px at least; twelve a row on his 2K, five at 1280): the picture whole on top and square (an adapter's tile keeps the square, empty), the box on its corner, the prompt held to three lines, what it is and when under it; a click on a tile ticks it (a frozen workspace's cover stays). The takes' Trash keeps its list. The guide says so in twelve languages. Probed on the kitchen page with the live lab's 336 (read only): all pictures loaded, 179 px tiles.

**Wait: binding the lyrics** (HERESY 1409; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Нужно дописать ПОДОЖДИ! ИДЁТ ПРИВЯЗКА ЛИРИКИ К ВОЛНЕ...»). His screenshots at 00:07 and 00:09: a fresh take of his КРИК v4.1.0 showed the score's marks without words over the wave («исчезла лирика из оверлея»), then, two minutes on, the lyrics' own sections with their words: the lab's Whisper had been binding them (`/lab/timing`, asked by itself on a machine of two cards). Now, while the take in the player is being bound (`sectionsAsked[name] === "running"`), the wave draws «Wait: binding the lyrics to the wave…» in its top right corner, in the accent colour (the pointer's own label over it), and the card of a score's mark says «Wait: Whisper is binding the lyrics to the wave; the words come here in a moment» instead of how to find them by ear; both go the moment the binding ends or fails. Added only (his word: «Do not break it before you make it! Do not fix what is working!»): the sections, the timing and the card are as they were. Both strings in eleven languages; a check (the corner's pixels change and come back, the card's hint).

**Epochs, not steps** (1410; Viktor 13.10.2026: «количество шагов в начале тренинга - это неправильно. Нужно просчитывать эпоху по количеству семплов и вместо макс шагов иметь макс эпох»). The Trainer's Steps (1500 for every kind, whatever the set) is Epochs now: the steps go to the lab as before, the set's tracks times the epochs (batch 1: a step a track), said beside the field from the set picked («= 15540 steps»); the kinds' own: Style 40 (1500 steps on Fosforida's 42 were 36), Voice 30 (his own voice runs), Language and Artist 20. His run that morning, typed as 15 540 steps on a set of 482, was rounded up to 33 epochs: the field says it as it is now. **Cancel run asks first** («Для `Cancel run` добавь диалоговое окно»): the three ✕ of a run (the form's, the away one, its own in the takes column) go through one question, the run named, its queued versions said; «Keep it running» keeps it. The base suite answers it both ways.

**The Trainer's knobs in three rows** (1411; «Тот блок с настройками обучения... сделай его изящным. Если нужно три строки, делай, и смотри, чтобы заголовки полей не схлопывались в две строки. Подсказки в тултипы»): five a row on a wide pane (the run: name, trainer, weights, mode · how long and how wide: epochs, save every, rank, learning rate · the music half: AR weight, anchor, speed, and the separation), four, three or two on narrower ones; a label on one line and 18 px high, cut with … rather than folded; «0 = sound only» and «epochs» out of the labels (the tooltips say them), the units inside their fields, «How to choose ›» and «How they work ›» a «?» each with their tips.

**The two guidances a row each; Shape a band** (1412; «Раздели CFG строку на две, отступи вниз немного от общего семплинга, выровняй оба вправо»; «Сделай столбец ФОРМА тоже жидким. И я бы вообще перенёс бы это в следующую полосу, в один ряд, заголовки над полосами с кружками»). Guidance (CFG) and Lyrics guidance a row each, 14 px under the samplers, the light before its field, the fields 9.5 em at the right edge as the samplers' are. The take's card: Song | Sound in two flowing columns, Shape a band under them, Composition, Performance and Style influence side by side (fewer a row on a narrow card), each name over its dots.

**WAV 24 by default** (1413; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Дефолт движка везде выставь на wav 24bit», after his screenshot of the night: «Почему даташит показывает mp3 формат?»). The engine's request default was yue2.cpp's own, MP3 128: the page, api.py and the regenerations always named WAV 24, so only a bare API call got MP3 (my whole-song scripts of the night, 24 takes, rendered again as WAV from their own codes). Now src/request.cpp defaults to wav24: /props says so, a request without a format is a WAV 24 take, yue-synth writes song.wav; the page's protocol fallback, both copies of the mock and docs/ARCHITECTURE.md say the same. A request.json is written whole, so an old take keeps the format it named. Tried on the Arc's engine (a request with no format: audio.wav, meta wav24).

**Replace a section** (1414; Viktor 13.10.2026 on the night's four probes: «Подверждаю: склейки не слышно. Всё идёт единым органичным потоком. Межтактная склейка - это правильный ход… Внедряй в полноформатный плеер и будем оба тестить»; his plan of 12.10: «разрешить замену микро частей лирики, но с честным предупреждением… со случайным сидом, две версии»). The wave's own menu, over a section the lyrics' timing placed by ear, offers «Replace this section…»: its dialog holds the section's own lines as the lyrics have them (directions with them; the second of two Choruses with the same words is the second one), one or two versions, and says when the new words have more syllables than the section held (the model sings them faster, or runs over the cut: in the night's probe two lines two syllables longer were sung into the same stretch) or fewer. The cuts lie in rests heard by ear: halfway from the last line before the section to its first, up to two seconds after its last (no further than halfway to the next); none where an edge line was only guessed. The lab (section.py): the take's codes kept up to the first cut (semantic_keep), the section sung anew with a new music seed and a budget four seconds past the second cut (a draft, the engine's own take, into the takes' trash after), its codes up to the second cut joined with the take's own from there and rendered once more with the take's own sound seed, so that what is not replaced sounds as it did; each version a take of its own, a child of the take, with replaced.json beside it, put where the take is except a 💎, locked or frozen workspace (a lock keeps a workspace from deletion, not from an add: said here), else in «Replace a section», with a note. The player tints its stretch, edges it, writes «anew» over it; the card over it says the rest is as its parent made it. An engine that goes away is said, not waited for: the first end-to-end try ran on the Arc, whose SYCL engine crashed (SEGV) allocating the long context of 2030 kept frames × three guidance branches (8 GB of KV), and the lab would have waited for ever. Tried end to end against a fake engine (head and tail the take's own codes, the middle new, the drafts in the trash, the marks), the page by the suite (the cuts, the lines found and replaced, the syllables, the menu, the dialog, the request, the mark). The guide follows once we have both tried it.

**Compare with its namesakes** (1415; Viktor 13.10.2026: «В библиотек можешь добавить опцию сравнения одноимённых треков? Выбор до четырёх. Одноимённость берём по основному соответствию имени трека с игнорированием суффикса типа версии и т.п.»). The Refiner's compare player (1138, 1311: one transport, a click or a number switches to another version at the same second, the one coming in started before the one going out stops) is opened for any takes now (HeresyDerived.compareList). A title's base: lower case, what follows « · » cut (sound 2, a section anew, a run's own words), (edit) and the like, a version (v4.0.10), «take 2», «#3», the dots and dashes it ends with: «01. МАНИФЕСТ_ Крик. v4.1.0 · sound 2» and «… v4.0.10 (edit)» are both «01. манифест_ крик». A take's menu offers «Compare with its namesakes…» when it has any: they are listed newest first, itself ticked, two to four to tick; the Librarian's bar a Compare button, on for two to four checked. Each version in the player says when it was made and its like and star.

**Free tempo** (HERESY 1416; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Мой Буратино реп без Q ожил… Даже пустой Q: работает, либо вообще удалённый»; on КРИК: «Совсем другое исполнение, свободное. И музыкальная часть ещё живее стала. Больше эмоций, абсолютно. Теперь лирика не зависит от такта»). My four probes that morning on a folk ballad (60 s, the Arc) kept a steady pulse without Q: (with Q 72–74, without 72–74, without Q and without BPM in the style 78, with «rubato, ad libitum, free tempo» 80.7; the beats' spacing as even), and I said so; his rap told the other half: the bars of the score still hold the music, but the voice and its words leave the beat, and in his КРИК the music itself livened. His design: «запихнуть его прямо в строку заголовка между STYLE и иконками выбора пола. И линтер/сторож на текст стиля… `Хочешь нелинейность или рубато, удали BPM из своего стиля!`»; «Авто удаление Q: по триггерным в стиле: non-linear, rubato, etc. Если в стиле есть BPM, не трогаем Q»; «на отдельное планирование "Создать только партитуру" тоже примени обязательно, чтобы руками не чистить»; «Не забудь убрать предупреждение». The engine: a request's free_tempo (written only when used) takes the header's Q: lines and the inline [Q:…] fields out of the score after it is planned or as it is supplied, before the music stage reads it and before a plan asked alone (plan_only) is returned; the log says how many marks went. The page: «Free tempo» in the Style's head between its name and the voice's toggles, sent with a score (never in Direct, which has none); on by itself, with a toast, when the Style comes to ask for it (rubato, non-linear, free tempo or time, ad lib, unmetered, free rhythm) and names no BPM, and not again once turned off until the Style asks anew; a BPM in the Style while it is on: his sentence under the box; the draft, a take's settings and a prompt file keep it; the medcard's Score Planner says it. A score without its tempo (an empty Q: or none) is native now in the page's two readers and the lab's (abc_tools): read at the nominal 120, MIDI's own, so the statistics, MIDI and transposition go on; the summary says «free tempo» for its BPM.

**The lyrics' timing in two passes** (1417; Viktor 13.10.2026: «stable-ts в июне отвергнут разработчиком. Лучше Whisper с Qwen-Omni допилить ещё малость»). stable-ts's forced alignment was tried first, his go given in the morning: alone it is not to be trusted on singing (on a take Whisper heard whole it pressed the first two lines into two seconds and pulled the fourth verse into the instrumental), and it is gone from the studio's Python. What came of it is the rule it taught: a second opinion may only fill what the first could not hear. When the first pass heard less than 85 % of the lines, a second Whisper pass gets the lyrics as its hotwords (on the take's vocal stem when the Refiner made one, else on the take); timing.merge keeps every line the first pass heard, and times a guessed one from the second only when the second heard it, it lies between the first pass's heard neighbours (half a second of slack), lasts a quarter second a word at least and does not start before the line before it ends: the hint alone makes Whisper «hear» the words it was given (it heard 100 % on a take the first heard 81 % of). Measured on seven Dual CFG takes heard 75–81 %: five went to 100 % (the first lines of a verse, guessed from 0:00 to 14 s, placed at 10–20 s where they are sung), one to 94 %, one to 81 % with a collapsed fill (a line of no length, the next pressed onto it) refused. The second pass is a help, never a failure: if it fails, the first pass stands and the activity says so. Omni has no part in it yet: it cannot transcribe singing.

**The guidances as roads** (1419; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Высота полей ввода в CFG не в стиле общем. Может ползунками сделаем как с LoRA адаптерами? Места в горизонтали хватает»; «приведи её в тот же дизайн, что и кнопки "Номера строк", "Орфография". И перепроверь все стили, чтобы закругление бордюров подхватывалось одной из трёх опций, что в Engine»; «Что это за кнопка, частично покрытая mp3?»). Guidance and Lyrics guidance: name, road, light, field in columns both rows share (a subgrid); the road 0–6, red under 1, green in Viktor's range (the music 1–4, the lyrics 1–2.6), amber to 4.6 / 3, red past it; the fields of the samplers' height and figures, 9.5em wide as before (the «as Guidance» placeholder whole). The number stays the truth: an empty Guidance is the profile's default, an empty Lyrics guidance follows Guidance (its thumb hollow there); the road follows the number whoever sets it (a value setter on the two fields, so a request loaded, a reset or a profile moves it too), and a drag writes the number as typing does (its input, then its change on release: the guard and the lights have their say). Free tempo: a lyrics' toggle (.lyr-tog), amber when pressed. The corners: 74 rules in app.css had their radius in pixels; each now takes the profile's token (--r-pill for pills, bars and round badges; --r-md for pictures and readouts; --r-sm for chips and frames; a new --r-xs, round 3 / soft 2 / square 1, for what was 2px, so the icon frames keep the «2px закругление» of 07.10 in the soft profile and follow Engine's choice in the others); circles, the 1px hairlines and the tiny lock (07.10: «Крошечные замочки не трогай») as they were; the suite finds no corner in pixels left. A take's hover tools: mp3, Refine, download, star, delete had fixed places from the right, measured for the English words; «Огранить» is wider than «Refine», and «mp3» lay over it. Now one flowing row (.take-row-tools; .take-tools was the take view's panel already), left to right as seen, the title kept clear of them by their measured width.

**Score mode in the studio's own trainer** (1420; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Убирай AI-Toolkit внешний из Студии»; «Режим можно выбрать только с AI-Toolkit. Это твоя недоработка в коде?»). The first of three steps before AI-Toolkit leaves the studio (score mode, the separation, then the removal). Ported from Ostris' MIT code line by line (yue2_model.py: _item_prefix_and_abc, _ar_inputs, get_noise_prediction): every track's score by the engine's own transcriber (yue-transcribe, SheetSage2 GGUF; Melody with --melody-only) before the model loads, cached as _ruach_cache/NNN_ruach.full.abc or .melody.abc; a track it cannot read keeps the reason as .fail, is named in the log and trains as Direct always. Each step: with probability abc_dropout (0.5, AI-Toolkit's) the track trains as a Direct prompt (the off instruction, no score), else the AR sees the cot's instruction and the score's ids before [ABC_END, MUSIC_START]: the score in the AR's targets (it learns to write it) and in the NAR's conditioning alike; the step log says which (score/sheet). The transcriber is AI-Toolkit's SheetSage2 ported to the engine, not its Python: the scores the trainer sees are the ones the studio's own covers see. Smoke run on two Fosforida tracks (GPU1, 8 steps): the two scores in 45 s (4181 and 4065 characters), the score on 2 steps of 8, their ar_ce lower (4.56 against 4.94–5.68: the score's text is easier to foresee than the music's tokens), VRAM peak 19.1 GB on 5½-minute songs. The page: Mode open for the Ruach trainer, Go on keeps the run's mode, Separate still AI-Toolkit's; the lab refuses score mode in words when the transcriber is missing, before the run, not hours into it.

**The separation in the studio's own trainer; AI-Toolkit out** (1422; Viktor 13.10.2026: «Убирай AI-Toolkit внешний из Студии»; «AI-Toolkit стоит отдельно ~/Documents/AI/AI_Toolkit»: his folder is his, untouched). Ported from Ostris' MIT code (yue2_model.py: encode_audio, _stem_ar_loss, get_noise_prediction): cache.stems splits every track into voice and instrumental with the studio's own separator (BS-Roformer ep317 through audio-separator, the Refiner's stems; AI-Toolkit splits with MelBandRoformer), tokenizes each stem with the realaudio head, cut to the mix's length (_ruach_cache/NNN_ruach.stems.safetensors), and in score mode transcribes each stem's sheet (NNN_ruach.vocals.COT.abc, .music.COT.abc); the stems' audio is not kept. Each step, when the AR learns: the voice from the lyrics alone (the prompt ("", lyrics)) and the music from the tags alone ((style, "")), each from the song's start, each with its own score dropout; the lesson (ce + 0.5·ce_vocals + 1.0·ce_music) / 2.5, the KL the mean of the three, the NAR's flow on the mix only (AI-Toolkit's weights). Each stem's part goes backward on its own after the mix's: the same gradient as one sum, without three graphs on the card at once. Smoke run on two 5½-minute Fosforida songs, score mode and separation together (GPU1): 6 steps, 22 s a step, VRAM peak 19.1 GB (as the mix alone); the logged sum checked against the formula (step 1: 1.08 + 0.5·(4.961 + 0.5·4.733 + 4.376)/2.5 = 3.42). The separator is slow (about 5 minutes for a 5-minute song), once a set. Then AI-Toolkit left: the lab's job file (config_yaml), its paths and its launch are gone, a spec asking for it is refused in words; the Trainer's select has Ruach Studio alone, Mode and Separate open; Go on keeps a run's mode and separation; the tips and the guide's Trainer and Separate in twelve languages. The old runs AI-Toolkit made keep their cards (their loss split from the sum, 1090).

**His small things** (1421; Viktor 13.10.2026). The compare player: a version's play resolves when it has loaded, and the old callback paused the one it had been switched from, which by then could be the chosen one again: a click back to the first while the second was loading stopped both; now whatever resolves last, every version but the chosen one is paused, and the second a switch goes from is the last one known. The Style goes to the engine as one line («Музыкальный стиль скармливаем движку одной строкой»): a comma between lines unless the line already ends in one. The find bar («кнопка X съезжает»): the bar as wide as its rows may be, the find field the one that gives way. Max length («Когда ввёл 9 и Таб, всё равно на 0:10»): a bare number up to 15 is minutes, above 15 seconds, m:ss as written, empty the engine's default (8:00), shown at once in m:ss. The guidances in tenths («CFG у Творца сделай с десятичными»). A take's card («К хуям убери mp3, огранить, скачать и удалить кнопки с лица карточки… перенести в хвост первой ИДЁТ/ИГРАЕТ и VAE»): of the hover tools only the star is left (the four stay unseen in the markup, the checks' handle on the files' names; the take menu has Download, Refine, Move to the trash), the title's row ends with playing (or a run's state) and the decoder. The sambuca («`sambuca - greek strings` заработала честно»): the sheet asks for it in his words, no longer among the failed; A and B made again on the probes' seeds (A 120 s; B he made again twice), approved into «💎 Instrumental Probe», the old «Sambuca Strings · A/B» kept in «Instrumental Probe CHECKOUT / Replaced»; the painter told it is Greek plucked strings, a bowl-backed lute, not a harp («Обложка тупо рисуется как арфа»); Krea 2 Muse drew lutes off the mark, Qwen-Image 2512 (Q6) drew them («Обложку может с Квеном сгенери»); the workspace published to the bucket over the old files (six changed, the voice guard clean).

**Covers by the cards** (1423; Viktor 13.10.2026: «ставь на обложки 2512, но это будет тяжело даже для RTX3090. Можно ли местно квантануть в int4 или подобное?… Либо для 24GB+ имеем 2512 а для менее жирных оставляем Krea 2 Muse? В Engine дать человеку выбрать… Ты там рулишь»). Measured on GPU1 (a 3090), a take's cover as a job of its own (art_job.py: the painter loaded afresh, Qwen3-4B writing the prompt, the critic's look): Krea 2 Muse Q4 65 s (paint 48), Qwen-Image 2512 Q6 131 s (paint 113), Q4 125 s (paint 105). So a local int4 would not help: Q4_K_M is int4 already and paints no quicker than Q6, the time is the card's arithmetic and the painter's loading, not its memory; what would (each a download and his word, for 2.1.0): covers painted by the Artist room's kept painter (no loading), nunchaku's SVDQuant int4 kernels, a 4-step Lightning. Chosen: by the cards (art_by_cards), Qwen-Image 2512 where a card the lab's jobs may take holds it whole (Q6 from 20 GB, Q4 from 15.5 GB), else Krea 2 Muse Q4 (whole on 16 GB, streamed below); an explicit choice in Engine → Artwork wins, as before (every painter there, the Qwen fine-tunes each its own line). Qwen-Image 2.1 is never the default: Qwen Research Licence, not for a song that is sold. Engine → Artwork's speeds were wrong (Qwen «about 30 s a picture», Krea «about 25 s»: the Artist room's kept painter, not a cover's job); now as measured, Q6's line catalogued in eleven languages (it never was). Viktor's own setting (Krea Q4, chosen before) taken off so his covers go by the cards.

**The Artist in depth in twelve languages** (1424; Viktor 13.10.2026: «У нас не переведён один раздел Гайда. Правильно? Доводи его до ума и на все языки»). The section was the Artist's mega-guide, docs/ARTIST.md (1389, English only «later on his word»); the in-app guide's twelve were whole already (13 sections, 85 subsections, 898 lines in each). Brought up to date first: covers by the cards (1423), a cover's own job measured (Krea Q4 65 s, Qwen Q4 125 s, Q6 131 s) in section 15 and the numbers. Then translated whole into ru, uk, be, el, es, it, fr, pt, de, ja, zh (ARTIST.<lang>.md), each control by the name the studio's catalog gives it in that language (a glossary drawn from the eleven catalogs: Рисовать, Сделать обложкой, Второй проход…, Dessiner, Mettre en pochette, 描く, 绘制…), the room by its own name there (app.js ARTIST_ROOM: Художник, Мастак, Καλλιτέχνης, Artista, Peintre, Pintor, Maler, 絵師, 画室; in French, Portuguese and German the room and its painters share a word, as the guide has them: the room capitalised); prompts, paths, API fields and measured numbers as they are. Each file's contents built by a script with GitHub's anchors for its own headings, and a line of the twelve under each title. README points to them.

**Before RC7, his list** (1425; Viktor 13.10.2026). ① Replace a section («B вообще без лирики… Может можно добить так, чтобы множественные секции редактировать одним махом?»; «При генерации семплов с заменённой секции в Takes не появляются 1/2 новых трека… только по F5»; «добавляй не просто anew, и название секции, но и временной отрезок»): lab/section.py takes parts [{a, b, section}], up to eight, sorted, apart; each version sings them in turn, the draft of each from the codes so far (the sections before it already new), its stretch laid in, the length unchanged, so the later cuts stay where the page heard them; the drafts trashed after; replaced.json keeps parts. The titles: «Song · Chorus 1:02–1:25 anew A» (sections joined by +), written by the lab, past the page's 80. The page found takes from the engine's log («[Library] Saved»), before the lab put them in their workspaces: now GET /section/job?id= is followed every 3 s and each version landed reads Takes and the Librarian again (ruach-catalog); jobs running when the page opens are followed too. The dialog: every section heard clearly as a chip, the clicked one ticked, a text and its syllable warning for each ticked one; the lyrics changed last to first, so no section's place moves. The B without words of the 13th is the model's luck on its seed; a check of the sung words is in 2.1.0. ② Compare («на 1200px ширины и с wavesurfer wave… лайк, дизлайк и заезду. Галочку „скрыть дизлайк“ как в Суно»): min(1200px, 100%); the wave drawn from the server's peaks of a take (a file of the tree read in the page), played part lit, a click or drag seeks (no library: the player's own way of drawing); thumbs and star through the Librarian's rate and the take's favourite; «Hide disliked» (yue2.cmpHideDisliked, on by default); a version disliked while it plays gives way to the next shown one from the same second. ③ Colour («Добавление винила, микропотрескиваний и т.п. ... как опцию в один из финальных шагов. Это как звуковой эффект»): the Debunker's stage «Окраска» between 432 Hz and the loudness, ffmpeg's anoisesrc bricks (pink, brown, velvet for the crackle and the clicks) each brought to the song's loudness plus colour_db by its RMS measured at a=1, every channel its own seed (L/R correlation 0.00), the seed new each run; measured on 20 s of the sambuca A: the layer at −59 dBFS for −36 under −23.1 LUFS, the song's loudness unchanged. ④ A cover to its namesakes («Скопировать обложку в одноимённые семплы»): POST /art/namesakes {name, to, replace}; never a frozen take, nor one being drawn; a replaced cover kept in artwork-removed/. ⑤ Engine («ПРЕСЕТ ПАМЯТИ GPU вообще нахер не нужен. Всё можно и нужно двигать ползунками в multi-GPU блоке»; «VAE дропдаун продублировать»; «На RTX3090 я бы однозначно советовал QWEN Image 2512»; «В МОДЕЛЯХ и LoRA только Krea 2 Muse доступна»): the preset hidden with its Auto kept underneath (a VRAM limit saved makes it Auto again, the knobs follow); #setVae the ☰ menu's twin; /machine suggests qwen2512-q6 or q4 where the room holds it and the page sets «By the cards»; the Krea set's row «Krea painter»; the Qwen notes say the 31 GB holds the text encoder, which leaves the card before the painter (artist_qwen and art_job: the encoder on, the prompt read, the encoder to the CPU, empty_cache, then the transformer, then the VAE in tiles). The internal list after 2.0.0 is heresy/docs/TODO-2.0.0-PLUS.md (kept out of releases by make-release).

**2.0.0-rc7, cut** (13.10.2026; Viktor: «Одобряю срез RC7 как посчитаешь готовым»): the release tree dcf52ad (a private set's name taken out of this file first), the clean clone green (790/0, player 3/0, Pinokio 74/0, places 26/0), igrbible/Ruach_Studio main 34e0b23 and tag v2.0.0-rc7, the pre-release https://github.com/igrbible/Ruach_Studio/releases/tag/v2.0.0-rc7 with Ruach_Studio-2.0.0-rc7.tar.gz (25.2 MB) and its sha256, the card on rc7 in twelve languages on Orpheus.

**The morning of the 14th** (1426; Viktor 14.10.2026). ① The voices («Бери экстра теги… Пометь их особо как не структурные»; «правильнее будет писать `[Vocal: Female/Male ... ]`, `[Spoken: Female/Male ... ]`. Без Solo»): the cheat-sheet's group marked voice, six rows in his form; heresy-complete.js offers them after the sections at a line's start and after a line's first tag, found by who (before the dash), never numbered, marked «not a section»; the meter's groupOf reads «Solo Female Voice» as who sings. The guide (line-aligned in all twelve: 104 the stress mark, 105 the capital, 110 the round brackets, 111 the Hebrew word): his ear of the 14th, the stress mark at about 90 %, a capital alone seldom, round brackets an echo or nothing, square brackets the command; the voices; one syllable moves the whole song; 8 to 9 minutes. ② The covers («Лучше вообще без картинки, чем визуальную ложь»): the prompter paraphrased the look and kept the name («A duduk with reddish-brown apricot wood…»), and the painter drew what it half knew by the name (a gourd with a cane through it); the critic, asked with the name, said it back (YES over the gourd, over a pipa as «a sambuca»), and three NOs kept the last picture anyway (the gudok a cello). art_job: the look first word for word, the prompter writing the scene only (ASK_SCENE), the name never sent; the critic asked by the look; a look's own words again on the next seed; three NOs, no picture (SystemExit, the job failed, said). Night batch of the 13th: 195 probes, 10 kept against the critic. Tested on copies: the duduk a straight pipe with its reed, the sambuca a bowl-backed long-necked lute; the gudok a pear-shaped body with two round holes, but a violin's scroll. ③ Unload («На кнопке выгрузки моделей нет сторожа… перепроверь»): the lab takes no POST without a body («send the file as the body») and the page sent /artist/unload with none: 400 since 1370, the kept painters never let go by the Bar (a busy painter is never let go: his run of 08:56 drew its three pictures). The page sends {} now. ④ Fonts («выставь эти же шрифты по умолчанию… перепроверь, чтобы абсолютно все typefaces были в Студии»; «Недостающие шрифты бери из моего ~/.fonts/ или из системного»): Liberation Mono 2.1.5 (OFL) from the system, Raleway whole from his /usr/local/share/fonts, Lato 2.015 and Noto Sans' bold and italics from Ubuntu's packages, as TTF (the lab serves .ttf too), declared after Google's subsets; check-vendored keeps them. Michroma is Latin only: its Cyrillic comes from the text's font. Still missing (Google only, on his word): IBM Plex, Space Grotesk, Bodoni Moda and Noto Sans Mono in bold and italic. ⑤ The Artist's stage: icons in two halves (made from it · seen and kept), three icons drawn (type, disc, scaling). ⑥ The lighthouse letterings of 12.10 (1394) copied from the test lab into his gallery: 180421 (Qwen-Image 2.1 lettering a base), 180627 (ПЕСНЯ МАЯКА), 181038 (LANTERN SONG, its second picture spelled right).

**The afternoon of the 14th** (1427, 1428; Viktor 14.10.2026). ① The lyrics' toggles («кнопки НОМЕРА СТРОК и ОРФОГРАФИЯ перенеси к тайтл ТЕКСТ в строку - вправо… SVG иконки»; «в подвале редактора отображение статистики разведи на читаемые блоки»): heresy-lyrics.js attach(area, {head}) puts the list-ordered and spell-check icons at the Lyrics' title's end (order 9, after help.js's (i)); the meter in blocks (words, tags, hand, look, tools), its numbers bold in the text between tags only. ② Fonts («твои три Unbounded…, Tektur…, Jura… тоже скачай и прикрути»): Google Fonts' woff2, Latin, Cyrillic and Greek, every weight, into lab/fonts with their OFL; check-vendored.sh keeps them. ③ Krea out («Krea вырезай. Мы обоюдно решили»): art_job, artist, artist_job, artist_qwen, artist_loras, lab and weights without Krea 2; the prompt writer and the nsfw filter stay as weights' «Covers' helpers» (fetch-heresy --artwork, 8.4 GB); launcher/extras.sh takes 2512 Q6 from 23 GB of VRAM, else Q4; on the Forge its files moved aside to removed-1428-krea/ (33 GB), the rm his. ④ The brushes («объединить в один спадающий дроплаун… "КИСТИ ХУДОЖНИКА"… Turbo/Lightning сможешь в этом спадающем списке пометить?»): #arBrush with optgroups by model, #arPainter and #arModel hidden as the state the room's code reads; the sampling kept per brush (the fp8 file in its key, the painter's own as the fine-tune's fallback); artist.is_base: a file named BASE… is 2512 itself, Lightning 1.0 and 8 steps by default, sampling()'s range by it; art_job names it «Qwen-Image 2512 (fp8)». A cover measured on the Forge: Q4 with Lightning 89 s of painting, fp8 BASE 78.5 s, the critic seeing both. ⑤ A note («к картинкам тоже прикрути поле для заметок»): POST /artist/note (star's way, refused while the run draws), Note… in Seen and kept, heresy-dialog's prompt with opts.area (Enter a new line, Ctrl+Enter keeps), the note in the stage's tip. ⑥ The Bar («отдельную позицию для названий комнат в Бар… подсветка под названием активной комнаты… арочно градиентной»): FONT_ROLES.rooms (#fontRooms, the text's by default); the active tab's pill gone, a ::before 18 px wider on each side painting radial-gradient(50% 100% at 50% 100%) of the ink from 14 % to nothing (the first try, «ellipse closest-side at 50% 100%», drew nothing: at the bottom edge its vertical radius is the distance to that edge, 0). ⑦ The checks: suite-1428.part; the old Artist sections moved off Krea's stubs (Q4 with Lightning, 2.1 for a painter without it, the sampling read under its brush). Kitchen and live 798/0. Patches 1427, 1428.

**Who may open the studio** (1429; Viktor 14.10.2026: «IP Студии и Лаба мы же раньше уже ограничивали локалхостом и масками локальных сетей. Оно не работало? … юзеры без файрвола... или на публичном вайфае - это проблема. Может в Engine настройки блок по безопасности IP сетки добавить? И напоминалку попапом для свежих инсталляций. С минигайдом там же, и чекбокс "Моё решение осознанное"»). 1114 worked as written: the engine answered 127/8 and the private ranges only, and the lab listens on 127.0.0.1 alone, behind the engine; but a café's Wi-Fi is a private range too. yue-server.cpp: Access {mode local|private|list, nets, acknowledged} in user/access.json beside settings.json (RUACH_ACCESS elsewhere), read again when its mtime changes, a broken file read as private networks and said in the log; the pre-routing gate asks access_allows (loopback always; list by CIDR through inet_pton, IPv4 and IPv6); GET /access says the decision and the asker (you, you_local, you_private, public, kept); POST /access validates (up to 32 networks, a list never empty), stamps acknowledged, and refuses with 409 {lockout} a choice that would shut out the asker unless lockout:true. Tried from the laptop over the VPN (10.8.0.2) against a kitchen engine on :41883: private, the lockout refused then taken, the list by 10.8.0.0/24, five bad requests 400, a corrupt file back to private. The page: Engine → Security (three choices, the networks, «+ 10.8.0.0/24» for the asker's own, Keep it; a row of its own under Updates); the first start's question (a mini guide naming the port, the same choices, «My decision is a conscious one» before Keep it, Later asks again next start) before the machine's scan, never in a test browser; the mock engine answers /access. suite-1429.part. Patch 1429.

**The audit** (1430; Viktor 14.10.2026: «полный аудит нашей Студии и найди все скрытые баги и подводные камни»). Measured, then fixed where a silence could lie (CLAUDE.md 2.8b: a failure's empty answer that a success could give too). ① systemd/reload-when-idle.sh waited for «"jobs": {}» literally: a failed art job whose take was deleted stayed in jobs and held the lab's reload for ever (13.10); idle now is busy false and nothing queued or running. ② Nine subprocess.run calls without a timeout (debunker ×7, collection, inspect_job): ffprobe 120 s, a pass 3600 s, an export or a loudness read 1800 s. ③ places._studio_busy took any failure for «not busy»: now only a refused connection; a studio silent for 4 s may be writing, the move waits. Found by the test itself: the first run read the studio busy because Viktor was rendering at that minute. ④ gpu_roles.load and gpu_guard._limits: a hand-broken user/gpus.json gave the lab's jobs every card, GPU0 too, with no limits; the last good roles and limits are kept now and the log says it (the file is written atomically, so only a hand breaks it). ⑤ section._place and lab.art_async said nothing when they failed; now the log does. 65 neutral excepts read in all; the rest are what they claim (a file not there, a count of nothing). ⑥ The critic: art_job.stand_in turns a YES into NO when its own first line names another instrument than the subject's look (the common stand-ins and the head words of the look's «not», less the look's own words); ten cases tried (duduk-violin, duduk-clarinet, sambuca-bouzouki, shofar-trumpet caught; the true ones passed). ⑦ The page: 610 pieces of HTML built from strings read by a scanner and the outside ones by eye; the escapes hold, but four links took what the internet wrote as is (an adapter's .civitai.info, Civitai's answer, an upscaler's OpenModelDB page, a cover's credit): http(s) only now; an adapter's architecture escaped. The engine's /library paths (canonical, under derived/) and the lab's fonts (a strict name) hold. Patch 1430.

**Nobody's network as the example** (1431). make-release's scan refused the bugfix tree: the Security card, the engine's answers, the guides ×12 and the suite gave the Forge's own LAN (192.168.2.0/24) as the example network. They give 10.0.0.0/24 and 172.16.0.0/24 now (the asker's own still comes from «+ my network»); the scan itself stays as strict as it was. Patch 1431.

**The page's small things from Viktor's screenshots** (1432). The Artist's Fine-tune field was still on screen: a `.field` rule's display beat its hidden attribute, and the check read the attribute (CLAUDE.md: «Проверь, применился ли CSS»); its select lives hidden beside #arPainter now and the checks read the computed display. The brushes say Base weights or Fine-tune first; Variations is Batch. ▶ Play this song a filled icon two icons wide in the take's row; Save style and Delete icons; the takes' ⋯ menu on one line, its items on one indent, opening upwards where the player leaves no room (seen in the probe's shots); the «[» list without a sideways scroll (a long hint gives way first); the MP3 bitrate drawn as its buttons; the ☰ 360 px with an 84 px label column; its «Theme» was a CSS word the translation never reached, now a text. A folded takes list opens over the take while the mouse rests on its strip (a fifth of a second first) and folds a second after it leaves; its Fold button then keeps it open. Patch 1432.

**The studio's port 7777** (1433). 41867 was the Kit's own; every script, the lab, the extras, the docs and the checks say 7777 (YUE2CPP_PORT picks another); start.sh prints the port in a bold line of its own, Pinokio's menu names it. The upstream Kit's docs under MISC keep theirs. Live switched at once: the studio restarted on 7777, the lab reloaded with it; reachable from the laptop. Patch 1433.

**The guard before every take made** (1434). Measured: the guard of 1167 stood in submitJob only, and only once the library had loaded; Replace a section and Import went without it, and a 💎 set in hand took new takes. One wsGuard now before all three: a workspace that takes new takes (never a 💎 set, its sections or a frozen one), else the question (runs sent together share it), else nothing; a Librarian that does not answer (the engine's 502) sends nothing, said why, and a studio with no Librarian at all (404: the page's test server) keeps nothing from being made. The only open workspace comes into hand unasked; a new install gets «My songs». Found on the way: giving the mock a Librarian broke eleven old checks written for a page without one, so it went back; the takes' list's shield carried a tip against the list's rule. Patch 1434.

**The veil** (1435): the head's 2.5 s timeout showed the veil's English logo before a slow page was translated; the line runs from the first moment, ten seconds; the words with the language; the logo and OSEM from 2.2 s in it. Patch 1435.

**The lyrics' arc, the Style's letters, Suggest** (1436): the arc's slips (verses 1, 2, 3…; Verse › Pre-Chorus › Chorus › Post-Chorus, a section left out, none turned back; a Bridge or an instrumental part opens the cycle; Intro before, nothing after an Outro) amber in the meter; letters other than Latin named under the Style; ✦ Suggest reads the lyrics whole, 8000 characters at most. Patch 1436.

Patch 1437.

**Models v2 rebuilt whole** (1440). The local Ruach_Studio_Models is the repo 1:1, every file a hard link to the studio's own (heresy/MISC/hf-v2/: hf-v2-mirror.sh, hf-v2.list: DEST | SOURCE | ORIGIN | LICENCE), SHA256SUMS and MANIFEST.tsv (each .safetensors with its tensors' SHA-256), guard-own-voice.sh over it; hf-v2-upload.sh sent it (Xet found nearly every byte already: 13 minutes) and took 24 paths off the tip (Krea 2's 17, its three adapters, the old names of four); Krea 2 off v1's tip too. 157 files, 239.9 GB, every LFS SHA-256 checked against the mirror. Left out and said in the card: what Civitai's authors do not let be shared (Outpaint, Turbo 4step, FinalCut and others: read by their SHA-256 from Civitai's API), the Kit's adapters whose repos carry no licence, the trainer's base, the private. A live break found on the way: artwork/Qwen-Image-2512/lightning/ pointed into a folder Viktor had renamed; pointed into PUBLIC/ now. Patch 1440.

**Files known by their content** (1441, Viktor: «пользователь волен переименовать скачанное»): tools/hf_expect.py --by-content and --manifest (a file missing or of another size is looked for in its folder by its SHA-256, or by its tensors' from v2's MANIFEST.tsv; the sums kept in tmp/hf/content-ids.json); fetch-heresy.sh asks so for the Artist's adapters; fetch-ruach-loras.py says the Forge's voices «here: its header its own, the same weights» where 1296 said «differs»; lab/weights.py's Artist's adapters from the manifest (nine, 6.3 GB). v2 pinned at ca8a1e2. Its check found a file named .safetensors that is none stopping the finder: no match now. Patch 1441.

**Qwen-Image 2.1's fine-tunes and Turbo** (1442). FinalCut v1 (ComfyUI fp8) fuses each block's img_mlp gate and up: split by cosine against 2.1's own weights (the first half 1.00 against gate_layer, the second 1.00 against proj); qwen21-fp8 is 2.1 in every other way (is21()). Turbo 4step as 2.1's Lightning: off by default, 4 to 16 steps. Measured on GPU2 (RTX 3090, 1280²): 30 steps 96 s, Turbo 4 steps 42 s, 16.6 GB at the peak; Turbo reached 227 layers. Patch 1442.

**Outpaint…** (1443): as AusBoss says Outpaint v2 is used: the picture whole in the middle of the new canvas, flat gray #808080 around, the instruction first word for word, «Scene:» and the words after it, 25 steps, the adapter at 1, the picture's own pixels pasted back. 1:1 into 16:9 on GPU2: 90 s, 20.2 GB, 160 layers, no seam seen. Its check found the stage kept a drawing made before the lab had said its painters (no Letter it since 1394, no Outpaint): its key says them now and the stage is drawn again when they come. Patch 1443.

**The private names lock the release** (1444, Viktor: «Давай решим проблему с моим приватом… раз и навсегда»). make-release's scan knew the machine's shapes (a home path, a private address, a mail, a token), not the names that are Viktor's own: the scripts that rebuild Models v2 name them in their stop-lists, and tracked under heresy/tools/ they would have gone public unseen (the classifier refused both to list them among make-release's kitchen files and, at first, to move them). The names live now in heresy/MISC/private-terms.txt, which no release carries; a release naming any of them in a file or a path stops, and without the list nothing is built. Checked over the release as it is (clean), a name in a text, a name in a path, no list. The scripts moved into heresy/MISC/hf-v2/. On the way: the check that cut the new block out of make-release counted to the wrong «fi» and ran its git step over Ruach_Studio.Release; nothing was committed (its tree was clean), and the check now cuts at the block's own end. Patch 1444.

**Every weight from one repo** (1445, Viktor: «MERT, realaudio и Comfy YuE2 клади в v2… Лучше всё тянуть Студии из одного репо, чем бегать по всему инету»). CC BY-NC 4.0 lets a work be shared, not for gain, with its authors named; Comfy-Org's YuE2 (bf16, int8), Mothersuperior's tokenizer head and m-a-p's MERT-v2-FullSong (its LICENSE and notices beside it) go into v2 unchanged, with Sketch Smudge (prithivMLmods, Apache-2.0; its source named by Viktor), every file checked byte for byte against its author's repo at the pin. The listener's GGUFs lie in v2 as the studio reads them (checkpoints/Qwen2.5-Omni-7B-GGUF/, unsloth/, mradermacher/); its licence in the card corrected: Qwen publishes Qwen2.5-Omni-7B under Apache-2.0 (ggml-org's card names it qwen-research and links to that Apache-2.0). download-models.sh takes YuE2's GGUFs from v2 (each the same as Serveurperso's at its pin, checked), fetch-heresy.sh Whisper, UniverSR (into universr/, which upscale_job.py reads first; an install holding woongzip1's in hf_cache/ keeps it), the listener, the Artist's helpers and painters and the Trainer's base, fetch-ruach-loras.py the studio's own adapters (the Kit's library left to its own downloader). From their authors still: the stems' models (audio-separator reads its own list beside them, from GitHub without it), the Kit's library of adapters (three of them carry no licence to copy), Qwen2.5-Omni-7B whole. The v2 card names v1 nowhere; About → Model weights points to v2; v1's and the old v2 card's sources leave docs/ for MISC/hf-v2-papers/. Tried in a fresh root (the big files there as hard links, the small ones and one adapter to fetch): eleven parts fetched from v2, each file's SHA-256 the mirror's, a second run all here; it found that one adapter missing brought all ten again, the nine here in a folder of the user's own included (the check knew them by their content, the downloader did not): hf_into fetches only what its check names now, by its own path (all of the pattern when the check names more than it shows). Found on the way: the live checks after 1443 never ran (a relative path, and a user unit starts in $HOME) and the last run's logs read as green; run again with the logs cleared first: 821/0, 18/0, 3/0. Patch 1445.

**A LoRA's wave and the budget its layers share** (1447, Viktor 14.10.2026: «LoRA ересь и алхимия»; 15.10.2026: «И не забудь за динамичную волну, именно ту, что ты воплотил в слайдерах стилей»). The engine (lora-rt.h): an adapter with a wave or in a budget is no longer merged at load: y = W x + g·B(A x), A and B in F32 on the model's backend, g set before each step from its strength × its wave × its budget's share; the music half moves g at every token it writes, the sound half takes a row of gains, one a frame of the chunk. A budget: the strengths of a group add up to its cap at most (the measured red lines, music 2.25, sound 1.5), shared in proportion or served in the request's order, and what one adapter's wave lets go, the next one takes. An adapter's full deltas (realaudio's llm2vae and the like) stay merged at their flat strength (LoraSet::lora_skip); the decode graph is keyed by the set's build; YUE2_NAR_GRAPH_NODES makes room for the adapters' nodes. The lab (lora_alchemy.py, GET /lab/lora/profile): each adapter's ‖B·A‖ by layer and module, attention against MLP, how far it moves a layer at 1.0, the overlap of two and the cosine of their deltas; the budget's groups are its word. The page: a wave beside each row's strengths (the sliders' editor: Flat, Arch, Draw; one wave for a pair's two files), Budget Off, Share, In order (the rows dragged), the request's "curve", "group" and "lora_groups", brought back by a take, a prompt and the draft. Measured on GPU2 (BF16): merged and on the graph the same token for token over all 600 frames at 0.75 (greedy); the sound half's latents 0.54 % apart (41 % from none); the set loads in under a second where a merge takes 9.6 s. The six library adapters all reach the 28 layers (overlap 0.7–0.97) and their deltas stand nearly at right angles (cosine 0.00–0.05): stacked, they reach further than either, less than their sum. The slider arch's line in the log at every step silenced (Viktor: «К хуям убери этот лог арки»). Patches 1447.

Как в Сайте-Визитке, так и в Студии»): the eleven catalogs of the page and of the visiting card say the name as its logo does (heresy-logo-words.js), in the genitive where the grammar asks for it («собственные Руах Студии»); a name quoted as a name, links and repositories stay as they are.

**The LoRA row, as Viktor tried it** (1457, Viktor 15.10.2026 with his screenshot of two adapters in order: «Попробовал LoRA по порядку. Это круто. Только ползунки общей музыки и звука активны в этом режиме… Три кнопки ВЫКЛ / В ДОЛЮ / ПО ПОРЯДКУ… А ВЫКЛ что делает? Режимов у нас же два? Тогда ВЫКЛ и не нужен, если ты не вшил в этот режим нечто. Выровняй начальную точку ползунков, чтобы они были симметричны по длине. И касательно надписей МУЗЫКА / ЗВУК предлагаю svg иконки с подсказкой по наведению. Для музыки аккордная svg, для звука — звуковая волна… колонку с названием адаптера можно ужать вдвое, обрезать троеточием имя адаптера, а при наведении мышью распахивать в полную длину, не смещая дизайн, — как ты сделал с обрезкой имён в волкспейсах»). ① The rows' sliders in order: not reproduced. A real mouse (claude-1457-probe.mjs, headless Chrome at 2560 px) dragged a row's strength 0.5 → 0.825 with the budget off, in Share and in order, on the page built after 1453; his screenshot of 12:55 already shows 1453's row, so it is not an old tab. Asked him for the browser and what the thumb does (stays, or moves and jumps back). His answer the same day: «По ползункам общей силы — двигаются как и раньше. В обоих режимах. Предлагаю в режим „По порядку“ переписать алгоритм, потому как показывает ту же общую силу как в „В долю“… N=(A+B+C+...)/count»: nothing was stuck, the point is the together's figure. The engine (lora-rt.h) adds the adapters at the same moment, y = Wx + Σ g_i·B_i(A_i x), and In order only says who takes the budget first; an average runs nowhere. Proposed instead: in a budget mode the together shows what runs (min of the sum and the red line) beside what is asked, and the row that gives way marks its cut; waiting for his choice. ② Off was no budget at all: each adapter at its own strength, the sum free to pass the red line (his «Звук вместе 2.00» in red; Share would hold it at 1.5). Not a mode, so no button: Share and In order only, none lit is no budget, the lit one pressed again lets it go (its tip says so in all twelve); the request and the saved yue2.loraBudget unchanged ("off" still the word). ③ Each half's strength opens with an icon of one width instead of a word, 18 px: a chord (three heads on one stem, drawn in Lucide's line) for the music half, Lucide's audio-waveform for the sound half, the half's name in its aria-label and the strength's own tip («Music 0.50…»); so the two sliders of a row and all the rows start and end alike in every language. ④ The name's column 0.42fr instead of 0.85fr (64 px at least), the name and its trigger word cut with an ellipsis; under the pointer the whole name lies over the row in .coll-fullname (the Librarian's workspaces, 1105), gone when the pointer leaves or the page scrolls. The guide in all twelve: the Off sentence is the none-lit one, the LoRAs bullet says the icons and the cut name. Kitchen suite 814/0: lr57 new (icons, alignment within and across rows, the column under 26 % of the row, the hover, the vtip, the two modes and their walk share → off → order → off); lw47 and lr53 turn the budget off by pressing the lit mode.

**The LoRA section finished** (1458, Viktor 15.10.2026 after the duduk's wave: «Волна реально работает… Не хватает ещё более чёткой сетки в бекграунд редактирования графика… поле для ввода примерного расчётного по длительности трека генерируемого. Выставлять по этому числу минут и секунд сетку в графе волны, чтобы по ней, а не на глаз, двигать волну»; «Редактирование параметров с волной адаптеров можно так же в full screen по кнопке — как у меня с редактором лирики. На 1800px»; on the together sliders: «сам решай, как правильно… Твоё предложение принимаю по показу более точных долей применения в выхлопе»; «Арку в Гайд. Возьми пока чо обрезку из моего скрина»). ① The wave's graph at its own size in pixels (190 px in the form, up to 440 over the page; round points and one-pixel lines at any width, drawn again when its width changes), a grid of the strength's quarters and twentieths and of minutes and seconds along the length the engine runs the wave over. Measured in the engine, not assumed: the request's duration caps the music half's frames (pipeline.h: budget = duration x 25) and the sound half's span follows it (lora_span = kept + max_tokens), so the wave runs over Output's length, not over the song's own; a song that ends earlier never hears the wave's tail. «Song ≈» (m:ss, minutes or seconds; yue2.loraSongLen) marks the song's expected end with a dashed line and dims what lies past it; longer than Output's length, a word says the song stops there. A read-out says where the pointer stands (time and percent); Shift lands a point on the grid (a tenth of a minute along 4:00, twentieths up). ② The button beside the LoRAs' name lifts the picker over the page, up to 1800 px wide (the lyrics' way, 1169); ⤡, Esc (before the frame's own Esc) or a click beside it puts it back, and so does another room; the notes keep their place in the form. ③ What the budget lets run, counted as lora-rt.h counts it with the waves at their top: in order the top row first, shared in proportion, capped at the red line. A strength that gives way shows its share amber and underlined, its road dimmed past a tick, its tip saying why, the floating strength «1.0 → 0.5»; Music, together and Sound, together show what runs of what is asked and are amber, not red, when the budget holds them. The engine adds adapters at the same moment (y = Wx + Σ g·B(Ax)), so the sum is the same in both modes and the rows differ; his first word, «Только ползунки общей музыки и звука активны», was this, not stuck sliders. The guide in all twelve: «The wave's grid», «An emotional arc» (a voice adapter on an arch; under In order a duduk on an arch over a voice hands the song between them), the shares under a budget, and his screenshot's wave as guide/lora-wave.png until the final shots. Kitchen suite 817/0 (three new checks: the grid, «Song ≈» and Shift; the shares in order, shared and off; over the page and back).

**A take's four blocks, and a right-click on a wave's point** (1459, Viktor 15.10.2026 with his picture of a take's Download / Refiner / Files / Edit: «реструктурировать эти четыре блока и сделать более восприимчивым для глаза? Где возможно, SVG иконки»; «Знаешь что не продумали? правой кнопкой мыши снятие точки с кривой»). Each block is a column of whole-width rows, every row a drawing then its name, so the four read as short lists of one rhythm: file-down for WAV, FLAC and MP3 (its bitrate beside it); a spectrum's bars and spell-check in the Refiner; braces, music and repeat in Files; crop, scissors and gauge in Edit; every block's name with its own drawing in the accent, Make again too (refresh). Seven Lucide shapes added to the page's set (crop, scissors, gauge, braces, music, spectrum, folder); the labels the page writes (WAV's format, MP3) go into the name and leave the drawing. A right-click on a wave's point takes it out as a double-click does, the first and the last staying; the hint and the guides in all twelve say so. Kitchen suite 819/0 (two new checks; the Artist's live preview failed once and passed on the next run).

**Steps of one's own in the Refiner** (1460, Viktor 15.10.2026: «написать всё как единый плагин, который мы не выложим в паблик, но он будет висеть у меня в пайплайне… И наверное на этом этапе и нужно начать прикручивать DAW от Plenio»; «И уходи к Огранщику. Ультракод включил»). The studio's general hook, with no plugin in it: a plugin is a folder in user/plugins/ (or a link) with a plugin.json (its id, name, its own python if it wants one, its steps: a script inside its folder and fields of three kinds, select, number, check). The lab (lab/plugins.py, 12 checks in lab/test_plugins.py) lists what reads whole and names what does not, tells the page only names, words and fields, cleans what the page sends into each field's kind and bounds, runs a step as PYTHON SCRIPT AUDIO OUTDIR PARAMS.json on the CPU one at a time, and lands its files as a branch of kind "plugin" with its fields and measure (GET /plugins, POST /plugin/run). The page puts each plugin step after the studio's own in the Refiner's row (a dashed edge), draws its panel from what it says (a source and its fields), asks the lab, says when it is in the tree; the tree heads the branch with the step's own name and fields, and its files play, compare (Compare all too), lie over the spectrum and serve as a source. docs/PLUGINS.md says the format. The kitchen's server now asks the studio at 7777 (it still asked 41867, the port before 1431). Kitchen and live suites 820/0, check-i18n 18/0, check-player 3/0.

**The Refiner as the Engine and the Guide** (1461, Viktor 15.10.2026: «Если считаешь, что нужна переделка дизайна комнаты Огранщика, или несколько вкладок внутри, — одобряю»). Wider than 1100 px the room is a grid: its head, the import and the chain across the top; the steps as tabs in a column at the left, the Engine's look (1450), grouped in the chain's order (Debuzz › Upscale › Stems › Remaster): Hear and look (Spectrum, Artifacts, Lyrics), Mend (Debuzz, Upscale, Stems), Finish (Remaster, then a plugin's steps with a dashed edge); the open step at the right, as tall as it is (the column's extra height goes to the tree's row), and what is made from the take right under it. Narrower, one column as before. A plugin's panels stand before the tree, its fields in a row of their own widths. The guides in all twelve say the groups and point at docs/PLUGINS.md. The kitchen suite's new check had left the suite in the Refiner and eight Creator checks after it saw nothing; it goes back to the room it found. Kitchen and live suites 821/0.

**Six stems** (1462, Viktor 15.10.2026: «BS-RoFormer-SW на 6 стемов — только ЗА. У нас сейчас 4 стема на выходе»; on ep317: «Вокал очень качественно отделён. Суновский стеммер - полная лаза в сравнении с твоим ep317»). stems_job.py six: ep317 for the voice and the instrumental as in four (it keeps the voice he praised), then jarredou's BS-Roformer-SW (audio-separator's MDXC entry, 699 MB, in separation/roformer) on the instrumental: drums, bass, guitar, piano, other; its own voice unused. Measured on the test lab: a 164 s folk ballad (balalaika, button accordion, strings) in 122 s on GPU1 — drums −21.7, bass −22.6, guitar −47.2 (the balalaika's share), piano silent, other −24.4 dBFS, the five summing to the instrumental within −60 dB; a 21 s waltz put all of it in other. The page: «Split: six stems» after four, six in the chain, the tree's «six stems» and its time estimate; the Remaster mixes them as they are (the Debunker knows guitar and keyboard). The guides in all twelve. For a fresh install the model is still to come into the install's parts (its source and licence to settle before 2.0.0). Kitchen and live suites 822/0.

**Eight stems and their proper names** (1463, Viktor 15.10.2026: «В выхлопе стеммера предлагаю сразу вшить правильное именование... bass/drums, percussion, strings, winds, pianissimo, lead vocal, backing/choir vocals, etc.»). Eight: six, then becruily's karaoke Mel-Roformer on the voice (its Vocals the Lead Vocal, its Instrumental the Backing Vocals; no vocals.flac then, so the Remaster does not hear the voice twice) and UVR's 17_HP-Wind_Inst on the rest (Winds, and the rest without them as Other). Strings and percussion have no open model of their own: they stay in Other and Drums, and nothing is called Strings that is not only strings. UVR's VR models write 44.1 kHz as if it were the take's rate: the winds came 13 s short and sped up (150.71 s of 164.04, 44.1/48); the VR step runs at 44.1 and a stem of another rate goes back to the take's rate and length. Every stem carries a title beside its plain file name (Lead Vocal, Backing Vocals, Drums, Bass, Guitar, Piano, Winds, Other, Instrumental): the tree shows, compares and downloads by it (Take - Lead Vocal.flac), the DAW projects name their tracks by it, the Debunker knows the new files. Measured on the test lab (the 164 s folk ballad, GPU1): 240 s; lead and backing sum to the voice within −32.7 dB, winds and rest to the six-set other within −46.8 dB, the six parts to the instrumental within −44.2 dB; the winds −28.1 dBFS (the button accordion went there). Models in separation/: the karaoke 1.72 GB, the winds 224 MB. The guides in all twelve. Kitchen suite 823/0; live 822/1, the one the Artist's live preview, failing now and then since the afternoon whatever the patch.

**A plugin step on a set of stems** (1464, the next of the Refiner's own steps after 1460). The hook: a step may say `input: stems` (the lab checks the source is a stems set of the take, by its manifest, and gives the step its folder; any other source is refused in words: split the take, then pick the set) and a `python` of its own that wins over its plugin's (lab/plugins.py, 16 checks). The page gives such a step «A set of stems» for its source: the take's sets each once, the newest chosen; with none it asks for a split and keeps Run shut. docs/PLUGINS.md says both. Kitchen suite 824/0; the private plugin's mixing step tried end to end on the test lab (a set of eight in, its branch out at −14.01 LUFS from seven stems, the silent piano left out).

**A plugin step on the GPU** (1465, the next after 1464). The hook: a step may say `"gpu": true` (and `need_mb`, 8000 when not said); the lab then runs it as its own GPU jobs, in the heavy jobs' turn, on a card given to the jobs with room for it (free_gpu, never the studio's card), that card as `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES`; a step without it still waits for the CPU lock. A step on one file may name the files it is made for (`prefer`: `["lead_vocal", "vocals"]`): the page picks the last such file of the tree as its source until the source is picked by hand, and marks a GPU step «GPU» beside its button (its tip in eleven languages). docs/PLUGINS.md and the guides ×12 say both; plugins.py 20 checks; kitchen suite 825/0. The private plugin got its voice step on it, tried end to end on the test lab and on GPU1: a lead vocal of eight stems through a denoise and a de-reverb Mel-Roformer at the models' own 44.1 kHz (the take's 48 kHz brought back, the length and the timing exact: lag 0), then the compressor and the de-esser after it, 75 s for 164 s of song. The order was measured, not assumed: a 2:1 compressor alone lifts 5–10 kHz by 2.2 dB, with the de-esser before it by 1.7, after it by 0.7. A whole take given as its source is refused in words (a de-reverb over the whole song would be a wrong thing done quietly).

**The Artist's live-preview check made steady** (1466, the suite only). It failed in about three of eight full runs on 15.10 with nothing in the Artist changed: the place being painted not developed, its picture, its step and the next place all empty. The cause was in the wait, not in the room: the section before it leaves exactly two runs of its own painted (its queued one and its done one), and this check waited for «two runs in the list», which was true at once, before the room had fetched this section's runs; when the check read the room first, the running run was not in it yet. It now waits for that running run itself with the two in the list, and checks the same things. Three kitchen runs in a row: 825/0 each.

**The guides: Output near the length and no words under an instrumental tag** (1467). The wave's grid says to set Output near the expected length (a take asked for 9:00 ended at 6:37 and left the last quarter of its waves unheard) and the arc says it is measured (the winds' stem split from that take followed the duduk's drawn line: −30 dB at the start, −59 in the middle, −36 toward the end). Viktor's rule (15.10.2026: «В `[Instrumental Outro]` нельзя лирику прописывать… галлюцинирует хвост») joins the lyrics' tips: the last words go under a `[Breakdown]` before it. Twelve languages.

**The song's length kept by its mode; Output's length in the LoRAs; Chrome's held-back downloads** (1468, 1469; Viktor 15.10.2026: «Для муз профиля… макс на 10 минут… дефолт 8 минут… В дикторском… 15 минут»; «выводи также время вывода… под замком»). Max length was held within 16:00 in every mode, so a 12-minute request went through; now music stops at 10:00 (8:00 by default) and Direct's speech at 15:00, a longer value said and pulled back, also when a take's request comes into the form and when the mode changes. Beside «Song ≈» in a LoRA's wave, Output's length in m:ss behind a lock; open, a length typed there is Max length's, within the mode's top. Export to DAW says that Chrome may hold back a .dawproject or .rpp download (Downloads, Ctrl+J, Keep). Kitchen 828/0, live 828/0, languages 18/0, player 3/0.

**The Refiner's DAW** (1470 the lab, 1471 the page; Viktor 15.10.2026: «нужно начать интеграцию Plenio's DAW… в DAW можно регулировать кучу всего по стемам»; «нужна опция для двух версий стемов, и при сведении нужна прослушка в общей компоновке»). A set of stems on lanes in a new Refiner step, DAW, before the Remaster: each stem a lane and one more, rest, what the source holds beyond them (Plenio's residual: all lanes as they are give the source back, so a separation's misses are heard only where the mix is changed); each lane its version (the stem as split or the first file of a branch made from it, of its length: a cleaned voice), level −60…+12 dB, mute, solo, silence drawn on its wave (a right-click gives it back), compression, sends to a room and a delay. Level, mute, solo, versions and silence are heard at once: the lanes stream from Forge in eight-second FLAC stretches and play on one clock, sample for sample; compression and the sends are heard in the mixed branch. «Mix» renders on Forge with Plenio's own stem mixer, carried unchanged in lab/plenio_core/ (Plenio 0.8.0, Apache-2.0, his LICENSE and NOTICE beside it), into a branch of kind «daw» with its loudness; a mix peaking above −0.3 dBFS is lowered to it and says by how much. The wave of a lane shows its upper half and the whole one under the pointer. GET /daw/set, /daw/chunk, /daw/peaks, POST /daw/mix; test_daw.py 13 checks (a neutral mix is the source, a mute leaves exactly its stem out, a solo only it, a version swapped, a gain and a range); kitchen 833/0.

**The Librarian's guards** (1473; 15.10.2026, 23:21: standing on All Workspaces while the topbar named Фосфорида, a filter and «select all» sent 1136 takes of every workspace to the trash, 💎 sets among them, all brought back from the trash on his word; his rule: «Сторожа на все удаления, перемещения и т.п. По максимуму. Лучше семь раз спросить, чем за один всё проебать»). A lock of either kind on a workspace, or on the workspace it is a section of, now keeps its takes from the trash and from a move out of every other workspace, and refuses a take-out (until now only a lock on the tracks did, and the 💎 sets were locked as workspaces); every mass trash, move, take-out and hiding says where you stand, which workspaces it reaches with their counts, the 💎 ones, the takes in none and those picked out of sight, and asks a second time from All Workspaces, a view across workspaces, a 💎 set, past 50 takes or with takes out of sight («Better to ask seven times than to lose them all at once»); the bar says «across ALL workspaces» in red. An export that could not find takes says how many instead of calling a short ZIP ready. test_collection_locks.py 7 checks; kitchen 833/0.

**The menus placed inside the window; the take's menu in groups** (1474; Viktor 16.10.2026: «когда я с самого права, оно убегает за границу экрана. Флипай его влево. И пересмотри все пункты в контекстнике. Если логично, сделай подпункты, потому что оно у нас скоро разбухнет до потолка»). A menu was measured in English and translated after it was placed, so in Russian it grew past the window's right edge; its rows are now written in the page's language before it is measured, and a menu that changes its size is placed again (a ResizeObserver), flipped left at the edge. The take's menu gathers its rows in groups: Open › (the rooms, the sheet, the datasheet, the artwork), Marks › (like, dislike, favourite, protect), Artwork › and Library › (workspaces, move, pin, hiding); a group of one stays a row of its own.
