Its trash, its move and its hiding of several takes ask through the Librarian's guards (1473).

**DAW exports open with the parts sounding** (1475, the lab). In a REAPER project or a DAWproject of a take with stems, the mix, the instrumental and the whole voice come beside the parts muted, and of two sets the lesser is muted too, so the project opens playing the parts once, not everything at once.

**One sound at a time** (1476; Viktor 15.10.2026: «Воспроизведение из Огранщика не останавливается при включении плей в другой комнате»). Whatever starts playing anywhere in the studio (the player, the Refiner's players, the compare window, the DAW's mixer) pauses every other sound first.

**The eight stems keep the whole voice** (1477; Viktor 15.10.2026: «в "восьми" оставлять и цельный голос»). The eight-stem split keeps vocals.flac beside lead and backing, titled «Vocals (whole)», so a voice step can work on the whole voice; a remaster of the set takes either the parts or the whole voice, never both (the voice would sound twice).

**Export to DAW without Waveform; Bitwig first** (1478; Viktor 16.10.2026: «К хуям удали этот DAW»). Waveform is gone from the DAW list and the hints; Bitwig (DAWproject) comes first, REAPER second.

**Keys spelled as the engine sings them** (1479; the keys probe of 16.10.2026: every key of the selector, each a score in that key, written down again by SheetSage2). All 24 keys hold, but a C♭ in the key signature (G♭ major, E♭ minor) is sung as C natural (9–17 % of the notes a semitone off), as is a B♯; F♯ major and D♯ minor are clean. So a tie of six accidentals goes to sharps (F♯, D♯m) and the selector names the other spelling beside it («D♯m (E♭m)»); a score written in an unusual spelling of the key asked is spelled again, not moved.

**Full with a key: one Generate** (1480; Viktor 16.10.2026: «сделай в Full полный проход при выбранном ключе… Каждый раз вручную генерить партитуру — гемор»). In Full with a key chosen and no score, Generate asks for the plan alone, moves the score to the key (when the plan wrote the other mode, its mode stays, and the toast says so) and sings it, in one press; before, the key needed «Create ABC score» first. The suite's server-log check now looks for the server's own lines in the card rather than its first line, which this test's plan pushed past the card's 400.

**Imports gathered in 🚨 IMPORTED** (1481; Viktor 15.10.2026: «докрути в Библиотеку авто-создание воркспейса `🚨 IMPORTED`»). Every take imported from disk lands in the workspace «🚨 IMPORTED» (made the first time), and the Librarian opens it there.

**«Cancel it»** (1482; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Кнопка `Отменить запуск` > `Отменить`»). The run's cancel button and its question's yes say «Cancel it» (ru «Отменить»); the ✕ beside the run keeps «Cancel run» for its tip.

**The take card's buttons in the take's menu** (1483; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Все действия по треку, что есть в карте, недостающие продублировать в контекстное меню»). The take's menu gains Make again › (Retake, Reuse, Re-render sound, Regenerate from…, Edit and re-render, Transcribe this take, Trim to the text, End at…, Fade in…), Edit › (Crop…, Remove a section…, Speed…), Refiner › (Spectrum, Lyrics check), and its Download › the card's FLAC, request, score, MIDI, markers and loop. Each opens the take into the card (in the Creator, for what works on the card) and presses that very button, so it acts, asks and refuses as on the card; one the card holds off for the take in hand is shown off. The card itself is unchanged; its simplification waits for his look at a before and after.

**A plugin step's report in the tree** (1484; for the AI detector Viktor approved 15.10.2026 as a private step: «бери ансамбль Soulless (Prototypr, MIT). Это наш независимый судья»). A file of a step named .txt, .json, .md or .csv is a report: the lab marks it `"kind": "text"`, and the tree gives it a row of its own with Read (the text in the studio's dialog) and its file, no player, and no version to compare or source to pick. A step may print one `summary` line (300 characters at most), shown beside its branch. The hook is any plugin's; nothing of a plugin is in the studio. test_plugins.py 22 checks; kitchen 844/0, live 844/0.

**The eight stems' tip names the whole voice** (1485). Since 1477 the eight-stem split keeps the whole voice beside lead and backing; its tip now lists «Vocals (whole)» after Backing Vocals, in the page and the eleven catalogs (the stems' names stay English there, as the tree shows them).

**The take's tree in the chain's order** (1486; Viktor 15.10.2026, on «Сделано из этого семпла» after a plugin's branches on his take: it read as chaos). Each branch stands under the branch its source lies in, indented by its depth: a set of stems under the debuzzed take it was split from, a cleaned voice under its set; siblings keep the lab's order, the newest first, and a branch whose source is gone stands at the top.

**The tree's own players** (1487 the page and the lab; Viktor 15.10.2026: players as in the compare window, the top half of the wave, the lower half on hover). Every sound of the tree, the take itself first, plays from a round button beside its wave: the top half of the wave stands on its line, the whole wave about its middle under the pointer, the played part lit, a click on it seeks. The waves come from the engine for the take and from the lab's new `GET /peaks?name=&path=[&n=400]` for any file of its tree (the loudest of each stretch, read in blocks, kept by the file's stamp), asked when a row comes into sight. The row's own audio element still sounds it, hidden, so one sound at a time (1476) holds; a voice's row, with its fourth button, keeps its FLAC on its line (1487b: a seventh column). test_daw.py 15 checks; kitchen and live 847/0.

**The lean card** (1488; Viktor 15.10.2026: «нужно упростить карту, а весь "гламур"… перенести в контекстуху»; 16.10, on the before and after: «Да, внедряй твою версию карты семпла. Также "Перегенерировать с новым зерном" в "Сделать ещё раз ›"»). The take's card no longer shows its rows of buttons by default; since 1483 the take's menu holds them all, and «⋯» beside the title opens that menu. From «⋯» the last row, «The card's buttons», brings them back to the card (kept in this browser) and takes them off again; a right-click elsewhere has no such row. Regenerate with a new seed now leads Make again ›; on several checked takes it is that group's one row. The suite's older checks see the buttons on, as they were written for them; the lean default is checked on its own.

**Content Credentials (C2PA) on the downloads** (1489 the lab and the page; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Подпись C2PA поддерживаю»; «для паблика нужен выключатель, и в настройках Engine нужные поля для ввода своей инфы»; «Принимаю твоё предложение»). A signed record of where a file came from goes inside every download of a take or of a file of its tree, WAV, FLAC or MP3, and inside each audio file of the Library's ZIP: made by AI (YuE2) in Ruach Studio (trainedAlgorithmicMedia); refined by a person, with the Refiner's steps from the take on, when the Refiner made it; the author and the site or contact given in Engine. The sound is not touched (a RIFF chunk, an ID3 frame, a FLAC metadata block; about 3.5 KB). The page sends a download through the lab's `GET /c2pa/file?name=&format=&from=/library/…`, which fetches the engine's own file and signs it on the way; a take imported from elsewhere goes out as it came (`X-C2PA: unsigned: imported`), and a download asked to be signed that cannot be signed fails out loud rather than coming unsigned. The signer is the install's own certificate, made at first use in the user's folder (a root and a leaf, P-256, the key readable by its owner alone): readers say the signer is not verified, and the signature holds; a certificate and key of one's own (PEM), e.g. bought from an authority on the C2PA trust list, are named as verified. Engine › Folders and Security: the switch (on by default), Author, Site or contact, Your certificate, Its key, and what signs now. lab/c2pa_sign.py (c2pa-python 0.38, MIT/Apache-2.0), test_c2pa.py 9 checks; the kitchen proof stays in heresy/MISC/c2pa.

**The IRA merge in the studio; backbones by their files' names** (1490 the lab, start.sh and fetch-heresy.sh; Viktor 16.10.2026: «прикрутить скачивание этих GGUF в студии и выбор активной модели к базовым m-a-p весам»; «В дропдаун селекторов давай более полные имена: стоковые веса `YuE2 ...`», the prefix keeping room for a YuE3). Engine › Models and LoRAs › More models has «IRA merge»: BF16, Q8_0 or Q6_K from goldhub/YuE2-3B-Ruach_Studio-IRA-Merge (pinned in hf-revisions.txt; since 1494 at b23685a, the rebuild from Real Audio v9's fp32 file) into models/YuE2-3B-IRA-<quant>.gguf (`fetch-heresy.sh --ira Q8_0`). start.sh offers every models/YuE2-3B-<name>-<quant>.gguf beside m-a-p's own, by its file's name; the bar and Compute say «YuE2 BF16 · 7.2 GB», «YuE2 IRA Q8_0 · 3.8 GB», m-a-p's own first, then each merge by its name, BF16 before its quants. The kitchen suite's 1490 section took the model to give back from the page, which an earlier section had left showing another: it reads the mock's own now (six engine-card checks after it had failed on a Q8_0 left behind).

**The tree's players draw the whole wave** (1491; Viktor 16.10.2026: «реально ugly смотрится половинка… вернём полную стерео волну и бар увеличим на 8-12px по высоте»). The players of 1487 draw the whole wave about its middle, always, in a bar 38 px tall (30 before).

**Every section named on the player's wave** (1492, 1494; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Pre-Chorus не прописывается среди других. И наверное и другие стандартные»; «По длинным названиям секций… делай двух-трёх рядную лесенку… надписи перенести в верх волны, и лесенка будет спускаться, а не подыматься при оверфлоу надписей»). A name was drawn only where it fit before the next section, so a short Pre-Chorus after a long name had none. The names stand at the wave's top now, whole, each at its section's start; one that would run into a name before it steps a row down (three rows on the full wave, two on the compact one). Only when every row is taken at a start does the name freed first give up its tail (shortened, PRE-CH. or INSTR., or cut with «…»); a section with no room even for three letters keeps its line. «anew» (a section sung again) went to the wave's foot, where the names stood.

**His fonts and sizes are the app's own** (1493; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Мой выбор шрифтов и скейлов забери в дефолты. ПО дизайну ДВИЖОК закрыт. Красиво и всё на месте»). Tektur on the headings at −1 pt, Noto Sans on the rooms, Raleway on the section marks at +1 pt, Jura on the buttons at +2 pt; the text Raleway and the numbers Liberation Mono as they were. The Fonts card marks them «(default)»; «Same as the text» and «IBM Plex Mono, as they were» stay to be chosen, and are kept when chosen; a size is kept only when it is not its own. Found on the way: since 1437 the sizes and the buttons' font were applied only when something changed in the Fonts card, so a reload lost them; they are applied at load now. His sizes kept to the studio's shapes (1493b): the bar stays one row at about 1100 px (the workspace box narrows first when the buttons would take a second row), Create ABC Score and Generate are one size (min-width 11.4em, still 156 px at 0 pt), the find bar's buttons have the field's height, Free tempo has the lyrics' toggles' size again (1419). The suite's checks of the old defaults (1426, 1428, 1437, Default fonts, the lifted lyrics' head) expect his now.

**A merge's adapters in the backbone selectors** (1494 the lab and the page; Viktor 16.10.2026: «В селекторе на наши мёрджи не забудь подсказки добавить и доли по слияниям, чтобы не потеряться никому»). merge-loras writes what it folded in, and at which strengths, into the GGUF (heresy.merged_loras), and quantize keeps it; the lab reads it from the file's header alone (lab/merges.py, no gguf package; `GET /lab/models/merges`), each adapter named as loras/sources.json names it. Compute's list says it after the name («YuE2 IRA Q8_0 (merge: Instrumental 0.75 + Real audio v9 0.85)»); the tips of both selectors and of each entry say it line by line, a half each, and not to load those adapters again on top; a file the lab cannot read is said, not passed over. test_merges.py, 4 checks.

**A skipped LoRA out of reach; the rows part under a dragged one** (1494; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Когда мы в активированных LoRA в Творце скипаем адаптер, добавь полную деактивацию всех изменяемых элементов в таком блоке — ползунок, волну. Только перетаскивание активно и удаление из тайлов. И при перетаскивании добавь эффект раздвижения между блоками адаптеров — как воронку для передвигаемого»). A skipped adapter's strengths and wave are disabled, faded and out of the pointer's reach (no editor opens, no field on a double-click); its name (dragged in order, a right-click brings it back) and its ✕ stay as in any row. While a row is dragged it waits faded in its place and the rows part where it would land, a gap of its height opening and closing smoothly; over its own place no gap opens; the drop goes into the gap.

**The log's own window keeps its GPU column** (1495; Viktor 16.10.2026: «В детаче фрейма серверного лога нет колонки с GPU графами. Допишешь, если это возможно?»). The column was there since 1343, and the suite saw its two cards in a pop-up; in his Chrome the window is Document Picture-in-Picture, which opens at the size it was last left, and under 700 px the column was hidden by design. Picture-in-Picture is asked at 1240 × 560 each time now (preferInitialWindowPlacement), and a window under 700 px keeps the cards in a row under the log instead of hiding them.

**A LoRA hidden or deleted for good; an adapter gone from loras/ asked about; the Trainer's epoch asks IN / OUT** (1496 the page and the lab; Viktor 16.10.2026: «То, чего нет у Тренера в локальных выхлопах, не убирается… те адаптеры, которые сторонние, либо в попапе согласие на безвозвратное удаление из loras/, либо просто прятать из вида, а в хедере LORAS справа галочку добавить Show hidden»; «В активных остаётся удалённый адаптер… предлагал заменить зомби на другой, с другой эпохой, суффиксом, либо удаление из списка активных»; «по клику на эпоху выводить модальное диалоговое окно с опциями действий - IN/OUT/Cancel»). Remove from the list took an adapter out only while its run's checkpoint was there (the lab refused the only copy), so a downloaded one, or one whose run was deleted, stayed. A right-click on an adapter now: Hide from the list (kept with the page's settings, yue2.lorasHidden; Show hidden with their count in the LoRAs head brings them into sight, marked) or Delete from loras/ for good (`POST /lab/lora/delete`: a dry run first says the files, a folder left with no adapter in it and the bytes; the dialog says them; only adapter files inside loras/; a file of one's own run is a hard link of its checkpoint, which stays; test_lora_delete.py 8 checks). An adapter of the song gone from loras/ is asked about once the list comes: those of a name like its own (the same run first, then the same voice at another rank or suffix; its strengths, wave and mute kept), or out of the song; Later leaves it marked (its name asks again), and Generate sends nothing until it is answered. The Trainer: the block under the epochs is gone; an epoch's click asks IN / OUT / Cancel with its step, size and place in loras/ (OUT needs no second word), a right-click gives its file. The Voice kind trains at rank 32 (Viktor: «нужно r32… если крупнее матрица адаптера, больше оттенков голоса попадёт туда»).

**The trainer's held-out tracks and its early stop; the reading voices whole at rank 32** (1497 the lab; Viktor 16.10.2026: «у Тренера… есть ранняя остановка обучения при стабильном оверфите в NAR половинке по последним 2-3 эпохам? И если такой сторож у нас будет идеальным, то можно и 100 эпох выставлять»; «Обучать на всём объёме семплов по персоне»; «из вебинаров не бери ничего»). There was none: the trainer saw only its training loss, which falls while a run learns its tracks by heart. A share of the tracks (val_fraction; 4 to 16) is held out by the seed, the same on every start; at every save their middle windows are measured: the NAR's flow at t 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7 and 0.9 with fixed noise (two measures differ by the adapter alone) and the AR's CE from the song's start, into loss_log.db (val/flow, val/ar_ce) and output/RUN/val.json; patience saves with no held-out flow lower than the best by 0.3 % stop the run, the best named. Smoke on GPU2 (60 tracks, 3 epochs, 4 held out): flow 0.679, 0.663, 0.657. The reading voices: the 23 women of his BIGGEST_RU_BOOK archive (the audiobook readers he picked for voice prompting), each whole (heresy/MISC/claude-probes/tts_voice_sets_full_1497.py: every clip of 2.5–16 s once, one-minute tracks; 1167's sets were capped at 60), trained one after another on GPU1 and GPU2 from one queue (voice_train_driver_1497.py): rank 32, the Voice knobs, epochs by the set's length (40000 steps and 40 epochs at most), 2 % held out, patience 3. His own voice run at rank 32 goes on from 12 to 24 epochs first on GPU1 (its own knobs; no held-out tracks: it has seen them all). Two runs on one card: no; a voice run at r32 holds 11 GB allocated and about 19 GB on the card, and two would share the card's speed besides.

**The denoising steps on a road; the solvers by name; other solvers from a take's own codes; the wave's ear; the probes' guard counting KV sets** (1498; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Замени поле ввода шагов семплинга в Выводе на ползунок с 8-160 границами. Шаг 8. Дефолт 32»; «что если сделать опцию для генерации семплов сразу по паре или тройке семпплеров?»; «кнопку выключения Whisper отрисовки секций и лирики… Режим минималки в волне плеера»; «убери "1 вызов" из надписи Multistep. Лучше всплывающими подсказками»; «batch=2 убил VRAM»). Denoising steps is a slider of nine stops (8, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 160; 32 by default), its road amber to 24, greening from 32 to its full at 64, amber at 128, red at 160, his ear on Heun in its tip; a take's own count (92) is shown as it is, the thumb at the nearest stop. The solvers are named short; their calls a step are in the selector's tip, measured from the engine's own count: Midpoint and Heun two (Heun's last step one), Multistep and Euler one (multistep 32 steps = 32 network calls, 957 ms a step for two variations; midpoint 64 calls, 1910 ms). Also render with: the solvers ticked under the run's own, each at its steps; every take of the run is rendered again by each of them from its own codes with its own sound seed, titled «· Heun 64». The wave's ear beside the scope in the player: off, the bare wave (no Whisper, nothing drawn over it). The probes' guard counted two KV sets a probe as measured on 07.10, while the engine allocates three since the lyrics' own guidance (generate.h: 3 split, 2 guided, 1 not), each 2.63 GiB at the whole context: two probes of two minutes on IRA-BF16 asked 16.1 GB of KV over 7.2 GB of weights and the sound had no room (16.10, 16:17 and 16:22); the guard counts the form's sets and the context's share now, a merge weighing as its quant.

**The DAW over the whole window** (1499; Viktor 16.10.2026: «DAW будет самым интересным. И сразу внедряй переключение в максимальный full screen режим, и перехватывай в нём браузерный F11 на вкладке Студии»). Its button lays the DAW over the whole window (moved to the body while so, out of the boxes that held a fixed one; the window full screen when the browser lets it; the page's dialogs, menus and tips still over it), the lanes sharing the screen's height, whole waves; Esc, F11 or the button back. F11 on the studio's tab is the studio's: the DAW in sight goes full and back, elsewhere the studio itself full screen.

**A seed with its word** (1500; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Звуковой сид на вторую строку, если сам сид отрывается от лейбла»). The run card's seeds each one piece: a seed that does not fit goes to the next line with its word.

**The DAW, Bitwig's way** (1501; Viktor 15.10.2026: «DAW UX должен быть как в Bitwig»; 16.10: «DAW будет самым интересным»). A ruler over the lanes in their own grid: a click goes there, a drag draws the loop and turns it on; the loop played round. Zoom about the pointer (Ctrl+wheel, − + and Fit; the time under the pointer kept), Shift+wheel goes along, the view follows the playhead. The keys while the DAW is in sight: Space plays and stops, Home to the start (the loop's when it is on), L the loop, M and S on the lane picked by a click on its name, ↑ ↓ another lane, + − 0 the zoom. A live meter on every lane and on the whole mix (the peak held a moment); each lane its colour by what it holds (the voices amber, drums red, bass violet, guitar green, keys blue, the rest grey).

**The model in a take's passport** (1502; Viktor 16.10.2026: «В паспорте семпла не указана модель и её разрядность. Добавь прямо над VAE»). The Librarian's passport of a take names, in its Song group right above the VAE, the backbone it was made with (a merge by its name, IRAE, Merge3; the base one as YuE2), its quant and the bits that quant keeps (BF16 16, Q8_0 8, Q6_K 6, Q5 5, Q4 4); imported sound has none. The DAW's ruler (1501b) is a row of the lanes' grid of its own, no longer counted as a lane, its time over their waves to the pixel (the lanes' 3 px left edge kept on it, transparent).

**The DAW in the guide** (1503; Viktor 16.10.2026: «DAW будет самым интересным»; the guide said nothing of the Refiner's own DAW since 1470). The Refiner's tab of the guide, in all twelve languages, has a stop of its own for it, *The DAW: Stems on Lanes*: the lanes and Rest, their colours, a lane's version, level, M and S, meter, compression and sends, silence drawn on a wave, Reset; the ruler with its loop, zoom and scroll; the full screen and F11; Mix with Plenio's stem mixer. The Finish group names DAW beside the Remaster, and the Shortcuts table has its keys (Space, Home, L; M, S, ↑ ↓ on the lane picked; + − 0 and the wheels; F11). Each translation measured by check_guide.py against the English says what it said before (the same numbers, code, rows and lists; the bold grown alike).

**Marks on branches and stems** (1504; Viktor 15.10.2026: likes, dislikes and stars on the branch and stem rows of the compare window). Every file made from a take carries 👍 👎 ★ in the compare window as a take does, kept by the lab beside the files (outputs/TAKE/derived/marks.json, sent with the take's tree; POST /derived/mark {name, path, rating, fav}, a path outside the take's derived/ refused); a mark shows at once and, refused by the lab, is taken back and said, never kept only on the page; Hide disliked hides a disliked branch too. The DAW's mixes (kind daw) stand in the comparison, which left them out. test_derived_marks.py, 15 checks.

**The Trainer's pictures, his own and retouched** (1505; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Последние четыре скрина снял только что для тебя в гайды, визитку репо и сайт»; «имена на скринах заретушируй»; «два ночных и два дневных»; «Не пережимай мои скрины в более низкое разрешение»). His four shots of the Trainer at work (1920×1080, kept so) replace the guide's and the README's studio-train, train-charts, train-epochs and train-runs, and the visiting card's studio-train (by day and, new, by night: the tile follows the page's theme and its lightbox opens the one shown) and train-charts. The names in them that are private (his own voice's run and set, another singer's set, the readers') are written over with neutral ones (voice-baritone, -alto, -mezzo, -tenor) in the page's own Raleway at the weight its CSS gives each place, sized to the original's width, the rest of each line moved to close the gap (retouch_1505.py, claude-1505-retouch.mjs). An OCR pass over every picture of the guide and the card found the same kind of name already published since rc1 in three of them (studio-train, train-runs: his voice's r16 run and the readers; studio-engine: his voice's run in the log); all three replaced or retouched, and the card on Orpheus carries the new ones. The OCR found nothing private in the eight new files.

**The Refiner as a canvas of nodes** (1506; Viktor 16.10.2026: «По нодам Огранщика… предложить хочу идею ComfyUI расположения нод на канвасе. Вертикально страница на 1080p разрешении не прокручивается, всё под рукой и на глазах. Щёлк по ноде - модальное окно как в Творце с Композитором и музыкантом, навигация вперёд-назад по нодам»). Beside its list (Canvas / List in the room's head, kept per browser), the Refiner as a canvas that fits the window above the player: the take, the import and every step (the studio's and the plugins') a node where the work flows, thin wires the chain's way and bright ones the way the take in hand went (each branch's source, from the lab's manifests); a node its number, name, what it does, ✓ when done for the take, how many branches it made, a pulse while it runs. A click lifts its panel over the room (moved there while open and put back where it stood), ‹ › and Alt+← → go along the steps, Esc or a click beside it back; another room puts it back too. Nodes dragged stay where put (Arrange lays them out anew). The take's node opens what is made from it.

**A run no longer dies of its own step log** (1507, the lab). Viktor's voice run (r32, on his whole archive) died at 20:21:51 at step 35189 of 48384, 4 h 15 min in, on «database is locked» in the step log's commit: the trainer waited sqlite's 5 s while the lab read the whole log (35 000 steps) for the run's curves in one query, three runs keeping the machine busy. The lab now copies the log into memory by sqlite's backup in short steps (the lock held milliseconds) and keeps it per file until it changes, which keeps the runs started before the fix safe too; the trainer logs in WAL, waits 60 s for a lock, and a commit or an insert refused is said in its log and never ends the run. His run went on from its epoch 17 on GPU1. test_step_log.py, 7 checks.

**Run up to here, bypass, the frame off** (1508; Viktor 16.10.2026: «на третьей ноде правой кнопкой мыши клц или в оверлее ноды - прогнать до этой ноды включительно… как в Комфи. В общем, взять по нодам самое лучшее из идеологии Комфи канваса и воркфлоу»; «В модальных окнах нод не забудь снять ту старую рамку»). A node's right-click: Open, Run up to here (the chain's nodes: Debuzz, Upscale, Stems, Remaster), Bypass in the chain / Back into the chain (its own check in the chain's row), Arrange; Run up to here in the node's window too. Up to here runs the chain with its own settings and no further (the node run up to runs even when bypassed; the checks after it left out): the lab plans the chain's steps in chain_plan.py with `until` (test_chain_plan.py, 9 checks). A bypassed node is dimmed with its wires. In a node's window the step's panel has no frame of the old tab: its name is the window's title.

**No dropdowns in a node's window** (1509; Viktor 16.10.2026: «предлагаю напрочь избавиться от дропдаун селекторов, а выстраивать прямо на канвасе все позиции в колонку, с чекбоксами и/или селекторами, и такие блоки в весьма неприметную рамку… А если списки реально большие… дополнительное модальное окно поверх как в фильтре библиотеки или в селекторе воркспейса… Всю Студию оформляем в такой красоте и удобстве»). HeresyChoices gives a select its choices in sight: up to eight a column of radio rows in a quiet frame (short ones in a row), more a button with their count that opens the list over the page, a filter above it when long (arrows, Enter, Esc; Esc takes the list before the window). The select stays what everything reads and writes: a choice sets it and says change, a value set by code shows at once, options filled anew are drawn anew, a select turned off turns its choices off. First in the Refiner's node windows; the list keeps its selects; the DAW's lane versions and buses stay selects. The rest of the studio follows.

**The chain as a node; wires drawn by hand** (1510; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Если имеет смысл, закодить перелинковку коннектов нод, где это имеет вариативный смысл»; «И та же полоска пайплайна... её тоже можно отдельной нодой и в попап оверлей, с её привязками к самим нодам/модулям/плагинам»). On the canvas the chain is a node of its own: its steps as they are set (a bypassed one left out), its run on it (done of all, the step running, a pulse), its strip off the page while the canvas shows and in the node's window when opened. A wire drawn from a node's output (its port at the right edge) onto another node makes the first one's newest branch the other's source, as a pick in its Source would (onto a node that takes none: said, nothing changed); the sources as they are set are drawn as wires of their own, beside the chain's way and the way the take went.

**The DAW's waves whole** (1511; Viktor 16.10.2026: «В DAW волну рисуй полную, а то строки бегают по движению мышки. В DAW не экономь на этом»). Every lane's wave whole (both halves) at one height, 64 px; no lane grows under the pointer any more, so the rows no longer move with the mouse. In full screen the lanes still share the screen's height.

**Audio I/O** (1512; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Предлагаю отдельной вкладкой в Огранщике создать детект и настройку аудио интерфейсов как физических, так и виртуальных»). A step of the Refiner's own in a Setup group, last (after a plugin's steps), and a node on the canvas: the machine's audio interfaces as the lab sees them, physical by card (a USB one with its formats: channels, bits, rates) and virtual (ALSA's software devices, a loopback card, PipeWire/Pulse, JACK), and what does not run said; the analog gateway's output, input and rate (the rates both take) kept by the lab in user/audio.json, where a plugin's analog step reads them; an input's floor measured (two seconds through plughw, RMS and peak dBFS per channel: the Studio 24's −103.0 / −101.5 dBFS). The lab's service started before its user was given the audio group, so it reaches the devices through sg audio and says so (restarting the user's manager would have ended the trainings). lab/audio_io.py, test_audio_io.py (12 checks).

**Every wave of the tree from one x to one x** (1513; Viktor 16.10.2026: «в равномерную ширину оформи все волны, чтобы они не бегали вправо-влево»). In «Made from This Take» a row's buttons stand in one column as wide as the widest set of them (a voice's fourth, Lyrics check, no longer shortening its wave) and the name's column is narrowed by the row's own indent (a branch under its source): every wave starts and ends where the others do, measured after each drawing and on every resize.

**The gateway's loop** (1514). Audio I/O's Loop test plays a 1 kHz tone at −20 dBFS through the chosen output and records it from the chosen input at once (aplay and arecord, through sg audio where the service needs it), then says whether it came back, its gain, the noise before it and its THD (2nd to 5th harmonic) per channel; no latency is claimed, two processes starting apart. A physical output is asked first (two seconds, the monitors being somebody's ears); a virtual one runs unasked. The virtual devices are chosen by their ALSA address (a loopback's two were both «Loopback PCM», which aplay cannot open). Through the loopback card (snd-aloop, loaded 16.10 on his word) the loop answers 0.0 dB, digital silence, no THD: the plumbing proven before the cable. lab/audio_io.py loop and loop_measure; test_audio_io.py, 16 checks.

**The Refiner's profiles** (1515; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Профили по сетапу нод»). A row of its own above the canvas (the chain's row, empty in the canvas view, so the nodes keep their room and the page does not scroll at 1080p): a chip per setup kept by name, and Save as…. A setup is what the Refiner's steps are set to (the chain's checks and modes, the remaster's preset and its numbers, the upscale's and the debuzz's, a plugin's own fields) and where the canvas's nodes stand; never a take's own: its sources and its cut points (Trim start, Trim end at) are left as they are. A chip's click sets every step as a hand would (each control says change, so each module keeps its own preference) and says how many settings it changed; a choice this studio has not (a plugin gone) is skipped, not forced. Its right-click: Set it, Keep the setup as it is now under this name, Delete (asked first). Kept by the lab in user/refiner_profiles.json, for every browser of the studio; a name is 1 to 60 characters, a setup at most 64 KB. lab/refiner_profiles.py, GET and POST /refiner/profiles; test_refiner_profiles.py, 10 checks.

**A step's node cloned** (1516; Viktor 16.10.2026: «Клонирование некоторых нод»). A right-click on Debuzz, Upscale, Remaster or a plugin's step offers Clone: «Remaster ②» stands beside its own in its column (half a row away, its top edge doubled) with its own settings, its main choice said on it (the remaster's preset, the upscale's mode). Its window is the step's own panel with the clone's settings put in while it is open; on closing, or on stepping to another node, the clone keeps what was set and the step's own come back, so a run from the clone's window runs with the clone's settings and its file lands in the tree as the step's always does. In the window's order a clone follows its own. A setup taken while a clone is open keeps the two apart; a profile (1515) carries the clones, one saved before them leaves them as they are. The clone's right-click: Open, Clone, Delete the clone (asked first), Arrange. Kept per browser (yue2.refinerClones), eight per step at most; the chain does not run clones (it runs the step's own settings). With it, Run up to here shows only in the windows of the chain's own nodes: since 1508 it had stood in every node window (a .btn's display beat its [hidden]), and the profiles' row is painted before the lab answers.

**The canvas's tools above it** (1517). The hint and Arrange left the canvas's bottom-right corner, where they lay over the last row's node (Audio I/O), for the row above the canvas (1515's), on its right; the profiles keep its left.

**The root out of the page's words** (1518; TODO 2.0.0 5а; Viktor's rule: the root «раб/роб» stays out of the studio's words). The Ukrainian and Belarusian catalogs carried the root «роб» about sixty times each (зроблено, робить, Зробити; be зроблены, робіць, Зрабіць), the Greek «δουλ» eight times (δουλεύει, δουλειά): all 127 places said anew by their sense, not by a swap of one word: створити, творити, виконати, діяти, поставити (be стварыць, тварыць, выконваць, дзейнічаць), el λειτουργώ, ενεργώ; «Where your work lives» became «Де живе все твоє» (el «Πού ζει ό,τι φτιάχνεις»), a laptop's desktop «стільниця», «Made from it» in the Artist its actions (Створити з неї, as the Russian «Сделать из неё»), the Belarusian Undo «Скасаваць» as the Ukrainian «Скасувати». The letters of проба, пробіл, дроб, хвороба, Афробіт, Електробас, перабор, храбусткі, драбней, Грабёнка are not the root and stay. **The guard:** check-i18n.mjs (in every live check) reads each catalog the page is served with and fails on any word of the root in uk, be or el, by the guide checker's own rule; check_guide.py learned the same letters that are not the root. Before the pass it failed on the live page, after it passes.

**The Creator's versions** (1519; Viktor's TODO 14.10.2026: «Version control of the Creator (style, lyrics, score kept under the base title on each Generate, a diff of two, restore into the form, labels and stars; its button never ⟲)», and 13.10.2026: «В Писателя нужно автоматом отправлять все версии как в отдельный бакет, чтобы потом, даже после удаления треков, можно было восстановить "потерянное"»). Every Generate's words (the title, style, lyrics and score as the form held them, not the request with the adapters' trigger words added) ride with its runs, an F5 included, and when the takes land the lab keeps them with the takes' names and the mode, one file per base title in KIT/writer-versions/ (writer-versions-SCOPE for a test page), apart from the takes: a take deleted never takes its words along. The same words again only add their takes to the version they made. **Versions**, beside Save to Writer in the Creator, opens the title in the form: the versions newest first, the newest picked, its style, lyrics, score and the takes it made (a deleted one struck through); Ctrl or Shift and a click on a second shows the two as a diff, the older first: the style by words, the lyrics and the score by lines, unchanged stretches folded; Compare with the one before, Compare with the form; a star, a label, a lock (a locked version cannot be deleted); Delete asks and moves the version into its title's trash, from where Bring back returns it to its place; Into the form puts the four words back (asked when the form holds other words), a score in the mode it was sung in. The Writer's bar has «Creator's versions»: every title kept, one picked opens its versions. Its button is a word, never ⟲ (which resets the text's size). lab/creator_versions.py, GET and POST /writer/versions; test_creator_versions.py, 13 checks; the stand-in server answers the same route with the lab's own code, in each of its three copies.

**The harvest's fifteen** (1520; TODO 2.0.0 5: every string in twelve languages). The whole kitchen suite run once more as a harvest (heresy/MISC/claude-i18n/1339/harvest_suite.py on today's suite: an observer records every text and translated attribute the page shows) saw 12 062 strings; less those that are a value of some catalog (the suite switches languages, so it sees translations too) and those not in Latin letters, 1 031 were left, most of them the mock's data, take names and strings the page composes from parts each translated on its own. Fifteen were true misses and have their keys now in all eleven languages: the font size steps' Smaller and Larger, the Trainer's two «?» buttons (How to choose, How they work), the find bar's «Find in the style» and «Find in the notes» and its Close tip, the presets' «Delete the saved profile», «built in», the heading font's «Same as the text», the tree's «Find it in the tree below», the player's back-15 s tip, the Artist's regions tip and two toasts (VAE for the songs to come: {0}; The score written in {0} (it came as {1})…). Three of them had shown half translated, a broader pattern taking hold of an English tip with no key («Up to three parts de the frame», «Volver a the last quarter-minute mark», «Seleccionadas: Close (Esc)…»): an exact key comes before any pattern. Left for a pass of its own: the info buttons that name a raw label or an element's id («About «assistBriefLib»»), and a few dialog texts that come from the lab. The 1519 checks take a title of their own each run (the mock's notebook folder outlives a run, and a second run had found the first's versions under the same title).

**The held-out's best is the run's LoRA** (1521; found 17.10.2026 in the night's voice runs). The early stop of 1497 measured each save on the held-out tracks, named the best in val.json and stopped patience saves after it, but the run's LoRA was still its last checkpoint: Korneva got e015 for her best e012, Bulekova e015 for e012, Chiffa e013 for e010, Nizhegorodova e017 for e014, the alto e020 for e017. Those five best epochs were published that night through the lab's own /train/publish (hard links, nothing removed, the last ones stay beside them). From now on a run that ends with a held-out best publishes that checkpoint too and it is the run's LoRA, the last one kept beside it as lora_last (training.publish_best). The stop also says what it measured: «no held-out flow under 0.618147 (the best 0.620006 less 0.3 %) for 3 saves», where it had said «no held-out flow lower than 0.620006» while 0.61950 and 0.61906 stood in the log above it: they were lower, only not by the 0.3 % min_delta. lab/test_publish_best.py, 5 checks.

**The Creator's versions in Export all** (1522; Viktor 17.10.2026: «бакет версий Творца включай в Export All как глобальный бекап/экспорт»). The Writer's backup file is version 2: beside the notebook it carries every title's book of the Creator's versions with its trash (creator_versions.export_all, called by writer.py's export). Import brings them back by title: a title missing here comes in whole, one here gains the versions it lacks (the same words at the same moment are the same version) under numbers after its own; nothing here is written over or taken away; a backup from before the versions imports as before. The two buttons' tips say so, the export's toast counts the titles, the import's says the versions and titles it brought. 1519's Chinese names the Creator 创作室, as the catalog does. test_creator_versions.py, 19 checks.

**The Engine's Refiner Room** (1523; Viktor 17.10.2026: «Холст не делай дефолтным пока что, но в настройках Engine предусмотри эту опцию, наверно даже отдельной вкладкой Refiner Room»). A tab of its own between Writer and Artwork and Folders and Security: how the Refiner opens, three cards to choose from as the Creator's modes are chosen: As it was left (the default, as before), Always the list, Always the canvas. Kept under yue2.refinerOpens (so in the studio's settings file too); a choice moves the room at once. The Engine's tab check counts eight tabs now.

**An (i) names what it is about** (1524; Viktor 17.10.2026: «Инфокнопки… можешь править»). help.js named a control with no label text by its element id (a reader of the screen heard «About assistBriefLib»); now by its own aria-label, then its placeholder, the id only last. «About models and LoRAs» and «About folders», static in the page, have their keys in all eleven languages (they had come out as «О «models and LoRAs»»).

**The Refiner's list without dropdowns** (1526; Viktor 17.10.2026: «Покажи мне стиль без дропдаунов. Пока что стандартные дропдауны у Огранщика. А... увидел в окнах нод. Супер, так отлично»; 16.10: «Всю Студию оформляем»). HeresyChoices.scope(room): the class hc-on on a room shows every choice box in it as the node windows do and hides its selects; a select added to it later (a plugin's panel, a list drawn anew) is enhanced as it comes. The rooms in this style are named in one list in heresy-menu.js (ROOMS), the Refiner's list view first: the chain's strip is chips now (Debuzz 50–100 %, the upscale's and the stems' modes), the remaster's preset a column, its colour a row. A field with its choices in sight stands at the top of its row (a tall column beside it had pushed the Source down), in the list and in the node windows; an empty list shows «—».

**A branch into its take's trash** (1527; Viktor 17.10.2026: «У Огранщика также добавь удаление ненужного артефакта, чтобы не плодить мусорку в пайплайне»). Every branch of «Made from This Take» has Delete at the end of its head: asked, it goes into the take's own derived/.trash/ as it was (lab/derived_trash.py). A branch other branches were made from is refused alone: the lab names them (409) and the page asks again, naming them, and they go together, each coming back on its own. Under the tree «Trash (N)» opens the trashed branches with Bring back and with which they went; Empty the trash is asked as for good, and is the only thing that deletes. Nothing moves while a job runs on the take. The head holds its button (flow-root), so the waves keep one x (1513's check had caught a float hanging into the row below). test_derived_trash.py, 11 checks.

**The guide's canvas** (1528; Viktor 17.10.2026: «Видел канвас, просто супер»). The Refiner's tab of the guide (F1) has a stop of its own before the DAW in all twelve languages, «The Canvas: Nodes and Wires»: the canvas and the list, a node's window and its keys, the right-click (Open, Run up to here, Bypass in the chain, Clone, Arrange), a wire setting a step's Source, clones, profiles, every choice in sight, and how the room opens set in Engine → Refiner Room; the tree's paragraph says a branch's Delete and the take's trash. Each language says it in its page's own words for the buttons, with the same lines and the same bold pairs as the English, so check_guide.py says of every translation exactly what it said before (Japanese numerals written as words for that).

**The DAW's console** (1531; Viktor 18.10.2026: «До утра доведи по максимуму DAW, пожалуйста. Преврати твой DAW начаток в профессиональный миксер как ты умеешь. У тебя есть на что смотреть»; 17.10: «Что там по DAW? Это пока что как бы mockup, мало пользы пока что от такого DAW»). Under the lanes a mixing desk (heresy-console.js): every lane a strip, its inserts (the EQ with its curve in small, the compressor with its reduction as it plays), the sends to the room and the delay after the pan, width and pan, polarity, mute and solo, the fader with its meter on the same scale (peak and RMS of both sides, the peak held, the loudest since cleared); the room and the delay as returns with their own level and mute; the master pinned at the right with its loudness by BS.1770 as it plays (momentary, short-term, integrated since play), a 4× true peak and the sides' correlation. A strip's devices: the input (trim, polarity, its version as chips), an EQ of up to eight RBJ bands drawn over the strip's own spectrum (a double-click adds a band, the point dragged, the wheel its width, a right-click away), the compressor (threshold, ratio, attack, release, knee, make-up, five starting points, its transfer curve and its reduction), the output; the room's three spaces, the delay's time in ms or as a note at a tempo, its feedback and low-pass; the master's spectrum. Knobs and faders: drag (Shift finer), wheel, arrows, a double-click back; Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Shift+Z over the whole mix; A and B to compare two mixes as it plays; +stems prints every strip and return beside the mix. **What is heard is what is mixed.** The page's graph runs the render's order (lab/daw_mix.py, the value ruach.mix/1): the silence drawn, trim and polarity, the EQ (the browser's biquads take the render's RBJ coefficients; the cuts their Q in dB), the compressor, the fader, width by mid and side, the browser's stereo panner (its law written out in the render), mute and solo, the sends after the pan; the room convolves with the very impulse the render uses (GET /lab/daw/ir), the delay is Plenio's loop with its one-pole low-pass. The compressor and the meters are one kernel (heresy-mixdsp.js) run in an AudioWorklet built from that same function where the page is a secure context, and in a ScriptProcessor where it is not (the studio opened by its network address): measured against Plenio on a signal pressed 10 dB, 3.8e-8 apart (float32), the integrated loudness and the true peak the same to 0.001. One difference, said in the guide: after a jump the compressor's memory starts afresh. The lab half: daw_mix.py (Plenio's EQ, compressor, room and delay; unknown fields refused before the job is queued), daw_job.py takes both values, test_daw_mix.py 29 checks (each stage against Plenio and the Web Audio law, the printed stems summing to the mix, daw_job end to end, the old value as before). A mix kept before 1531 keeps its one compression knob as a compressor of the same threshold and ratio. The guide's DAW stop in twelve languages; the console's words in eleven catalogs; the suite's section 1531 (both engines heard on a tone, the knobs, the fader, undo, A and B, the EQ and the compressor, Mix with its stems); 1471's check follows the new value.

**The song's sections on the DAW's ruler** (1532). Under the ruler's time, the sections as the player's wave has them (the lyrics' own tags placed by Whisper's word times, HERESY 1315; window.ruachWaveSections), a block each coloured by its kind (chorus amber, verse teal, bridge violet, intro and outro grey, instrumental blue) with its name, a thin line through every lane at each start; a click on a block goes to its start, a double-click loops it to the next, [ and ] the section before and after. The ruler grows to 36 px when there are sections.

**The DAW's mixes on Forge** (1533). Beside the set of stems they mix (derived/<set>/mixes.json, lab/daw_mixes.py): the working mix, sent 1.5 s after the hand stops and taken back on opening when it is newer than the browser's own (so the DAW opens as it was left on another machine or in another browser), and mixes kept by name in the console's third tab, Mixes: Save as, a click to call one back (Undo behind it), ✕ to delete one, asked. A file that cannot be read is said broken and set aside as it was before the next write, never taken for «nothing kept»; where Forge did not keep a mix, the tab says why. The mixes are asked after the lanes are painted, and a fetch that throws at once counts as a refusal (the older checks' mocks had answered every address starting /lab/daw/mix: now exactly /lab/daw/mix). test_daw_mixes.py 13 checks.

**The glow's ring off inside a node's window** (1534; Viktor 17.10.2026, his picture of Audio I/O: «вот та красноватая рамочка - это ненужная обводка. Данные и так хороши в общем канвасе оверлей окна. УБери её отовсюду в таких паттернах»). The look's glow (html[data-glow="on"]) rings every .card, .drawer, .panel and .meta-card; inside a node's window the step's panel kept it, 1508's rule being the weaker. Off there for every step, and off the DAW over the whole window. The Engine's cards keep theirs (asked).

**A lane's volume drawn over time** (1535). «A» on a lane takes its automation in hand: a click on the wave puts a point there and drags it (between its neighbours), a right-click takes one away, a double-click sets it to 0 dB, Undo over all of it; the line drawn over the wave on the fader's taper (its 0 dB where the fader's is), faint when not in hand. Heard after the fader as the gain's exponential ramps between the points, which is the render's curve: linear in dB (lab/daw_mix.py, ruach.mix/1's strip auto, [[t, dB], …]; the first point's level before them, the last's after). Measured on a tone: the line at −20 dB takes −12 dBFS to −32. test_daw_mix.py 32 checks. The guide's DAW stop says all four in twelve languages; a row of keys ([ ] Ctrl+Z Ctrl+Shift+Z).

**The master's monitor, and the night's mends** (1536). Mono and Dim under the master, for the ear only: after the meters, the loudness and the spectrum, never in the mix's value (the sides summed by four gains as a lane's width; −20 dB). The script processor's banks at 4096 frames (86 ms, every strip alike, the playhead takes it off): on the page's own thread a redraw of a few tens of milliseconds no longer breaks the sound when the studio is opened by its network address. A lane's «A» lit only while its line is in hand (the lane's buttons were lit by s[key], and an empty list is true). The spectra behind the EQ and on the master on a floating scale, 72 dB under their own top falling slowly (the browser's analyser gives its bins some −90 to −120 dB for a voice at −14 dBFS). Two latent faults the night's checks brought up: the Mix watcher (since 1471) read the name of no take when the take changed while a mix was on Forge, now it stops; and HeresySpectrum asked app.js for the takes other than none (on heresy-derived with its panel open), now none is no take. The suite's section 1536 opens the console's own tab first (the console keeps its tab between sets).

**The compressor's key** (1537): a sidechain, the band ducked under the voice. A strip's compressor may listen to another strip as that one enters its own compressor (after its silence, trim and EQ); a muted key still keys. The page's kernel bank holds every strip's block, so its detector reads the key's (a silent key is silence, not the strip itself); the render's compress_keyed (lab/daw_mix.py) is Plenio's compressor with its detector fed by the key, Plenio's own sample for sample on its own strip, the page's kernel 1.5e-8 from it at 12 dB of reduction (MISC/claude-tc/dsp_key_check.py). In the compressor's device: «Listens to» (own, or another strip) and the starting point «Duck» (−28 dB, 2.5:1, 15/250 ms, keyed to the lead vocal). ruach.mix/1 comp.key; refused: a strip keyed by itself or by one not of the set. The catalogs' «Key» is the song's key (Тональность, Tonart), so the label is «Listens to». test_daw_mix.py 37 checks; suite 1537 hears the silent drums pressed by the voice's tone in both engines and not by themselves when own.

**No dropdown on a DAW lane, and the goniometer** (1538; Viktor 16.10.2026: «…избавляемся от этих голимых html'евских дропдаунов»). A stem's version on its lane is a button showing which (1/2, 2/2…): a click steps to the next, so two versions are compared as the song plays; a right-click opens the strip's devices, its versions there as chips; the select stays hidden under it, read and written as before (the 1471 check sets it). The master's devices draw a goniometer from the master's two sides (mid up, side across, a phosphor trail).

**The DAW's Stop** (1539; Viktor 18.10.2026: «Кнопка MIX запускает сведение, но нужна и кнопка STOP»). While a mix runs on Forge a **Stop** stands beside **Mix**: the lab's `POST /daw/mix/stop` takes a queued mix off at once, or ends the running one's process, takes its `.part` away and says it; no branch is made. The page's watch also ends when the mix is gone from Forge without a branch (a lab restarted under it), and says so.

**«Made from This Take» its own place** (1540; Viktor 18.10.2026: «Блок со всеми артефактами Made from This Take висит в хвосте каждой вкладки и докучает… куда вынести… чтобы он не надоедал глазу, но и был доступен всегда»). The tree is no longer under every step: it is an entry of its own at the end of the Refiner's sticky column of steps (under «This Take»), its mark the number of branches and a light while something is made; a click opens it where a step stands, lighting the branches of the step one came from. Not a step of the chain: the canvas, where it is a node of its own, and the steps' numbers stay as they were.

**Two-word buttons on one line** (1541; Viktor 18.10.2026: «Некоторые кнопки с двумя словами переносятся на две строки»). `.btn` keeps its words together (`white-space: nowrap`); measured in every room at 1920 and 2560 px in en, ru and de: no button wraps, none overflows.

**A cut's fades** (1542; on «фейды на краях вырезок»: «ТВОРИ»). A silenced stretch carries its own fall and rise: its top corners are handles that drag them (a double-click gives back 10 ms), its sides move it. The render's `cut_envelope` (lab/daw_mix.py) is Plenio's `mute_envelope` sample for sample at 10 ms; the page schedules the same raised cosines on the clock, picking a fall up mid-way where play starts, and draws the stretch as it sounds (dark as the gain is low, the gain's line). The value: `[start, end]` while both are 10 ms, else `[start, end, fall, rise]`.

**Groups as VCAs** (1543; on «группы/VCA (например, вся группа одним фейдером)»). `groups` in ruach.mix/1: a VCA holds no sound; its fader adds its dB to each member's own (theirs stay where they are), its M mutes them, its S solos them; a strip may be in more than one. In the console: «+ VCA» (the strip picked its first member), a VCA strip after the strips, its devices (name, members as chips, Delete). Heard and rendered alike; kept, undone and A/B'd with the rest.

**The master's chain, live as the render** (1544; on «лимитер и цепочка на мастере (живьём совпадающая с рендером)»). The master has EQ, compressor and Plenio's limiter before its fader, in the render (`master.eq`, `master.comp`, `master.limit`) and on the page. The limiter is Plenio's `limit` streamed in the page's kernel (resample_poly's Kaiser FIR 4× up and down, the demand's lookahead maximum and its mean, the release linear in dB): against Plenio 5·10⁻¹³ at 48 and 44.1 kHz (MISC/claude-tc/lim_vs_plenio.py), the whole master node (compressor, then limiter) within one float32 step of the render (master_vs_plenio.py). Its node is in the path only while its compressor or limiter is on (on the script processor's road, Viktor's, a node is another 85 ms), its delay counted in the playhead's. With a chain on, the printed strips are what enters the master, and the report says so.

**A take's mix stays its own** (1545; found by the 1543–1544 checks run after the earlier ones). On a change of take, or of set, the DAW kept the mix in hand under the NEW take's key before reading that take's own, so the previous take's mix became the new one's, in this browser and on Forge as its working mix (since 1531/1533). The mix in hand now knows whose it is (the key it was read for) and is kept only under that key, before the take or the set changes. Forge's two kept mixes were looked at: both as split, nothing carried.

**An input's floor after it settles** (1546; found measuring Viktor's new M-Audio M-Track Plus 18.10.2026). «Measure the input's floor» said −47.6 dBFS where the settled floor is −95: the M-Track's converter, after a change of rate, opens with a thump of −35 dBFS dying over 0.4–1.5 s, and the probe read its first two seconds. The probe now records 1.5 s more and reads what follows; the loop test lets the input settle as long before the tone goes out.

**The Digital Gateway** (1547; Viktor 17.10.2026: «Вот цифровой шлюз в паблик, и обогати его по максимуму. Аналоговый — это приватный Pro»). A step of its own in Finish, between the DAW and the Remaster: the take or any branch through an analog character in numbers (lab/digital_gate.py, numpy and scipy only): tape at 30, 15, 7.5 or 3.75 ips (record emphasis into a soft asymmetric curve and back, the head bump and the top by the speed, hiss, wow and flutter as a wandering read position, azimuth as an exact fractional delay), console (odd harmonics, the other side's highs leaking in), tube (even harmonics, a slow sag), transformer (the lows saturate first) or vinyl (the lows folded to mono zero-phase, the top rolled off, surface noise, crackle, rumble, wow). The curve runs 2, 4 or 8 times oversampled in blocks (exact to the whole-file way: a long song costs no more memory), the stateful parts at the file's own rate; width, a parallel mix, the level as it came (BS.1770) or as it comes out, a trim, the true peak held under −0.3 dBTP and said. Each branch says what its curve adds to a −12 dBFS tone (2nd and 3rd harmonic, THD). Eight starting points; clones on the canvas with settings of their own. test_digital_gate.py 22 checks (as loud as it came per character, wow measured as asked, tube even over odd and console odd over even, vinyl's lows in mono, crosstalk and azimuth as set, one seed one sound, the blocks' seams exact).

**The monitor** (1548; Viktor 18.10.2026: «допиши в Audio I/O возможность подключения второго устройства как Output/Monitor девайса… И не отключай нативный браузерный выход аудио»). Audio I/O keeps a second output on Forge beside the gateway (a tone through it, Stop). With one kept, each row of the tree has «On Forge»: the file plays through it from where its player stands, again to stop (POST /lab/audio/monitor/play, streamed from that second). The browser's own output is untouched.

**Progress above the player, in every room** (1549; Viktor 18.10.2026: «Порогресс бары повсюду прикрути… Сделай как в Художнике… снизу прямо над плеером отцентрованно, примерно 800px»). Whatever the lab runs has a bar each above the player, centred, at most 800 px: a training run and the listener by their steps, a job of the Refiner by its time against what it is expected to take (the take's length times the step's measured pace, or the job's own), counted in the lab from when it was first seen running, so neither the queue nor the page's clock bends it; a job that says nothing of its length striped, its time running. The Artist's own runs keep their bar in its room.

**Before and after at equal loudness** (1550; Viktor 18.10.2026, the mix against the analog gateway's: «нужно сравнить ДО и ПОСЛЕ»). The compare window shows each version's integrated loudness (GET /lab/loudness, Plenio's BS.1770, kept per file) and, with «Equal loudness» on (the default, remembered), turns the louder ones down to the quietest: a version a decibel louder sounds better by that alone.

**The chain as a pipeline** (1553; Viktor 18.10.2026: «различные эпизоды сразу в граф нод предусмотри. И сотвори несколько профилей пайплайна, как мы обсуждали и как ты видишь для Про и Паблик»). The chain's row has a Profile: the classic chain as it was, or a pipeline, a list of steps of any kind in their own order. POST /lab/chain with `pipeline` takes debuzz, upscale, stems (split, or `set`: a set the take has), daw (`mix`: a ruach.mix/1 value, or the DAW «as set now», neutral when it holds none), gate, remaster, plugin (a built-in step or a plugin's, on a file, on the set, or on one `stem` of it), inspect, spectrum and gloss; `until` is an index. Each step works on what the step before made: a file, or the set with the versions its steps made. The remaster on a set mixes those versions in place of the stems as split; a step on a set is told them (`_versions`) and each stem's way through the chain (`_history`). `lab/chain_plan.pipeline` checks a pipeline before it runs: a DAW or a step on a set with no stems before it, a step that is not here, one stem for a step that takes the set. The studio ships seven (`lab/refiner_pipelines.json`): Quick polish, Song: stems and remaster, Song: my DAW mix, Song: full air, Warm tape, Spoken word and Look only; a plugin may bring its own (`pipelines` in its plugin.json), listed only while every step they name is here (GET /lab/refiner/profiles, `pipelines`). On the page a pipeline's steps are chips, one unchecked bypassed (kept per pipeline in the browser); on the canvas its way is drawn node to node, and Run up to here and Bypass in the chain work on it. A version of a version is a version: `lab/daw.py` follows a stem's branches through each other, and a branch's file may name its stem by `source`. On the sandbox lab, the 21-second waltz's eight stems went stems → Whole lead → DAW → Digital Gateway (tape, 30 ips) → Remaster → Spectrum in 20 s, the DAW mixing the whole lead and the backing less it. `lab/test_pipelines.py`: 40 checks.

**The studio's own steps** (1553). Steps shipped with the lab (`lab/builtin_steps/ruach/plugin.json`) run as a plugin's do (a panel, a node, a branch in the tree, the GPU queue, the chain), but speak the page's language. A step says its `group` and the step it stands `after`; its `rate` (its pace, for the progress bars) is kept now, where plugins.py dropped it; a file a step makes may name the stem it is a version of (`source`, inside the take only), and a picture it makes is shown as one (`kind: image`). A user's plugin with a built-in step's id is left out, said.

**Whole lead** (1554; Viktor 15.10.2026 on «Крик», 2:08–2:22: «вокал в яме»). On a set of eight stems, where the backing stem is louder than the lead by 6 dB, louder than −40 dBFS and centred (its middle 9 dB over its sides), the stretch is the lead's: stretches under a second apart are joined, those under a second left, and the backing there is moved into the lead with 50 ms ramps. Lead and backing together are what they were, sample for sample; both come back as versions of their stems. Measured on «Крик»'s eight stems: six stretches, 21 s in all, among them 2:08–2:14 and 2:16–2:22; over 2:12–2:20 the lead went from −52.9 to −29.0 dBFS and the backing from −29.2 to −67.5; the sum differs from before by 1.2·10⁻⁷. On the waltz the split had lost the lead for most of the song (lead −81 to −113 dBFS while the backing held a centred voice at −20): 19 of its 21 s moved.

**Voice upscale** (1555; the Refiner's open order of 15.10.2026, «Vocal upscale per stem»). UniverSR (the studio's own upscale_job.py, one variant, the original kept below the cutoff) on the voice stems only, the lead and backing or the lead alone, then back at the set's rate and length, so each is a version of its stem; the measure says each voice's level at 12–20 kHz before and after. Its first run on a card is still to come: on 18.10 both cards of the lab's jobs were training voices.

**Before and after** (1557; Viktor 18.10.2026 among the ideas he approved, «отчёт до/после на каждую ветку»; and after his first print through the lamp: «Нужны твои замеры и сравнение спектрограмм, в доказательство»). A studio's own step (`builtin_steps/ruach/report.py`) and a button on every file of the tree: the file against what it was made from (its branch's source; a set of stems, or a mix or remaster of one, goes back to the file the set was split from; the take when nothing else). Both measured the same way: integrated loudness, true peak and loudness range by Plenio's BS.1770, crest, width (side over mid), correlation, the share of four bands; the average spectra (Welch) in sixth-octave bands at equal loudness and their difference; the short-term loudness (3 s windows) through the song. A picture (three panels) and a page of numbers land in the tree; a step's picture is shown there now (`kind: image`). On «Крик» v4.2.0 against the tube of the Digital Gateway: the same −14.62 LUFS, true peak +0.30 → −4.61 dBTP, range 11.39 → 9.47 LU, crest 17.8 → 13.1 dB, lows below 40 Hz +1.2 dB and 89–178 Hz −1.2 dB at equal loudness, the quiet stretches 1–2 LU up: in 21 s.

**Match to a reference** (1558; among the ideas he approved: «reference match»). A studio's own step (`builtin_steps/ruach/match.py`) on Plenio's tone matching (`plenio_core/audio/eq.py`: each spectrum smoothed to a sixth of an octave with a silence gate, the difference weighted by how sure each band is, peak bands added one at a time where the most is left and refined together, each kept only if it helps and stays within the bound). The reference is a step's new kind of field, `file`: a file of this take's tree or another take (`take:NAME`), checked by the lab before the queue and given to the step as its path. Amount, the bound in dB and the number of bands are set; the bands are the DAW's own (peak, frequency, gain, Q), the file is the same EQ rendered, as loud as it came or as the reference, never over −1 dBTP; the branch's **Onto the DAW's master** puts them on the master's EQ of the set in hand, one step of the DAW's undo. On «Крик» v4.2.0 toward the SUNO take of «Крик» at 50 % and ±3 dB: six bands, 50 Hz +3.0, 356 Hz −2.6, 568 Hz +2.0, 732 Hz −1.4, 5.7 kHz −3.0, 9.8 kHz +2.7; the weighted difference 1.49 dB before, 0.81 dB after. `lab/test_builtin_steps.py`: 15 checks.

**Automation beyond the volume** (1559; Viktor 18.10.2026, the DAW to production: «автоматизация не только громкости»). A lane's «A» line draws its volume, pan, width, room send or delay send; the chip beside «A» says which (Vol, L–R, Wid, Rm, Dly), a click goes to the next. Points are put, dragged, taken away and given the knob's rest (0 dB, the centre, as it is, none) as the volume's were. A knob with a line plays it in place of its own value: the StereoPanner's pan, the width's four gains and the sends ramped straight between the points (a-rate params), a line taken away gives the knob its value back. In the render (`daw_mix.py`): `autos` {pan, width, reverb, delay}: [[t, value], …], the pan law per sample as the panner's, the width's mid and side per sample, a send per sample; each knob drawn takes its knob's place. `lab/test_daw_autos.py`: 10 checks.

**Inserts and Print** (1561; «свободные инсерты, Цифровой шлюз как инсерт»). A strip's Input has Insert: its version as it plays, through the Digital Gateway (its five characters) or a plugin's step on a file, on Forge; what comes back is the strip's new version (a version of a version, 1553), set at once and heard as it will be rendered; the versions keep the way back. The master's Print sends the mix the DAW holds (its value, its versions) through the gateway as a pipeline (the set as it is, the DAW, the gateway): a branch beside the DAW's own mixes. A pipeline's DAW may now name the versions it mixes.

**The chain on many takes** (1562; among the ideas he approved: «пакетный Огранщик по пространству»). POST /lab/chain/batch {names, …a chain}: the chain checked once, then run on each take one after another (one batch at a time); GET /lab/chain/batch says each take's state; POST /lab/chain/batch/stop lets the take in hand finish and skips the rest. On the page: «On the workspace…» beside Run the chain (the workspace in hand, or the whole library, asked first), the Librarian's «Refine N with the chain» on checked takes, the batch's line under the chain's row. On the sandbox lab two short takes went through Spectrum and Artifacts one after the other.

**Audio buses** (1563; Viktor 18.10.2026, the DAW to production: «аудиошины»). `buses` in ruach.mix/1 (at most eight): a bus sums its members' strips after their pan (their sends still leave from the strips) and runs the sum through a strip's own chain, EQ, Plenio's compressor (no key), fader, width and pan, into the master; muted, its members are not heard; a strip goes to one bus. In the browser each bus is a lane of the kernel after the strips (its compressor the same cells as a strip's, its meter after the master's), the members' strips wired into it; a change of members builds the sound anew and plays on from where it was, and undo takes it back. On the console a bus is a strip after the VCAs («+ Bus»): EQ and Comp, width and pan, M, fader and meter; its devices choose its members, its name, its EQ, compressor and output. Heard on the mock: a −12 dBFS tone through a bus at −6 dB reads −18 on the bus and the master, −12 on its lane before it; muted, the master hears nothing of it. `lab/test_daw_buses.py`: 10 checks.

**Moving and copying along a lane** (1564; Viktor 18.10.2026, the DAW to production: «правка регионов — перенос, вырезка, копия»). `edits` of a strip in ruach.mix/1 (at most 64): `{op: copy|move, a, b, to}`, the stretch a…b of the stem as it came laid at «to», what was there replaced with 10 ms raised-cosine crossfades at both edges, a move's source silenced as a cut of its own edges; laid after the strip's cuts, so a paste lies over them; one past the end is cut at the end. In the browser: Shift and a drag picks a stretch (a plain drag still silences), Ctrl+C copies, Ctrl+X takes it to move (once: the next Ctrl+V lays a copy), Ctrl+V lays it at the playhead on its own lane, Delete silences the stretch in hand, Esc lets it go, a right-click on a laid stretch takes it away, undo takes it all back. Heard as rendered: the lane's own sound silenced under each laid stretch and a move's source (the cut envelope), each laid stretch fetched from its source and played over through its own crossfade; drawn with the wave it brings. `lab/test_daw_edits.py`: 9 checks.

**A release with nothing of a private plugin, and every picture read** (1565; Viktor 18.10.2026, before 2.0.0: «Пропиши сторожей»). `heresy/tools/check-no-pro.sh`, called by make-release after the private lock, stops a release on what a word cannot catch: a built-in step other than ruach in `lab/builtin_steps/`, a pipeline calling a plugin other than ruach, anything in `user/` or in a `plugins/` folder (a user's own plugin lives linked in `user/plugins/`), and a picture not read. The private lock reads text, never pictures, and on 16.10 the guide's shots carried a voice run's and the readers' names since rc1. `heresy/tools/read-pictures.sh` reads each picture with tesseract (English, Russian, Ukrainian, at twice its size) for the private terms and for the words lawful in text but never on a screenshot (`heresy/MISC/picture-terms.txt`: the readers' names, the author's, the machine's address); a clean one goes into `heresy/MISC/pictures-read.txt` by its sha256, and a release lets out only those bytes; a picture that could not be read is not a clean one (its empty text would pass every word). Forge has no tesseract: there the release stops and lists the pictures to read where it is. Its first pass over the release's 70 pictures found the author's name on the Librarian's shot and on its tile of the visiting card. The C2PA test named a private plugin's step as a sample label, which the private lock would have stopped; it names a neutral step now. And the 2.0.0 shots: ten scenes, by day and by night at 1920×1080, from the live library and read only (`heresy/MISC/creator/tools/claude-2000-shots.mjs`): every write held in the page, the lab's running jobs, a private plugin's steps, pipelines, branches and versions, a private voice's workspace and its takes and a private painter's adapters and runs kept out of every list; the readers' voices named `voice-reader-N` in the data and again in each frame; the takes of «Крик» one shared cover for the pictures (Qwen-Image 2512, JibMix, seed 1919). The guide in its twelve languages shows the canvas and the DAW at their sections' heads, the README's rooms table both, the visiting card's tiles the new shots.

**The install's own checks without the sliders** (1566; found cutting 2.0.0-pre). The sliders went in 1449 and fetch-heresy and start.sh dropped them, but three of the install's checks still asked: `tools/test-real.sh` stopped at once («sliders/ is not converted») and gave its server `--sliders`, `tools/verify-install.sh` counted a missing catalog as a failure, `convert-models.sh` printed it in red. Now none asks; the old copies in `heresy/MISC/claude-tc/pre/before-1566/`. Past that stop the real-server test meets what it predates (the sliders' chips it counts, Generate with no Librarian behind a dead lab port, which since 1473 sends nothing): INSTALL.md and the agents' install guide say it is out of date in the pre and renewed for 2.0.0; the page suite's expected count there is 987.

**2.0.0-pre cut, tagged v2.0.0-rc9** (18.10.2026, Viktor: «Разрешаю срез RC9 и пуш по всем репо»; the tag his choice that evening, so a studio on an rc offers it in Engine → Updates; «-pre» alone it would never see, and semver puts it below rc8): live checks 987/0, 19/0, 3/0; the release tree passed make-release's three locks (the machine's shapes, the private names, 1565's steps, user/ and every picture read) and a look of my own; cutting it found 1566, then the tree built again and from a clean clone the engine built in 142 s, the page 987/0, the player 3/0, Pinokio 74/0, the places 26/0. Ruach_Studio.Release e2c9658, tag v2.0.0-rc9, the pre-release «Ruach Studio 2.0.0-pre» https://github.com/igrbible/Ruach_Studio/releases/tag/v2.0.0-rc9 with its tarball (SHA-256 c39d1c8c…), the visiting card on 2.0.0-pre in twelve languages with the new shots.

**The install's own test alive again, an older version's weights named, the card by night** (1567; Viktor 19.10.2026: «готовь 2.0.0», «На сайте визитке ночные темы не прикручены»). `tools/test-real.sh` and `tools/cdp-real.mjs` predated two changes: the sliders' chips (1449: counted 16, clicked Metal, read it on the song page) and a Librarian behind Generate (1473: a new take lands in a workspace, and with the lab's port dead the page sends nothing). The checks of the sliders are gone (one says there are none), and the test's lab port now answers 404 to everything: a studio with no Librarian, where the page lets a take land in no workspace, as it does for a studio without one. Run on Forge from its own tree: 6 passed, 0 failed (its page check 13 of 13), 53 s, Q5_K_M on the CPU. The install guides say so again. `launcher/update.sh` ends by naming the weights an older version left that nothing reads now (SDXL since 1405, Krea 2 since 1428, the sliders since 1449) with their size and the line that frees them; it removes nothing. The visiting card's room tiles carry their night shots as the Trainer's did (1505): by night the Creator, the Writer, the Refiner, the Artist and the Librarian show the night theme too; the Creator's tile no longer names the sliders, in all twelve languages.

**No monitor on the gateway's own card** (1568; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Monitor Out листит активный девайс в Шлюзе. Разве возможно без глюков на тот же девайс посылать монитор?»). It is not: an ALSA card opens once, and a monitor on the card the gateway plays out of or records from would take it from the gateway, or be refused. Audio I/O now offers the monitor every output but those on the gateway's card, as the gateway's selects stand (move the gateway, and the monitor's choices follow); a monitor kept there before is said in red and offered none. The lab refuses it too (`audio_io.set_monitor`; `lab/test_audio_io.py` +2). The 1548 check had written the bug down («every output as the monitor»): it expects the other card now.

**The canvas laid out as the work flows, on a grid; the chain's row and the jobs' strip fold** (1569; Viktor 19.10.2026, with two pictures of the canvas: «Канвас с нодами внахлёст. Arrage их тулит одну к одной, тогда как есть место на холсте», «Для нод на канвасе прикрути snapping к решётке канваса, - 10 pt шаг решётки. Настроечные/входные ноды влево вниз на канвасе. Типы нод перепроверь по раскраске», and of the list: «Добавь возможность схлопывания блока цепочки»). **Arrange** (`cvLayout`) puts the nodes in six columns, the steps' order left to right: the take and the chain; Debuzz and Upscale; Stems and the steps that mend; the DAW and the steps that work on stems or on one stem; the steps that finish the file; the looks at it. A row is as tall as the tallest node and a gap, measured once the nodes are laid and laid again when that moved, so none stands over another, and the columns take the canvas's whole width; Import a Track and Audio I/O, what feeds the way, stand at the left's bottom; a canvas narrower than six columns takes them in two bands of three and grows as tall as they need. Every place is on a 10 px grid, a drag's too, and the canvas's dots are drawn at that step. The colours checked against the groups: the take amber, Mend gold, Finish green, Look blue, the import grey, and Audio I/O, whose group «setup» had no colour of its own (a bare line), grey as the import is; a plugin's step that names no group stands with Finish, and its `group` in plugin.json (now in PLUGINS.md) puts it in its own column and colour. In the list, ▾ beside «Chain» folds the chain's row to one line naming its steps (`yue2.chainFold`), and the jobs' strip folds to its first job and «and N more» (`yue2.jobStripFold`).

**The chain's wires, a way of one's own** (1570; Viktor 19.10.2026: «В wiring не возможно разорвать цепь для построения собственного воркфлоу», and «Две ноды источника: Take и Upload - их тоже нужно разводить проводами»). The wires of the chain are its way, and now the hand's. A click on one (a wide unseen path over it) cuts the way there, the steps after it off. A wire dragged from a node's dot onto another puts that step right after it (from the take's dot, first; a step already on the way moves with its own settings), and the node takes the first one's newest file as its source as before (1510); every step can be wired to now, not only those with a Source to choose. *Off the way* takes one step out and joins the steps around it. The way so edited, from the classic chain or a pipeline, is «My way» in the chain's Profile: kept in the browser (`yue2.chainPipeOwn`) and in a profile saved by name (1515), which sets it again. Ctrl+Z on the canvas (no window open, no field in hand) gives back the way before the last cut, wire or step taken off. A way has two sources, the take or Import a Track; one wired from the import runs by itself on each track imported next, once the track is in hand (the page's `heresy-imported` event).
