**The way tested as it flows, and a target** (1571; Viktor 19.10.2026: «К канвасу добавь ещё визуальные тесты цепочки. Идея - правый клик на ноде, команда "тест цепочки". Визуальная прорисовка потока. Такого в Комфи нет. А у нас будет. ЛОЛ Также пометка "таргетной ноды"»). *Test the way up to here* walks the way to the node step by step, drawn as it flows along the wires, and checks each step against what comes to it: a set of stems for the DAW and a step that takes stems, the very stem a step mends in the set it gets (four, six, eight or vocals: `lead_vocal` is only in eight), the gateway's interfaces in Audio I/O for a step through gear. The steps that hold turn green; the first that does not turns red with its reason («it takes the stem lead_vocal: put Stems before it»), and the test stops there. Nothing runs. *Make it the target* puts ◎ on a node: Run the chain then runs up to it and no further, and a test goes to it; *No target* takes it off. The target is kept in the browser and in a saved profile.

**A plugin's step names its stem and its gear** (1572; the same day's audit, Viktor 19.10.2026: «Гоняй DAW, шлюзы и всё остальное в Огранщике. Нужно вычленить все скрытые баги и непоследовательности»). The test of 1571 had nothing to read a step's needs from. Two fields of a step in plugin.json (`lab/plugins.py`, PLUGINS.md): `stem`, the one stem of a set it mends (an id, cleaned like the others), and `hardware`, a step that plays through gear outside the machine in real time. Such a step waits on the gear, not on the CPU, and held the CPU's turn of the lab's other jobs for the minutes of its pass; it does not now (`lab.py`), so it queues its own passes. Both are said to the page with the step. `lab/test_plugins.py`: 24 checks.

**A node's window as large as what it holds; the room's head without a row of profiles** (1573; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Пройдись через все попап она нод и постарайся выстроить css так, чтобы каждый оверлей был адекватного размера, а не всё фиксировано по размерам. В одно ноде 80% пустоты в оверлее, а в другой нормальная плотность по элементам», and «строка с профилями имеет экстра паддинг свержу. Съедает кучу вертикального места. Её вообще можно убрать и двумя svg иконками вынести в строку с Canvas/List External DAW кнопками»). A node's window was a box of the room's size whatever it held; now it is a column of up to 1100 px for a step's settings and the window's width for what is wide (the take's tree of waves, the spectrum, the gloss, the DAW, the Remaster, Audio I/O), as tall as its panel up to the room's height, centred, and centred again as the panel grows or folds. The row of profiles above the canvas is gone: in the room's head, beside Canvas/List and External DAW, a sliders icon opens the profiles (each by name, **Save as…**, **Keep the setup as it is now under ›**, **Delete ›**) and a grid icon is Arrange. The 48 px left above the canvas were the head's row gap, none in canvas mode now. The guide's canvas section tells 1569–1573 in every language.

**An answer that does not read is said, not shown as nothing** (1576; Viktor 19.10.2026, a picture: «в Made from this Take вообще всё исчезло, только оригинальный семпл»). One branch's manifest held `Infinity` in its measure (a file held against itself: no noise, no number); Python writes it, `/lab/derived` passed it on, the page's JSON.parse refused the whole reply, and «Made from this Take» said «Nothing made from this take yet» — an empty that lied. The lab now reads NaN and Infinity in a manifest as null (`derived_list`, `parse_constant`), and the page says the answer could not be read when it could not (a branch's manifest.json at fault), never «nothing».

**The Takes column's own «From all Workspaces»** (1577; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Для Творца в Takes нужно допилить собственный фильтр по пространствам, чтобы листить и фильтровать все постпроцессинги из любого… `From all Workspaces` в спадающем меню схлопывающейся колонки Takes»). In the column's ⋯: every workspace's takes in the column, the workspace in hand left as it is (new takes still land in it); with Refined, every refined take of the library; the menu's dot lit while it is on (`yue2.libFilters.allWs`).

**The compare window's rows on one line again** (1579; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Попап сравнения поплыл в болото по дизайну»). 1550 put each version's loudness in the row, a fifth cell in a grid of four, and the length fell to a second line under every name; the grid has five columns now (four when narrow, the group hidden).

**The canvas's wires say one thing each** (1580; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Я не могу пока что прикрутить провод из апскейлера вокала… Провода пунктиром - это подсказка по рауингу а цельный жирный - реальный поток в воркфлоу?»). The way the chain runs thick in the accent; what was made from what thin and quiet; a step's own Source dotted, and only for a step off the way; the roads a wire may take drawn only while one is dragged, the nodes that can take it lit and the others faded; a legend in the canvas's corner, folding. A wire from a step not on the way puts it there where its kind stands (a step on stems after Stems, Stems put in when missing), then the other after it; a wire into the DAW also sets its lanes to that step's newest files.

**The Refiner is its canvas** (1581; Viktor 19.10.2026: «полностью отказаться от варианта List, но из него в канвас забрать самое лучшее. Автонумерацию нод в их логическом потоке воркфлоу, всплывающие тултипы с подсказками по нодам, Нода `Made from this Take` - вправо вниз»; «нода 4 по клавише 4… До 10 уложимся?»). No List / Canvas button and no Engine choice of how the room opens (the Engine's card says what the room is); the panels the nodes open stay where they lived. The nodes are numbered as the work flows: the way's steps 1…N in its order, the rest after them column by column, Audio I/O none; the digits 1…9 and 0 open the node of that number, from the canvas or from another node's window; every node says what it does under the pointer; «Made from This Take» a node of its own at the bottom right, its window the tree; the take's node opens the Takes, to pick another.

**Slots of a class each** (1582; Viktor 19.10.2026: «маркировать инпуты и аутпуты, чтобы всё визуально не херачилось на одну точку… по классам инпутов/аутпутов»; «идеология и код Комфи… наш эталонный донор»). A node's inputs are named on its left and its outputs on its right, as ComfyUI's slots, of three classes: a file (amber), a set of stems (teal), one stem (violet). The DAW takes stems and versions and gives a mix; the Remaster takes a file, stems or versions; a step on one stem takes it out of a set. A wire leaves the slot of its class, lands on the slot that takes it, and wears its colour; while one is dragged only the nodes with a slot that takes it light up, and a drop on any other is said, not wired.

**The Voice upscale's bundle** (1583; Viktor 19.10.2026, after listening to the A/B on Krik's lead: «тройка в среднем и громчайший чистят отдельно в своём ракурсе… одиночно каждый апскейлер что-то улучшает, а что-то режет на вокале… связка даёт баланс. Пропиши несколько профилей для этих связок, по стилю суно ремастера с разными силами и причудами»). Measured first on a minute of Krik's lead, cut at 12 kHz and held against what was there (log-spectral distance): UniverSR 11.7 dB (4 dB short), FlashSR 11.6 (6 dB too bright), Apollo-vocal 13.9 (its shape the closest, 11.5 dB short), the three averaged as UVR averages separators 9.5 (1.8 dB short): the closest. The Voice upscale's Upscaler now offers the bundle: each of UniverSR, Apollo-vocal (baicai1145, CC BY-NC-SA 4.0) and FlashSR (Jaekwon Im) draws the voices' top, each is joined to the original below the cut, and above it they are averaged, the loudest taken in each cell, or air (averaged to 16 kHz, the loudest above); the drawn band is lifted or lowered (+2, 0, −2, −4 dB). UniverSR alone stays the default. Five pipelines: Voices: subtle, normal, high, air, bright. Apollo-vocal and its model code come with the studio's models (goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 at `models/upbundle/apollo/`, as their licences let them be shared); FlashSR comes from its author on first use, at a pinned revision, and is never shared by the studio: its author states no licence yet (asked on 19.10.2026). `lab/test_vupscale_bundle.py`: 8 checks.

**The Creator's piano roll** (1578, 1584; Viktor 19.10.2026: «визуальный редактор партитуры в творце… не забывай, что в партитуре YuE2 два стана - Vocal и Ins»; «реальный горизонтальный 7-8 октавный фортепиано ролл и идти по нему сверху вниз как по настоящей партитуре… бумажная партитура… оригинальная, справа - исправленная»; «синтезатор… Дефолт - инструментал… распевки»; «MIDI device»; «SHIFT для набора мышью полных аккордов… белые на A-', чёрные на Q-]… профили 25, 37, 61, 88»; «клавиши живыми… клаву роланд или корг»; «88 клавиш… мелко… центральные октавы»). A staff with an octave on it beside the score opens it over the whole window: above, the score on paper twice (as YuE2 wrote it, and as it stands now, drawn again as one edits); below, the lane where time flows down to two weighted keyboards, Vocal's over Ins's, the keys one size on every screen, the octaves beyond at the edges (a small keyboard of them on hover, a click brings them in). The staff in hand bright, the other faint behind it; the lyrics' syllables on Vocal's notes, a syllable a note within each section, as Recite lays them. A click writes a note of the grid's length, a drag moves it, its lower edge stretches it; a voice of YuE2 sings one note at a time, so a note laid over another makes it give way. Shift and keys pressed together (the mouse, the computer's keys, a MIDI keyboard) make a chord set on the cursor's bar, named among the fifteen qualities the score's dialect reads; the computer's keys play a keyboard and a half (white on A…', black on W…], Z and X an octave), their letters on the keys; Web MIDI listens to any keyboard plugged in; Rec writes the key played at the cursor and steps on; 25, 37, 61 and 88 mark one's controller's reach. Space plays from the cursor, the original or the edited, Ins as keys (the default), Vocal sung on the notes' own names (a formant voice: Do, Re, Mi…), or both, the keys going down as they sound. «Write into the score» goes through `HeresyAbc.rewrite`: only the lines whose notes or chords changed are written anew, every other line byte for byte as YuE2 wrote it (the library's 174 scores read back to themselves, 870 edits read back exactly).

**The roll by day and by night, its keys with depth** (1585; Viktor 19.10.2026: «страницы партитуры в чёткой ночной инверсии. В дневном режиме лист партитуры сделай немного сероватым, чтобы белое не било в глаза»; «Подлиннее инструментальные клавиши… А вокальные пусть остаются как верхние на клавесине. 3D клавиши слабо?»; «Нижний блок поясняющий очень высокий… слева на бекграунд миди ролла наложить… При подключении Миди устройства скрывать этот хелпер, с чекбоксом»). The papers take the page's ground: by night dark with light ink, by day a sheet a little grey; abcjs draws in `currentColor`, so the paper's colour is its ink, and the roll turns with the page's theme while open. Ins's keyboard is 120 px deep and Vocal's 64, a harpsichord's upper manual over the lower; each white key has its top (in the felt's shade at the back) and its front lip, each black key is a raised block with a sloped front and a soft shadow on the white, and a key down sinks (a shorter lip, its neighbours' sides in sight) in its staff's colour. The keys' guide lies faint over the lane's low octaves, which are mostly empty, its box the one thing in it that takes a click (kept in `yue2.rollGuide`), folded when MIDI listens; under the roll one line. MIDI is the browser's only on a secure page, so a studio opened by its address from another computer says so and how to open it as localhost (`ssh -L`); keyboards plugged in while the roll is open are met; a controller's own control port (an Oxygen's DAW port: `MIDIIN2`, `MIDI 2`; Mackie, HUI) and its pads (channel 10) are not taken for keys.

**The roll's voices real** (1586; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Синтезатор звучит как на дешёвой ATARI видео пристаки. Бьюсь об заклад, ты сдешманил 8 битным синтезом»). He won the bet: the roll played a triangle and a sine for Ins and a sawtooth through three formants for Vocal. Now Ins plays the Salamander Grand Piano (Alexander Holm) and Vocal the choir «aah» and the voice «ooh» of FluidR3 GM (Frank Wen), both CC BY 3.0: a note every three semitones, played at its neighbours' pitches, mono MP3 at 80 kbps, 1.9 MB in `lab/sounds/`, served at `/sounds/NAME.mp3` as the fonts are (lab 62b064b). Vocal still sings the notes' names: «o» and «u» on the «ooh», the rest on the «aah» with the vowel's formants lifted, the consonant a breath of noise as before; a long note holds on the voice's middle. The built voices stay only for when the samples do not load, and the roll says so; Play waits for them.

**The papers follow the roll** (1587; Viktor 19.10.2026: «В пиано ролле нужна синхронизация бумажной партитуры с самим midi роллом и её параллелью - с листом правки/редактуры. Play не прокручивает ролл и партитуру. Перепроверь все синхронизации»). abcjs draws each group of the score's lines as one system and classes its marks by line, voice and measure (`add_classes`); its own measure count runs on the first voice and takes `Z4` for one bar, so the roll maps a bar by `HeresyAbc.groups` (the group and its first bar) and by the measure inside its voice's line, where a run of empty bars is one. Both papers follow the playhead, else a picked note, else the bar the lane was scrolled to, else the cursor, system by system, and the bar in hand is lit on both; a paper scrolled by hand takes the other paper and the lane to its first system in sight; a click on a paper's note puts the cursor on its bar with that staff in hand. Play did not scroll because it began at a cursor out of sight and followed only a playhead below the lane's three quarters: now a cursor out of sight starts Play at the lane's first whole bar, and the lane follows the playhead both ways.

**The jobs' strip clears the controls** (1588; Viktor 19.10.2026, with his picture of the strip over Probes and Variations: «Прогресс бар закрывает кнопки синтеза в Творце. Давай при наведении указателя мыши в область этих кнопок и движков временно растворять этот оверлей прогресса. Кнопку пиано ролла уведи вправо»). The strip never took a click but its fold button, so what lay under it worked, unseen; a control under the pointer there (a button, a slider, a box, a chip) now dissolves the strip to 6 % until the pointer leaves it. The piano roll's button stands at its row's end.

**The studio's MIDI, and a MIDI API** (1589; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Хорошо, беру M-Audio Oxygen 61 MKV»; «официально с 2.0.0 релизом выкатим early-release поддержку M-Audio миди контроллеров. И предусмотреть интерфейс для 3rd party плагинов для MIDI. Откроем "MIDI API", люди начнут писать свои плагины»; on the two repositories he found for it, reaKontrol and flmidi-kompletekontrol). Those two speak NI's own NIHIA to Native Instruments' Komplete Kontrol keyboards through NI's service, which exists for Windows and macOS only; the Oxygen MKV's DAW port speaks Mackie Control (or HUI) in its presets, an open protocol every DAW reads. So the studio speaks Mackie Control itself. `heresy-midi.js` holds the page's one MIDI: a keyboard's keys go to whoever listens (the piano roll, at once when MIDI is on), a control port goes to a script that turns its buttons, faders and knobs into actions by name (play, stop, record, rewind, forward, loop, the tracks, the banks, the strips', the octave), and the room that offered an action last answers it (the roll over the DAW in sight over the player); a handler that says false hands the button on. Built in: Mackie Control on a port named Mackie or MCU and on an Oxygen's second port (Play and Record lights sent back as the roll plays and records); another maker's DAW port is left alone until a plugin's script claims it. A plugin's MIDI scripts (plugin.json `"midi"`, a plugin may be made of them alone; lab d5cb778) run each in a worker of its own with no page and no network: ruach.midi/1 is `onMidi(bytes, port)`, `studio.act`, `studio.say`, `studio.send` (PLUGINS.md, «MIDI scripts»). Engine → Hardware → MIDI turns listening on and off (kept), lists every port with what it is taken for, and says why there is no MIDI on a page that is neither localhost nor https. Early support: written to the protocol and tried through the browser with made-up ports; the Oxygen itself comes later.

**The roll's ends, its lever, its tempo and its wheels** (1590; Viktor 19.10.2026: «По широкой клаве тултип с остатком клавиш не очень хорошо. Нужно слева/справа добавить кнопки-стрелки для сдвига стана, и кнопку возврата в центр»; «монитор 2K в портретном… Нам нужен рычаг сдвига между партитурами и пиано роллом… запоминание положения. И в таком расположении монитора клавиши инструментала можно в полноформатном рояльном соотношении»; «Строку состояния тоже перенеси… под шпаргалку клавиш»; «В баре кнопку Play/Stop сделай плеерной svg иконкой. Скорость воспроизведения высокая… Не вижу переключателя скорости»; «Во фрейме партитуры нужна как в чатах стрелка вверх»; «для MIDI контроллеров переопределить колесо pitch-shift'а. В миди мы рисуем ноты, а не эффекты»). Arrows over the keyboards' ends with the count of the notes waiting beyond, and ◎ the keys back on the score's notes (the edges' small keyboard gone). A lever between the papers and the roll, kept in `yue2.rollSplit`, a double click as at first. Ins's keys as long as the room lets them: a third of the stage, 120 px at least, a grand's own six widths (240 px) on a tall screen. The line of state under the keys' guide. Play and Stop as the player's icons; the tempo beside them, − and + by four, the wheel by one, a double click the score's own: a free-tempo score has no Q:, so the roll had played it at the nominal 120, which is what sounded fast; such a score now takes the last tempo set for one (`yue2.rollFreeBpm`, 80 at first). ↑ at a paper's corner once it is scrolled. A MIDI keyboard's pitch wheel runs the cursor through time (up on, down back, the further the faster, the grid at rest), its modulation wheel sets a new note's length.

**The roll's Vocal sings in phrases** (1592; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Рояль хорошо звучит, но вот вокал… може что-то подшаманишь в этом плане?»). The notes that touch are one breath on one sample (a leap of more than nine semitones starts another): the pitch glides into each note, a vibrato grows on the long ones, the vowel's formants flow from syllable to syllable, a breath before the phrase, a small hall around it (a convolution of falling noise, a fifth wet). It sings the lyrics' syllables (their vowel and first consonant, Cyrillic or Latin) or the notes' names, kept in `yue2.rollSing`.

**Icons for the common acts** (1593; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Перерисуй все кнопки `Cancel ...` svg иконкой 🗙. Применить - ✔️, и т.п. по стандартной системе иконографии функций кнопок, - там, где до сих пор текстовые надписи»). `heresy-iconbtn.js` looks at every button that appears: one whose whole text is Cancel, Close, Send nothing or Dismiss shows ✕; OK, Apply, Done, Confirm, Make it or Write into the score ✓; Save a diskette; Delete or Remove a bin; Clear an eraser; Reset a turn back (Lucide, ISC). Its word stays inside, unseen, so a screen reader still says it, the catalogs still translate it and the checks still read it; it is the button's tip when it has none of its own. A button a script fills with other words is words again.

**Transpose a segment** (1594; Viktor 19.10.2026: «раз уж ты предложил транспозицию… как в LoRA графах рисуем редактируемую прямую… двигаем по 1/4, 1/3, 1/2, 1 ... 2 октавным шагам… с минорной/мажорной части в мажорную/минорную»; «кнопку "Транспозировать сегмент", поворачивать ролл горизонтально, по кривой рисовать сдвиг и возвращать обратно в вертикальное движение»; «По кривой транспоза принимаю твоё решение как зрелое и рабочее»). A drag along the bar numbers picks a segment's bars (a click there still sets the cursor; Shift from the cursor). ⇅ Transpose lays the segment across the stage: its notes above, faint as they are and bright as they will be; below, the shift as a line of points, moved by a semitone, a quarter, a third or a half of an octave, an octave or two; held from point to point or gliding (a note takes the shift at its start, in whole semitones: YuE2's score holds no microtones). To minor and To major turn a part in its own tonic (the third, the sixth and the seventh a semitone; I and IV minor or major, V kept; the lowered degrees' chords major), In the key moves a note pushed out of it to the nearest note in it, both staves or the one in hand. The pitch wheel and ↑ ↓ move the picked point; Apply is one step to undo.

**A voice adapter's road** (1595; Viktor 19.10.2026, after Chonishvili's r32 on his Есаул: «AR макс 0.5 (затем красное), NAR - макс 1.0, и затем красное. И по таким по именам адаптеров, где "voice" или "vocal", такое правило применять к лимитам»; «AR адаптера напрямую влияет на построение партитуры обеих Ins и Vocal… голосовые - чистая наррация, вот тебе и приглушение нотного ряда»). An adapter whose name says voice or vocal: the music half green to 0.4, amber to 0.5, red past it; the sound half green to 0.8, amber to 1.0, red past it. The guide says why (a voice taught on speech writes narration into the score through its music half; the timbre lives in the sound half). On his ear the same day: AR 0.4 with NAR 1.0 on the IRAE merge, «Музыка не перебивается. Голос стабилен». Its first build put a note mid-line in YueLoras' exports and made the rest of the line a comment: Open of a file with adapters failed on the live studio until the live checks caught it (74581d6).

**The Creator's form kept on every change** (1596; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Помнишь, мы замышляли для Творца прикрутить version control? Сейчас наверное тот самый случай из-за ABC редактирования. Испортил, применил, F5, и ... проебал основу»; «Каждый Ctrl+S или запуск генерации на изменённых текстах, параметрах, данных ABC сохраняет свою diff»). 1519 kept the words on each Generate only, so a score written from the roll and a reload left nothing of the one before. Now the form is kept, with its knobs (the request as Generate would send it, without the texts) and why, before each place that replaces it (an example, a score example or its removal, Make instrumental, a take or a recipe into the form, a plan's score, a cover's score, the Writer's brief, the assistant's apply, a version into the form, the roll), on Ctrl+S, after the roll writes into the score, and on Generate; the same words and knobs again are no new version (lab 6e337d1). A first build caught the boxes' value instead: it took away their own catches (the lyrics' counts and marks, Recite, the score's lights) and could not tell overwriting from typing; the kitchen suite caught it. The second step, a Version field beside the Title (numbers and dots), revisions inside it, a branch where an old one is edited, a tag as a star, the whole tree of a root title deleted as the Writer's drafts are, waits for his word on its shape.

**The dev repository and the models' mirror** (1597; Viktor 19.10.2026: «на Орфее под git@192.168.2.1/home/git/ruach_studio_dev.git создавай bare repo и пушай туда всё наше как на внутренний upstream. Всё внутреннее и Pro… Модели не пушь. Их лучше на Големе миррорить»; 20.10.2026: «Используй один репо… лучше запушить всё в ruach_studio_dev.git как в полный наш dev репо»). `tools/backup-push.sh` pushes build, lab, heresy (the Pro plugin and the kitchens with it), kit, upstream and the public Release into one bare repository, every branch and tag under its prefix (remote «dev»); the old ruach_studio.git held nothing the dev repository lacks (its 11 branches compared commit for commit on 20.10) and is no longer pushed. `tools/mirror-models.sh` copies the weights and the data (models, loras, checkpoints, hf_cache, artwork, training, separation, whisper, writer, user) to the cold disk, never deleting there.

**The Refiner without a chain** (1598; Viktor 20.10.2026: «К хуям удаляй предопределённый `Chain`. Это - шлак без настроек. Никаких шорткатов… Реальный погружённый воркфлоу, который человек должен понимать, а не клацать на кнопки мыши. И в Гайде убери Chain авто воркфлоу»; «По режиму List… Мы договорились к хуям убрать Список»; «у нас больше, чем 10. Сделай микро задержку в 400-600ms для последовательного нажатия двух цифровых клавиш»). On the canvas the way is My way or a pipeline, never the classic chain: the room opening as its canvas turns a classic chain once set into the first My way, a studio that never ran it starts with the take alone. The chain's row is the way's bar in the room's head (the way in hand as a button with its list, New way, On the workspace…, Run the way), no Chain node; an empty way runs nothing and says so. The List button is gone for good (1581 hid it with the hidden attribute, which `.btn`'s display overrode, so it stayed in sight; the checks read the attribute). Two digits within half a second open a node past ten (1 then 2: the twelfth); a digit that starts no number past ten opens at once. The list stays in the checks' hands with the classic chain. Words: Run the way, Bypass on the way, Refine N along the way, ×11; the guide's Refiner ×12 without the chain. suite-1598.part; 1506, 1508, 1510, 1553, 1569, 1581 brought to it.

**Own plugins off for a public view** (1599; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Добавь также в настройки Огранщика, если есть в активе мои Pro плагины, отключение оных ради теста Паблик воркфлоу. Так сможем имитировать публичный код в Промо и на скринах… помести его в системный бар Студии в ниспадающее меню»). `heresy-pro.js`: a row in ☰, shown only when a plugin that is not the studio's own lies in user/plugins/; off (kept per browser), the Refiner's steps and pipelines, the DAW's inserts and the MIDI scripts are read as a public studio would; the lab keeps them, nothing is deleted, the page reloads; a plugin's pipeline in hand gives way to My way. suite-1599.part.

**The roll opens the scores YuE2 writes** (1600; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Кнопка Transpose не ложит ролл горизонтально… Пробую Ctrl+R. Та же хрень»; «Лирика не поётся и на стане не выводится»; «Tempo взял в одну кнопку жирную, и оверлеем ниспадающим… выбор размера клавиатуры… тоже в единую кнопку с ниспадающим, и с толковыми подсказками. Доли bar'а оставляй как есть»). His Есаул did not open at all: its Ins wrote a bar of 18/16 (`A4z4E2G8`) and the strict read failed. `HeresyAbc.mend` evens such a bar for the roll (what runs over cut at the bar's end, a short bar filled with a rest, an odd length as tied supported ones), says so under the roll and in a toast, and a line the roll does not change goes back as YuE2 wrote it (written untouched: the score byte for byte). The lyrics stand under the Vocal staff of both papers (`HeresyAbc.withWords`, abcjs w: lines, the score keeps none), and an intro's few Vocal notes before the verse sing the verse's first syllables. Transpose with no bars picked takes the section at the cursor. The keyboard's size and the tempo are a button each with its list under it (88, 61, 37, 25 said; −4, −1, +1, +4, a slider, the score's own); the head one line at 100 % and 110 % on 1920. suite-1600.part.

**The paper's words in Noto Sans** (1601; Viktor 20.10.2026: «для титлов Vocal ...и Inst ... замени сраный таймс роман на нормальный Noto Sans с фейковой шириной 76%. И аккордные пометки с остальными - тот же Noto Sans, но дай ему фейковую ширину 90%. Количества для пустых bars - тот же шрифт, ширина 100%»). `HeresyAbc.dress` after every engraving (the Creator's staff, its print, the datasheet's, the roll's papers): Noto Sans, narrowed as LaTeX's FakeStretch does (scaled across about the point each word stands at), as attributes so a print window carries them; the print window takes the page's Noto Sans faces.

**Free tempo on one line** (1602; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Добавь в кнопку Free tempo неразрыв»): `white-space: nowrap`, in every language.

**The song licence row** (1603, README): M-A-P's individual creator permission of 16.09.2026 (MODEL_LICENSE): a musician or creator acting on their own may publish, sell and license the songs; a company needs the authors' licence.

**The engine at upstream's level** (1604; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Всё забирай и даже не жди утра! Мы паритетимся с апстримом yue2.cpp без вопросов… Если это даёт нам силу, забирае их коммиты без рассуждений»). yue2.cpp merged to 8af0174, 22 commits: continuation from a section of another song (`source_tokens`), upstream's adapters stacked on the backbone beside ours (the model store keys both, one load merges both), the audio tokenizer (Mothersuperior's realaudio v4 head, `/tokenize`, `--tokenizer`), MERT-v2-FullSong and the SheetSage2 head as separate GGUFs (the transcriber reads both), a stream with no score renders without one, every track's VAE latents in the reply (the page reads its first two parts), `--device`, ggml 0.27 with Pascal's snake and conv_1d kept. Ours kept whole: the LoRA wave and budgets, the sliders, decoders by name, the library, `decode_from`, fades, the medcard's timings. The sound is bit for bit the same (SNR 252.7 dB against the old engine's, the same tokens, the same length). ⚠ «27–30 s before, 12 s after», first written here, was not the engine: the new one had run on the idle GPU0 and the old one on GPU2 beside a training, as upstream's --device had dropped GGML_BACKEND (1613). On one card the two are as fast.

**Multistep is AB2** (1605; Viktor 20.10.2026: «И внедряй солвер AB2»). It was in already: our Multistep (1004) is Adams–Bashforth of the second order, one network call a step, the step before it reused. Now named so in the solver's list (*Multistep · AB2*), its tip and the guide, which say each solver's calls a step. On the take above: 9 s against Midpoint's 12, the same tokens, SNR 25.5 dB against Midpoint's sound.

**Chords for the planned score** (1606 engine, 1607 page; timoncool/yue2.cpp 1e21e63, MIT, after Yeufonic). The planner tends to loop one progression through a whole song. `src/harmony.h` whole: in the ABC stage, a chord heard among the recent changes loses logits by its share, a root held past `hold_limit` pays more, out-of-key roots may gain (`outside_bonus` up to `outside_limit`), a section's opening chords are kept apart from the one before, sections may be followed in order; off unless the request's `harmony` asks, a refusal said by the server (400). The Creator: *Chords* under the planning modes, Off · Gentle · Varied · Bold (strength 4, 8, 16; Bold also lets a chord step outside the key), sent only when the model writes the score; kept in the draft, the versions, prompt files and a take's request, said on its card. Measured on Есаул's plan (one lm_seed, Q8_0): off, 6 distinct chords and one four-chord run ×13; strength 8, 9 distinct and the commonest run ×4. suite-1607.part.

**Only the opening** (1608 engine, 1609 page; timoncool/yue2.cpp 7810749, MIT, after Mothersuperior's hum-to-song). With `abc_continue` the score is the opening of a plan, not the whole of it: it joins the prompt after ABC_START with its end token left out, the model writes the rest of the song on from it in its key and meter, and the opening is put back before the plan's own tokens (ported by its meaning: our `yue2_generate` has no carried tokens); `plan_only` takes it too; Direct or a semantic stream refused (400); a take made from an opening replays its whole score. The Creator: *Only the opening* under the score, in Full or Melody, with *Chords* steering what the model adds. Measured: Есаул's intro as the opening, the plan starts with it and goes on (verse, interlude, verse…). The kitchen suite caught a note left mid-line in the prompt files' keys (it had made `abc_sampling` and the rest a comment) before it went live. suite-1607.part.

**The transcriber as the merged engine reads it, in the install** (1610). Since 1604 the engine reads SheetSage2 as a head with MERT-v2-FullSong of the same kind beside it; the models the install fetched still held both in one file (957 MB as Q8_0), so a fresh install would have had no covers from a recording. goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 815098b0 (`MISC/claude-tc/hf-1610.py`): SheetSage2's head in place of the old file, MERT-v2-FullSong beside it and Mothersuperior's realaudio tokenizer v4 head, F32 and Q8_0 each, byte for byte as Serveurperso/YuE2-GGUF publishes them (e630f2b8f6, every sha256 compared before the upload), with their rows in MANIFEST.tsv, the notices and the card; the pin moved. `download-models.sh --transcriber` takes the head and its MERT; `fetch-models.sh --check` names both; `start.sh` passes the transcriber only with its MERT beside it and not the older combined file, and otherwise says to run `./download-models.sh --transcriber` (the old file against the new pin is fetched again); `convert-models.sh` makes and checks MERT-v2-FullSong-F32 too and moves an older combined SheetSage2-F32 to tmp/rejected/ to be made again as two; the engine's `convert.py` skips the tokenizer when its checkpoint is not there and says so (upstream had made it a step every conversion needed). Checked: the live install against the new pin, all four files «have»; the start guard on the old file and the new; a conversion with no tokenizer checkpoint.

**FlashSR kept beside the studio's models** (1611; Viktor 19.10.2026: «Свежие веса, что идут в Паблик, не забудь в наш HF репо выложить и прописать в загрузках»; 20.10.2026: «Не дай Бог репо будут удалены или закрыты»). Its author answered on 20.10.2026 (jakeoneijk/FlashSR_Inference issue 1): his code MIT, the pretrained weights Apache 2.0, the parts from Diffusers, AudioSR and BigVGAN under their own licences; we said there we would keep a copy, and nobody objected. The three weights went into goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 at `models/upbundle/flashsr/weights/` unchanged, with a card, the licence and their rows (2e7a1b08, `MISC/claude-tc/hf-1611.py`); the pin moved. `lab/upbundle_job.py` takes them from there on first use and from him only when ours does not come (said on stderr); the code still comes from him at its pinned revision, as it carries third-party parts (AudioSR's, with Meta's AudioMAE inside) whose terms are their own. Checked: the weights fetched from our copy, ten seconds of a voice through FlashSR on GPU2, the same length and loudness back.

**The 2.0.0 test tree** (1612, local only, nothing published). `make-release.sh` into tmp/release-next stopped twice, both times rightly, on things never published. First, `tools/mirror-models.sh` (1597, the cold disk's mirror, dev only) carried this machine's paths: it now stays home beside backup-push.sh. Second, a note in `heresy-post.js` (1572) named a private plugin step: the words are gone from the page (live). The same words remain in the engine patch series (`engine/1465…`), which `apply.sh --export` writes from build's history, so taking them out needs a rewritten commit or a series left out of the release, and that is Viktor's word. `check-no-pro.sh`: steps of ruach only, user/ empty, 76 pictures read and clean. The clean clone (`MISC/claude-tc/t2000-clean.sh`): built in 145 s, the page's suite 1081/0, the player 3/0, the Pinokio launcher 74/0, the places 26/0.

**The card named again; the speed told right; Chords at Gentle; the patch series home; the transcriber in an update** (1613; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Ты говоришь о трёхкратном ускорении с новыми патчами, но не вижу этого, реально»; «Выставь Gentle в дефолт»; «да, бери (б) не класть heresy/engine/*.patch в релиз»; «Докачиваем новый транскрайбер (1 ГБ) прямо в апдейте»). His question found a real fault. Upstream's `--device` (83b65f5, merged in 1604) had dropped the `GGML_BACKEND` variable by which start.sh's STUDIO_GPU, the lab's extra engines and every test engine name their card, so each landed on the best device, the studio's own; 1604's «2.4× faster» was that: the old engine on GPU2 beside a training, the new one on the idle GPU0 (and the night's harmony, opening and transcriber tests ran on GPU0 unknowingly, short, while Viktor slept). `src/backend.h` reads `GGML_BACKEND` again when no `--device` is given, and a name matching no device stops loudly as before (checked: CUDA1 on GPU1 by its uuid, CUDA9 refused). The honest A/B (`MISC/claude-tc/ab-1613.py`): the 2.0.0-pre engine and this one alternately on one card, one 60-second request, Q8_0: the music half 20.3 and 20.2 ms a step, the sound half 484 and 486 ms a step, the sound bit for bit the same (SNR 351.7 dB); the merge brought no speed. What is faster is AB2: on his own takes of the day the sound half took 40.7 s for a 339-second song against Midpoint's 106 s for 403. Chords start at Gentle: the markup, New song and a draft kept before Chords existed; a take or a prompt file without harmony opens as Off, as it was made. `make-release.sh` no longer ships `heresy/engine/*.patch`: the series is written from build's history and stays home (build/ carries the code). `build.sh`, which the updater runs after its checkout, fetches the transcriber's head with MERT beside it once when an install has the older one-file kind (`download-models.sh --transcriber-only`); checked on a copy with the old file: both fetched by the new pin in 18 s, the old one replaced. Chonishvili's NAR-only adapter (e029, the AR of e010 frozen) sung on Есаул at AR 0.4 / NAR 1.0 and AR 0.3 / NAR 0.8 for his ear, as the 19.10 rounds were (GPU2 refused it beside a training: BF16 and its KV cache did not fit; GPU0 for three minutes while idle).

**Version beside Title, one tree per title** (1614; Viktor 19.10.2026: «привязывая древо изменений к имени трека как к базе, и ветвя по суффиксным номерам версий… второе поле ввода в навес к Title… сторож только по числам и точкам, это всё наподобие git branch, git commit & git tag»; 20.10.2026: «Не вижу, чтобы ты Title разделил на два поля как договаривались»). The Creator's Version field takes digits and dots (the guard cleans «v4..2a.3» to 4.2.3); what goes out (the request, the draft, the Writer, a file's name, the score's) is «Title vN.N», as his tracks are named, and a title written so (a take's, an example's, a file's, a version's, typed or pasted) comes back split into the two. `lab/creator_versions.py`: one book per root title, every version with its branch; the books kept under «Name vN.N» titles came into their root's book once, numbered in their time order, the old files in `writer-versions/.adopted/` (his two Крик books, v4.2.2 and v4.2.3, are now one tree of ten); the same words again only add takes to their branch's last version. The versions' window shows the branches, newest first, each with its versions; *Delete the tree…* sends a title's whole tree into `.trash/` (a locked version keeps it, and the lab says which), and the Writer's *The Creator's versions* lists the deleted trees and brings one back whole. Tests: `lab/test_creator_versions.py` (the split, the guard, the adoption, a branch's same words, a new branch from a title's tail, a tree deleted and back), suite-1614.part. Live: his two Крик books, kept under «01. МАНИФЕСТ_ Крик. v4.2.2» and «… v4.2.3», are one tree «01. МАНИФЕСТ_ Крик.» of two branches. A diff's head names the branch once when both versions are on it («v4.2.3 · #1 → #2»).

**The score drawer's buttons as wide as their words** (1615; Viktor 20.10.2026, with his picture of the roll's button fallen to a row of its own: «Кнопки съезжают. Слишком широкие. Пиано ролл ужми до размера svg»). `.drawer-act` gave every button 172 px, so *Make instrumental*, *Remove score* and the roll's staff did not fit one row; now each is as wide as its words and the roll's button as its staff (96 px), on one row. The 1167 check of «both one size» reads «each as wide as its words» now.

**fosforida's velvet, the sound half on** (1616; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Адаптер fosforida глянь, или дообучить, или переобучить. Тот, что использую, даёт тот самый Суно бархат, но недостаточно»; «Бери GPU0 после моей генерации. Я подожду»). The adapter he sings with is fosforida-v2-tags-r32 at e065 (AI-Toolkit, 02.10.2026: 42 FOSFORIDA tracks, r32, Direct, 120 epochs; he uses it at AR 0.3–0.6, NAR 0.5–0.8). The velvet is the sound half's, so it went on as Chonishvili's did: `fosforida-v2-nar` starts from e065 (step 2730) in our trainer, the AR of e065 standing still (its loss and KL weights and its learning rate 0, no optimizer state carried), the NAR on to e125, every epoch saved and no tracks held out (42 are few), on GPU0 after the studio was quiet a minute and its models unloaded (`MISC/lora-merge/fosforida-nar-start.sh`). Every tenth epoch goes into `loras/fosforida-v2-nar/` for his ear against e065.

**Training over the Server log** (1617; Viktor 20.10.2026: «LoRA training progress bar сделай уже и размести прямо над свёрнутым Server Log фреймом»). The training runs left the strip above the player (1549) for a bar of their own over the Server log, wherever it stands, folded or not, left or right: a run a line, *LoRA training*, its name and card above the bar, its step, the share done and about how long it has left under it; a click opens the Trainer. The lab's `/activity` now says a run's time run and left, read off the trainer's own line. The 1549 and 1569 checks feed the strip other jobs; suite-1617.part checks the bar over the dock.

**A plan the page made is read before it is sung; the take is called planned** (1618; Viktor 20.10.2026, a Bold plan sung with its voices out of order: «В добавок галлюцинации и смазывание голоса в этом ране»; «у трёх последних дублей партитура подана готовой - это баг»). With a key chosen (or an instrumental) the page plans first (`plan_only`), moves the plan to the key and sings it as a given score: the engine's guard (1087, no key, no meter) never saw it, and his Bold plan of 10:20 with two Ins lines in a row («group 1, Vocal: expected V: Vocal») was sung as it was. Now the page reads the plan as the score box does before singing it: a broken one is planned again once with a new music seed when the seed was random, or stopped and said. The engine's `score_planned` keeps such a take from being called supplied (`provided_score` false); takes before it, whose request carries the Chords (sent only for a plan), read as planned too. His no-key plans of 09:46–09:53 were the adapters on the music half and one fixed music seed: harmony steers chord symbols only (its own header says so). suite-1618.part (the broken Bold plan and its sibling).

**The namesakes' chooser wide** (1619; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Нужно растянуть попап выбора треков для сравнения, чтобы не было обрезки имён»): up to 960 px, and a take's moment a date and an hour (a created time in seconds had been printed raw).

**The plan's fuse** (1620; Viktor 20.10.2026: «больше ломает AR сам LoRA адаптер в его передозе или в суммарном жёлтом-красном. Что, если прикрутить предохранитель при генерации партитуры, понижая общее адаптеров до безопасного уровня, а синтез на выставленных повышенных пределах?»). Measured on the studio's journal since 13.10 (51 plans with adapters on the music half): the sum decides, not the largest: up to 1.0 none of 7 broke, up to 1.25 3 of 37, up to 1.5 4 of 7 (all «no key line»; today's six were one seed). In Full with no score, adapters past 1.0 together plan the score eased in proportion to 1.0 (a plan request of its own), and the song is sung on it at the strengths set, through the plan-first path with 1618's check; said in the log and a toast. suite-1620.part.

**The compare window from memory, up to six** (1621; Viktor 20.10.2026: «предусмотри полное кеширование треков, потому что задержки до нескольких секунд. И выбор в сравнение не даём больше 6 треков»). Every version is fetched whole as the window opens (the chosen one first) and plays from memory from then on; the one playing takes its copy when paused or left; the transport row says how many are in memory; the memory given back on close. Two to six, in the namesakes' chooser and the Librarian's Compare (the 1415 check reads seven off, six on).

**The sound half on alone after the plateau, in the Trainer** (1622; Viktor 20.10.2026, on Chonishvili's NAR-only takes: «да, чище голос. Это правильный ход с заморозкой AR после плато и дообучением. Внедряй в пайплайн. Но всё равно не голос Чонишвили»). `lab/trainer/train.py`: at the held-out plateau a run whose AR trained does not end: the AR of the save with the lowest held-out ar_ce stands still (its weights, lr 0, no AR loss), the NAR goes on from the best held-out flow with a fresh optimizer, 6 saves of patience at 0.1 %, up to `nar_phase_epochs` (12) more epochs, in the same run, val.json saying the phase. The lab gives every run that holds tracks out the phase, and the Trainer room's «Stop at the plateau, then the sound half alone» (on by default, beside Separate) holds two in a hundred out with 3 epochs of patience; the guide ×12 says it, and the plan's fuse. Its first real test is the voice queue's next run.

**Adapters in tenths on his roads, the seed's guard, the Babel window told true** (1623; Viktor 20.10.2026, after three Babel windows in a row: «К ползункам адаптеров прикрути шаг 0.1, потому что сейчас тысячные, и они никому не нужны»; «По движкам Music перепиши макс на 0.75 (0.5 - это уже красное), по Sound - 1.0 (0.75 - уже красное). Sound (together) - макс 1.5. Зелёнка до 1.2. Music (together) - тоже макс 1.4 (здесь и ломается), зелёное до 1.2. По готовой партитуре можно поднимать общее и выше, но при генерации - это предел»; «Нужен сторож на сиды. Предупреждение и действие при изменении параметров адаптеров. Держать на том же сиде, или с рандом»; «"A New Score" = "New seeds"»; «На v3 нужно понижать общее AR адаптеров»). `loras.js`: every strength slider in tenths, the two together-sliders too (their drag on the same grid), a double-click types hundredths; every adapter on one road, the music half green to 0.4, red from 0.5, 0.75 at most, the sound half green to 0.6, red from 0.75, 1.0 at most (a score adapter keeps its own; none is in loras/); a new adapter comes in at its green; Music, together green to 1.2, 1.3 amber, red at 1.4, its most, while the model writes the score (the plans since 13.10: up to 1.0 together none of 6 broke, from 1.0 to 1.2 14 of 35, 8 of 10 of them on IRAEv3, past 1.2 8 of 9), the old ceiling (2.25, at most 3.5) with a score in the form; Sound, together green to 1.2, red at 1.5; the budgets' red lines follow. `app.js`: the plan's fuse by the backbone, 0.8 on IRAEv3 and 1.0 on the others; a plan no longer writes its seed into the music seed field (26 of his 63 runs since 19.10 had gone on one seed so: the next Generate took a seed in the field as Fixed); each fixed music seed is remembered with its adapters' music halves and its backbone, and one that broke a plan with them, and Generate on it asks first, A new seed or Keep the seed, when they moved or it broke; A new score takes new seeds; a job's summary keeps its adapters and backbone, so the Babel window names what weighed (it had said «No adapter weighed» with three on, and the score's guard after a plan had read the same empty list), with the backbone, the seed and the fuse, and plans again with a new seed on a word; a plan the engine refused goes the way of a plan the page found broken (again once on a random seed). `lab/training.py`: a set that is a link in its own place is the set of its name (the datasets moved to the Patriot, each linked in its place). Guide ×12 (lines 80, 140–142, 144); suite-1623.part; the old checks of the sliders to tenths and these roads.

**The roll sings the vowels, its piano under a pedal, in a room, under its lid; the folded takes wide enough** (1624; Viktor 20.10.2026: «По пиано роллу всё ахерно. Для лирики ... в обеих режимах поёт Фааааа.... можешь переписать семплы.... аааааа .... ооооо .... Рояль для миди эмуляции звучит отлично. На затяжные приделай педаль, комнаты (зал, комната, открытое/закрытое деко), плиз»; «Да, дека - grand piano's lid»; «Колонка Takes узковата в сложенном виде. Звезда вылетает за границу экрана»). The «Ф» was the roll's own noise: a band of it before every phrase and a burst for a syllable's first consonant. Now Vocal sings the vowels themselves, a, e, i, o, u, from VocalSet (CC BY 4.0, Wilkins, Seetharaman, Wahl, Pardo 2018): `heresy/MISC/claude-tc/vowels-1624.py` found every steady stretch of pitch (pYIN) in twenty singers' straight tones, scales and arpeggios and took for each vowel a note every three semitones (66 of 85: none below D♯2 and above A♯5 is sung there; such a pitch takes the nearest of its vowel), tuned, levelled, looped on whole periods (lab/sounds/vX-NN.mp3, vowels.json, vowels-sources.tsv, the README's credit); a phrase is one voice, each note on its own vowel under a 35 ms crossfade, the glide and the vibrato as before, no noise; the choir's «aah»/«ooh» only when the vowels do not load. **Sound ▾** beside Play: the **Pedal** (Long notes by default: a note of a beat or longer rings on past its end, dying away, until the pedal is changed 0.15 s past the next bar line, three seconds at most; Always; Off), the **Acoustics** around the piano and the voice (Dry, Room 0.8 s, Hall 2.6 s: impulses made in the page; the voice's own small hall of 1592 is the Room now) and the grand's **Lid** (Raised; Closed: a shelf of −7 dB from 2 kHz, a low-pass at 5.2 kHz, softer), kept in this browser; a key played or pressed sounds in them too. The folded takes peek 440 px wide (380 cut the star), and a compact card's first column may shrink, so the votes are always in. The mock serves the vowels (only they) to the page's checks. Guide ×12 (line 165); suite-1624.part.

**The plan's count on the song's first step, the empty card gone, the cards' graphs gathered all the while** (1625; Viktor 20.10.2026: «После генерации Score не отображаются токены для быстрого чекапа. Прикрути, плиз, чтобы не исцезали на первом шаге»; «А что это за пустышка?»; «Графы видеокарт в серверном лог фрейме начинают рисоваться по факту разворота фрейма… правильнее рисовать их постоянно… И шаг увеличь с секунды до 2-х»). A song sung on the score the page planned for it carries that plan's count: its step 1 stands completed with the plan's tokens and seconds, said «planned first», instead of «Supplied score»; the engine's lines about a supplied score do not take it back, and it outlives an F5 (the page keeps it in the job's own record). The card under Chords that held only the VAE field, hidden since 1168, is hidden with it. The cards' graphs: the page asks every two seconds whether the log is folded or not, and draws at once what came in when the log unfolds or the Engine room shows; the lab's sampler (gpu_live.py) at two seconds, its 120 bars four minutes. Guide ×12 (line 63); suite-1625.part.

**Datasets as parquet** (1626; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Касательно датасетов… они в сжатом parquet. Я глянул на HF. Докодишь поддержку parquet и упаковку в этот формат? Так сэкономим место мы, и юзеры»; «Делай паркет после 1624, доустанавливай нужные библиотеки в .venv»). `lab/trainer/tracks.py`: a set's tracks read alike as NNN.wav or packed into tracks-NNNNN.parquet (a row a track: FLAC 24-bit with the WAV's very samples, the caption, the seconds, the samples' sha256; a row group a track, so one is read alone); packing reads every row back against the WAV before the WAVs go, unpacking the same the other way. On ten of Chonishvili's tracks FLAC is 21.7 % of the WAV, and the cache's tensors are the same bit for bit. The cache's builder, its sheets and its stems read through it (a tool that wants a file gets a copy unpacked for the while). A raw folder may hold a Hugging Face dataset's parquet shards: the Train room counts their rows from the footers, scans them as tracks (SHARD.parquet#ROW; the style from the genres and tags, ClassLabel numbers read as their names; the licence, instrumental and derivatives said per row) and prepares from them without unpacking the shards. POST /train/pack {name, undo} packs a set in the background; a set that is a link (on the Patriot) is refused, as writing there is. Mended on the way: preparing from a raw folder linked to the Patriot (1623) failed, its files resolving outside the link. `tools/pack-set.sh NAME [--unpack]` and `tools/fetch-hf-parquet.sh REPO NAME [FIRST [LAST]]` in bash; pyarrow 26 into .venv; lab/test_tracks.py all passed. One shard of benjamin-paine/free-music-archive-full fetched as datasets/raw/fma-probe (110 tracks, 511 MB): none of them allows commercial use, 46 allow no derivatives — the fine-tune's passport counts the licences.

**Lead and choir in the guide** (1627; Viktor 20.10.2026, from 20261020-170502: «Что в скобках на том же языке, и если в стиле указан choir backing, то практически всегда это поётся хором… Очень много воздуха теперь в ведущем голосе… три точки добавляют короткую паузу, однозначно. Я словил льва за ухо. Лирику разделять на ведущий и хор»; then: «(Choir: ...) не всегда срабатывает. ( более стабильна… с англоязычной лирикой может быть хикап… (Choir backing: ...) - не работает… Только два варианта - Choir: и просто в скобках лирика хора»). A bullet after Round brackets, ×12 (line 117): the lyrics split into the lead's lines and the choir's answers; with `female choir backing` and `solo lead` in the style a short line in round brackets in the song's language is mostly sung by the choir; plain brackets the steadiest, `(Choir: …)` not every time, `(Choir backing: …)` fails; English lyrics may slip; three dots a short pause; the length it may take.

**The take's Play/Pause in a column of its own** (1628; Viktor 20.10.2026: «В паспорте кнопка PLAY, ... зарезервировать это место, чтобы не прыгали иконки по полосе. По нажатию на ИГРАТЬ переводим в ПАУЗА, и не убираем. Немного уже, а то при сужении колонки кнопка FULL SCREEN прыгает на вторую строку… Второй строкой идёт тайтл с краткой инфой. Туда узкой колонкой кнопку Play/Pause. Так верхняя строка начнёт дышать»). The take's head: its buttons alone on the first row with ⇆ ⤡ at the right; on the second, ▶ 44 px wide (was 75) in a narrow column before the title, its numbers under the title beside it. The button never hides while a take is shown: pressed, it plays the take and turns into ⏸ Pause in the same place (aria-pressed, its tip «Pause»); pressed again it pauses, and a take paused in the player resumes from where it stood. Six old checks brought to it; suite-1628.part.

**Past the limits, and an adapter out of the score** (1629; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Ограничителям LoRA адаптеров… добавь кнопку/checkbox для оверрайда на случай, если хочется выкрутить ползунки выше автолимитов. Но в Партитуру мы всё равно шлём с лимитом. Также можно секинить адаптеры, которые вообще не должны участвовать в написании ABC, хотя у них есть AR, - но это идёт в синтез»; the Duduk, its wave high only in the intro). loras.js: a «Past the limits» box at the end of the budget's row (from one adapter on), kept with the page (yue2.loraFree): each slider's most lifted to 1.5 on the music half and 2 on the sound half, the together ones to 3.5 and 3, the zones kept as measured so the colours still show the red; Share and In order asleep, no budget sent. Unticked, a strength past its own most comes down to it. Right-click a row: «Out of the score», tagged *sung only*, said in the notes, sent with `noscore`. app.js planEased: the plan (Full's and an instrumental's) is made without the adapters out of the score and with the rest eased to the plan's fuse (1.0 together, 0.8 on IRAEv3), said in the log and a toast; the Score button eases only past the limits; the song is sung at the strengths set. And the roll's vowels (Viktor: «вокальные аоэ... LOL. Ты какой взял? Альто? Возьми низкий голос, контральто»): 1624 took each note from a different singer, so the voice changed under the hand; now one voice, VocalSet's female2, the darkest of its nine women (VocalSet has no contralto), twelve notes a vowel from C3 to A5, the notes under her own range tuned down (lab/sounds, vowels-1629.py, the sources in vowels-sources.tsv). suite-1629.part; the 1624 check to sixty. And found on its kitchen runs, the Creator's versions one at a time (heresy-versions.js): «Generate sends the chosen LoRAs» failed, nothing sent; not the load (a trace: the page's thread free) but the browser's six connections to the studio, all held by a burst of 116 keeps (examples loaded one after another, each keeping the form first, 1596) that the lab answers one by one, ~200 ms each; the song's request waited ~20 s behind them. The keeps now go on one connection, in the order asked, each still kept; a check fires thirty and wants a request beside them answered under half a second (it took 5 ms).

**The log by rooms, and the Trainer's bars under Engine** (1630; Viktor 20.10.2026: «В серверном логе вот как ты добавил вкладку Artist, добавь ещё вкладку с фильтрацией по Creator, Trainer, Refiner. При открытом Engine прогресс бары Тренера поверх идут и не скрываются как открытая консоль лога»). The dock's head has five views: Server, Creator, Trainer, Refiner, Artist. app.js logSrc gives each server line its room: the engine's own lines and the page's are the Creator's (regenerate and Whisper's timing too), a lab line the room of its kind (LoRA training, dataset, prepare: the Trainer; artwork: the Artist; stems, remaster, upscale, debuzz, the gateway, spectrum, the listener, refined, the DAW's mix: the Refiner; a card left for one, its room); the rooms' views are the server's lines filtered by CSS (data-only on the dock's body, data-src on each line), the folded line says the view's own last, a room with none says so, the view kept for each room. heresy-progress.js: the Trainer's bar steps aside while Engine is open (the strip repaints each second, so within one) and is back when it closes. The guide ×12 (line 481). suite-1630.part.

**The Trainer on cards** (1631; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Хорошо, заменяй TP на DDP. Отлично, тогда сможем и нечётные количества по картам гнать»; «по батчингу с градиент аккумуляцией допили в тренере… автоматизация без костлявых пальцев пользователей»; RIVALS 10, EMA and resume_from, «ОДОБРЯЮ в 2.0.0»). lab/trainer/train.py under torchrun: each card holds the whole model and learns its own tracks, the adapters' gradients averaged before each step (NCCL; gloo for a check of two on one card); accum tracks a card a step, the batch cards × accum, the learning rate × √batch, `step` still counting tracks; every save the moving average of the adapter (EMA over about ema_epochs, warmed up as timm's (1+n)/(10+n)), the held-out measure taken on it, the raw weights and the optimizer in resume.safetensors.raw and optimizer.pt; resume_from a save's step; checkpointing on (measured: without it a song of minutes wanted more than 24 GB), «auto» turns it on at the first step out of memory. The lab (training.py, lab.py): cards from the ones given to training, the freest first; the run's GPUs in the activity and on its card (GPU1+2). The page: **Cards** (1, 2, 3) at the end of the Trainer's knobs, its tip saying nothing else needs setting, kept in this browser; the question before the run says the cards. Checked on GPU0: one card and two (gloo) to the same adapter sum. The guide ×12: Cards, Memory (below 16 GB no fat ranks; 24 GB the measure), the moving average. 1642 and 1643 rode on it: a rank a half (rank_ar, rank_nar) and a track without the AR's lesson (no_ar in dataset.json), the AR's gradient the mean over its own tracks, the cards sending the same tensors.

**The bar** (1632; Viktor 20.10.2026, his picture: «Убери пожалуйста в баре бордюр из выбранного воркспейса. Лучше полужирным выделить… примени шрифтовой стиль вкладок комнат… Значок ВНИЗ тоже убери. Выровняй по центру между TRAINER и кружком активности… реверсивный бекграунд… чтобы утопал… справа в баре убери бордюры с кнопок… хайлайтятся только иконки и селектор языков. Бекграунд с кнопки выгрузки модели тоже убери. Достаточно свечения самой кнопки»). The workspace in hand: no frame, no caret, bold, the rooms' letters, on a sunk ground, its middle at the middle between the Trainer's pill and the lamp (app.js centreWs moves it by --ws-shift; with no room, where the flow put it). The bar's right buttons with no frame and no ground, the pointer lighting their icon; Unload, a model loaded, glows red with no ground. Found on its kitchen run: the take's head has a .head-actions too, and its Delete lost its frame; the rules are the bar's own (.topbar .head-actions), a check keeps it so.

**Add a LoRA, found** (1636; Viktor 20.10.2026: «Добавь в `Add a LoRA` блок Творца поиск/фильтр по ключевому слову… сейчас три колонки при 100% 1080p. Очень узко. Дай две колонки, а уже при приближении к 2K резлюшн 3-4 колонки. Раскраска левой полоской bootstrap по типам LoRA адаптеров… voice-* красить второй чёрточкой после базовой - разными цветами как в андроид контактах - по начальной литере имени абонента»; «ограничить высотой… 800px… в прокрутку»). loras.js: a search over each adapter's name, id, kind, halves, tag, trigger, note and about (every word must be found), the kinds as buttons that filter (voice, vocal, style, instrument, score; an adapter's kind read from its name and words), «N of M» while filtering; a stripe by kind at the tile's left, a speaker's voice (voice-…) a second one coloured by the first letter of the name; the grid at least 250 px a column (two at 1080p, three or four toward 2K), at most 800 px tall with its own scroll. The guide ×12 (the LoRAs bullet).

**The chapter look** (1637; Viktor 20.10.2026: «такие подзаголовки как `Budget` и другие по всей студии ... придумай красивый chapter style... чтобы было видно, что то раздел/опция/триггер/настройка… приятно, когда всё ассоциируется в интерфейсе»). The studio's chapter look is the .label of Profile, Planning mode, Chords, Text profile: small capitals, spaced, muted. The adapters' Budget and Music/Sound, together, plain words until now, take it; a check keeps them equal to .label. A survey of the other rooms' headings, and a richer chapter mark to choose from, wait for Viktor's eye.

**The Creator cards' bottom as their sides** (1645; Viktor 20.10.2026, his picture of Style and Lyrics: «Нижние паддинги со скрина очень большие. Уменьшить на 5+ px. Оставить такой же паддинг как и со сторон»). Measured: under Style and Lyrics 26 px against 16 at the sides; their last row (Saved styles, Text profile: .lib-row) added its 10 px margin to the card's padding. A .lib-row closing a Creator card keeps none: 16 px under it, as at the sides; a check measures every card. suite-1631/1632/1636/1637/1645.part, one kitchen run.

**The lyrics timed word by word** (1646; RIVALS 7, Viktor 20.10.2026: «ОДОБРЯЮ»; timoncool: «enhanced LRC with a timestamp on every word, aligned by Parakeet or Whisper. Your lyrics are kept; only the timing is borrowed»). lab/timing.py: align keeps each word's time (tt, a token's start and end, or none); lrc_words writes the enhanced LRC, «[mm:ss.xx]<mm:ss.xx>word <mm:ss.xx>word…», the lines as written: a word at the time Whisper heard its first token, an unheard word and a choir's (words) between their heard neighbours by their place, a guessed line (or one timed before 1646) its span shared evenly, never earlier than the word before. /timing?fmt=lrcw. A timing cached before is aligned again from Whisper's words already heard (no new pass): checked on a take of 101 lines timed on 16.10: every start as it was, the words now timed. The page: **⤓ .lrc (words)** beside .lrc and .srt in Refiner › Lyrics, its tip in twelve languages; the guide ×12 (line 281). lab/test_timing_words.py; suite-1646.part.

**Add a LoRA's kinds told right** (1647; found on the live studio after 1636). The Instrumental score adapter (its note «no vocals») and JPop (its note «melodic vocals») took the singing voice's stripe: the kind was read from the notes. A score adapter is now told first (its mode, or cot-full/abc in its name), a singing voice by its name alone (vocal, singer, gtsinger, alto…, sv-). suite-1647.part.

**Tags in every download** (1635 the lab, 1648 the page; RIVALS 8, Viktor 20.10.2026: «ОДОБРЯЮ для 2.0.0»). Checked at night: the FLAC carried its title and lyrics (Vorbis comments, build/src/flac-enc.h) but no cover; the MP3 and the WAV carried nothing. lab/file_tags.py (mutagen): the title, the lyrics, the style, the cover, the author (Content Credentials' own) and the studio in the file, a FLAC picture block or ID3 v2.4 frames in the MP3 and the WAV; a file it cannot tag goes out as it came (never the download for its tags); before C2PA signs, so the signature stays valid (checked on a live take). /lab/c2pa/file tags first, signs after; sign=0 gives the tags alone; X-Tags says what was done. The page (app.js c2paUrl): whenever the lab answered, a take's WAV, FLAC and MP3 go through it, with sign=0 when Content Credentials are off or cannot sign; no answer from the lab, the engine's own link as before. lab/test_file_tags.py; the 1489 check of signing off brought to it; suite-1648.part (the lab silent).

**A run its trainer called done is done** (1649; found at night 21.10.2026 on the voice queue: a run «ended: stopped · step 0» whose trainer's last line was «done: 19305 steps»). The lab's watcher read a step only from a progress line whose total was the run's planned steps; an early stop (1497) ends before them and the sound half's phase (1622) starts a bar of its own total, so the step stayed at the last match and the run was called stopped: thirty-nine voice runs since 16.10, every one of them done with its LoRA published. Now the trainer's own bar (its name first) whatever its total, and «done: N steps» makes the run done at step N; a run stopped by hand stays stopped. The thirty-nine run cards put right (fix-runs-1649.py, each run.json kept beside as run.json.bak-1649), and a sound-half run of 20.10 that still said «training».

**Twin tiles told apart** (1650; seen on the live Add a LoRA after 1636: two tiles both «Instrumental», the same adapter in a bf16 file and a full one, one title in sources.json). A tile whose name another tile shares (not its own music or sound half) says, small beside the name, what tells them apart: the precision its file name carries (bf16, fp16, fp32, q8_0…), else its size. YueLoras.twin for the checks; suite-1650.part.

**Extend…** (1651; RIVALS 1, Viktor 20.10.2026: «ОДОБРЯЮ»; timoncool: «Re-render composes on from the track's last frame by 15 seconds to 2 minutes»; the night's note: «Это SUNO-шный Extend, которым ты пользовался больше всего»). Beside Regenerate from… on the take and in its menu (Make again): every music code of the take kept (semantic_keep, 1037; the engine counts the kept part in the budget), the music goes on 0:15 to 2:00 past the end, new lines (if given, in a box) after the take's own; the length and the music half's max raised to the new total, its min past the kept part by three quarters of the extension, so it does go on; a take planned with a score goes on Direct (its score ends where the take ends; going on with the engine's abc_continue is the next step). The title says «· +0:40». The guide ×12 (Make again), eight strings ×11; suite-1651.part (the codes kept whole, the length, the min and max, Direct, the new lines, the title).

**Room under the Trainer's bars** (1652; seen at night on the live studio, two voice runs training). The bars stand over the folded Server log (1617, Viktor's place for them), but the rooms kept room for the log alone: in the Artist they covered Paint, the room's own button; in the Creator and the Trainer the last takes and runs went under them. heresy-progress.js keeps the bars' height on the page (--train-strip-room, nothing while they are gone); the takes' list and the runs' list take it under their last row; the Artist's form ends above the bars as above the folded log (1345b), measured again with each word of the lab's activity. suite-1652.part.

**Every room's column clear of the Trainer's bars** (1653; after 1652, seen on the live Writer: the bars over the Writing Room's last fields). While the bars stand, the page carries has-train-strip (roomFor, 1652) and every room's column (.col) ends with a spacer of their height after its last block; with no bars there is no spacer at all, the columns exactly as they were (checked: the Writer's 2667 px, 2753 with one bar, 2667 again). suite-1653.part.

**The workspace sunk softly by day** (1654; 1632 seen in daylight on the live bar: the press-in, black at 45 %, drew a frame round the workspace in a day theme, the frame Viktor had asked away; at night it reads as sunk). In a day theme (:root[data-tone=light]) the press-in is one faint inset of the ink; by night as 1632. suite-1654.part.

**The fine-tune heard in the studio** (1655; Viktor 21.10.2026: «По пробам файнтюна - выкладывай мне на прослушку прямо в Студию. Заведи для этого отдельное пространство»; «И нужна проба Фосфориды по моему последнему синтезу стиль и лирика. Это важно»). The Arc's A/B of YuE2-3B-Ruach-pilot1 (108 takes: the base; 1, 2 and 3 epochs at 1.0; the third at 0.75 and 0.5; three seeds, the same for each) copied into the library, the audio linked and the json its own, so a new title leaves the Arc's copy as the scorer read it. They form the workspace «🧪 Файнтюн · пилот 1», with a section per probe and mode (Дудук, Госпел, Рок · Direct, Full) and each take named for an ear («s1 · 3 эп ×0.5»): `heresy/MISC/claude-arc/to_studio.py`. Its section «Фосфорида v4.3.0.1» holds his last take beside the same request played again on the Arc: IRAEv2 Q8_0, his style, lyrics, planned score, seeds and four adapters, the take's own codes left out so each variant is made, not re-rendered. It is played without pilot1 and with it at 0.5 and 0.75, the pilot outside his budget: `song_probe.py`. 404.7 s of song took 1047 s on the B50.

**Intel Arc, as a beta** (1656; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Отличная новость по Arc. Конечно, выпускай бета поддержку для интеловских карт»).
- `build.sh --sycl` builds the engine with oneAPI's icpx into `build/build-sycl` and leaves the CUDA one in `build/build` as it is. icpx gets the newest GCC that has its libstdc++ headers: 13 on Ubuntu 24.04, whose GCC 14 has none. Built on the Forge in 157 s.
- `start.sh` runs it under oneAPI's runtime with `YUE2_ENGINE=sycl`, or by itself where there is a SYCL engine and no CUDA one. NVIDIA's guard and `GGML_BACKEND=CUDAn` are left out, and the card is named by `sycl-ls`.
- `SYCL_CACHE_PERSISTENT=0`: with the kernels' disk cache, libsycl 2026.1 dies at its first kernel, a fresh cache too (found 21.10 on the B50, 1639).
- Docs: INSTALL.md «Intel Arc (beta)», README's requirements, the guide ×12 under GPUs, CHANGELOG.
- The 13.10 refusal of going on from kept music on SYCL (`arc-beta-1418-engine.patch`) is not taken: it guarded against the cache, not the device.
- Measured on the Arc Pro B50 (16 GB) with the live engine, 30 s songs:
  - 43 s in BF16 (continuing after 10 s kept);
  - 52.6 s in Q8_0 plain;
  - 56 and 60 s with an adapter in Direct and Full;
  - Q5_K_M no faster than Q8_0.
  BF16 is the fastest copy on the Arc. The night's 186 probe takes and 108 A/B takes ran without a failure.

**Saves in tenths of an epoch** (1658; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Допиливай Тренера как мы договорились с DDP, десятыми эпох на сохранение»). The Trainer's Save every goes down to 0.1 (ten saves an epoch, as the night's pilot was run by hand); the lab saves every round(tracks × N) steps, at least one; a save at a tenth is named RUN-eNNN.T (RUN-e001.3) in loras/ and the run's list, a whole epoch as before (RUN-e001). DDP on cards stands as 1631.

**Pause, Resume and the runs by state** (1659; Viktor: «к тренеру прикрутить PAUSE/RESUME помимо STOP. Отдельный фильтр в колонку с ранами по состояниям»). Pause on a training run (its menu and its head): the lab's /train/pause, a stop the run is called paused by (the trainer saves on SIGINT, as for Stop); Resume on a paused run: /train/resume starts it again with the same set, knobs, steps and number of cards, and the trainer goes on from its last save (1631). Under Active/All/Archived/Trash, a row of states: Training, Paused, Done, Stopped, Failed, each with its count, one at a time, kept in this browser. The guide ×12 (Save every, the runs' cards), twelve strings ×11; suite-1658.part.

**The bar's logo on the song's beat** (1660; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Добавь на логотип Студии тот же бит при воспроизведении, как на играющей карточке в Библиотеке. Давай оживим хедер Студии во время воспроизведения мелодии»). While a song plays, the page is marked as sounding and the bar's logo pulses evenly (1.6 s); once the song's peaks are in (the rubato of 1118), the logo takes the same --beat as the sounding card: a breath of 2.5 % and an amber glow, and the bar's lower line lights by the same beat. Stopped, all of it goes; still for those who ask for less motion. The kitchen caught the beat's rule under the pulse's (specificity 0,3,0 against 0,3,1: the even pulse ran on and the glow never followed the beat); the beat's rule now stands at 0,4,1. suite-1660.part.

**A job may name its backbone** (1661): a request's "backbone" field, one of the names the engine was started with (--model); a job that names none sings on the Engine's choice. The worker switches between jobs, as settings_apply does (about a second on the Arc B50); an unknown name fails the job in words, naming the backbones the engine has. meta.json records the backbone that sang the take, request.json keeps the field. So the fine-tune's probes sing on the bare YuE2 beside the Studio's own backbone, on one engine. build-sycl/ in build/.gitignore. Tried on the B50 (a SYCL build): no field, Q8_0, no field again (switched back), and an unknown name (refused in words). `build.sh` (heresy/root) rebuilds the SYCL engine beside the CUDA one in the same pass (③b), so the studio on the Arc restarts on fresh code too; an install with a SYCL engine and no CUDA one goes the SYCL way by itself (the updater runs `./build.sh`), and restarts the studio only when it runs the SYCL engine. Tested 21.10 on the B50: a job naming Q8_0 sang on the bare YuE2, the next with no name back on IRAEv2, an unknown name failed in words; live on :7777 the same with BF16.

**Blind tests, and the first wave heard blind** (1664; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Нам не хватает "Blind Tests"»; «Нам нужна эта чесность, против самих себя братко!»; «все пробы на файнтюнах, на чекпоинтах первой волны - тот самый инструментал с короткой лирикой… если лирика начинает проседать - это первый звонок»). `blind_set.py`: a workspace's probe takes copied blind, each named only «№NN», shuffled in its section, its sound without the tags that name it, its request without the adapters and their strengths, no star (his marks on the labelled ones had come along), a random folder name; «🙈 Слепой · пилот 1», 108 takes. `blind_reveal.py`: his marks (👎 −1, 👍 +1, ⭐ +2, 👍⭐ +3) against the key, per section and in all. `fw_watch.sh` with `blind_add.py`: each save of a wave published through the lab (`/train/publish`, a hard link as RUN-e000.1) and heard on the Studio's own engine on the bare YuE2 (1661): `fw-probes.jsonl` (duduk with four lines of Lermontov, gospel with Amazing Grace, rock with Blake; out of copyright) in Direct and Full at 0.6, 0.75 and 0.9 shuffled as №1…, 120 s, the same seeds every save; every fifth save his Fosforida whole at 0.5…1.0 with his adapters left out; all of it blind in «🙈 Волна · RUN / <0.1 эп>», the key in `heresy/MISC/claude-arc/blind/RUN.json`. Tried on pilot1's third epoch: 6 takes in 227 s, blind as meant.

**The workspace in the bar, lighter** (1663; Viktor 21.10.2026, with his picture of «🙈 СЛЕПОЙ · ПИЛОТ 1 /…» cut short in the bar: «Пространство не красиво смотрится… расширь на 40px, убери грёбаный бекграунд, а сверху и снизу по 2px полосы такой градиентной, в центре 60% прозрачности по альфа каналу. Дугу делай базовым цветом темы. С текста убери п/ж, но оставь капс»). The badge is 40 px wider (280 px; centreWs's steps 240 and 150 still win when the bar is short of room: the kitchen caught the width outranking them at 1100 px, and the bar went two rows); no ground and no press-in by night or by day (1632's sunk look and 1654's day inset gone); a 2 px line in the theme's accent above and below, fading from 60 % in the middle (30 % with no workspace, whole under the pointer); capitals, not bold. 1632's and 1654's checks rewritten to say so; suite-1663.part.

**A LoRA by the song's sections** (1665; Viktor 20.10.2026, RIVALS 3: «LoRA по секциям — маска из лирики… голосовой адаптер только на куплеты, инструментальный только на проигрыш»: «ОДОБРЯЮ»). An adapter's wave has a fourth mode, Sections: the song's sections as chips; the adapter sounds in the ticked ones and rests in the others, with a 1.5 s ramp at each border. The borders come from the score's «% verse» blocks counted in bars at its metre and tempo when the form has a score (Full), else from the lyrics' tags, each as long as its lines (one with none, an intro or a solo, as four), over Song ≈ (else Output's length). The curve is worked out again from the form as it is at each Generate and kept with the draft. On his «Крик v4.3.0.1» score: 18 sections over 445 s, «verses only» 34 points. The guide ×12, five strings ×11; the 1457 check counts four editors now; suite-1665.part.

**The logo's fog** (1666; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Вижу в логотипе свечение. Слева и справа добавь больше пикселов тумана»): the beat's glow spreads sideways, a wide soft fog behind the logo 48 px past each end, as strong as the beat (an even breath while the song's peaks are not in), none for less motion. **The Librarian's counts break whole** (1667; his picture of «… 18 / GENERATED · 1624 IN THE LIBRARY»: «Переносы только блоками»): each piece of the counts' line keeps together. suite-1666.part.

**The Arc in Engine → GPUs** (1680; Viktor 21.10.2026: «В Engine>Hardware вообще реально прикрутить метрику Arc карт ка у нас с CUDA?»). The lab samples an Intel card from the kernel's own files, no xpu-smi: the busiest engine class from DRM fdinfo, resident vram0, the package temperature, the card's watts from hwmon's energy (the lab's half, live since the morning; studio_now() says the Arc when the Studio's engine is the SYCL one, so a Generate no longer waits behind a training run on GPU0). The page's half: Engine → GPUs lists each Intel card below the CUDA ones with its live graph; a Studio running on it is said so, its card marked, no «restart on GPU0» offered; the live graphs name it Arc0, Arc1… with its short name. Two strings ×11; suite-1680.part.

**A chapter looks like a chapter** (1683; Viktor 21.10.2026 on 1637's two looks, A as it was and B with a tick before each chapter: «B, но дай растворение на 40-60%, чтобы не было резкости на этих вертикальных полосках»). Every chapter heading of the studio (a label standing alone, not a field's caption: Profile, Planning mode, Chords, Style, Lyrics, Score, Key, Voice, Sampling, LoRA, Cards…, and the LoRA budget's and togethers' names; 25 in the page) has a 2 px tick before it in the theme's accent, dissolved: 40 % at its ends, 60 % in its middle, rounded, the words 9 px in. The 134 field captions keep none. CSS alone; suite-1683.part.
