**Planning mode and Chords, chapters too** (1684; caught by eye on the live page after 1683 shipped): the two headings stand inside the plan's fieldset, so 1683's «not a field's caption» took them for captions and gave them no tick. They are named in 1683's two rules, edited in place (no second copy of the gradient); every other field caption still has none. suite-1684.part.

**A workspace's sections fold** (1685; Viktor 21.10.2026: «В колонке воркспейсов в Библиотеке прикрути ручку для сворачивания подкатегорий»). In the Library's column a workspace with sections has a small handle in its row's left padding (▾ open, ▸ folded; the names do not move): a click folds its sections, deeper ones with them, out of the column, a second brings them back; the place in hand stays, and a parent folded over it shows a dim accent edge. The folded names are kept in this browser; a double click on the handle does not rename. Two strings ×11; suite-1685.part.

**The halves 1:2** (1668; Viktor 21.10.2026: «как правило, можно AR:NAR по умолчанию херачить в соотношении 1:2 (AR=16 / NAR=32 как пример)»). The Trainer's Rank is the sound half's; a new run given its rank alone trains the music half at half of it (4 at the least): the kinds' presets (32) now train 16 · 32. A run that names its halves through the API (1642) keeps them; a run that goes on keeps what it was trained with (its saves would not load otherwise). The field says so, goes up to 512, and the run's line says both (rank 16 · 32). The tip ×11; suite-1668.part.

**A run without fragments** (1669; Viktor 21.10.2026 on the first wave's ranks: «гоним r192/r384»). Measured on one 3090 with the wave's knobs and a sheet on every step (the longest sequences a run meets): r192/r384 ran out of memory at 23.3 GB, 3.17 GB of it reserved by PyTorch and never used; with the allocator in expandable segments it peaks at 21.1 GB, 3.7 s a step (r128/r256: 18.1 GB, 3.6 s). The lab starts every training run so (PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True); a small run loses nothing by it.

**The first card no longer carries the average** (1671, 1671b). The first wave fell twice on 22.10.2026, both times «CUDA out of memory» on GPU0 alone: r192/r384 at track 111, then r160/r320 at track 3057, 255 tracks before its first save. Under DDP the first card alone kept the moving average (1631): at r160/r320 that is 440 M weights in fp32, 1.76 GB the other cards don't carry, and 1669's measure on one card had no NCCL beside it. Now, under DDP, the average lives in the host's pinned memory: after each step the weights are copied there and the average follows them in one pass (`_foreach_lerp_`, eight threads lent for the pass, since torchrun gives each process one: 0.38 s a step in one thread, 0.12 s in eight). Measured: the pace the same as before (1.35 s a track), GPU0 at 21.7 GB instead of 23.3; the host's average matches the card's to 7e-7 relative after 400 steps; three cards at r160/r320 with the sheet on every step end with the same adapter on each. A single card keeps its average where it was. The first wave started again at 12:28 on the same set (`fw-relaunch.sh`).

**The Library's cards, look B** (1686; Viktor 21.10.2026: «одобряю карточки библиотеки твоим B предложением, но их фон сделай более прозрачным… Для ночной схемы может менее прозрачную»). A card's ground is the panel at 55 % by day and 82 % by night; a 1 px edge and a 3 px stripe on its left by its verdict (👍 green, ⭐ amber, 👎 red at 70 %, grey otherwise); a soft shadow, the chosen one an inset accent ring; a little more room inside, the title 14.5 px, ▶ in an accent ring, a hairline over the check and the likes; Refine and the badges outlined. CSS alone; 1474's day-wash check says look B now; suite-1686.part.

**The rooms' names, a line that glides** (1687; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Сверху никакой линии. Снизу 4 пиксела такая же, но жирнее… бегать по именам комнат перекатами… Активную комнату полужирным… Индикатор… ближе к лого, 10-15 пикселов от правого края»). The rooms have no ground and no pill; the room in hand is semibold (a hidden semibold copy keeps every name's width, so nothing shifts) and 1428's arch under it goes («АМЭН»); a 4 px line in 1663's gradient sits under the room in hand and glides to the room under the pointer, back when the pointer leaves (still for less motion). From 1660 px wide the engine's lamp stands 12 px right of the logo, and the Trainer is centred from the lamp's right edge (1329's checks say so now); the workspace takes the head's buttons as its right end. suite-1687.part.

**The workspace column under the Library's head** (1688; Viktor 21.10.2026: «В библиотеке колонку со списком воркспейсов нужно добить. Она до сих пор перекручивается с основным фреймом. Сделай так, чтобы она была фиксирована и имела собственную независимую прокрутку»). The Library's head sticks 18 px above the top and is 76 px high, and the column stuck at 8 px, so after a scroll All Workspaces and Favourites sat under the head. The column now sticks just under the head (its height and top, and 8 px), kept right when the head's counts wrap. suite-1688.part.

**The guide reads again after a failed read** (1670). The guide kept a failed read of its text for good, so one read that failed (the lab out for a moment, or in the kitchen the first-visit opening a minute after the start against a server with no guide) left F1 and ? dead until the page was loaded again; now the next open reads it again. Found because the suite's F1 check failed whenever the Forge was loaded enough to reach it more than a minute after the page's last load. suite-1670.part.

**The backbones in their lineage, the IRAE merges fetched** (1689; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Собирай IRAE* мёрджи, пушай их в IRA репо и прикручивай к загрузчику в наш грядущий код 2.0.0»). The bar's model menu orders the backbones by lineage: the stock YuE2, IRA, IRAE, IRAEv2, IRAEv3, IRAE-IR, the studio's own fine-tunes (RS-…) by name, then the rest; each by format (BF16, Q8_0, Q6_K_L, Q6_K, Q5_K_L, Q5_K_M), and a merge's name kept whole («YuE2 IRAE-IR BF16»). Engine › More models offers the IRAE merges beside the IRA merge: IRAE, IRAEv2, IRAEv3 at BF16, Q8_0 and Q6_K, each fetched by heresy/fetch-heresy.sh --irae <name>-<format> from goldhub/YuE2-3B-Ruach_Studio-IRA-Merge at its pin (e2e5c42, the IRAE family in, IRA's own files unchanged; the card credits monsterovich). suite-1689.part.

**The volume to 150 %** (1700; Viktor 21.10.2026: «через стопорный барьер усиление в промежутке 100-150? Постусиление»). The player's volume goes past 100 % to 150 %: up to 100 % the sound's own volume, past it a gain through Web Audio (made only at a hand's request, for the studio's own sound) with a limiter behind it while it boosts; a drag that starts below 100 % stops there, one that starts at it goes on, the keys step through. The track marks the stop and tints the boost; the boost is kept in this browser. The tip ×11; suite-1700.part.

**The locks lined up** (1701; Viktor 21.10.2026: «Замки выровняй, бро»). In the Library's workspace column the counts take a width of their own (four figures, right-aligned, tabular), so every 🔒 and ❄ stands at one x whatever the count. suite-1701.part.

**The guide for the evening's studio** (1702). A sentence at the end of five paragraphs, in all twelve guides: the player's volume to 150 % past its stop (1700); a card's stripe by its verdict (1686); a workspace's sections folded by their triangle and the column scrolling on its own under the room's head (1685, 1688); in Compute, the fetched merges standing in their line after m-a-p's; in More models, the IRA and IRAE merges with their authors (1689). check_guide: nothing new against the guides as they stood.

**The voice transposer** (1703; Viktor, RIVALS 4: «приделать транспозитор голоса на Vocal ряд партитуры… только в FULL режиме… предлагать сдвиг регистра в самой партитуре однократно»). Measured first on the Arc (bare BF16, Full, the wave's gospel and Jesus Freak probes with the Vocal an octave down, the voice split by htdemucs and its median pitch read): D4 → F3 and D♯4 → F3, so the model follows the score's Vocal octave. In the row of a chosen voice or singing adapter, ▼ ▲ move the score's Vocal an octave down or up, once, in the score itself (1028's own one-voice move, seven scale steps; its note says how many notes moved and the new range), shown only in Full with a score in the form. The tip and two labels ×11; suite-1703.part.

**The chord bed** (1704; Viktor 20.10.2026, RIVALS #2 «Chord bed — аккордовая подложка по секциям, модель пишет поверх: кость песни без нот»: «ОДОБРЯЮ»). In the own-score drawer, above the key's transposer, a row writes a score at once from a key (24), a meter (4/4, 3/4, 6/8, 2/4) and a tempo (both read from the style when it names them): every section of the lyrics gets bars of chords over rests in the Vocal voice and an empty Ins voice (two bars a line, in fours, sixteen at most; a progression for the verses, one for the choruses, one for a bridge; no two neighbouring bars on one chord), read back by the page's own parser before it is written; the plan mode turns to Full and the model writes the melody and the arrangement over that harmony. A score already there is replaced only on a yes; each of the three fields says what it takes and where it comes from. Measured first on the Arc: the model sings over a bed (the voiced share of the vocal stem 75 % against 79 % on its own plan for gospel, 34 % against 49 % for Flood). Ported from timoncool's YuE2-Studio (score/chord_bed.rs, MIT, Ilya Timonin; after gary4juce's yuey scaffold): the same progressions (15 major, 16 minor) and seeded generator. The strings ×11; suite-1704.part.

**Gold for the star** (1705; Viktor 22.10.2026 on the Library's cards: «Лайк стоит зелёный. Хорошо. Дизлайк - красный.. Тоже правильно. А вот на комбинацию звезды и лайка красится в красный, и сама звезда красится в красный. Дай этим комбинациям тёмно-золотой. И в ахуй звезды с пальчиком добавь ещё мелкий, но не токсичный золотой песок в бекграунд»; on the chord bed: «на скрине где поле с 96, высота не равная общему стилю»). The star is gold wherever it marks a favourite (the card, the player, the sheet, the passport, the takes' list), no more the theme's accent; a starred card's stripe is gold, a starred and liked card has a fine gold sand in its ground (still). The chord bed's tempo field stands as tall as its selects. CSS alone; suite-1705.part.

**Raw** (1706; Viktor 22.10.2026: «В карточках метку `created` замени на `raw / сырец`»). A take straight from the model wears «raw» (ru «сырец») where it wore «generated»: its badge on the card and the sheet, the Library's line of counts and the Filter's «Made» group, in all twelve languages; the kind's key stays «generated» (the catalog, the saved filters). The guides' Filter line ×12. suite-1706.part.

**Open in the Creator asks** (1707; Viktor 22.10.2026: «по опции контекстного меню у меня давно был диссонанс по "Открыть в Творце"… Предлагаю при таком переходе с карточки трека в комнату Творца попапить диалог со всеми опциями… И обязательно сохранение текущего перед перезаписью фрейма»; «что, если я хочу сравнить с версией на 10 правок в историю?»). «Open in the Creator» from another room asks what to bring into the Composer: just show it (as before), Reuse, Retake, Edit and re-render (with a score), only its style, only its lyrics; the form is kept as a version first, and the last answer is marked and remembered. The versions' window has «Compare with…»: the next plain click compares the two, as Ctrl/Shift+click did. Fifteen strings ×11; suite-1707.part.

**The rooms' grey lines, a quieter theme button, the model menus** (1709; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Для большей красоты подписей комнат в баре давай на неактивные тоже внизу полосочку, но меньше, серенькую… При наведении мыши на имя модели подсказку сделай уже, во-первых, 600px, и влево, чтобы не перекрывала список… В хедере кнопка переключения режима темы очень выделяется. Дай ей 40% прозрачности»). Under each room not in hand a short grey line, level with 1687's glide, that fades under the pointer; the day/night button at 60 %, whole on hover. The model menus (the bar's and the Engine's) open the studio's own list instead of the browser's; every model has one tip of one shape (its name and size, what the copy is, a merge's lines), 600 px wide to the left of the list, over the dialogs' veil. Nine strings ×11; suite-1709.part.

**IRAE-IRv2** (1708; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Для IRAE-IR предлагаю две версии. Вторую - силу опустить ниже 0.4 > 0.32»). IRAE-IR (Industrial Rock's music half at 0.4, the sound half at 0.6) and IRAE-IRv2 (0.32 / 0.6) stand in the models' line after IRAE-IR and in Engine › More models at BF16, Q8_0 and Q6_K, fetched by fetch-heresy.sh --irae from goldhub/YuE2-3B-Ruach_Studio-IRA-Merge at its pin 1cb090f; their words say what they are: monsterovich's Industrial Rock in them as well, experimental, highly unstable on lyrics, mostly for rock instrumentals.

**AB2 at 48 steps, the default** (1710; Viktor 22.10.2026, after the blind test AB2 against Midpoint, AB2 32 +3, AB2 64 +3, Midpoint 32 −3: «Да, ставь AB2 32 решателем, и во всех профилях замени также. А если в два раза дешевле, чем Midpoint, то давай лучше 48 шагов по умолчанию»). The page's default solver is Multistep (AB2) at 48 steps (24 network calls against Midpoint 32's 64), sent in every request now; the engine keeps its own defaults, so a Retake or Reuse of an older take comes back as it was made. Reset gives AB2 at 48; his three saved profiles and the built-in EAR profile moved to it; an open form at Midpoint 32 moved once. The steps' tip ×12. suite-1710.part.

**The chord bed paced as YuE2 paces a song** (1712; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Хорошая задумка, и она даёт свободу вокалу. Но ты перепроверь свои догадки, и это, конечно отдельным пунктом в наш мега-гайд Студии»). Measured: the sound follows the bed's chords closer than all 300 shuffles of them and than the same chords in the eleven other keys (gospel 0.82 against 0.67 / 0.77, Flood 0.74 against 0.57 / 0.58), and past the bed's end it keeps cycling the last progression. His «Крик» got 312 bars, 13:00, where YuE2's own plans of it run 400–440 s; now about two and a half seconds a sung line in pairs of bars (sixteen at most a section), a break five seconds' worth, an intro, outro, interlude or solo eight, «End» none; refrain and hook on the chorus's progression, a breakdown on the bridge's: «Крик» 254 bars at 2/4 69 (the model's own plan has 255). The guide ×12 has the chord bed as its own item. suite-1712.part.

**Crossfade in the playlist** (1713; RIVALS 11, Viktor 20.10.2026: «ОДОБРЯЮ с созданием функционала плейлиста. Сторонимся от слова РАДИО»). ⋈ just before Play on: 0 (one take after another, as before; the default), 2, 4, 6 or 8 s, kept in this browser. With Play on or Repeat all (never Repeat one), the take's last seconds go on in a second, hidden player that fades out while the next take comes in under them in the one player, which keeps the wave, the spectrum, the boost and the place kept. A take shorter than twice the crossfade plays whole; a pause, a refusal or a failed second player ends the crossing with the take as loud as the slider. The guide ×12 has it after the player. suite-1713.part.

**A take arranged anew from its sections** (1714; RIVALS 13, Viktor 20.10.2026: «Редактирование трека по операциям… ОДОБРЯЮ»; 22.10: «Гони 11 и 13… По операциям пункта 13 я понял теперь»). In the Edit row after Remove a section and in the take's menu: Repeat a section… plays the sections ticked twice, the copy right after them; Insert a part… puts a copy of one section at the start or after another. Cut on the score's bar lines (parts the score plans past the take's end left out, the last one cut at it), each join healed by the lab's crossfade (takeedit's `arrange`: the stretches in the order given, repeats allowed, half a second each at least), the score joined in the same order. A new take each time; the take itself stays. The guide ×12 has an Edit item with all five operations. suite-1714.part; the older checks of the take's menu (1483) and of the Edit block (1459) count the two new rows.

**A section sung once more** (1715; Viktor 22.10.2026, on 1714's Repeat a section: «гипотетически будет делать 1:1 клон. На музыке это ОК, а вот на лирике это не годится. В таких моментах нужно сид на +1 временно ставить, и проверить, как получается, чтобы была вариация в повторе, а не тупо клонирование куска секции»). «Sing it once more…» (⇉) in the wave's menu beside «Replace this section…», on a section heard by ear: the take's music codes kept to the section's end, the model goes on Direct (as Extend does) with the lyrics holding that section's lines twice and the take's own music seed + 1 (+ 2 for version B); the copy ends at a rest in the draft within 2 s of the section's own length, or at that length when nothing is quiet there (the job's log says which); then the take's own codes from the section's end on, all rendered once more with the take's own sound seed. The take grows by the section; its score gets the section's bars twice, its lyrics the lines twice; an ended take ends as much later. A kept start that comes back changed fails the job with that reason, nothing made from it. 1714's audio copy stays as the exact repeat. suite-1715.part.

**Compare loads ahead; the score's length said** (1716; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Попап сравнения так и не загружает треки заранее… Форсированную загрузку треков для сравнения, кеширование и прогресс бар, пока не загрузится, чтобы переключение было моментальным, а не с удалённой машины… ждать 10-20 секунд»; «в статистику партитуры в Композиторе добавь приблизительный просчёт длительности»; «В списке выбранных дублей мигалку сделай дополнительным столбцом, чтобы имена треков не передвигались вправо»; «Градиентную бегалку под именами комнат подними на 2px»; «я выбрал два одноимённых трека, и исчезла опция… а можно же в таком случае "Сравнить выбранные (до 6 треков)"»). The Compare window fetches every version whole as it opens, three at a time, the chosen one first, into memory, with a bar of the bytes and «N of M MB» until all are in (1621 fetched them one by one, unseen); the playing dot is a column of its own, so the names stay put; with two takes or more checked, the take's menu offers «Compare the selected…» (six at most). The score's check line says its length: «… 312 bars · ≈ 13:00 long · K sections» (it was there, bare). The rooms' gradient runner 2 px up, 1709's grey line with it. The guide ×12: the check line as the page says it, and the voice shift's measured way with a singer's register (an octave down, a female contralto came out a male tenor; Viktor: «Это - не баг, а не задокументированная фича»). suite-1716.part.

**Music before a repeat; an interlude written by the model** (1717; Viktor 22.10.2026 on the 1714 and 1715 probes: «Оно заработает, если будет музыкальный переход хоть 5-7 секунд. А так - это не годится»; «лирика сразу же вторглась, резко и брутально, ни секунды для передышки. И здесь тоже нужен микро проигрыш, который настроит лад для повторения»). «Sing it once more…» asks how the section comes back: straight on, after a breath of about 4 s, or after a short interlude of about 7 s; the wave's menu gains «Insert an interlude after it…» (about 5, 7 or 10 s, nothing sung again). The lyrics carry an [Interlude] block. The cuts follow the draft's own voice, split off on the processor (`lab/vocal_rest.py`: htdemucs_ft's vocal model alone, behind the training at nice 10 and six threads, about 0.9 s a second of sound) instead of 1715's loudness of the whole mix, which found no rest in a dense one; and they sit on the score's bar lines, so what goes in is whole bars and the take's own music after it stays on its beat. The model plays longer than asked (Flood: 10.5 s before its lines for 4 asked, 9.4 s when none was), so whole bars of its music come out of the middle, its first bar and its lead-in to the voice kept. A voice that cannot be split, or one still singing at the draft's end, fails the version with that reason. Probed on Flood: every seam in a rest of the final take's voice. suite-1717.part. 1717b: when no rest follows the lines and where they should end falls inside a sung line, the version fails with that reason instead of cutting the line.

**Re-sing this line** (1718; RIVALS 13, «перепеть одну строку»; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Жду закрытия всех долгов по Творцу»). «Re-sing this line…» in the wave's menu over a line heard by ear: that one line sung anew on a new music seed, its words changed or kept, the rest of the take as it was, through Replace a section's own job (1414), cut halfway between the line and its neighbours (the section's own cut at its ends), the lyrics with only that line changed. The guide ×12 has both in «Replace a Section». suite-1718.part.

**Match sections** (1719; RIVALS 9; Viktor 22.10.2026: «9 - Match sections с основной лентой»). In Cover or Remix › From a Recording, after the recording is transcribed (its score's sections named by SheetSage2), «Match sections» gives each block of the lyrics the tag of the score section it is sung in. The lab's Whisper hears where each block's first line begins in the recording (POST /lab/timing/recording: the recording kept in the cache by its hash, so it is heard once however often the lyrics change; a card through the guard, else the processor), and the blocks are matched to the score's sections. Ported from timoncool's YuE2-Studio score/section_match.rs (MIT, Ilya Timonin), after HOT-Step's Cover Studio: words never change; a block keeps its tag when it names the same kind of section; a block not heard, or one that starts in a section's last quarter, keeps its tag and place, unsure; a sung section with no block gets a copy of the last block of its kind; sections the voice does not sing get an empty tag. The proposal is shown, editable, before it goes into the form (the form as it was kept as a version first). The Rust tests' cases pass in the page. 1719b: the job keyed by the recording and its lyrics (the words heard once a recording all the same). The guide ×12 has it after «Cover or remix». suite-1719.part.

**A take's exact time** (1720; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Я в карточках треков не вижу точного времени. Добавь это, потому что только показывает как давно. Можно, чтобы не занимало лишнее место, время создания трека по идее флип карточки сделать. И в настройках интерфейса в Engine опцию для дефолта "Точное время создания трека" или "Как давно был создан трек". Дефолт пусть будет как есть сейчас - как давно»). A take's time, wherever it is shown (its card in Takes and in the Library, a pinned one, the peek, the sheet, the datasheet, the take's own line), is «how long ago» or its exact date and time, as Engine › Appearance › Take time says (how long ago by default; Default look resets it). On a card a click on the time flips it to the other for that take, and its tip always says the other. The guide ×12 says it under Appearance. suite-1720.part.

**Sing it once more opens on a breath** (1721; Viktor's ear on the 1717 probes, 22.10.2026: «Звездой пометил то, что на слух идеально. Лайк - хорошо, но не совершенно»). The verse sung again after about 4 s of music got ⭐ with both tags, «straight on» 👍, every cut made blind 👎; «Sing it once more…» now offers «After a breath» first when nothing was chosen before (a last answer is still remembered).

**The crossfade cuts nothing** (1722; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Кроссфейд пробовал на 4 и 6, Не работает как ты задумал. Обрезает кусок с конца текущего и с начала следующего трека»; «Кроссфейд на строке кнопок плеера очень широкий. Вообще убери стрелку»). 1713 faded the take's last seconds out and the next one's first seconds in from silence, on straight lines, so both ends were heard cut. Now the next take comes in whole, at its own level at once (a 0.15 s ramp against a click), as soon as the take playing goes quiet after its last loud moment (its wave's peaks: under −24 dB of its loud body), at most the crossfade's seconds before its end; only that quiet tail goes on under it in the second player, fading on a cosine; a take that ends loud crosses only its last half second, as does one with no wave known. The second player is made ready four seconds ahead (its file in, muted), so the crossing starts on time. The ⋈ picker loses its arrow and its width. The guide ×12 says it so. suite-1722.part.

**💎 Keys Probe in the bucket** (1723; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Да, выкладывай `💎 Keys Probe`»). The keys probe (1475: one folk ballad, female voice, no adapters, in every key, as the planner wrote and moved it) is a 💎 set of its own in the bucket, folder keys-probe, among the lab's sources (diamond.py), so the Librarian's «💎 From Hugging Face…» offers it like the others.

**The Kit's licence and notice** (1725; IronWolve, 22.10.2026, in yue2-kit issue #2: «The original yue2-kit contributions are now licensed under Apache License 2.0… Please include the LICENSE and preserve the applicable attribution in NOTICE, crediting SeattleSysop (IronWolve), as required by section 4»). The release carries the Kit's licence text (`LICENSES/yue2-kit-Apache-2.0.txt`, the Apache 2.0 text word for word) and a `NOTICE` with the Kit's own notice kept word for word; the README credits SeattleSysop (IronWolve) and says the Kit's original contributions are under Apache 2.0 since its commit 5df6eaa, instead of «without a licence of its own»; make-release ships NOTICE and LICENSES/. Nothing on GitHub until the next release.

**Compare: star and dislike, twelve takes, a note** (1724; Viktor 22.10.2026, on the Compare window: «В сравнении при дизлайке это 100%, что звезда не снимается»; «Сделай умолчанием "Скрыть 👎"»; «Для сравнения можно смело 12 вместо 6»; «В сравнение последней после звезды добавь иконку заметки. Тут как раз и нужно комментировать трек»; «"Ровная громкость" перенеси вперёд в полосе после времени с отступом в 20px»; «Два раза время трека в полосе. Лучше объединить ТЕКУЩЕЕ/ОБЩЕЕ»). A dislike takes the star off and a star the dislike; the star is set, not toggled, and painted as asked at once (a slow answer no longer flips it twice). «Hide disliked» is on whenever the window opens (unticked, for that window only). Up to twelve takes. A note button after the star writes the take's note as its card's menu does and lights when there is one. The bar: one time, now / length, «Equal loudness» right after it. While a dialog is up, the window's keys rest. suite-1724.part; the older compare check (1425) no longer expects the unticking kept.

**The star's sand as grain** (1726; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Песок на карточке имеет предсказуемый паттерн. Смотрится дёшево»). 1705's four lattices of dots (29, 37, 53 and 61 px tiles) showed their period at once; the ⭐+👍 card's sand is now one SVG of fractal noise (feTurbulence) keeping only its brightest grains in the star's gold, a 320 px tile, each Library card starting it at its own place (a hash of its name), the corner wash as before. 1705's check knows the grain.

**The take's menu: Edit, then Regenerate; the crossfade gone** (1727; Viktor 22.10.2026: «из раздела меню "сделать ещё раз" можно в "править" перенести несколько пунктов, таких как "Продлить", "Заменить на", "Нарастание". Это же реально правка трека, а не перегенерация. И тот пункт меню "Сделать ещё раз" переименовать в "Перегенерить", спустить под "Править", оба перенести выше блока Библиотека как отдельную секцию. И пройдись по контекстному меню… не дублируется ли под разными именами»; «удалить тот кроссфейд, что ты попытался сочинить. Он тупо не работает»). Edit (Crop, Remove a section, Repeat a section, Insert a part, Speed; then Extend, End at, Fade in, Trim to the text) and under it Regenerate (Regenerate with a new seed, Retake, Reuse, Re-render sound, Regenerate from, Edit and re-render) stand right after the wave's own items, before the Library's block. «Transcribe this take» was the datasheet's «Write it from the sound» under another name: it is that name now, in the Refiner's group. ⋈ leaves the player's bar; its code sleeps (0 s) for the playlist to come. Engine › About credits the Kit with its Apache 2.0 licence and thanks. The older menu checks (1483, 1488) read the new groups; 1713's and 1722's crossfade sections leave the suite; the guide ×12 loses its Crossfade item and says where Regenerate with a new seed stands now.

**The star's sand as faded gold blots** (1728; Viktor 22.10.2026, on 1726's grain: «Песок сделай гуще. Там сейчас на глаз меньше 5%. 20-25% смело, со скоплениями/пятнышками, пусть выглядит как выцветшие золотые кляксы»). A soft low-frequency noise picks about a fifth to a quarter of the ⭐+👍 card, a faint gold wash lies in it and the fine grain gathers inside it, nothing outside; a 480 px tile, each card from its own place.

**The take's wave over its edits** (1729; Viktor 22.10.2026: «По всем редакторским командам из контекстного меню предлагаю для визуальной ориентации по таймлайну трека вставлять его же волну и тегировать по волне донора и грядущей»). Each edit dialog of the take's menu shows the take's own wave under its words, its regions marked by kind (the donor amber, the place it goes patina, a cut red, what stays green), each with its word: Crop (what stays and goes), Remove a section, Repeat a section (the copied stretch and its copy after it), Insert a part (the part under the pointer, then where it goes in), Sing it once more and Insert an interlude (the section and what the model adds, following the breath or length chosen), Re-sing this line (the line). The marks follow the row under the pointer or in focus, and the ticks of a pick. HeresyDialog's confirm, prompt, choose and pick take a wave. The guide ×12 says it under Edit. suite-1729.part.

**The playlist** (1730, 1730b, 1731; Viktor 22.10.2026: «В библиотеке в воркспейсах в самом верху левой колонки создаём воркспейс Playlist. Этот воркспейс вечный… Ручная сортировка, перетаскивание композиций по списку. Удаление из плейлиста не затрагивает саму композицию/трек»; «В контекст. меню добавляем "Добавить в плейлист > Первым, Последним, Рандомно"»; «Вместо кнопки кроссфейда… кнопку для вызова модального окна с плейлистом»; «при активном плейлисте добавляем нижнюю полоску тонкую в плеер… В левой предыдущий трек, в правой — грядущий. Имена кликабельные»). The lab keeps one ordered list of takes apart from the workspaces, in user/collection.json, with its crossfade (0–4 s, kept for 1732): add first, last or at random (a take already in it moves), remove (the take itself untouched), the whole order as dragged (compared with its takes still in the library, 1730b, so a take deleted while in it never locks the order), a deck's shuffle. The Librarian's «▶ Playlist» stands first among the places, never renamed or deleted, with its own view: Play from the top, Shuffle the deck, and 720 px cards in three rows (title; when, length, plan; the marks as icons), play first, the cover last; dragged into order, × takes a card out, a right click opens the take's menu. The take's menu has «Add to playlist ›» First, Last, Random (a checked batch too). The player's ☰ opens the same view in a window over it; while the playlist plays, a strip under the player says the previous take, «Playlist · N / M» and the next, the names clickable, and the playlist plays on whatever Play on says. The guide ×12 says it under the Librarian. suite-1731.part.

**Section tags stay as they are** (1732; Viktor 22.10.2026, with his picture of «Повторить секцию» showing «припев»: «В таких моментах как редактирование и т.п., держи все секции на английском… пусть будет CHORUS. Ни на один язык не переводим служебные теги, что у нас в промпте лирики идут в [...]»). The eleven catalogs held five tag words (chorus, pre-chorus, post-chorus, «verse + bridge», interlude): 55 entries out; the edit dialogs' section rows (Crop, Remove, Repeat, Insert and the rest) carry translate="no", as the choose window, the wave's card and the tag completion already did. suite-1732.part.

**The workspace column scrolls on its own** (1733; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Ты так и не зафиксил независимую прокрутку в колонке областей. Она до сих пор тянется подвалом страницы»). Scrolled to its end, the room pushed the sticky column up for a moment; its height was measured there, so it grew by as much, and each batch of cards loaded at the end added more (under the mock at 2560 × 1440: 1172 → 1271 → 1568 px, its foot 378 px under the player). Now it is measured from where the column sticks, its foot capped at the room's, and the room's end no longer pushes it: at 2560 × 1440 and 1920 × 1080, at the top, half way, the end and five loads after, its foot is always 10 px above the player. suite-1733.part.

**The wave's section items, said where they can't act** (1734; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Хоть убей, но не вижу в конт. меню… «Спеть ещё раз…», «Вставить проигрыш после неё…», «Перепеть эту строку…», «Заменить эту секцию…». Либо названия не те, либо я не там смотрю. Пробую прямо над маркером секции над волной»). Replace this section, Re-sing this line, Sing it once more and Insert an interlude stay in the player wave's menu where they cannot act (over INTRO or OUTRO, an instrumental part, a guessed line, the score's marks, the ear switched off), greyed and saying why on hover or click; the menu stays open. A muted item (heresy-menu.js) keeps its hint, which a disabled button cannot show. suite-1734.part.

**No rule over Seeds** (1735; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Над СИДЫ в блоке Семплинга лишняя полоса и лишнее место. Убери её»). The drawer's first block has no separator; the rules between the blocks below stay. suite-1735.part.

**The Trainer's epochs with their tenths; the held-out best** (1736; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Смотрю в Тренере, 0.6 и 0.7 не показывает лучшими зелёным»; «Мы изначально не предусмотрели дробные. Откорректируй это в коде, чтобы писались метки чекпоинтов правильно, с их десятичными»). The chips, the run's card and the epoch dialog say the tenths (e0.7, e1.3; a whole epoch without a point); the green marks now come from the held-out measure (the lowest flow, and the lowest ar_ce when it is another save), with a tile «held-out best …», instead of 1093's guess off the training curves; a run with no held-out measure keeps the guess, said so. suite-1736.part.

**The playlist's crossfade** (1737; Viktor 22.10.2026: «кроссфейд именно в плейлисте/воркспейсе выставляем ползунком от нуля до 4 секунд… при PREV и NEXT кроссфейд нулевой»; «грядущий трек не кешируется»; «Если нет, то к хуям этот кроссфейд»). 1713/1722's crossing wakes in the playlist alone, at the playlist's own 0–4 s: the slider is back in its head and the strip says «⋈ N s». Why 1722 cut the end of one take and the start of the next: its second player sought far into a file over the line for the tail, and the next take was fetched at the seam. Now, while a take of the playlist plays with a crossfade set, it and the next one are held whole in the page (at most three, never one still playing), the player and the second one play from them, and the next take starts at its very beginning; Prev and Next cross nothing; outside the playlist nothing is fetched ahead and the crossfade is 0. The guide ×12 says it with the playlist. suite-1737.part.

**Mono Maker, Peak glue, True-peak ceiling** (1738; Viktor 22.10.2026, on Shaumarov's «TOP plugins of 2026» read for the Refiner: «Берём это всё, но как у нас всё в нодах, так и выстраиваем, новые ноды, рекомендуемые этим звукотехнарём, с его дефолтными пресетами»). Three of the studio's own steps (lab/builtin_steps/ruach) on Plenio's DSP, in the Finish group: Mono Maker (the side under 120 Hz taken out, zero-phase, the mid as it was; 80–200 Hz, an amount), Peak glue (his preparing compressor: the loud moments shaved by the dB asked, 3 by default, the loudness kept) and True-peak ceiling (the last step: an oversampled limiter at −0.5 dBTP at the file's own loudness, or driven to −14, −11 or −9 LUFS). On his КРИК: the lows' side 18.2 % → 2.0 %; 3.0 dB off the loud moments; true peak +1.25 → −0.59 dBTP at the same loudness. lab/test_mixtools.py on made sounds, all green. The guide ×12 has them as steps 13–15.

**The Trainer's run filters as icons** (1739; Viktor 22.10.2026: «Если всё в SVG иконки можешь превратить — гони огонь. Высоты элементов в общий стиль»). The Runs column's views and states are icon chips of one height (28 px) in two even rows, their words in their tips; «Done» no longer turns into a check with its count fallen under it (1593's icon button had caught it). suite-1739.part.

**The spectrum's views** (1740; Viktor 22.10.2026: «добавь в визуализацию спектрограмм и в плеере и в Огранщике переключатели всех возможных видов: моно усреднение дефолтное, Mid/Side рядом с Log/Linear, и стерео… в две строки, и… единый спектр с проявкой разниц»). The player's scope and the Refiner's Spectrum have Mono (the default, as before), L/R and Mid/Side in two rows, and Pan, one spectrum coloured by where each part stands; the choice is shared and kept. The lab's spectrum_job.py makes each view (mono byte for byte the old file), cached apart, GET /spectrum &mode=. suite-1740.part.

**The player mirrored around its centre** (1741; Viktor 22.10.2026, night, his ruler over the player: «Укороти описаловку слева в семпле… разворачивать строку при наведении, скажем, до 800px. Никаких попап тултипов здесь… В правильном 100% скейле 1080p выравниваем дистанцию слева и справа к центру. Всё, что слева, укорачиваем. Зеркальная точность»). Measured at 1920 × 1080, the controls centred at 960, the left group stopped 65 px short of them and the right one began 181 px after. Now the left group takes the right group's visible width, so the gaps mirror within a pixel (kept as the window, the time or the hidden controls change), and the style line unfolds on hover up to 800 px, with no tip. 1741b: the two-row player's song name wraps to two lines at most, as the one-row player's did (it had been cut since 3401, its old width hiding it). suite-1741.part.

**Blurred pastel blots for every card** (1742; Viktor 22.10.2026, night: «на твои золотые SVG кляксы добавь нехилый блюр. И если хочешь, гони их все динамично на все типы состояний карточек, в разных пастельных цветах, в блюре. Это будет органично и неповторимо»). 1728's blots for every state of a Librarian's card, blurred hard, in pastels: gold for a star and a like (keeping 1728's wash), a paler gold for a star, green for a like, rose for a dislike, grey-blue for none; lighter by night; each card from its own place. suite-1742.part.

**Full sings an octave lower by default** (1743, the page; Viktor 22.10.2026, night: «А что, если мы будем автоматом в FULL режиме херачить октавой ниже? И в `Engine > Creator Heresy` дадим настройку по октавам?»). A new Engine tab, Creator Heresy, sets the octaves a score the model plans whole in Full moves its Vocal line before it is sung: −1 by default, −2 to +2; a score given or opened is sung as written, and the instruments never move. The request carries vocal_octave; the take's Score Planner column says the Vocal line's octaves. The engine's side (yue2.cpp's vocal_octave, matched on 240 library scores to the page's own shift) is live since 23.10.2026 02:56 (build 9b168cc, the SYCL and CUDA engines built beside the running Studio, which restarted onto them between the probe watcher's jobs). suite-1743.part.

**The page opens when everything has come** (1745; Viktor 23.10.2026, on the road, with a screenshot of the Studio half drawn: «Перепроверь Loader. Открываем страницу Студии только после того как всё загрузилось, а не как на скрине при медленном соединении. Сейчас сеть какая-то гнилая»). The veil lifted at twenty seconds whatever had come (1435), and «ready» never waited for the load event or the web fonts; on a slow road the page showed with the fallback font and its icons unpainted. Now it lifts when the page has loaded, the fonts are in, the room is set and the words are translated: ten seconds at the least, a sweeping line after twenty, and two minutes at the most. Checked at about 3.5 Mbit/s: the old page lifted at 20.6 s still loading, the new one only after its load. suite-1745.part. 1745c: the live check still saw a face loading at the lift: the wave's section marks (IBM Plex Mono, drawn on its canvas only once a take is in), and any role picked in the Fonts card or a language's own letters can do the same. Now at the load event the page asks the faces of all six font roles at 400 and 700 (with the language's letters), waits for them, and holds while any face is still loading.

**The player holds a take whole** (1746; Viktor 23.10.2026, on the road: «в плеер Студии 100% кеширование, иначе рвёт трек на проигрыше ужасно при херовом моём соединении»). Every take is fetched whole into the page before it plays (1737 held only the playlist's). On a fast line it comes within 1.2 s and plays from the page; on a slow one the stream gives its length and wave, ▶ waits with the bar saying «NN %», and the whole takes the stream's place at the same moment and play state when it lands. The next take of the list is fetched while one plays; eight takes or 450 MB are kept, never the one playing. suite-1746.part; the suite reads a take's own address (suite-fix-1746).

**Composed texts in the page's language** (1672; TODO 2.0.0 #5, «all strings in twelve languages»). The page's English harvested over the whole suite (15 499 texts, 23.10.2026), its half-translated ones read by hand: three places where a broad pattern swallowed a text the page composed in English whose parts each have their key. The Trainer's «…is missing {0}:» joined the names first, so its «{0}» was a list no key knows: each name is now in the page's language before the join. The Writer's OpenRouter line (model · price · context) was not painted again when the language changed, so «{0} context» kept the rest English: it is painted on the change now. The instrument sheet's notes are the probes' own words, with no keys; a pattern turned their «of» into «de»: they are kept as written. The other half-translated texts were a moment between the language's walk and the repaint that follows it, or the mock's own data. suite-1672.part. 1673: «{0} MB» and «Compare with…» stood twice in all eleven catalogs; in a script's object the later one wins, so the earlier was dead text (and in ja and zh it said another thing): the earlier goes, the later, the one the page shows, stays.

**Voice tamer, Voice attack and body, Bass under the kick, Details up** (1748–1751; Viktor 23.10.2026: «Идеи Шаумарова обязательно, но не как закон»). The rest of Shaumarov's tools as the Refiner's own steps on numpy and scipy, tuned to the Refiner's motto rather than the plugins' presets. The tamer lowers what rises over the voice's own colour at that moment, from 2.5 kHz up by default (a singer's formants stay: on Krik and Buratino a band from 1.2 kHz took 2–3 dB out of the words), never notching an overtone or shortening an «s»; attack and body raise the voice's beginnings and its dying away a little, capped under full scale sample by sample; the bass's low makes room under each kick found on the drum stem; and a three-band upward lift at 10 % brings up the detail under the loud parts at the same loudness. In a set of eight the voice steps take the lead and backing vocals only, as the DAW does. lab/test_refiner_nodes.py on made sounds; measured on Krik's eight stems (the tamer 1.1 dB off 2.5–6 kHz in the mix, mostly at 4.7 kHz; 195 kicks, the bass's low −2.3 dB under them). The guide ×12 has them as steps 16–19.

**The learning rate honest, with a fade** (1752; Viktor 23.10.2026, evening: «у нас LR на обучении первой Волны не слишком низкий?»; then «делай затухание на 3.5+4.0, и впиши это не только в код, но сделай настройкой и в UI Тренера»). The trainer set its rate once, when the optimizer was made, and a run gone on from a save took the rate saved with it whatever its config said, while the progress line printed the config's: a changed rate was a number in the log and nothing in the training. Now both halves take their rate from the config before every step (the sound half at the rate × √cards, the music half at its multiplier of it) and the line prints the rate the step used. The Trainer has two more knobs after Learning rate: «LR fade, epochs» (the run's last so many epochs, 0 none) and «LR at the end, %» (where the line ends, 0 by default); the fade's start is found from the run's own length, so a run gone on further fades at its new end. «Go on to more epochs» now carries every option the run was made with: it named eight before, and the lab's defaults silently took the rest (three cards became one, the moving average's span 1.0 instead of 0.25, the held-out set lost). The first wave fades over 3.5 → 4.0 epochs. lab/test_lr_tail.py; suite-1752.part; the guide ×12 says it after Learning rate.

**The DAW, a sound engineer's pass** (1753–1756; Viktor 23.10.2026: «пройдись по нашему DAW как профессиональный звукарь и всё перепроверь, удобство и фичерсет. Чего не хватает, дописывай»). The DAW's head row is one height (its controls measured at 20, 24, 28, 30 and 35 px before). A solo is shown in the head («S · n», how many lanes are soloed), and Alt+click on any S clears them all: a forgotten solo made Mix render one lane. The reference is the set's own source, matched in loudness to the mix over the stretch being played (the lab's /daw/loudness gives its K-weighted 100 ms blocks; /daw/chunk with strip=source plays it); with it a gate fault mended: a block of digital silence read as null loudness, and null passed «> −70» in JavaScript, so silence counted in the master's integrated loudness. The song gets a fade in, a fade out and «Ends at» on the master after its fader, linear in dB from −60, drawn dark over the lanes, in the render too. The DAWproject export does not carry the fade (no DAWproject concept we map). lab/test_daw_fade_ref.py and the six older test_daw*.py; suite-1753.part; the guide ×12 after «Moving and copying».

**A window sung anew with adapters of its own** (1757; TODO 6а, Viktor 15.10.2026: «растяжка до и после выбранного сегмента, в окне свои адаптеры, силы и волны»; «на волне плеера выбирается окно»). «Sing a window anew…» in the player's wave menu, anywhere on the wave: no sections heard by ear are needed (the section under the pointer is the first guess, else ten seconds round it). Its dialog lays the window on the take's wave by a drag (its edges move), with ramps before and after it (0–10 s, 2 s at first) drawn fading; the window's adapters (the take's own at first, eight at most), each with its music and sound strength and its wave over the window (flat, arch, rise, fall); the lyrics; one version or two. The lab (section.window, POST /section/window) keeps the take's codes up to the ramp before, sings on with the take's adapters fading out over that ramp and the window's fading in, the window's alone over the window and back over the ramp after; the draft's codes up to the end of that ramp are joined with the take's own, then one replay of the whole stream on the take's sound seed with the same waves (the sound half follows a wave frame by frame), so the rest of the take sounds as it did. Each version is a take of its own, a child of the take, marked as Replace a section's are (replaced.json, kind «window»). A take whose own adapters ride a wave of their own is refused for now, and said. Replace a section (1414) is untouched. lab/test_section_window.py (a fake engine, 22 checks); suite-1757.part; the guide ×12 at the end of «Replace a Section»; sixteen keys in each of the eleven catalogs (ru, uk, be: «растяжка» for the ramps, his word).

**A value typed in the DAW's console** (1758; Viktor 23.10.2026: «пройдись по нашему DAW как профессиональный звукарь… Чего не хватает, дописывай»). As a desk's numbers are: a click on a knob's number or a fader's, or a digit typed while a knob or a fader has the focus, opens a small field over the number. Enter sets the value in the knob's own words: dB; Hz with «k» for thousands; ms, with «s» for seconds; %; the ratio (4:1); L30, R30 or C for the pan; −∞ or dB for a send (kept as an amplitude). Esc, or what cannot be read, leaves the value as it was; one undo a value. The console's hint says it («…; a click on a number, or a digit, to type one»). suite-1758.part; the guide ×12 in «Knobs and faders», before Ctrl+Z; two keys in each of the eleven catalogs.

**Parallel compression: a compressor's Blend** (1759; Viktor 23.10.2026: «пройдись по нашему DAW как профессиональный звукарь… Чего не хватает, дописывай»). A compressor gets «mix» (0–1, 1 as before): the compressed sound with its make-up blended with the dry one, dry × (1 − mix) + wet × mix, on a strip, a bus and the master alike. The page's kernel blends its gain the same way ((1 − mix) + mix × its own), so what is heard is what Mix renders: lab/test_daw_parallel.py runs heresy-mixdsp.js under node and finds the page a cell later and equal to float precision. At 1, or unsaid, nothing changes to the sample. The console: a seventh knob, «Blend», in the compressor's devices (four columns now), a preset «Parallel» (−30 dB, 6:1, 5 ms, 120 ms, knee 0, +8 dB, Blend 40 %), the curve drawn blended; the report says comp_mix. suite-1759.part; the guide ×12 at the end of the Devices paragraph; two keys in each of the eleven catalogs.

**A strip's gate** (1760; Viktor 23.10.2026: «пройдись по нашему DAW как профессиональный звукарь… Чего не хватает, дописывай»). A downward expander against what the separation leaves between the phrases (the cymbals in the voice, the voice in «Other»): threshold, ratio, range, attack and release, in the compressor's own 1 ms cells, listening to the strip after its EQ (when the compressor is keyed to another strip, the gate still listens to its own); below the threshold the level goes down by the ratio, never more than the range; its gain multiplies the compressor's and its blend. The lab's render (daw_mix gate_env) and the page's kernel run the same cells: lab/test_daw_gate.py finds them equal to float precision. A third slot, «Gate», between EQ and Comp on a strip, and its card in the devices; one insert height on strips, buses, VCAs and returns, so the faders stay in a row. suite-1760.part; the guide ×12; ten keys in each of the eleven catalogs.

**The lab's reload waits for its own threads** (1790). /health's «jobs» counted the lab's job table and a weights download (1289), not the threads of section.py, a 💎 fetch or a starter set's download; the reload that waits for an empty lab (reload-when-idle.sh) restarted it under them. At 21:11:34 on 23.10.2026 the 1759 reload cut the fork's live window (1757): its draft done, its replay not yet sent. Each running one is now a job in /health.

**The returns' pre-delay and cuts** (1762; Viktor 23.10.2026: «пройдись по нашему DAW как профессиональный звукарь… Чего не хватает, дописывай»). The room gets a pre-delay of 0–250 ms on top of its preset's; both returns get a low cut (20–2000 Hz) and a high cut (1–20 kHz) on the wet, Plenio's RBJ bands at Q 1/√2, 20 Hz and 20 kHz meaning Off (said in the value only when on; «off» may be typed). The delay's cuts sit after its loop. The page's graph: input · delay · convolver · two biquads · return, a cut that is off a 0 dB bell; the lab's render the same (lab/test_daw_returns.py, 12 checks; the suite reads the biquads' response in Chrome: a 300 Hz high-pass at 60 Hz −27.97 dB against −27.968 computed). Six keys in each of the eleven catalogs; the guide ×12.

**LRA and PLR on the master** (1763). The loudness range from the short-term blocks since play, as Plenio counts it (7.26 against Plenio's 7.259 on a synthetic minute), and the peak-to-loudness ratio, the true peak over the integrated loudness; in the master's devices only, so the console strip keeps its height. «Mixed» says the render's LRA too, which the lab's report had. Two keys in each catalog; the guide ×12.

**Strip names whole** (1764). «.mc-name { font: 600 12px/1.25 inherit }» was an invalid declaration (inherit cannot be the family in the shorthand), so it was dropped and the names drew at the page's size: «Lead Voc…» cut. Now longhands, the strip's whole width; a name that does not fit goes to 11 px, then 10 px, then two lines at 10 px in the same 19 px, so the faders stay in a row. Seven more DAW rules carry the same invalid shorthand (.mx-print, .mx-ref, .mc-tab, .mc-chip, .mc-monb, .mc-addvca, .mx-verb); they are left as they have always looked, for Viktor's word.

**The mp3 tip at any rate** (1791; TODO 2.0.0 #5, all strings in twelve languages). The harvest of 23.10.2026 at 22:50 over the whole kitchen suite after 1753–1764 saw 15 731 texts; 87 were new since the 17:10 harvest by their text, 17 by their shape (digits masked), and one of those was a miss: the take row's mp3 link says «Download MP3 (N kbps)» in its tip, N the rate chosen (128, 192, 256 or 320), and the catalogs knew only the whole text at 320, so at any other rate the tip stayed English in every language. A pattern «Download MP3 ({0} kbps)» in each of the eleven catalogs, made from its own words at 320. The rest of the 17: translated already (1672's trainer list and OpenRouter line), the mock's data, texts composed of parts translated before the join (the room chips, «deleted with {0}», the chord hint), and LRA and PLR as they are. suite-1791.part.

**Decided on the morning of 24.10.2026** (Viktor, relayed by the fork; no patch). EAGLE3 is dropped: «Отбой по EAGLE3. Нет смысла в этом.» (K 2 gave 1.77 codes a round; the draft head refuses CFG; heresy/MISC/claude-eagle3 kept as a record). The decode ladder is kept, as a Creator option beside the standard decode, at the user's own risk: «Если вшиваем Лесенку, давай как опцию в Творце…»; by his ear 20 s chunks with 2 s of lookahead and overlap «отлично», the whole song «хорошо», 10 s chunks «говно», no lookahead «треш в склейке» (heresy/MISC/claude-ladder). The window anew (1757): «нужно ещё тестить»; drums came in inside the window where the original has none. All three in TODO.md.

**The Babel window over a run from elsewhere** (1765; Viktor 24.10.2026: «У меня само выскочило по Вавилону… написала нада модель IRAEv2 и LoRA адаптер, но не смог прочитать в ночном режиме темы»). At 06:11 the wave's watcher sent 40 ring probes through the API on the bare BF16 with the FW 2.9 save; every score broke, and his page, which reads the engine's log, opened the Babel window over one of them. It said «On IRAEv2»: a run's record took the page's own backbone, never the one the run names; now the run's own when it names one. Its buttons (a new music seed, the strengths into the green) would have changed his Creator's form for a run that was not his: a run from elsewhere (the API, another browser, a probe) that stops on a broken score is now said in a toast, at most one a minute, with its title; no window, nothing of the form touched. The adapters' rows were dark red on dark at 12.5 px (#c8372d on #282828, about 2.6:1): the adapter and its strength now in the text's own colour at 13 px, the zone alone coloured by the theme's red and green, and an amber that reads on dark and on light. One key in each of the eleven catalogs; suite-1765.part.

**A slow answer keeps the run the page's own** (1766; the kitchen's question on 1765). A reload is safe: the page keeps its own live runs by id and finds them again in the log's replay, so they never go provisional. But a run whose POST answered more than 700 ms after the log showed it was first taken for someone else's and, with 1765, marked external; registerJob then claimed it back and left the mark, so a broken score on the user's own run would have been a toast, not the window. registerJob now clears it. suite-1766.part.

**A take made again is made on the backbone it was sung on** (1767; Viktor 24.10.2026: «Добро, гони все 53 регена»). Before the regens of the 💎 takes under 90 s the fork found that 47 of the 53 requests name no backbone: they were sung on the bare BF16 when it was the default, and /regen would have sung them on today's default, IRAEv2, putting other takes in the 💎 sets' places. A request that names no backbone now takes the one its meta says (BF16, one of the engine's --model names); one that names its backbone keeps it. The Keys Probe («keys-probe-…», 95 s requests that ended at 85–89.8 s) joins the probes' full length, 120 s with at least 90. 29 of the 53 were already 90.0 s, the 1165 minimum, so only the 18 Keys Probe takes are made again. lab/test_regen_backbone.py, 5 checks.

**The decode ladder, a Creator option** (1768; Viktor 24.10.2026: «Делай это опцией Sampling блоке Творца. Везде в остальных местах Студии пусть остаётся оригинальный стабильный предсказуемый декод. А этот в тултипе подсказки пометь как экспериментальный и отличающийся выхлопом»). «Sound decode» in Sampling › Sound and output, after Peak clip: *Whole song · standard* (the default) or *Ladder · experimental*, its tip saying the output differs. The engine's request field nar_ladder: the sound half in even pieces of about 20 s (no short tail), each solved over a window that hears 2 s past both its edges, neighbours joined by complementary half-cosines 1 s either side of their common edge in their latents; the noise kept until every piece is solved; written into the take's request only when on. Off, or a song of one piece, as before to the byte: the fork's CPU check (16 threads, base-Q8) found a replay of 300 codes and a 30 s song from nothing md5-identical with and without the patch; a ladder replay of ru-01 byte-identical to the probe Viktor called «отлично». Everywhere else the standard decode: the player's re-renders (New sound, Trim, End at, fades, VAE version, Regenerate from a moment, Extend) drop it, and so do Replace a section, Sing once more and the window anew (section.py); /regen keeps the take's own request; the lab's API never passes it. The engine went live first, at 15:10:34 in the fork's window (both binaries prebuilt, the Studio restarted between jobs), then the page and the lab. Not in it yet: the pieces solved while the music half sings on, and streaming. lab/test_section_ladder.py; suite-1768.part (8 checks); the mock keeps the field (all three copies); the guide ×12 in «Sound and output»; four keys in each of the eleven catalogs.

**A run trains on a base it names** (1792). The Trainer took its weights by the quant alone, the studio's own bf16 or int8 file, so a run could not start from a merge; spec «base» names a .safetensors file of checkpoints/comfy/checkpoints/ (no folders, there already, in the trainer's own layout), said in the run's options (lab/test_train_base.py). A merge folded into that layout was checked three ways: loaded strictly by the trainer, each delta strength·B·A within bf16, and equal to the GGUF merge of the same adapter and strengths to one bf16 step.

**The score guard and the held-out reasons in every language** (1793; TODO 2.0.0 #5). The harvest of 24.10.2026 at 20:45 over the kitchen suite after 1765–1768 saw 15 825 texts; 13 were new by shape, and three of them were misses that stayed English in every language: the score guard window's zone column («past its limit 0.45», «amber (green to 0.3)», «green», written raw; now through tr(), «green» as «green zone» so that no bare word becomes a key), the engine's reasons a score is broken (pipeline.h: «no key line (K:)», «the meter is not a meter (M:…)» and six more, each a key or a pattern), and the Trainer's held-out «worth hearing first» reasons (1736), which carry their numbers (a pattern each). Sixteen keys in each of the eleven catalogs; suite-1793.part.

**The DAW and the piano roll, a beta** (1795; Viktor 25.10.2026, the 2.0.0 briefing: «Наш DAW и Piano Roll в разы лучше, чем у Plenio. Так что всё это идёт уже в 2.0.0, но как beta»). A «beta» badge, the kind badge's look in amber, with one tip (we make our songs there every day, it is still growing, what it keeps and how it looks may change in a later release): on the DAW's step in the Refiner's list and in its summary, on its canvas node, beside its window's title (the title's own text as it was: 1506 reads it), and in the piano roll's head after its title. «beta» and the tip in the eleven catalogs (Russian «бета», Japanese «ベータ»…). The Plenio bundle of the TODO closes with it: ours instead. suite-1795.part; the kitchen suite 1275/0.

**Ruach Studio's own fine-tune, the studio's default** (1794; Viktor 25.10.2026: «Все шесть видов мёрджей финализируй и начинай заливать на HF. Привязывай все эти варианты в Студию и в downloader»; «2.4 ep - самый безопасный, и пусть он будет базовой моделью»). YuE2-RS-FW folded into m-a-p's base at seven points of its training, epoch x strength (0.7x0.75, 1.5x0.75, 2.4x0.75, 3.4x0.7, 3.4x0.75, 4.0x0.65, 4.0x0.7), each at BF16, Q8_0 and Q6_K, from goldhub/YuE2-RS-Finetunes (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; the adapters themselves are not published). lab/weights.py: the extra «fw» in Engine → Models, all 21 files, the default noted; the part «fw» the install brings, RS-FW 2.4x0.75 at the quant of m-a-p's backbone here (BF16, Q8_0 or Q6_K: there is no Q5_K_M of it, so a Q5_K_M install keeps m-a-p's alone), its size in the card and in the fetch's progress (test_fw_weights.py). fetch-heresy.sh: that part, and --fw MERGE-QUANT for any of the seven, asked with its size. start.sh: with no model named (YUE2CPP_QUANT, settings.json), RS-FW 2.4x0.75 starts, the fullest quant here; a model named is kept (test-start-1794.sh, nine cases). The page knew a merge «RS-…» by its file's name already (1490c, 1689): its words in the eleven catalogs, and the guides' Compute line in twelve languages. Lesson: the lab's first reload answered /weights «'list' object is not callable» for two minutes: the new function parts() was a list inside _last_run, which keeps one of that name; renamed parts_of, and the test now reads a last run. suite-1794.part.

**The workspace column's two headings in the chapter look** (1796; Viktor 25.10.2026: «В колонке пространств "С глаз долой" раздел пометь полоской "главы". Также и "Пространства", чтобы было видно разделение»). Workspaces and Out of sight carry 1683's tick, the accent dissolved 40 % → 60 % → 40 %, with a little more air above: the places, the workspaces and what is out of sight read as three parts. CSS alone, both themes by their tokens. suite-1796.part.

**The DAW over the whole window carries its «beta»** (1797; found by the 2.0.0 screenshots, 25.10.2026). The DAW's «beta» (1795) in its own head while it is over the whole window, where the drawer's summary that carries it is hidden. Found beside it, not mended: painted while its drawer was shut, the console measures 0 px (--mx-dock-h), so over the whole window the lanes keep their height and the console stands under the window's edge until something paints it again (its own button twice does); setFull asking it to paint did not change that in the kitchen (1280/1), so it was taken out before the ship and the finding went to the TODO (2.0.x). The screenshot tool now uses the page's own full screen and presses the console's button twice. suite-1797.part.

**2.0.0 cut and published** (25.10.2026; Viktor: «Все шесть видов мёрджей финализируй и начинай заливать на HF. Привязывай все эти варианты в Студию и в downloader. Перепроверяй срез и заливай на github и HF, и на сайт визитку»). Ruach Studio's own fine-tune in goldhub/YuE2-RS-Finetunes (b29378f: seven merges at BF16, Q8_0, Q6_K and BF16 safetensors, 148.3 GB), pinned and wired (1794); VERSION 2.0.0, the CHANGELOG dated, README's Done › 2.0.0, the card in twelve languages. From a clean clone of the release tree: the page 1280/0, the player 3/0, Pinokio 74/0, the places 26/0, the private scan 0. Ruach_Studio.Release ba7fec4, tag v2.0.0, the release https://github.com/igrbible/Ruach_Studio/releases/tag/v2.0.0 (the latest, not a pre-release) with its tarball (SHA-256 2ee35112…), the visiting card on 2.0.0 in twelve languages. The first stable release; what holds back goes to 2.0.x and 2.1.0.

**The ring on the visiting card** (1798; Viktor 25.10.2026, after the cut: the ring on the card with a proper player for its samples, not the browser's own). The fork built and applied it (heresy/MISC/site-ring: twenty cards, five sides each, SUNO v6, musicful V3, the stock YuE2-3B and RS-FW at two saves, every file at the same loudness; the player with its wave, wavesurfer.js vendored in assets/; the means table and its note); the 41 new units in the eleven languages here (ring-1798.py: the nav from each language's own with «The ring» after «Honestly», the genres translated inside their markup, the names as they are); build.py: no unit still English. The audio (100 MP3s with their peaks and ring.json, 354 MB) goes up to /ring/ on the card before the pages.

**The studio's models never read from a cache** (1799; Viktor 26.10.2026: «По UniverSR, Apollo и FlashSR и остальному ... это наш продакшн модели, и их нужно из кешей вывести в правильные папки Студии, привязать в HF репозитории и в загрузчики… В идеале удаление .cache и hf_cache никогда не должно сломать ни одного из пайплайнов Студии»). UniverSR comes only from universr/: upscale_job.py fetches it there itself when an install lacks it (the studio's copy in goldhub/Ruach_Studio_Models_v2 at its pin, else woongzip1's at his), never from hf_cache/; the upscale part of fetch-heresy.sh copies an older install's woongzip1's out of hf_cache/ into universr/ (the same bytes as v2's) and checks it by the pin, and hf_cached, whose only caller that was, is gone. Apollo-vocal's checkpoint and FlashSR's weights, when the studio's copies are missing, come from their authors into models/upbundle/ as well, not into the hub's cache. heresy/MISC/claude-arc/no_cache_check.sh loads every model the pipelines load, the way their jobs load them, with HF_HOME, XDG_CACHE_HOME and TORCH_HOME empty and the hub offline: UniverSR, Apollo, FlashSR, Whisper, MERT, the separators of the stems and of the voice's cleaning (seven), the prompt writer, the content filter, Qwen-Image 2512 and 2.1, all clean (26.10.2026); the three upscalers also took a 15 s clip through on a 3090 that way. On the Forge the studio's hf_cache/ (7.3 GB: woongzip1's UniverSR, the Kit's CLAP and MERT, the first copies of Apollo and FlashSR) went to /Trash. Unchanged: the three are in the studio's own repo and loader since 1445, 1583 and 1611; nothing new is mirrored there.

**What still said or did SDXL and Krea 2** (1800; Viktor 26.10.2026: «Ищи, где ещё в нашем коде подвязан SDXL и к хуям подчищай это непотребство. Мы отказались от SDXL и от Krea2 в пользу QWEN IMAGE 2512 и 2.1»). Searched through the lab, the page, the root, the guides, the loaders and the Forge's disk: nothing paints with either since 1405 and 1428. What was left, now gone: the lab's API said «a Krea 2 Muse picture» for a take's artwork and the MCP tool «Krea 2 Muse paints it» (now Qwen-Image); Civitai's fetch still named Krea 2 a family (a Krea 2 adapter was offered a folder no painter reads) and the LoRA loader kept Krea 2's key names; the Artist guide in twelve languages listed `krea2` among the adapters' families; the headers of art_job.py, artist.py and artist_job.py and the artwork's notes in lab.py described the gone painters as the present ones, and ART_NEED_MB (SDXL's room) was read by nothing. Kept on purpose: the LoRA library still knows an SDXL or Krea 2 adapter by its layers, to say it is not for these painters; the gallery keeps the name of a picture Krea 2 painted; the Writer's style tags are still written «as an SD/SDXL prompt is» (a way of writing, not a painter); launcher/update.sh still offers to clear an older install's SDXL and Krea 2 folders; their history in HERESY.md, the CHANGELOG and the code's notes. Left for a page patch: 26 entries in each of the page's eleven dictionaries whose English the page no longer says (never shown).

**The evening's Studio orders, the night T1 started** (1802; Viktor 27.10.2026). *The star after a like* («после того как я поставил лайк… по клику на звёздочку нужно ждать иногда до 10 секунд, пока станет активной»): a like's answer is the whole list as the lab read it when the like was asked, and a star set while it travelled was put out again by it until the next reload; what the page sets (the star, 🔥, 💎) is now held as the ratings were, by its turn, over any older list, and the star lights at the click (its server's word confirms it or takes it back). *🔥 and 💎* («Может давай повсюду в Студии прикрутим к картам треков 🔥 и 💎? После кнопки thumbup?»): two marks of a take's own after 👍 on the Librarian's cards and sheet, the Creator's takes, the take's head, the player and the playlist's cards; the lab keeps them (collection.json fire and gems, op mark) and the filter's Marked group has them. *Notes* («в воркспейсы и их подкатегории прикрутим Notes… к LoRA адаптерам заметки пользовательские… ограничить длину заметок разумными 500-600 знаков»): a workspace's or a section's note (⋯ → Note…, op ws-note; it follows a rename, goes with a delete, moves to a merge's target when that has none), its tip in the column and its name underlined with dots; the user's note on an adapter (right-click → Your note…, op lora-note, GET /collection/lora-notes), under a line in its tip, one note for its bf16 and its q8; every note field 600 characters, a short one counting its characters. *A double-click* on a card's ground plays it or stops it; the sounding take's round button is filled, its sign inverted. *On a reload* the Librarian's active workspace stands in the middle of its column. *The Librarian's foot* (his picture at 23:24, «Сдвинулось над плеером вверх после переключения с Творца»): while the Trainer's bars stand (T1 had started), 1653's spacer under the layout left a band above the player and the sticky column rode up with the layout's end, as 1733 had cured for the room's own 60 px; the cards' grid carries that room now under its last card, measured on his window (993 px, scrolled to the end): the column ends 16 px above the player, it ended 138. *The cards' Refiner badges* («На карточках наш Огранщик рисует свои прометки, но что это — одному Клоду известно»): the filter's flags were named DERIVED too and declared over the badges' names in the same scope, so a card said «1» for stems, Debuzz, a remaster or an upscale, and «daw», «gate», «plugin» raw; the filter's map is HAS now, every kind has its name (the DAW mix, the Digital Gateway, a plugin) and «Made from it in the Refiner: …» under the pointer, and the filter's Has group lists the three. *The ♪ sheet's First Wave tab* («В Студию ты шпаргалку по First Wave оформил?»): the vocabulary page F1 links to, in the page's language and tone, the user's own vocabularies (user/vocab/, GET /vocab/) laid over it. *RU*: «ран» → «прогон» in all its forms, on the page (89 lines) and in the guide (23) (his: «омонимизм»). *The guide*: «A Voice of Your Own: Questions from the Field» in the Trainer's chapter, twelve languages, the answers to manchuwook (issue #2) kept («Такое грех терять»). The suite told the new rules (the take's head, «прогон», nineteen ticks); uk and be say «створено», the root «роб» kept out. MISC/claude-arc/apply1802.py, suite1802.py, guide1802.py, lib-gap.mjs.

**A take starts before all of it has come, on a slow line** (1803; Viktor 28.10.2026: «У нас сейчас кешируется весь первый трек. На удалёнке время занимает. Что, если начинать воспроизведение с первой минуты закешированной?»). 1746 holds a take whole before ▶ (his line tore streams). Now, on a line slow enough that the whole would keep ▶ waiting, the take starts once its first minute is in and the rest will come before the playing reaches it, by the measured pace with a margin; a fast line (the whole within 3 s) waits for the whole as before. What came plays as one file. Measured first in headless Chrome: a FLAC cut short says the whole take's length and, where its bytes end, jumps to that end and fires ended, and a seek past them stands silent. So a longer piece goes in its place 20 s before it runs out and the whole when it is in, each at the quietest moment of the wave ahead (its peaks, up to 40 s on). A line that falls behind, or a seek past what came, waits on the bar as the hold did and goes on from where it stood. While the take playing still comes, the next one's prefetch waits for it (one line, one download), and the stream no longer starts beside the hold. Tested on a line throttled to 1.2 MB/s, a 4-minute take (47 MB): it started at 10.9 s instead of about 40, its time never stood but once, 0.3 s where the whole took its place. Engine → Defaults → Start playing: «when the whole take is in» is 1746 as it was. window.ruachEarly for the checks; MISC/claude-arc/apply1803.py, flac-part.mjs, early-test.mjs.

**Engine → Defaults; the server log's head** (1804; Viktor 28.10.2026: «подумай, что можно добавить в Engine в определение юзерских дефолтов. Например ПРЯТАТЬ ДИЗЛАЙКИ или нет, перекрутка по треку в плеере кнопкой вправо/влево… Дать юзеру максимальную свободу изменять дефолты в Engine настройках… Дефолты выставляем как у нас сейчас в Студии»). A card in Engine: the ⏪ ⏩ step (15 s), the ← → keys (5 s) and Shift + ← → (30 s), when a take starts playing (1803), a click on a take plays it, new takes play when done, a double-click on a card plays or stops it (1802), disliked takes shown in the lists; each a yue2.* key (user/settings.json keeps it as every key), empty being the studio's own, and the player's and the lists' own switches set the same keys; «The studio's defaults» clears them; the seek buttons' tips say the steps. The server log's head (his picture: «Forge · 540 мс» under the Russian tabs): its tab keeps 220 px and clips its own, the buttons wrap under it; and its buttons are icons now («захерачь понятные и внятные svg иконки»): Follow (lit while it follows), full height, copy, Engine, a window of its own (Lucide's copy, cog, arrow-down-to-line, app-window; ISC). MISC/claude-arc/apply1804.py, logdock-test.mjs.

**The studio's https door** (1805; Viktor 28.10.2026: «по твоему совету с прошлой недели касательно самописного ssl сертификата для Студии... прикручивай, но не на всю Студию, а только на удалённый MIDI-контроллер и для будущих имплементаций»). A browser gives Web MIDI (and the clipboard, an AudioWorklet) only to a secure page, and the studio is http on its port. lab/tls_door.py is a second way in beside it, changing nothing of it: https on 7443 with a certificate the studio makes for itself (user/tls/, ten years, for its names and addresses), passing every byte to the studio's port and back. Who may come in is Engine → Security's answer (user/access.json, the engine's rule repeated by the visitor's own address, since through the door everyone would reach the studio from this computer); a refused one gets a page saying why. systemd/ruach-tls-door.service (install-units.sh writes it; started only when asked; on the Forge it is on). Checked: from the laptop over the VPN the page and the lab answer through it; headless Chrome there says a secure context with navigator.requestMIDIAccess (the grant is the user's click). The MIDI card names the door first, ssh -L beside it.

**The Trainer's tab of the log says the run's way** (1806; his picture: «ВО вкладке Тренера ничего нет, тогда как в других есть инфа»). A run said only its start and its end, and on a busy engine the start scrolled out of the log's kept lines within the hour; now a running training says a line at each new percent of its steps, or every 20 minutes, with the time it has left (lab.py act_watch).

**Not on free cloud GPUs; what the ear chose** (1807; Viktor 28.10.2026: «на всех наших карточках чёрным по белому умельцев юзать гугловские GPU халявные предупреди, что у них нихрена с нашей Студией там не получится. Слишком массивная она у нас»; and, the ring heard: «сваять статистику моих прослушек и дописать в карточки то, что мы пообещали»). The warning under the declaration on the five Hub cards (the merges, IRA, the models, the LoRAs, the starter sets), in the README, and in «What you need» on the visiting card in its twelve languages (live on Orpheus; the README's push to GitHub waits for his hand: the permission classifier held it, MISC/claude-arc/cards-1807/push1807.sh). The First Wave's card says what the blind ring gave: 20 styles, 232 takes marked one by one outside industrial metal (all eight on fire there), 226 heard; per heard take 2.4 × 0.75 2.36, 0.7 × 0.75 2.34, 3.4 (music 0.75, sound 0.6) 2.27, 3.4 × 0.75 2.17, 1.5 × 0.75 2.10, 4.0 × 0.7 1.93, 3.4 × 0.7 1.79, 4.0 × 0.65 1.76; the gaps within chance (p 0.67); 2.4 × 0.75 stays the default. MISC/claude-arc/ear/ring_final.py, cards-1807/cards1807.py.

**A workspace moved into another** (1808; Viktor 28.10.2026: «добавь подалуйста в функционал воркспейсов `Move into ... Workspace` > `Move as a subfolder` / `Move just the samples`»). A workspace's menu has «Move into…»: the workspace and the place it goes into, then as a subfolder (it goes whole: its own sections, its note, its locks, its frozen mark) or only its samples (they join the other, the family left empty). The lab's ws-pour op; a frozen or locked workspace refuses as the other ops do. Checked on throwaway workspaces, gone after. MISC/claude-arc/apply1808.py.

**The log's own window has every view, and the dock folds for it** (1809; Viktor 28.10.2026: «Лог сервера в отдельном окне имеет только Сервер и Художник. Синхронизируй там все вкладки и при отсоединении фрейма серверного лога схлопывай наш разворачивающийся логгер»). The window has the dock's five views (Server, Creator, Trainer, Refiner, the Artist), a room's view the server's lines of that room as in the dock, with its «nothing from this room yet»; detached, the dock folds whatever it was and leaves its full height; while the window is open a click on the dock brings the window up; closed, the dock comes back as it was. MISC/claude-arc/apply1809.py; the suite's check of the window now counts five views.

**Engine: Writer and Artwork the other way round; its chapters as the Creator's; Defaults laid out** (1810; Viktor 28.10.2026, his pictures: «Нелогичная ширина колонок. Наоборот я бы сделал… В Engine сделай "Главы" так же красиво, как в Творце. И в ШАГ иконки emoji замени на реальные плеерные, И нарисуй svg иконки клавиш-стрелок клавиатуры»). The Artwork card wider than the Writer's (its painters' names are long); the Engine's chapter heads (h4.mini) with the Creator's chapter look (1683: the .label's size, weight and colour, the accent tick); Defaults as Appearance (two parts under a thin line, the three seek steps on one row, a field no wider than its words); «Step» with the player's own ⏪ ⏩, the keys drawn as key caps (←, →, Shift's ⇧; heresy-icons.js key-left, key-right, key-shift). MISC/claude-arc/apply1810.py, engine-shot.mjs.

**The studio's own Writer** (1811; Viktor 28.10.2026: «Не многие смогут настроить LLM для Писателя. У меня есть очень хороший квант QWEN 3.5-9B, а именно Qwopus3.5-9B… забирай копию этого кванта в Студию, прикручивай как Local Studio LLM, через llama.cpp… и пусть это будет опция по умолчанию для установок, где VRAM позволяет. Так не нужно будет юзеру коннектиться на внешние сервисы»; «перепакуй тензоры, выбрасывай vision голову»; «дай выбор юзеру выбрать либо мой качественный квант, либо более сжатый, чтобы в 8 или 12GB точно лёг»).
